import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Navigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { TopBar } from "../components/TopBar";
import { useToast } from "../components/toastContext";
import { useAuth } from "../hooks/authContext";
import { api } from "../lib/api";
import { PREDICTIONS_ENABLED } from "../lib/features";
import { Button, Card, Num, SectionHeading } from "../components/ui";
import { haptic } from "../lib/telegram";

interface AdminStats {
  totalUsers: number;
  totalMatches: number;
  totalPredictions: number;
  totalFavorites: number;
}

export default function Admin() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { push } = useToast();
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<AdminStats | null>(null);
  const [syncing, setSyncing] = useState(false);
  const [broadcasting, setBroadcasting] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.isAdmin) return;
    void api
      .getAdminStats()
      .then((res) => setStats(res.stats))
      .catch(() => {});
  }, [user?.isAdmin]);

  if (!user?.isAdmin) return <Navigate to="/matches" replace />;

  const handleSync = async () => {
    haptic("light");
    setSyncing(true);
    try {
      await api.adminSync();
      push({ text: "ESPN sync complete", tone: "volt", icon: "âœ“" });
    } catch {
      push({ text: "Sync failed", tone: "danger", icon: "âœ•" });
    } finally {
      setSyncing(false);
    }
  };

  const handleBroadcast = async () => {
    if (!message.trim() || broadcasting) return;
    haptic("medium");
    setBroadcasting(true);
    try {
      const res = await api.adminBroadcast(message.trim());
      push({ text: `Broadcast sent to ${res.sent} users`, tone: "volt", icon: "âœ“" });
      setMessage("");
    } catch {
      push({ text: "Broadcast failed", tone: "danger", icon: "âœ•" });
    } finally {
      setBroadcasting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <TopBar title="Admin Dashboard" />
      <div className="mt-4 space-y-4">
        <section>
          <SectionHeading title="Stats" />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <StatCard label="Total Users" value={stats?.totalUsers} />
            <StatCard label="Total Matches" value={stats?.totalMatches} />
            {PREDICTIONS_ENABLED ? (
              <StatCard label="Total Predictions" value={stats?.totalPredictions} />
            ) : null}
            <StatCard label="Total Favorites" value={stats?.totalFavorites} />
          </div>
        </section>

        <section>
          <SectionHeading title="ESPN Sync" />
          <Card className="space-y-3 p-3">
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-2">Trigger a full scoreboard + standings sync immediately.</p>
            <Button onClick={() => void handleSync()} disabled={syncing} className="w-full">
              {syncing ? "Syncingâ€¦" : "Force ESPN Sync"}
            </Button>
          </Card>
        </section>

        <section>
          <SectionHeading title="Broadcast" />
          <Card className="space-y-3 p-3">
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-2">Send a message to all Telegram users.</p>
            <input
              className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-card border border-line bg-surface px-3.5 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-3 focus:border-volt focus:outline-none"
              placeholder="Message to broadcastâ€¦"
              value={message}
              onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}
              maxLength={4096}
            />
            <Button
              onClick={() => void handleBroadcast()}
              disabled={broadcasting || !message.trim()}
              className="w-full"
            >
              {broadcasting ? "Sendingâ€¦" : "Broadcast to All Users"}
            </Button>
          </Card>
        </section>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

function StatCard({ label, value }: { label: string; value?: number }) {
  return (
    <Card className="p-3">
      <p className="text-xs text-ink-3">{label}</p>
      <Num className="mt-0.5 text-lg text-ink">{value !== undefined ? value.toLocaleString() : "â€”"}</Num>
    </Card>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\pages\Cups.tsx =====

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { TopBar } from "../components/TopBar";
import { CompRowSkeleton, MatchCardSkeleton } from "../components/Skeletons";
import {
  Chip,
  CollapsibleSection,
  EmptyState,
  ErrorState,
  LiveDot,
  Num,
  SectionHeading,
  Segmented,
} from "../components/ui";
import { buildBracket, type BracketTie } from "../lib/bracket";
import { useKnockoutCompetitions, useMatches } from "../hooks/useApi";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { competitionName } from "../lib/leagues";
import { formatKickoff } from "../lib/format";
import { haptic } from "../lib/telegram";
import type { Match } from "../types";

/**
 * Knockout competitions: live scores and fixtures for the club cups â€” the three
 * UEFA competitions, UCL qualifying and the Super Cup, plus the big five's six
 * domestic cups â€” and the international tournaments (national teams). Sourced
 * from the same sync as the leagues and selected on the server's `kind`
 * (anything that is not a league), so promoting a competition into the catalog
 * adds it here with no client change. No table here â€” knockout only.
 *
 * A small Cups / International toggle swaps the competition strip, because one
 * fifteen-item scroller mixing club cups with World Cup qualifying is hard to
 * find things in. The toggle follows a deep link, so Browse's tournament rows
 * open straight onto the International tab.
 */
export default function Cups() {
  const { t, lang, localize } = useI18n();

  const compsQuery = useKnockoutCompetitions();
  const comps = compsQuery.data ?? [];
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<"cup" | "tournament" | null>(null);

  // Deep link from Browse (?cup=uefa.champions) wins until the user picks one.
  // Default: the Champions League until the picker loads.
  const linked = searchParams.get("cup");
  const active = picked ?? linked ?? comps[0]?.slug ?? "uefa.champions";
  // The toggle follows whatever competition is active, so Browse's tournament
  // rows open onto International instead of an empty club-cup strip.
  const tab = (kind ?? comps.find((c) => c.slug === active)?.kind ?? "cup") as "cup" | "tournament";
  const visible = comps.filter((c) => c.kind === tab);
  const matchesQuery = useMatches({ league: active });
  // Memoized so the identity stays stable across renders: the derived `byRound`
  // map below depends on it, and a fresh `[]` every render would rebuild it.
  const matches = useMemo(() => matchesQuery.data ?? [], [matchesQuery.data]);

  const live = matches.filter((m) => m.status === "live");
  const scheduled = matches.filter((m) => m.status === "scheduled");
  const finished = matches.filter((m) => m.status === "finished");

  // Bulk expand/collapse for the bracket rounds. null = no override, so every
  // round uses its own persisted fold state; the control only shows when
  // there's a bracket, and flips back to null whenever a round is toggled
  // singly (the override is transient, the per-round choice is the truth).
  const [roundsOverride, setRoundsOverride] = useState<boolean | null>(null);

  // Knockout structure from the sync's round labels: rounds in play order,
  // two-legged pairings folded into one tie with an aggregate, winners
  // inferred (honestly undecided when penalties decided a level tie), and
  // feeder links connecting each tie back to the round that produced it â€”
  // that is what turns five flat fixture lists into one bracket. Cups with
  // no round data fall back to the status sections below, unchanged.
  const bracket = useMemo(() => {
    if (matchesQuery.isLoading || matchesQuery.isError) return { rounds: [], connected: false };
    return buildBracket(matches);
  }, [matches, matchesQuery.isLoading, matchesQuery.isError]);

  return (
    <>
      <TopBar title={t("cups.title")} subtitle={t("cups.subtitle")} back />

      {compsQuery.isLoading ? (
        <RowPlaceholder />
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-3 space-y-2">
          <Segmented
            value={tab}
            onChange={(next) => {
              haptic("light");
              setKind(next);
              const first = comps.find((c) => c.kind === next);
              if (first) setPicked(first.slug);
            }}
            options={[
              { value: "cup", label: t("browse.cups") },
              { value: "tournament", label: t("browse.tournaments") },
            ]}
          />
          <Segmented
            value={active}
            onChange={(next) => {
              haptic("light");
              setPicked(next);
            }}
            options={visible.map((c) => ({ value: c.slug, label: localize(c.name) }))}
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Name the competition the fixtures below belong to â€” without it a strip
          of knockout fixtures has no context. */}
      <h2 className="mt-4 text-md font-extrabold text-ink">
        {localize(comps.find((c) => c.slug === active)?.name ?? "") ||
          competitionName(active, lang)}
      </h2>

      {bracket.rounds.length > 0 ? (
        /* Round structure when the sync carries it: live first, then the
           bracket from the first round to the final. */
        <>
          {matchesQuery.isLoading ? (
            <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2">
              <MatchCardSkeleton />
            </ul>
          ) : null}
          {matchesQuery.isError ? (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <ErrorState onRetry={() => matchesQuery.refetch()} />
            </div>
          ) : null}
          {live.length > 0 ? (
            <section className="mt-4">
              <SectionHeading
                title={t("matches.live")}
                action={
                  <Chip tone="live">
                    <LiveDot />
                    <Num>{live.length}</Num>
                  </Chip>
                }
              />
              <CupMatchList matches={live} />
            </section>
          ) : null}
          {bracket.rounds.length > 1 ? (
            // Bulk control for the bracket: folds or unfolds every round at
            // once. The label names the action ahead â€” "Expand all" once the
            // rounds are collapsed, "Collapse all" otherwise.
            <div className="flex justify-end">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  haptic("light");
                  setRoundsOverride((prev) => (prev === null ? false : !prev));
                }}
                className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-lg border border-line px-3 text-xs font-bold text-ink-2 transition-[color,background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated hover:text-ink active:scale-[0.97]"
              >
                {roundsOverride === false ? t("common.expandAll") : t("common.collapseAll")}
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : null}
          {bracket.rounds.map((round) => {
            const legCount = round.ties.reduce((n, tie) => n + tie.legs.length, 0);
            return (
              // Collapsible like the status sections; the fold state keys off
              // the round label, so collapsing "Qualifying" once keeps it
              // folded in every cup. Toggling one round releases the bulk
              // override so the per-round choice is the ongoing truth.
              <CollapsibleSection
                key={round.label}
                id={`cups-round-${roundId(round.label)}`}
                level={2}
                title={localize(round.label)}
                count={legCount}
                openOverride={roundsOverride === null ? undefined : { open: roundsOverride }}
                onUserToggle={roundsOverride === null ? undefined : () => setRoundsOverride(null)}
              >
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {round.ties.map((tie) => (
                    <TieCard key={tie.key} tie={tie} />
                  ))}
                </div>
              </CollapsibleSection>
            );
          })}
        </>
      ) : (
        /* No round labels in the window: the original status sections. */
        <>
          {/* "Live now" only exists when something is live â€” an empty heading
              with a "Try again" button reads as a failure that never happened.
              Loading/error still render (they are states, not emptiness). */}
          {matchesQuery.isLoading ? (
            <section className="mt-4">
              <SectionHeading title={t("matches.live")} />
              <ul className="space-y-2">
                <MatchCardSkeleton />
              </ul>
            </section>
          ) : matchesQuery.isError ? (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <ErrorState onRetry={() => matchesQuery.refetch()} />
            </div>
          ) : live.length > 0 ? (
            <section className="mt-4">
              <SectionHeading
                title={t("matches.live")}
                action={
                  <Chip tone="live">
                    <LiveDot />
                    <Num>{live.length}</Num>
                  </Chip>
                }
              />
              <CupMatchList matches={live} />
            </section>
          ) : null}

          <section className="mt-6">
            <SectionHeading title={t("matches.upcoming")} />
            {scheduled.length > 0 ? (
              <CupMatchList matches={scheduled} />
            ) : (
              <EmptyState icon="ðŸ†" title={t("cups.empty")} hint={t("cups.emptyHint")} />
            )}
          </section>

          {finished.length > 0 ? (
            <CollapsibleSection
              id="cups-finished"
              level={2}
              title={t("matches.previous")}
              count={finished.length}
            >
              <CupMatchList matches={finished} />
            </CollapsibleSection>
          ) : null}
        </>
      )}

      <p className="mt-6 text-center text-xs text-ink-3">{t("cups.noTable")}</p>
    </>
  );
}

/**
 * Cup fixture row: teams, score/clock, summary link. The whole footer is
 * the touch surface â€” the link meets the 44px floor inside it.
 * In `bare` mode the row drops its own card chrome: a TieCard supplies the
 * border and the rows become the tie's legs, separated by hairlines.
 */
function CupMatchList({ matches, bare = false }: { matches: Match[]; bare?: boolean }) {
  const { t, lang, localize } = useI18n();
  return (
    <ul className={bare ? undefined : "space-y-2"}>
      {matches.map((match) => {
        const home = localize(match.home.shortName);
        const away = localize(match.away.shortName);
        return (
          <li
            key={match.id}
            className={
              bare
                ? "border-t border-line first:border-t-0"
                : "rounded-card border border-line bg-surface"
            }
          >
            {/* The whole card is the link: one tap target, no redundant footer
                row that only repeated the card. */}
            <Link
              to={`/match/${match.id}`}
              aria-label={`${home} ${t("common.versus")} ${away}`}
              className="flex min-h-14 items-center gap-2 px-3 py-3 transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated active:bg-elevated"
            >
              <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-end text-sm font-bold text-ink">{home}</span>
              <span className="flex-none">
                {match.status === "live" ? (
                  <Chip tone="live">
                    <LiveDot />
                    <Num>{match.minute ?? ""}</Num>
                  </Chip>
                ) : match.status === "finished" ? (
                  <Chip tone="muted">
                    <Num>
                      {match.homeScore ?? 0}â€“{match.awayScore ?? 0}
                    </Num>
                  </Chip>
                ) : (
                  <Chip tone="volt">
                    <Num>
                      {formatKickoff(new Date(match.kickoffAt), lang === "fa" ? "fa-IR" : undefined)}
                    </Num>
                  </Chip>
                )}
              </span>
              <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">{away}</span>
            </Link>
          </li>
        );
      })}
    </ul>
  );
}

/**
 * One tie of the bracket â€” the node that connects rounds. The card carries
 * the pairing's aggregate header when the tie spans two legs, the legs
 * themselves (bare rows), and a footer that states what the data proves:
 * who advances (an honest "undecided" when penalties decided a level tie)
 * and which round the participants came from â€” the visible edge of the tree.
 * A first-round tie with nothing to say renders as a plain fixture card.
 */
function TieCard({ tie }: { tie: BracketTie }) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const twoLegs = tie.legs.length > 1;
  const showFooter = twoLegs || tie.winnerId != null || tie.feedRound != null;
  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-surface">
      {twoLegs ? (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-line bg-elevated px-3 py-2">
          <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-end text-xs font-bold text-ink">{tie.homeName}</span>
          <span className="label flex-none text-ink-3">
            {t("cups.tieAgg")}{" "}
            <Num className="text-xs text-ink">
              {tie.aggHome ?? "â€“"}â€“{tie.aggAway ?? "â€“"}
            </Num>
          </span>
          <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-xs font-bold text-ink">{tie.awayName}</span>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <CupMatchList matches={tie.legs} bare />
      {showFooter ? (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t border-line px-3 py-2 text-xs">
          {tie.winnerId ? (
            <span className="min-w-0 truncate font-bold text-volt">
              <span aria-hidden="true">â†’ </span>
              {tie.winnerName}
              <span className="sr-only"> â€” {t("cups.winnerAdvances")}</span>
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-ink-3">{t("cups.tiePending")}</span>
          )}
          {tie.feedRound ? (
            <span className="flex-none text-ink-3">
              <span aria-hidden="true">â† </span>
              {localize(tie.feedRound)}
              <span className="sr-only"> â€” {t("cups.feedsFrom", { round: localize(tie.feedRound) })}</span>
            </span>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * Stable, DOM-id-safe key for a round label, so its fold state survives
 * reloads and is shared across cups with the same round naming. Latin labels
 * slugify ("Quarter-final" â†’ "quarter-final"); anything else (Persian round
 * names) falls back to a deterministic hash so the id is still valid HTML and
 * unique.
 */
function roundId(label: string): string {
  const slug = label
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "");
  if (slug) return slug;
  let hash = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < label.length; i += 1) {
    hash = (hash * 31 + label.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;
  }
  return `r${(hash >>> 0).toString(36)}`;
}

function RowPlaceholder() {
  return <CompRowSkeleton rows={3} />;
}

===== D:\football\src\pages\Leaderboards.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type CSSProperties } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { TopBar } from "../components/TopBar";
import { RulesButton } from "../components/RulesSheet";
import { ChartIcon, ChevronDownIcon } from "../components/icons";
import {
  Avatar,
  Chip,
  CountUp,
  EmptyState,
  ErrorState,
  Num,
  SectionHeading,
  Segmented,
} from "../components/ui";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";
import { slugForLeague, useLeaderboard, useLeagueTabs, type LeaderboardScope } from "../hooks/useApi";
import { useFavorites } from "../hooks/useFavorites";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { boardRowView, scopeRankDelta, viewerStanding } from "../lib/leaderboard";

type BoardTab = "global" | "league" | "club";

/**
 * The stakes layer, made visible: season prediction points across the three
 * boards from the concept â€” global, per-league, per-club. Public by design;
 * ties resolved by first-to-reach (the server ranks, we render).
 *
 * A viewer ranked below the board's page limit gets an honest "you" row
 * appended under a divider (server-computed with the board's own tiebreak),
 * so rank 137 is a fact on screen instead of an absence.
 */
export default function Leaderboards() {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const { teams } = useFavorites();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<BoardTab>("global");
  const [league, setLeague] = useState<string>("eng.1");
  const [clubId, setClubId] = useState<string>("");

  const scope: LeaderboardScope =
    tab === "league" ? { league } : tab === "club" && clubId ? { club: clubId } : "global";

  const board = useLeaderboard(scope);

  const rows = board.data?.board ?? [];
  const you = board.data?.you ?? null;
  // The viewer's own placing, whether they sit on the page or below it. It
  // leads the board as a summary: the stakes layer should answer "where do I
  // stand" before it answers "who else is here".
  const standing = viewerStanding(rows, you);

  // Session-scoped rank movement: the board refetches on window focus, so
  // comparing consecutive observations of the SAME scope gives an honest
  // â–²/â–¼ without a server field (scopeRankDelta in lib/leaderboard.ts â€” a
  // scope switch or a repeat observation at the same rank yields no chip).
  const scopeKey = JSON.stringify(scope);
  const prevObsRef = useRef<{ scope: string; rank: number } | null>(null);
  const [rankDelta, setRankDelta] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const standingRank = standing?.rank ?? null;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (standingRank === null) return;
    const delta = scopeRankDelta(prevObsRef.current, scopeKey, standingRank);
    prevObsRef.current = { scope: scopeKey, rank: standingRank };
    setRankDelta(delta);
  }, [standingRank, scopeKey]);

  return (
    <>
      <TopBar
        title={t("board.title")}
        subtitle={t("board.subtitle")}
        right={
          <div className="flex flex-none items-center gap-2">
            {/* Ranks is where ranking-curious users land; Player Stats is the
                same instinct one layer over â€” season stat leaders â€” so it
                gets the navigation slot instead of a tab of its own. */}
            <Link
              to="/stats"
              aria-label={t("statsPage.title")}
              title={t("statsPage.title")}
              className="grid h-11 w-11 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full border border-line text-ink-2 transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated hover:text-volt"
            >
              <ChartIcon />
            </Link>
            <RulesButton />
          </div>
        }
      />

      <div className="mt-3">
        <Segmented
          value={tab}
          onChange={(next) => setTab(next)}
          options={[
            { value: "global", label: t("board.global") },
            { value: "league", label: t("board.league") },
            { value: "club", label: t("board.club") },
          ]}
        />
      </div>

      {tab === "league" ? (
        <section className="mt-4">
          <SectionHeading title={t("board.pickLeague")} />
          <LeaguePicker value={league} onChange={setLeague} />
        </section>
      ) : null}

      {tab === "club" ? (
        <section className="mt-4">
          <SectionHeading title={t("board.pickClub")} />
          {teams.length > 0 ? (
            <ul className="-mx-3 flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-3 pb-1">
              {teams.map((team) => (
                <li key={team.id} className="flex-none">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setClubId(team.id)}
                    className={cx(
                      "flex w-[76px] flex-col items-center gap-1 rounded-card border p-2.5 text-center transition-[background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.97]",
                      clubId === team.id
                        ? "border-volt bg-elevated"
                        : "border-line bg-surface hover:bg-elevated",
                    )}
                  >
                    <span
                      className="grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-full border text-xs font-extrabold"
                      style={{
                        borderColor: `color-mix(in srgb, ${team.color} 55%, transparent)`,
                        backgroundColor: `color-mix(in srgb, ${team.color} 18%, transparent)`,
                      }}
                      aria-hidden="true"
                    >
                      {team.abbreviation}
                    </span>
                    <span className="w-full truncate text-xs font-bold text-ink">
                      {localize(team.shortName)}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          ) : (
            <p className="rounded-card border border-dashed border-line px-3 py-4 text-center text-xs text-ink-3">
              {t("table.noClubs")}
            </p>
          )}
        </section>
      ) : null}

      <section className="mt-4">
        {board.isLoading ? (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="shimmer h-12 rounded-card border border-line" />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : board.isError ? (
          <ErrorState onRetry={() => board.refetch()} />
        ) : tab === "club" && !clubId ? (
          <EmptyState title={t("board.pickClub")} hint={t("board.pickClubHint")} />
        ) : rows.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState title={t("board.empty")} hint={t("board.emptyHint")} />
        ) : (
          <>
            {standing ? (
              <StandingStrip
                rank={standing.rank}
                total={standing.total}
                belowPage={standing.belowPage}
                delta={rankDelta}
              />
            ) : null}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-[2rem_1fr_3.5rem] gap-2 px-3 pb-1 label text-ink-3">
              <span>{t("board.rank")}</span>
              <span>{t("board.player")}</span>
              <span className="text-end">{t("board.points")}</span>
            </div>
            <ul className="space-y-1.5">
              {rows.map((row, index) => {
                // The YOU chip is the viewer's own row and nobody else's â€”
                // boardRowView owns that rule (see lib/leaderboard.ts).
                const view = boardRowView(row, t("board.you"));
                return (
                  <BoardRow
                    key={row.username ?? row.display_name}
                    index={index}
                    rank={row.rank}
                    name={row.display_name}
                    total={row.total}
                    highlight={view.highlight}
                    youLabel={view.youLabel}
                    delta={row.is_you ? rankDelta : null}
                  />
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          </>
        )}
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

/**
 * The viewer's own line, promoted to the top of the board. A list of strangers
 * is honest but inert; leading with "you are #12 on 87" is the fact this screen
 * exists to deliver. Uses the same volt/gold grammar as the highlighted row.
 */
function StandingStrip({
  rank,
  total,
  belowPage,
  delta,
}: {
  rank: number;
  total: number;
  belowPage: boolean;
  /** Session rank movement: positive = climbed, null = no honest comparison. */
  delta?: number | null;
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  return (
    <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-card border border-volt bg-elevated p-3">
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <p className="label text-ink-3">{t("board.yourStanding")}</p>
        <p className="truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">{t("board.you")}</p>
        {belowPage ? <p className="text-xs text-ink-3">{t("board.belowPage")}</p> : null}
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-none items-center gap-4 text-end">
        <div>
          <p className="label text-ink-3">{t("board.rankLabel")}</p>
          {/* Keyed on rank: a movement between focus refetches remounts the
              number, so marker-pop replays instead of the digits just swapping. */}
          <Num key={rank} className="marker-pop inline-block text-lg font-black text-volt">
            #{rank}
          </Num>
          <RankDelta delta={delta} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <p className="label text-ink-3">{t("board.points")}</p>
          <CountUp value={total} className="text-lg font-black text-gold" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * â–²/â–¼ movement since this viewer last saw this board â€” purely session-scoped,
 * so it only ever claims what the client actually observed. The chip mounts
 * (and so pops via marker-pop) only when a delta exists; leaving the board and
 * coming back re-observes and starts fresh.
 */
function RankDelta({ delta }: { delta?: number | null }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  if (!delta) return null;
  const up = delta > 0;
  return (
    <Chip tone={up ? "pitch" : "muted"} className="marker-pop ms-1.5">
      <ChevronDownIcon aria-hidden="true" className={cx("h-3 w-3", up && "rotate-180")} />
      <span className="sr-only">{t(up ? "board.rankUp" : "board.rankDown", { n: Math.abs(delta) })}</span>
      <span aria-hidden="true">
        <Num>{Math.abs(delta)}</Num>
      </span>
    </Chip>
  );
}

function BoardRow({
  index,
  rank,
  name,
  total,
  highlight,
  youLabel,
  delta,
}: {
  index: number;
  rank: number;
  name: string;
  total: number;
  highlight: boolean;
  youLabel?: string;
  /** Session rank movement for this row; only the viewer's row carries one. */
  delta?: number | null;
}) {
  return (
    <li
      className={cx(
        "list-in grid grid-cols-[2rem_1fr_3.5rem] items-center gap-2 rounded-card border p-2.5",
        highlight ? "border-volt bg-elevated" : "border-line bg-surface",
      )}
      style={{ "--stagger": `${Math.min(index, 8) * 40}ms` } as CSSProperties}
    >
      <Num
        // Keyed on rank: a movement between focus refetches remounts the
        // number so marker-pop replays for the viewer's own line.
        key={highlight ? rank : "static"}
        className={cx(
          "text-sm font-bold",
          highlight && "marker-pop",
          rank === 1 ? "text-gold" : rank <= 3 ? "text-ink" : "text-ink-3",
        )}
      >
        {rank}
      </Num>
      <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
        <Avatar name={name} size="sm" />
        {/* dir=auto: display names are user data. Forcing LTR mis-orders
            Persian names and any trailing punctuation; auto picks the
            direction from the name's own first strong character. */}
        <span
          className={cx("min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-bold", highlight ? "text-volt" : "text-ink")}
          dir="auto"
        >
          {name}
        </span>
        {youLabel ? <Chip tone="volt">{youLabel}</Chip> : null}
        <RankDelta delta={delta} />
      </span>
      <CountUp value={total} className="text-end text-md text-gold" />
    </li>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ league picker */

function LeaguePicker({ value, onChange }: { value: string; onChange: (slug: string) => void }) {
  const tabs = useLeagueTabs();
  // Board state is a slug end-to-end; tab values are big-five aliases, so
  // aliases translate back through SLUG_BY_LEAGUE and extras pass through.
  return (
    <Segmented
      value={value}
      onChange={onChange}
      options={tabs.map((tab) => ({
        value: slugForLeague(tab.value),
        label: tab.label,
      }))}
    />
  );
}

import { TopBar } from "../components/TopBar";
import { Card } from "../components/ui";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { LEGAL_SECTIONS, LEGAL_TITLES, type LegalKind } from "../lib/legal";

/**
 * One legal document, rendered from the {title, body} list in lib/legal.ts.
 * Layout is direction-agnostic (logical spacing only), so the same component
 * is correct in EN (ltr) and FA (rtl) without a branch.
 */
export function LegalPage({ kind }: { kind: LegalKind }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  return (
    <>
      <TopBar title={t(LEGAL_TITLES[kind])} />
      <div className="mt-3 space-y-3 pb-8">
        {LEGAL_SECTIONS[kind].map((section) => (
          <Card key={section.title} className="p-4">
            <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-ink">{t(section.title)}</h2>
            <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-ink-2">{t(section.body)}</p>
          </Card>
        ))}
        <p className="px-1 text-xs text-ink-3">{t("legal.updated")}</p>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

}

}

===== D:\football\src\pages\Login.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useState, type CSSProperties, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { Navigate, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { LanguageToggle } from "../components/TopBar";
import { Button, Card, Segmented } from "../components/ui";
import { useAuth } from "../hooks/authContext";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { serverErrorKey } from "../lib/api";
import { isTelegram, haptic, telegramDisplayName } from "../lib/telegram";
import type { StringKey } from "../i18n/strings";

/**
 * The entry screen. "Enter" silently creates (or reuses) an account â€” inside
 * Telegram that's the Telegram identity, on the web a persistent guest
 * account for this device. Username sign-in and registration (username +
 * password) sit behind a link for people returning from another device or
 * creating a roaming account.
 */
export default function Login() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const { user, isLoading, enterApp, loginWithUsername, registerWithUsername } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();
  const from = (location.state as { from?: string } | null)?.from ?? "/";
  const [showUsername, setShowUsername] = useState(false);
  // Sign-in and registration are explicit, separate modes: an unknown
  // username on sign-in is NO_ACCOUNT (never auto-created â€” a typo must not
  // silently mint a new account).
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<"signin" | "register">("signin");
  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<StringKey | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  // The Telegram profile, read once the SDK is present â€” the continue button
  // says who is signing in instead of a generic "Enter".
  const [tgName, setTgName] = useState<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isTelegram()) return;
    void telegramDisplayName().then(setTgName);
  }, []);

  // Already signed in (session survived a reload): skip the screen.
  if (user) {
    return <Navigate to={from} replace />;
  }

  const enter = async () => {
    if (busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    haptic("light");
    try {
      await enterApp();
      // Once the user row lands, the `if (user)` branch above redirects to `from`.
    } catch (err) {
      // Coded failures (BAD_DEVICE_ID, TOO_MANY_REQUESTSâ€¦) map to localized
      // messages; only truly unexpected throws land on common.error.
      setError(serverErrorKey(err) as StringKey);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const submitUsername = async (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    haptic("light");
    try {
      if (mode === "register") {
        await registerWithUsername(username.trim(), password, displayName.trim(), true);
      } else {
        await loginWithUsername(username.trim(), password, true);
      }
      // Once the user row lands, the `if (user)` branch above redirects to `from`.
    } catch (err) {
      // Server codes (INVALID_CREDENTIALS / USERNAME_TAKEN / BAD_USERNAME /
      // BAD_PASSWORDâ€¦) map to localized inline messages, never the generic
      // fallback.
      setError(serverErrorKey(err) as StringKey);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const inputClass =
    "min-h-12 w-full rounded-card border border-line bg-base px-3.5 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-3 focus:border-volt focus:outline-none focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--volt)_16%,transparent)]";

  return (
    <div className="floodlight flex min-h-[100svh] flex-col">
      <header className="pad-safe-top">
        <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-[var(--app-max-width,768px)] justify-end px-[var(--space-page-x,16px)] py-3">
          <LanguageToggle />
        </div>
      </header>

      <main
        className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-[var(--app-max-width,768px)] flex-1 flex-col justify-center px-5"
        style={{ paddingBottom: "calc(40px + max(var(--safe-bottom, env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px)), 8px))" }}
      >
        {/* Hero: the mark over the wordmark, over the floodlit wash. */}
        <section className="rise-in px-1 pb-10 text-center">
          <img
            src="/vkick-mark.svg"
            alt=""
            width={84}
            height={84}
            className="mx-auto mb-3 h-16 w-auto sm:h-[84px]"
          />
          <h1 className="font-num text-[clamp(2.25rem,11vw,3.25rem)] leading-tight font-black tracking-[-0.03em] text-ink">
            {t("app.name")}
          </h1>
          <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-[34ch] text-sm leading-relaxed text-ink-2">{t("auth.subtitle")}</p>
        </section>

        <Card className="rise-in p-4 sm:p-5" style={{ "--stagger": "70ms" } as CSSProperties}>
          {showUsername ? (
            <form onSubmit={submitUsername} className="space-y">
              <Segmented
                value={mode}
                onChange={(next) => {
                  setMode(next);
                  setError(null);
                }}
                options={[
                  { value: "signin", label: t("auth.signIn") },
                  { value: "register", label: t("auth.register") },
                ]}
              />
              <label className="block space-y-1">
                <span className="label text-ink-2">{t("auth.username")}</span>
                <input
                  className={inputClass}
                  value={username}
                  onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                  autoComplete="username"
                  autoCapitalize="none"
                  spellCheck={false}
                  dir="ltr"
                  maxLength={24}
                  required
                />
              </label>

              {mode === "register" ? (
                <label className="block space-y-1">
                  <span className="label text-ink-2">{t("auth.displayName")}</span>
                  <input
                    className={inputClass}
                    value={displayName}
                    onChange={(e) => setDisplayName(e.target.value)}
                    autoComplete="nickname"
                    dir="auto"
                    maxLength={40}
                    placeholder={t("auth.displayNamePlaceholder")}
                  />
                </label>
              ) : null}

              <label className="block space-y-1">
                <span className="label text-ink-2">{t("auth.password")}</span>
                <input
                  className={inputClass}
                  type="password"
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  autoComplete={mode === "register" ? "new-password" : "current-password"}
                  dir="ltr"
                  maxLength={72}
                  placeholder={t("auth.passwordPlaceholder")}
                  required
                />
              </label>

              {mode === "register" ? (
                <p className="px-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-ink-3">{t("auth.createHint")}</p>
              ) : null}

              {error ? (
                <p
                  className="rounded-card border border-danger px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-danger"
                  role="alert"
                >
                  {t(error)}
                </p>
              ) : null}

              <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={busy || isLoading}>
                {busy ? t("common.loading") : t(mode === "register" ? "auth.createAccount" : "auth.signIn")}
              </Button>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="inline-flex min-h-11 w-full items-center justify-center text-center label text-ink-3 transition-colors hover:text-volt"
                onClick={() => {
                  haptic("light");
                  setShowUsername(false);
                  setError(null);
                }}
              >
                {t("auth.backToEnter")}
              </button>
            </form>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y">
              {error ? (
                <p
                  className="rounded-card border border-danger px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-danger"
                  role="alert"
                >
                  {t(error)}
                </p>
              ) : null}

              <Button className="w-full" onClick={() => void enter()} disabled={busy || isLoading}>
                {busy || isLoading
                  ? t("common.loading")
                  : tgName
                    ? t("auth.continueAs", { name: tgName })
                    : t("auth.enter")}
              </Button>

              {isTelegram() ? (
                <p className="px-2 text-center text-xs leading-relaxed text-ink-3">
                  {tgName ? t("auth.telegramSignedHint") : t("auth.telegramHint")}
                </p>
              ) : null}

              <button
                type="button"
                className="inline-flex min-h-11 w-full items-center justify-center text-center label text-ink-3 transition-colors hover:text-volt"
                onClick={() => {
                  haptic("light");
                  setShowUsername(true);
                  setError(null);
                }}
              >
                {t("auth.haveAccount")}
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </Card>        <p
          className="rise-in mt-2xl px-1 text-center text-xs leading-relaxed text-ink-3"
          style={{ "--stagger": "140ms" } as CSSProperties}>
          {t("auth.legal")}
        </p>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\pages\MatchDetail.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useParams, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { ConfettiBurst } from "../components/Confetti";
import { LineupsSkeleton,
  ScoreHeroSkeleton,
  StatsTableSkeleton,
  TimelineSkeleton,
} from "../components/Skeletons";
import { TimelineList } from "../components/TimelineList";
import { adaptComments } from "../lib/adaptComments";
import { TopBar } from "../components/TopBar";
import { LineupsPanel } from "../components/panels/LineupsPanel";

import {
  Card,
  Chip,
  CountUp,
  EmptyState,
  ErrorState,
  LiveDot,
  Num,
  TeamBadge,
} from "../components/ui";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";
import { api, type ApiTimelineEvent } from "../lib/api";
import { TEAM_FALLBACK } from "../lib/colors";
import { leagueIdOf, useCompetitionLabel } from "../hooks/useApi";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { RATINGS_ENABLED } from "../lib/features";
import { flipIntoPlace, getFlipFirst } from "../lib/flip";
import { formatClock, formatKickoffDate, kickoffParts, toFaDigits } from "../lib/format";
import { useNow } from "../hooks/useNow";
import { useLiveUpdates } from "../hooks/useLiveUpdates";
import type { Team, TimelineEventType } from "../types";

type Tab = "timeline" | "stats" | "lineups";
const TABS: Tab[] = ["timeline", "stats", "lineups"];

export default function MatchDetail() {
  const { id = "" } = useParams();
  const { t, lang, localize } = useI18n();
  const locale = lang === "fa" ? "fa-IR" : undefined;
  const fa = lang === "fa";
  useLiveUpdates();

  const matchQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["match", id],
    queryFn: () => api.getMatch(id),
    enabled: Boolean(id),
    // 20s while the match is in play: the server's live pulse rewrites the
    // minute and score straight from ESPN every 15s, so anything slower left
    // the header showing a clock minutes behind the game (the "not synced with
    // time" report). Paused when the tab is in the background â€” a hidden tab
    // holds the last good payload instead of spending the battery.
    refetchInterval: (query) =>
      (query.state.data?.match.status === "live" || query.state.data?.match.status === "halftime") &&
      !document.hidden
        ? 20_000
        : false,
    refetchIntervalInBackground: false,
  });
  const match = matchQuery.data?.match;
  const live = match?.status === "live";
  /**
   * Live OR the half-time break. The clock keeps moving INTO the break and the
   * second half starts from it, so every refresh decision polls on this â€”
   * polling on `live` alone left a match frozen on "HT" until the user
   * touched the screen again.
   */
  const inPlay = live || match?.status === "halftime";
  // Competition name for the header: big-five alias, else the catalog name
  // (cups/tournaments), so a cup match never shows a raw "uefa.champions".
  const leagueLabel = useCompetitionLabel(match?.league ?? "");

  // Tabs live in the router's search params so a panel is shareable and
  // deep-linkable. `useSearchParams` (not window.history.replaceState) is what
  // makes the tab actually re-render: replaceState fired without touching React
  // state, so the panel only changed once something else re-rendered â€” and on
  // a HashRouter the hand-built `pathname?tab=â€¦` URL dropped the `#/match/â€¦`
  // part, so a reload landed on the home route. `replace` keeps tab switches
  // out of browser history.
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const requested = searchParams.get("tab") as Tab | null;
  const tab: Tab = requested && TABS.includes(requested) ? requested : "timeline";
  const setTab = (next: Tab) =>
    setSearchParams(
      (prev) => {
        const params = new URLSearchParams(prev);
        params.set("tab", next);
        return params;
      },
      { replace: true },
    );

  // Goal flash when polling reveals a new score.
  const [burst, setBurst] = useState(0);
  const [flashScore, setFlashScore] = useState(false);
  const scoreRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!match) return;
    const score = `${match.home_score}-${match.away_score}`;
    if (scoreRef.current && scoreRef.current !== score && match.home_score !== null) {
      setBurst((v) => v + 1);
      setFlashScore(true);            window.setTimeout(() => setFlashScore(false), 1400);
    }
    scoreRef.current = score;
  }, [match?.home_score, match?.away_score, match]);

  // FLIP: the tapped feed card recorded its score block's First rect; the
  // hero's score plays the inversion once it exists. Ran off a ref + the
  // hero's presence (the hero mounts after the match query resolves, so the
  // plain mount effect would fire before it), and each rect is consumed on
  // read â€” one tap, one play, never on re-renders or background refetches.
  // Reduced-motion users get the hero immediately, no transition.
  const flipPlayed = useRef(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!match || flipPlayed.current) return;
    const heroScore = document.querySelector('[data-flip-key="match-score"]');
    if (!(heroScore instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
    const first = getFlipFirst("match-score");
    if (!first) return;
    flipPlayed.current = true;
    if (window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches) return;
    // Two frames: the page-in wrapper is still settling its transform on
    // frame one, so the Last rect is measured after layout has committed.
    //
    // The frames are deliberately NOT cancelled on cleanup. `prefetchMatch`
    // warms the query cache, so the hero usually exists on the FIRST render
    // â€” and React StrictMode's dev double-invoke then runs this effect twice,
    // with the cleanup firing in between. Cancelling there killed the
    // transition before it could play. One play per navigation is guaranteed
    // by the store instead: the First rect is consumed on read, so the second
    // pass finds nothing to play.
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        if (heroScore.isConnected) flipIntoPlace(heroScore, first);
      });
    });
  }, [match]);

  const currentMinute = useMemo(() => {
    const m = /\d+/.exec(match?.minute_display ?? "");
    return m ? Number(m[0]) : 0;
  }, [match?.minute_display]);

  const now = useNow(30_000);

  // Days, then hours+minutes in words â€” never a bare "335h 45m", whose Latin
  // h/m scramble the bidi run of a Persian sentence.
  const nf = new Intl.NumberFormat(locale);
  const countdownLabel = useMemo(() => {
    if (!match || match.status !== "scheduled") return null;
    const ms = new Date(match.kickoff_at).getTime() - now;
    if (ms <= 0) return t("match.awaiting");
    const totalMinutes = Math.floor(ms / 60_000);
    if (totalMinutes < 1) return t("predict.startingSoon");
    const days = Math.floor(totalMinutes / 1440);
    if (days >= 1) return t("match.startsInDays", { count: nf.format(days) });
    const hours = Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60);
    const minutes = totalMinutes % 60;
    return t("predict.startsIn", {
      time:
        hours > 0
          ? t("match.hoursMinutes", { h: nf.format(hours), m: nf.format(minutes) })
          : t("match.minutes", { m: nf.format(minutes) }),
    });
  }, [match, now, t, locale]);



  if (matchQuery.isLoading) {
    return (
      <>
        <TopBar title={leagueLabel || t("match.summary")} />
        <div className="mt-md">
          <ScoreHeroSkeleton />
        </div>
      </>
    );
  }

  if (matchQuery.isError) {
    return (
      <>
        <TopBar title={leagueLabel || t("match.summary")} />
        <div className="mt-md">
          <ErrorState title={t("error.title")} hint={t("error.hint")} onRetry={() => matchQuery.refetch()} />
        </div>
      </>
    );
  }

  if (!match) {
    return (
      <>
        <TopBar title={leagueLabel || t("match.summary")} />
        <div className="mt-md">
          <EmptyState title={t("matches.empty")} hint={t("matches.emptyHint")} />
        </div>
      </>
    );
  }

  const home = {
    id: match.home_team_id,
    name: match.home_name,
    shortName: match.home_short ?? match.home_name,
    abbreviation: match.home_abbr ?? "â€”",
    color: match.home_color ?? TEAM_FALLBACK,
    league: leagueIdOf(match.league) ?? "PL",
  };
  const away = {
    ...home,
    id: match.away_team_id,
    name: match.away_name,
    shortName: match.away_short ?? match.away_name,
    abbreviation: match.away_abbr ?? "â€”",
    color: match.away_color ?? TEAM_FALLBACK,
  };



  // Cup matches carry ESPN's round ("Quarter-final"); league matches have no
  // round in the payload and no gameweek either â€” the API sends neither â€” so
  // the subtitle renders only when a round exists (never a bare placeholder).
  const roundLabel = match.round ? localize(match.round) : null;

  return (
    <>
      <ConfettiBurst trigger={burst} />

      {/* Title = the competition, subtitle = the round (cups). No round back
          chevron next to the title â€” the scoreboard owns the screen and
          navigation lives in the mini app chrome. */}
      <TopBar title={leagueLabel} subtitle={roundLabel ?? undefined} />

      {/* Scoreboard: crest with the team NAME under it, big score centered,
          live pill with the minute. Home first â€” the first flex child, which
          sits on the START edge (right in Persian, left in English) â€” the same
          home-first order the Matches feed shows, with each crest's number
          being that side's goals. */}
      <div
        className={cx(
          "score-hero relative mt-md overflow-hidden rounded-card border p-3",
          flashScore ? "goal-flash border-volt/60 bg-elevated" : "border-line",
        )}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          {/* HOME â€” the side the first score digit belongs to. */}
          <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col items-center gap-1.5">
            <TeamBadge team={home} size="md" />
            <span className="max-w-full truncate text-center text-sm font-bold text-ink">
              {localize(home.shortName)}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-none flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1.5 self-stretch py-1">
            {/* Before kickoff there is no score â€” the kickoff clock is the
                honest read, isolated LTR so the colon and digits never flip.
                Once a side has a number the score rolls up as it moves. */}
            {match.home_score === null && match.away_score === null ? (
              <Num className="text-lg leading-none text-ink-2">
                <span dir="ltr">{formatClock(new Date(match.kickoff_at), locale)}</span>
              </Num>
            ) : (
              /* This row is the FLIP's Last frame: same key the tapped card
                 recorded its First rect under (see MatchCard onPointerDown).
                 NOT dir-ltr: in Persian the home number has to sit beside the
                 home crest (start edge, right). */
              <span data-flip-key="match-score" className="flex items-baseline justify-center gap-1">
                <ScoreDigit value={match.home_score} fa={fa} />
                <Num className="text-display leading-none text-ink">:</Num>
                <ScoreDigit value={match.away_score} fa={fa} />
              </span>
            )}
            {inPlay ? (
              <Chip tone="live">
                <LiveDot />
                {match.status === "halftime" ? (
                  t("timeline.halftime")
                ) : (
                  <span>
                    {t("status.live")} {"\u200F"}
                    <Num>
                      <span dir="ltr">{nf.format(currentMinute)}â€²</span>
                    </Num>
                  </span>
                )}
              </Chip>
            ) : countdownLabel ? (
              <Chip tone="volt" className="motion-safe:animate-pulse">
                {countdownLabel}
              </Chip>
            ) : (
              <Chip tone={match.status === "finished" ? "muted" : "volt"}>
                {match.status === "finished" ? (
                  t("status.finished")
                ) : (
                  <Num>{formatKickoffDate(new Date(match.kickoff_at), locale)}</Num>
                )}
              </Chip>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* AWAY â€” the side the second score digit belongs to. */}
          <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col items-center gap-1.5">
            <TeamBadge team={away} size="md" />
            <span className="max-w-full truncate text-center text-sm font-bold text-ink">
              {localize(away.shortName)}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Date/time (and venue when the payload ever carries one â€” today
            there is no venue field at all) as simple lines below the pill.
            The clock is its own dir=ltr run so the bidi order never scrambles. */}
        <div className="mt-2 text-center text-xs text-ink-2">
          <p>
            {kickoffParts(new Date(match.kickoff_at), locale).day}{" "}
            <span dir="ltr">{kickoffParts(new Date(match.kickoff_at), locale).clock}</span>
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <UnderlineTabs
        id="match-tabs"
        value={tab}
        onChange={setTab}
        options={[
          { value: "timeline", label: t("match.tabTimeline") },
          { value: "stats", label: t("match.tabStats") },
          { value: "lineups", label: t("match.tabLineups") },
        ]}
      />

      {/* The panel that the tabs above (role=tab/aria-controls) point at: the
          labelled-by ties tab â†’ panel so screen readers announce the owning
          tab, and arrow keys on the tablist land here. */}
      <section
        id="match-tabs-panel"
        role="tabpanel"
        aria-labelledby={`match-tabs-${tab}`}
        className="panel-in mt-md"
        key={tab}
      >
        {tab === "timeline" ? (
          <TimelineTab
            matchId={id}
            live={inPlay}
            minute={currentMinute}
            teams={[home, away]}
          />
        ) : null}
        {tab === "stats" ? <StatsTab matchId={id} home={home} away={away} /> : null}
        {tab === "lineups" ? <LineupsTab matchId={id} home={home} away={away} /> : null}
      </section>


      {/* The A-ADS unit used to be the page's last element here. It is now a
          static iframe in index.html so the no-JS A-ADS verifier can see it. */}
    </>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ tabs */

/**
 * One score digit: a goal rolls up in EN, but Persian renders statically â€”
 * CountUp prints Latin digits, and an animated Persian-digit roll isn't worth
 * its own clock component here.
 */
function ScoreDigit({ value, fa }: { value: number | null; fa: boolean }) {
  if (value === null) return <Num className="text-display leading-none text-ink">â€“</Num>;
  return fa ? (
    <Num className="text-display leading-none text-ink">{toFaDigits(value)}</Num>
  ) : (
    <CountUp value={value} className="text-display leading-none text-ink" />
  );
}

/**
 * Underline-style tabs for the match page: a volt underline marks the active
 * tab instead of the shared sliding pill, per the redesign. Same tab wiring
 * as Segmented: tab id `${id}-${value}`, panel `${id}-panel`, tabs carry
 * role="tab" + aria-selected + aria-controls (the panel below depends on it).
 */
function UnderlineTabs<T extends string>({
  id,
  value,
  options,
  onChange,
}: {
  id: string;
  value: T;
  options: Array<{ value: T; label: string }>;
  onChange: (value: T) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div role="tablist" className="mt-md flex border-b border-line">
      {options.map((option) => {
        const active = option.value === value;
        return (
          <button
            key={option.value}
            type="button"
            role="tab"
            id={`${id}-${option.value}`}
            aria-selected={active}
            aria-controls={`${id}-panel`}
            onClick={() => onChange(option.value)}
            className={cx(
              "relative min-h-11 flex-1 px-2 text-sm font-bold transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)]",
              active ? "text-ink" : "text-ink-3 hover:text-ink-2",
            )}
          >
            {option.label}
            <span
              aria-hidden="true"
              className={cx(
                "absolute inset-x-3 bottom-0 h-0.5 rounded-full",
                active ? "bg-volt" : "bg-transparent",
              )}
            />
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

function TimelineTab({
  matchId,
  live,
  minute,
  teams,
}: {
  matchId: string;
  live: boolean;
  minute: number;
  /** Both sides, for the timeline markers' club tint and the team label. */
  teams: Array<{ id: string; shortName: string; color: string }>;
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["timeline", matchId],
    queryFn: () => api.getTimeline(matchId),
    refetchInterval: live ? 20_000 : false,
  });
  // Pinned minute-comments, for the rows' comment count trigger. Read-only: the
  // composer was deliberately unmounted by the "core five" trim (8d73687) â€”
  // this restores the reading side, not the writing side.
  const commentsQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["comments", matchId],
    queryFn: () => api.getComments(matchId),
  });
  const events = query.data?.events ?? [];
  const pinned = adaptComments(commentsQuery.data?.comments ?? [], undefined);
  const adapted = events
    .map(adaptTimelineEvent)
    .filter((e) => e.type !== "other"); // drop commentary noise; keep kickoff + structured events

  if (query.isLoading) return <TimelineSkeleton rows={4} />;
  if (query.isError) {
    return <ErrorState title={t("error.title")} hint={t("error.hint")} onRetry={() => query.refetch()} />;
  }
  if (adapted.length === 0) {
    return <EmptyState icon="â±" title={t("timeline.empty")} hint={t("timeline.emptyHint")} />;
  }

  return (
    <TimelineList
      events={adapted}
      comments={pinned}
      liveMinute={live ? minute : undefined}
      homeTeamId={teams[0]?.id}
      awayTeamId={teams[1]?.id}
    />
  );
}

function StatsTab({
  matchId,
  home,
  away,
}: {
  matchId: string;
  home: {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    shortName: string;
    abbreviation: string;
    color: string;
    league: "PL" | "LL" | "SA" | "BL" | "L1";
  };
  away: typeof home;
}) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["matchPlayers", matchId],
    queryFn: () => api.getMatchPlayers(matchId),
  });
  const rows = (query.data?.players ?? []) as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  // The synced box score (possession/corners/pass accuracy only exist here).
  // A failure or a missing pair falls back to the player aggregates below â€”
  // the tab never blanks because one feed went quiet.
  const teamStatsQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["matchTeamStats", matchId],
    queryFn: () => api.getMatchTeamStats(matchId),
    staleTime: 60_000,
  });
  const served =
    teamStatsQuery.data?.home && teamStatsQuery.data?.away
      ? { home: teamStatsQuery.data.home, away: teamStatsQuery.data.away }
      : null;

  if (query.isLoading || teamStatsQuery.isLoading) return <StatsTableSkeleton />;
  if (query.isError) {
    return <ErrorState title={t("error.title")} hint={t("error.hint")} onRetry={() => query.refetch()} />;
  }
  if (rows.length === 0 && !served) {
    return <EmptyState icon="ðŸ“Š" title={t("stats.noneYet")} hint={t("stats.noneHint")} />;
  }

  const asNum = (v: unknown): number => (typeof v === "number" ? v : 0);
  // A substitute who never came on is a row of zeros, not a player â€” hide them.
  const teamRows = (teamId: string) =>
    rows.filter((r) => r.team_id === teamId && (r.started || asNum(r.minutes_played) > 0));

  // Fallback totals aggregated from the per-player stat rows, used only when
  // the synced box score has no row for this match â€” never alongside it.
  const totalsFor = (teamId: string) => {
    const rowsOf = rows.filter((r) => r.team_id === teamId);
    const sum = (key: string) => rowsOf.reduce((acc, r) => acc + asNum(r[key]), 0);
    return {
      goals: sum("goals"),
      shots: sum("shots"),
      onTarget: sum("shots_on_target"),
      fouls: sum("fouls_committed"),
      offsides: sum("offsides"),
      saves: sum("saves"),
    };
  };

  const homeTotals = totalsFor(home.id);
  const awayTotals = totalsFor(away.id);
  // Synced box score first: all eight stats in display order. Possession and
  // pass accuracy are shares of 100 shown as "63%", with the bar split by the
  // raw percentage instead of a ratio.
  const statRows: Array<{ label: string; homeValue: number | null; awayValue: number | null; pct?: boolean }> =
    served
      ? [
          { label: t("stats.possession"), homeValue: served.home.possession_pct, awayValue: served.away.possession_pct, pct: true },
          { label: t("stats.shots"), homeValue: served.home.shots, awayValue: served.away.shots },
          { label: t("stats.onTarget"), homeValue: served.home.shots_on_target, awayValue: served.away.shots_on_target },
          { label: t("stats.corners"), homeValue: served.home.corners, awayValue: served.away.corners },
          { label: t("stats.fouls"), homeValue: served.home.fouls, awayValue: served.away.fouls },
          { label: t("stats.offsides"), homeValue: served.home.offsides, awayValue: served.away.offsides },
          { label: t("stats.passAccuracy"), homeValue: served.home.pass_accuracy_pct, awayValue: served.away.pass_accuracy_pct, pct: true },
          { label: t("stats.saves"), homeValue: served.home.saves, awayValue: served.away.saves },
        ]
      : [
          { label: t("stats.goals"), homeValue: homeTotals.goals, awayValue: awayTotals.goals },
          { label: t("stats.shots"), homeValue: homeTotals.shots, awayValue: awayTotals.shots },
          { label: t("stats.onTarget"), homeValue: homeTotals.onTarget, awayValue: awayTotals.onTarget },
          { label: t("stats.fouls"), homeValue: homeTotals.fouls, awayValue: awayTotals.fouls },
          { label: t("stats.offsides"), homeValue: homeTotals.offsides, awayValue: awayTotals.offsides },
          { label: t("stats.saves"), homeValue: homeTotals.saves, awayValue: awayTotals.saves },
        ];

  const homeRows = teamRows(home.id);
  const awayRows = teamRows(away.id);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {/* Comparison card: both sides' totals on the same row with a proportion
          bar. Forced LTR so the home half is always the left one, whichever
          direction the page runs. */}
      <Card className="p-3">
        <div dir="ltr">
          <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-ink">
              <span
                className="h-2.5 w-2.5 flex-none rounded-full ring-1 ring-ink-3"
                style={{ backgroundColor: home.color }}
                aria-hidden="true"
              />
              <span className="truncate">{localize(home.shortName)}</span>
            </span>
            <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-ink">
              <span className="truncate">{localize(away.shortName)}</span>
              <span
                className="h-2.5 w-2.5 flex-none rounded-full ring-1 ring-ink-3"
                style={{ backgroundColor: away.color }}
                aria-hidden="true"
              />
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {statRows.map((row) => (
              <StatCompareRow key={row.label} {...row} />
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Every player who appeared, both sides in one pass â€” no Home/Away
          toggle to remember to tap. */}
      {homeRows.length > 0 ? (
        <MatchPlayerTable title={localize(home.shortName)} color={home.color} rows={homeRows} />
      ) : null}
      {awayRows.length > 0 ? (
        <MatchPlayerTable title={localize(away.shortName)} color={away.color} rows={awayRows} />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

/** One team-stat row: home value, label, away value, and the proportion bar
    underneath â€” volt half for home (left), live-coral half for away. A synced
    percentage row (`pct`) prints "63%" and splits the bar by the raw
    percentage itself; a count splits by ratio. A null rides as "â€“" and counts
    as zero; zeros on both sides settle into an even, muted 50/50. */
function StatCompareRow({
  label,
  homeValue,
  awayValue,
  pct = false,
}: {
  label: string;
  homeValue: number | null;
  awayValue: number | null;
  pct?: boolean;
}) {
  const { lang } = useI18n();
  const fa = lang === "fa";
  const show = (v: number | null) =>
    v === null
      ? "â€“"
      : fa
        ? toFaDigits(pct ? `${v}%` : String(v))
        : pct
          ? `${v}%`
          : String(v);
  const hv = homeValue ?? 0;
  const av = awayValue ?? 0;
  const total = hv + av;
  // With nothing on either side the track is an even 50/50 in a muted grey â€”
  // an equal-looking bar that never reads as "someone is winning something".
  // A percentage splits by its own raw value: 63% possession means a 63/37 bar.
  const homePct = total > 0 ? (pct ? hv : Math.round((hv / total) * 100)) : 50;
  const fill = total > 0;
  return (
    <div dir="ltr">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
        <Num className="w-8 text-end font-black text-ink">{show(homeValue)}</Num>
        <span className="flex-1 truncate text-center text-xs font-bold text-ink-2">{label}</span>
        <Num className="w-8 font-black text-ink">{show(awayValue)}</Num>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-1 flex h-1.5 gap-0.5 overflow-hidden">
        <span
          className={`rounded-full ${fill ? "bg-volt" : "bg-ink-3/40"}`}
          style={{ width: `${homePct}%` }}
        />
        <span
          className={`rounded-full ${fill ? "bg-live" : "bg-ink-3/40"}`}
          style={{ width: `${100 - homePct}%` }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function MatchPlayerTable({
  title,
  color,
  rows,
}: {
  title: string;
  color: string;
  rows: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
}) {
  const { t, localize, lang } = useI18n();
  const fa = lang === "fa";
  const num = (v: unknown): number => (typeof v === "number" ? v : 0);
  const str = (v: unknown): string => (typeof v === "string" ? v : "");
  return (
    <Card className="overflow-hidden p-0">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-line p-3">
        {/* The ring keeps a black (or near-black) club colour visible on the dark
            background â€” a bare dot in the club's own colour can vanish. */}
        <span
          className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full ring-1 ring-ink-3"
          style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
          aria-hidden="true"
        />
        <span className="text-sm font-bold text-ink">{title}</span>
      </div>
      <table className="w-full text-xs">
        <thead>
          <tr className="label text-ink-3">
            <th className="p-2 text-start font-bold">{t("stats.player")}</th>
            <th className="p-2 text-end font-bold">{t("stats.goals")}</th>
            <th className="p-2 text-end font-bold">{t("stats.assists")}</th>
            <th className="p-2 text-end font-bold">{t("stats.shots")}</th>
            {/* The points-style stat score is the ratings feature's number;
                with the flag off the column is gone and the remaining four
                share its width (the table is already w-full). */}
            {RATINGS_ENABLED ? (
              <th className="p-2 text-end font-bold">{t("ratings.points")}</th>
            ) : null}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((r) => (
            <tr key={str(r.player_id)} className="border-t border-line">
              <td className="p-2">
                <span className="block truncate font-bold text-ink">{localize(str(r.full_name))}</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-ink-3">
                  {fa ? toFaDigits(num(r.minutes_played)) : num(r.minutes_played)}â€²{" "}
                  {r.started ? `Â· ${t("match.xi")}` : ""}
                </span>
              </td>
              <td className="p-2 text-end">
                <Num className={num(r.goals) > 0 ? "font-bold text-volt" : "text-ink-2"}>
                  {fa ? toFaDigits(num(r.goals)) : num(r.goals)}
                </Num>
              </td>
              <td className="p-2 text-end">
                <Num className={num(r.assists) > 0 ? "font-bold text-gold" : "text-ink-2"}>
                  {fa ? toFaDigits(num(r.assists)) : num(r.assists)}
                </Num>
              </td>
              <td className="p-2 text-end text-ink-2">
                {fa ? toFaDigits(num(r.shots)) : num(r.shots)}
              </td>
              {RATINGS_ENABLED ? (
                <td className="p-2 text-end">
                  <Num className="font-bold text-ink">
                    {fa ? toFaDigits(num(r.stat_score)) : num(r.stat_score)}
                  </Num>
                </td>
              ) : null}
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </Card>
  );
}

function LineupsTab({
  matchId,
  home,
  away,
}: {
  matchId: string;
  home: { id: string; shortName: string; color: string };
  away: { id: string; shortName: string; color: string };
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["lineups", matchId],
    queryFn: () => api.getLineups(matchId),
  });
  const data = query.data;
  if (query.isLoading) return <LineupsSkeleton />;
  if (query.isError) {
    return <ErrorState title={t("error.title")} hint={t("error.hint")} onRetry={() => query.refetch()} />;
  }
  if (!data || data.players.length === 0) {
    // The Lineups tab is about the XI, not the stat sheet â€” an upcoming match
    // must not say "stats land here after the first whistle".
    return <EmptyState icon="ðŸ“‹" title={t("lineups.noneYet")} hint={t("lineups.noneHint")} />;
  }

  const toPlayer = (r: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
    id: String(r.player_id),
    name: String(r.full_name),
    position: String(r.position ?? "MID") as "GK" | "DEF" | "MID" | "FWD",
    number: typeof r.jersey_number === "number" ? r.jersey_number : 0,
    // The pitch is only as good as these three: the face, the granular role
    // that decides which side of a line a player stands on, and ESPN's slot for
    // the leaked XIs that carry no role.
    headshot: typeof r.headshot_url === "string" ? r.headshot_url : null,
    espnPosition: typeof r.espn_position === "string" ? r.espn_position : null,
    formationPlace: typeof r.formation_place === "string" ? r.formation_place : null,
  });
  const homeTeam: Team = {
    id: home.id,
    name: home.shortName,
    shortName: home.shortName,
    abbreviation: home.shortName.slice(0, 3),
    color: home.color,
    league: "PL",
  };
  const awayTeam: Team = {
    id: away.id,
    name: away.shortName,
    shortName: away.shortName,
    abbreviation: away.shortName.slice(0, 3),
    color: away.color,
    league: "PL",
  };
  const sidePlayers = (teamId: string) =>
    data.players
      .filter((p) => p.team_id === teamId)
      .map((p) => ({
        ...toPlayer(p as unknown as Record<string, unknown>),
        starter: p.started === 1,
        minutes: p.minutes_played ?? 0,
      }));

  const homeAll = sidePlayers(home.id);
  const awayAll = sidePlayers(away.id);
  const homeLineup = {
    formation: data.home.formation ?? "â€”",
    coach: "",
    starters: homeAll.filter((p) => p.starter),
    subs: homeAll.filter((p) => !p.starter && p.minutes > 0),
  };
  const awayLineup = {
    formation: data.away.formation ?? "â€”",
    coach: "",
    starters: awayAll.filter((p) => p.starter),
    subs: awayAll.filter((p) => !p.starter && p.minutes > 0),
  };

  return <LineupsPanel home={homeTeam} away={awayTeam} lineups={{ home: homeLineup, away: awayLineup }} />;
}/* ------------------------------------------------------------ adapters */

function adaptTimelineEvent(e: ApiTimelineEvent) {
  return {
    id: String(e.id),
    minuteDisplay: e.minute_display,
    minuteSeconds: e.minute_seconds,
    // Trust the server's normalization â€” it already maps ESPN's many type
    // strings to the 8-value union. The previous fallback to "var" mis-tagged
    // kickoff and other events as VAR reviews.
    type: e.type as TimelineEventType,
    teamId: e.team_id,
    description: e.description,
    participants: e.participants.map((p) => p.name),
    commentCount: e.comment_count,
  };
}

===== D:\football\src\pages\Matches.tsx =====

import { Fragment, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type CSSProperties } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { ConfettiBurst } from "../components/Confetti";
import { TopBar } from "../components/TopBar";
import { useToast } from "../components/toastContext";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "../components/ui";
import { useLocalState } from "../lib/useLocalState";
import {
  BallIcon,
  CalendarIcon,
  CloseIcon,
  PlusIcon,
  StarFilledIcon,
} from "../components/icons";
import { LeagueGroupSkeleton } from "../components/Skeletons";
import { useNow } from "../hooks/useNow";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";
import { useMatches, useFollowedLeagues, useSyncHealth } from "../hooks/useApi";
import { useFavorites } from "../hooks/useFavorites";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { formatClock } from "../lib/format";
import { competitionName } from "../lib/leagues";
import { haptic, notifyHaptic } from "../lib/telegram";
import { DateStrip, dayKey as chipDayKey } from "../components/DateStrip";
import { InlineAd } from "../components/InlineAd";
import { LeaguePickerSheet } from "../components/panels/LeaguePickerSheet";
import { CalendarSheet } from "../components/panels/CalendarSheet";
import { usePullToRefresh } from "../hooks/usePullToRefresh";
import { useLiveUpdates } from "../hooks/useLiveUpdates";
import { PullIndicator } from "../components/PullToRefresh";
import { LeagueGroup } from "../components/LeagueGroup";
import {
  addJalaliMonths,
  jalaliMonthLength,
  jalaliToGregorian,
  toJalali,
} from "../lib/jalali";
import type { Match } from "../types";

/**
 * One chip in the league scope row: smaller than a 44px target on purpose, with
 * an invisible `after:` band that keeps the real hit area at 44px â€” the visual
 * language wanted a tighter pill without shrinking what a thumb can hit.
 */
const LEAGUE_CHIP ="relative h-9 flex-none rounded-lg border px-3.5 text-sm font-bold transition-[color,background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.97] after:absolute after:-inset-x-0.5 after:-inset-y-1 after:content-['']"
;

/** Selected league chip: filled volt pill â€” the row's one "picked" affordance. */
const LEAGUE_CHIP_ON = "border-volt bg-volt/15 text-volt";
const LEAGUE_CHIP_OFF = "border-line text-ink-3";

const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
const localDayKey = (d: Date) => `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}`;

/**
 * The calendar sheet spans today Â± this many months (see CalendarSheet), so
 * the feed query must cover every day in that band: a picked day three months
 * out HAS fixtures to show, not the old â€œno matchesâ€ lie. With `from`/`to`
 * set the server runs its range mode, unbounded by the 3-week relevance
 * window â€” hence the explicit limit matching the server's range ceiling.
 */
const CALENDAR_HORIZON_MONTHS = 3;
const FEED_RANGE_LIMIT = 2000;

/**
 * The band's first/last day, anchored at month bounds of the ACTIVE calendar
 * (Solar Hijri under Persian, Gregorian under English) so every tile the
 * sheet can clip into â€” its grid steps whole months of that calendar â€” is
 * inside the fetched range. Computed once per language: an app left open
