 * across a month boundary would rebolt the range on reload, and the mid-band
 * days it misses next session are caught by the cache-busting refetch.
 */
function feedRange(lang: "en" | "fa"): { from: string; to: string } {
  const now = new Date();
  if (lang === "fa") {
    const t = toJalali(now);
    const min = addJalaliMonths(t.jy, t.jm, -CALENDAR_HORIZON_MONTHS);
    const max = addJalaliMonths(t.jy, t.jm, CALENDAR_HORIZON_MONTHS);
    return {
      from: localDayKey(jalaliToGregorian(min.jy, min.jm, 1)),
      to: localDayKey(jalaliToGregorian(max.jy, max.jm, jalaliMonthLength(max.jy, max.jm))),
    };
  }
  return {
    from: localDayKey(new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - CALENDAR_HORIZON_MONTHS, 1)),
    to: localDayKey(new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() + CALENDAR_HORIZON_MONTHS + 1, 0)),
  };
}

/**
 * Local calendar-day key. NEVER use toISOString() for this: it is UTC, so east
 * of UTC (this app's users are UTC+3:30/+4) local midnight is still the previous
 * UTC day and every match would be filed under the wrong date.
 */
const dayKey = (d: Date) => `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}`;
const startOfDay = (d: Date) => {
  const x = new Date(d);
  x.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  return x;
};

/**
 * Pick-banner dismiss is persisted so a user who already said â€œdonâ€™t show me
 * this againâ€ keeps that choice after reload â€” the stored id beats the
 * computed â€œwe donâ€™t have any favoritesâ€ default.
 */
const PICK_BANNER_KEY = "verdikick.pickBannerDismissed";

/** One competition's slice of the selected day; kickoff-sorted rows. */
interface DayLeagueGroup {
  league: string;
  matches: Match[];
  /** Earliest kickoff, numeric â€” group ordering's second key. */
  firstKickoff: number;
}

export default function Matches() {
  const { t, lang, localize } = useI18n();
  const { push } = useToast();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const locale = lang === "fa" ? "fa-IR" : undefined;
  /** Localized short day label ("Wed 21" / "Ù¾ØŒ Û²Û±") for the empty-day copy. */
  const dayLabel = (d: Date) =>
    new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, { weekday: "short", day: "numeric" }).format(d);  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  useLiveUpdates();

  // Deep link from Browse: /matches?league=eng.1 narrows the feed to one
  // competition. Absent param = the followed-leagues feed (or everything).
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const league = searchParams.get("league") ?? undefined;
  const { count: favCount, isFavorite } = useFavorites();
  const followed = useFollowedLeagues();

  // Followed leagues narrow the default feed when set; the deep-link param
  // (from Browse) takes precedence over both.
  const [leagueFilter, setLeagueFilter] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const activeLeague = league ?? leagueFilter ?? undefined;

  const [bannerDismissed, setBannerDismissed] = useLocalState<boolean>(PICK_BANNER_KEY, false);
  const showPickBanner = favCount === 0 && !bannerDismissed;

  // The calendar's full reach, not just the relevance window: the day feed and
  // the sheet's fixture dots both read this one list.
  const range = useMemo(() => feedRange(lang), [lang]);
  const matchesQuery = useMatches({
    league: activeLeague,
    from: range.from,
    to: range.to,
    limit: FEED_RANGE_LIMIT,
  });
  // Stable identity: every memo below keys on it, and `data ?? []` would hand
  // them a fresh array on each render (query data is stable between refetches).
  const matches = useMemo(() => matchesQuery.data ?? [], [matchesQuery.data]);

  // The Favorite Leagues sheet. Local to the feed (not a route) so opening and
  // closing it never loses the scroll position or the selected league chip.
  const [leaguesOpen, setLeaguesOpen] = useState(false);
  // The month picker, for the days the seven-chip week cannot reach.
  const [calendarOpen, setCalendarOpen] = useState(false);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [lastSync, setLastSync] = useState<Date | null>(null);
  const [burst, setBurst] = useState(0);

  // Date strip: 21 day chips. `picked` is null while "today" is selected â€” the
  // day is then recomputed from the clock every minute, so an app left open
  // past local midnight rolls over to the new day instead of sticking on the
  // one it booted with.
  const now = useNow(60_000);
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<Date | null>(null);
  const selectedDay = picked ?? startOfDay(new Date(now));
  const selectedKey = dayKey(selectedDay);

  // Filter by LOCAL calendar day. The day's matches no longer group by state;
  // grouping is by competition (see `leagueGroups`).
  const dayMatches = useMemo(
    () => matches.filter((m) => dayKey(new Date(m.kickoffAt)) === selectedKey),
    [matches, selectedKey],
  );

  // Day-state filter chips: all / live / followed clubs. The live chip hides
  // when the day has no live football and the mine chip hides with no picks;
  // a stale selection (e.g. "live" after the day rolled to a quiet one) falls
  // back to "all" instead of holding an empty screen.
  const [dayFilter, setDayFilter] = useState<"all" | "live" | "mine">("all");
  const liveCount = useMemo(
    () => dayMatches.filter((m) => m.status === "live" || m.isHalftime === true).length,
    [dayMatches],
  );
  const effectiveFilter =
    dayFilter === "live" && liveCount === 0
      ? ("all" as const)
      : dayFilter === "mine" && favCount === 0
        ? ("all" as const)
        : dayFilter;
  const filteredMatches = useMemo(() => {
    if (effectiveFilter === "live") {
      return dayMatches.filter((m) => m.status === "live" || m.isHalftime === true);
    }
    if (effectiveFilter === "mine") {
      return dayMatches.filter((m) => isFavorite(m.home.id) || isFavorite(m.away.id));
    }
    return dayMatches;
  }, [dayMatches, effectiveFilter, isFavorite]);

  // Strip/calendar markers, one pass over the feed. The strip's dot marks ONLY
  // the days a followed club plays â€” a day full of fixtures the user doesn't
  // follow stays quiet â€” while the calendar sheet keeps its "has fixtures" dots,
  // because there the user is hunting open dates, not their own matchdays.
  const { daysWithMatches, daysWithFavorites } = useMemo(() => {
    const all = new Set<string>();
    const favorites = new Set<string>();
    for (const match of matches) {
      const key = chipDayKey(new Date(match.kickoffAt));
      all.add(key);
      if (isFavorite(match.home.id) || isFavorite(match.away.id)) favorites.add(key);
    }
    return { daysWithMatches: all, daysWithFavorites: favorites };
  }, [matches, isFavorite]);

  // THE GROUPING: the day's matches come together in one rounded container per
  // competition. Followed leagues lead (in the user's own follow order â€” that
  // IS their interest ranking, so it beats every computed heuristic); among the
  // rest, earliest kickoff first. Within a group, kickoff order top-to-bottom.
  const leagueGroups = useMemo<DayLeagueGroup[]>(() => {
    const byLeague = new Map<string, Match[]>();
    for (const match of filteredMatches) {
      const list = byLeague.get(match.league);
      if (list) list.push(match);
      else byLeague.set(match.league, [match]);
    }
    // The followed list is at most 5 slugs, so building an index map per render
    // is cheaper than a `.find` inside the sort's comparator.
    const followOrder = new Map(followed.leagues.map((l, i) => [l.league, i]));
    return [...byLeague]
      .map(([leagueSlug, list]) => {
        const sorted = [...list].sort(
          (a, b) => new Date(a.kickoffAt).getTime() - new Date(b.kickoffAt).getTime(),
        );
        return {
          league: leagueSlug,
          matches: sorted,
          firstKickoff: new Date(sorted[0].kickoffAt).getTime(),
        };
      })
      .sort((a, b) => {
        const rankA = followOrder.get(a.league) ?? followOrder.size;
        const rankB = followOrder.get(b.league) ?? followOrder.size;
        return rankA - rankB || a.firstKickoff - b.firstKickoff;
      });
  }, [dayMatches, followed.leagues, filteredMatches]);

  // First later day that has matches, for the empty state's shortcut.
  const nextMatchDay = useMemo(() => {
    const later = matches
      .map((m) => new Date(m.kickoffAt))
      .filter((d) => dayKey(d) > selectedKey)
      .sort((a, b) => a.getTime() - b.getTime());
    return later[0] ? startOfDay(later[0]) : null;
  }, [matches, selectedKey]);

  // The server's sync ledger (the sentinel's snapshot), polled on a slow loop:
  // the footer's data-age line tells the truth about the DATA, not just about
  // the last browser fetch â€” sync can lag behind a 200-OK, and "silently
  // quiet" is the one failure a live-score feed must never have.
  const syncHealth = useSyncHealth();
  const serverSyncedAt = syncHealth.data?.latestLastSyncedAt
    ? new Date(syncHealth.data.latestLastSyncedAt)
    : null;
  const syncAt = serverSyncedAt ?? (lastSync as Date | null);
  const syncStale = syncAt ? Date.now() - syncAt.getTime() > 30 * 60_000 : false;

  // No data AND an error: an error over cached matches would replace a usable
  // feed, so the error card is reserved for the cold-failure case.
  const hasFeedError = matchesQuery.isError && !matchesQuery.data;

  // Localization: localized short-month header date and localized digits.
  // calendar: "gregory" keeps fa-IR on the same month/day number the strip's
  // chips print (fa-IR's default Persian calendar renames them).
  const headerDayFormatter = useMemo(
    () =>
      new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, {
        weekday: "long",
        day: "numeric",
        month: "short",
        calendar: "gregory",
      }),
    [locale],
  );
  const digitsFormatter = useMemo(
    () => new Intl.NumberFormat(lang === "fa" ? "fa-IR" : "en", { useGrouping: false }),
    [lang],
  );

  // Goal detection: when a refetch brings a new score for a match, fire the
  // volt sweep + toast. The server polls ESPN every minute; this picks that up.
  const scoreRef = useRef<Map<string, string>>(new Map());
  const seeded = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!matchesQuery.data) return;
    const incoming = new Map(
      matchesQuery.data.map((m) => [m.id, `${m.homeScore ?? "â€“"}-${m.awayScore ?? "â€“"}`]),
    );
    if (!seeded.current) {
      scoreRef.current = incoming;
      seeded.current = true;
      // The first successful fetch counts as a sync, so the feed can say when
      // it was last updated before any score has changed.
      setLastSync(new Date());
      return;
    }
    for (const [id, score] of incoming) {
      const prev = scoreRef.current.get(id);
      if (prev && prev !== score && score !== "â€“-â€“") {
        const match = matchesQuery.data.find((m) => m.id === id);
        if (match) {
          setBurst((value) => value + 1);
          notifyHaptic("success");
          push({
            text: `${localize(match.home.shortName)} ${match.homeScore ?? 0}-${match.awayScore ?? 0} ${localize(match.away.shortName)}`,
            tone: "volt",
            icon: <BallIcon className="h-4 w-4" />,
          });
        }
      }
    }
    scoreRef.current = incoming;
    setLastSync(new Date());
  }, [matchesQuery.data, push, localize]);

  function refresh() {
    haptic("light");
    setRefreshing(true);
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["matches"] }).then(() => {
      setRefreshing(false);
      const now = new Date();
      setLastSync(now);
      push({
        text: t("matches.lastSync", { time: formatClock(now, locale) }),
        tone: "muted",
        // A successful sync, not a spinner: the checkmark says "done".
        icon: "âœ“",
      });
    });
  }

  // Pull down from the top of the feed to resync: the gesture this screen's
  // users expect on a live-score view. The hook grabs the downward drag only
  // while the feed is at the very top and preventDefaults it, which is exactly
  // the shape Telegram's swipe-to-claim needs to lose: the app also calls
  // `disableVerticalSwipes()` once on boot (lib/telegram.ts), so the native
  // collapse gesture never forms to begin with, and this manual refresh is the
  // gesture that stays. Distance is capped and rubber-banded so the drag never
  // reaches the header collapse threshold.
  const pull = usePullToRefresh(refresh, !refreshing);

  return (
    // â‰¥600px: the whole screen (header row, strip, feed) re-centres into a
    // 480px column instead of stretching edge to edge â€” the companion web
    // layout. The inline `--app-max-width` feeds TopBar's own max-w (its
    // default is 768px), so the header's gutters land on the same column as
    // everything below it; below 600px the cap is inert and nothing changes.
    <div
      className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[480px]"
      style={{ "--app-max-width": "480px" } as CSSProperties}
    >
      <ConfettiBurst trigger={burst} />
      <TopBar
        title={t("matches.title")}
        // The subtitle is the SELECTED day plus its count: "ÛŒÚ©Ø´Ù†Ø¨Ù‡ Û²Ûµ Ø§Ú©ØªØ¨Ø± Â·
        // Û²Û² Ù…Ø³Ø§Ø¨Ù‚Ù‡" â€” the DateStrip below can move the feed a week, so both
        // the weekday and the number must track the picked day, not the boot
        // clock. Digits localize (Persian digits under fa), the word is an
        // i18n key, and the old standalone count chip is gone.
        subtitle={
          /* The selected day, its count, and the ONE mention of Tehran time:
             every clock on this screen is Tehran time, so it is stated here
             and the per-row captions were removed. */
          `${t(
            dayMatches.length === 1 ? "matches.headerCountOne" : "matches.headerCount",
            {
              date: headerDayFormatter.format(selectedDay),
              count: digitsFormatter.format(dayMatches.length),
            },
          )}${
            dayMatches.length > 0 ? ` Â· ${t("matches.headerTz")}` : ""
          }`
        }
        right={
          // One header action: the calendar. Refresh's button went away â€” the
          // gesture above owns the resync, and the toast after every sync is
          // still the visible confirmation. The calendar keeps its 40px visual
          // circle with an invisible 44px hit band (after:-inset-1) so the
          // thumb target never drops under the 44px floor.
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              haptic("light");
              setCalendarOpen(true);
            }}
            aria-haspopup="dialog"
            aria-label={t("matches.pickDate")}
            title={t("matches.pickDate")}
            className="relative grid h-10 w-10 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full border border-line text-ink transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated after:absolute after:-inset-1 after:content-['']"
          >
            <CalendarIcon className="h-[18px] w-[18px]" />
          </button>
        }
      />

      {/* Everything below sits under the sticky header. The wrapper gives the
          pull-to-refresh marker an anchor so it slides in between header and
          feed instead of fighting the header for the same band. */}
      <div className="relative" >
        <PullIndicator pull={pull} />
        {/* A full refresh in flight: the subtle carousel is the only visible
            state, since the header no longer carries a refresh button. */}
        {refreshing ? (
          <span
            role="status"
            aria-label={t("common.loading")}
            className="absolute end-0 top-0 z-10 grid h-7 w-7 place-items-center"
          >
            <span
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-line border-t-volt"
            />
          </span>
        ) : null}

        {/* Date strip: seven day chips across the full content width, today
            selected by default. mt-3 is the 12px gap under the header's bottom
            border; the calendar moved up into the header, and the sheet below
            is rendered from here so the header button has one owner. The dots
            under the chips mark the days a FOLLOWED club plays (teal), not
            every fixture-bearing day. */}
        <div className="mt-3">
          <DateStrip selected={selectedDay} onSelect={setPicked} daysWithFavorites={daysWithFavorites} />
        </div>
        <CalendarSheet
          open={calendarOpen}
          onClose={() => setCalendarOpen(false)}
          selected={selectedDay}
          onSelect={setPicked}
          daysWithMatches={daysWithMatches}
        />

        {/* State filter chips: all / live (with its count) / followed clubs.
            A chip that can never show anything hides itself â€” the live chip
            vanishes on a day without live football, the mine chip without
            followed clubs, so the row only offers real views of this feed. */}
        {dayMatches.length > 0 && (liveCount > 0 || favCount > 0) ? (
          <div className="-mx-[var(--space-page-x,16px)] mt-sm flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto px-[var(--space-page-x,16px)] py-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                haptic("light");
                setDayFilter("all");
              }}
              aria-pressed={dayFilter === "all"}
              className={cx(LEAGUE_CHIP, dayFilter === "all" ? LEAGUE_CHIP_ON : LEAGUE_CHIP_OFF)}
            >
              {t("matches.filter.all")}
            </button>
            {liveCount > 0 ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  haptic("light");
                  setDayFilter(dayFilter === "live" ? "all" : "live");
                }}
                aria-pressed={dayFilter === "live"}
                className={cx(
                  LEAGUE_CHIP,
                  dayFilter === "live" ? "border-live text-live" : LEAGUE_CHIP_OFF,
                )}
              >
                {t("matches.filter.live", {
                  count: digitsFormatter.format(liveCount),
                })}
              </button>
            ) : null}
            {favCount > 0 ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  haptic("light");
                  setDayFilter(dayFilter === "mine" ? "all" : "mine");
                }}
                aria-pressed={dayFilter === "mine"}
                className={cx(
                  LEAGUE_CHIP,
                  dayFilter === "mine"
                    ? "border-volt bg-volt/15 text-volt"
                    : LEAGUE_CHIP_OFF,
                )}
              >
                {t("matches.filter.mine")}
              </button>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {/* Favorite leagues â€” the start page's own way in. One tap re-scopes
            the whole feed (concept pillar 1); the last chip opens the picker,
            and the row is here even with nothing followed so leagues are
            pickable from the feed rather than only during onboarding.

            py-1 keeps the chips' 4px invisible hit band INSIDE this scroll
            container: overflow-x-auto clips vertically too, so without the
            padding the extra target area above and below is unreachable. It is
            a plain 12px margin above now, not the old -my-1 compensation: the
            row is part of the 8px rhythm (12px under the strip, and the first
            group 16px under the row), and a -4px bottom margin was
            eating 4px of that gap. */}
        {!league && (followed.count > 0 || favCount > 0) ? (
          <div className="-mx-[var(--space-page-x,16px)] mt-md flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto px-[var(--space-page-x,16px)] py-1">
            {followed.count > 0 ? (
              <>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    haptic("light");
                    setLeagueFilter(null);
                  }}
                  aria-pressed={leagueFilter === null}
                  className={cx(LEAGUE_CHIP, leagueFilter === null ? LEAGUE_CHIP_ON : LEAGUE_CHIP_OFF)}
                >
                  {t("browse.all")}
                </button>
                {followed.leagues.map((l) => (
                  <button
                    key={l.league}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      haptic("light");
                      setLeagueFilter(l.league);
                    }}
                    aria-pressed={leagueFilter === l.league}
                    className={cx(LEAGUE_CHIP, leagueFilter === l.league ? LEAGUE_CHIP_ON : LEAGUE_CHIP_OFF)}                    >
                    {l.name ? localize(l.name) : competitionName(l.league, lang)}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </>
            ) : null}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                haptic("light");
                setLeaguesOpen(true);
              }}
              aria-haspopup="dialog"
              className={cx(LEAGUE_CHIP, "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 border-volt text-volt")}
              style={{ backgroundColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--volt) 12%, transparent)" }}
            >
              <PlusIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              {t(followed.count > 0 ? "leagues.edit" : "matches.pickLeagues")}
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        <LeaguePickerSheet open={leaguesOpen} onClose={() => setLeaguesOpen(false)} />

        {/* Single-league deep link: name the scope, let the user leave. Without
          this the screen looks like a broken feed when the window is quiet. */}
        {league ? (
          <div className="mt-md flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface px-4 py-3">
            <p className="min-w-0 truncate text-xs text-ink-2">
              {t("matches.leagueScope", {
                league: competitionName(league, lang),
              })}
            </p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                haptic("light");
                navigate("/matches");
              }}
              className="inline-flex min-h-11 flex-none items-center rounded-lg border border-volt px-3 text-xs font-bold text-volt transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated"
            >
              {t("matches.showAll")}
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {/* No favorites yet: above the groups, because it is the answer to an
            empty feed rather than a footnote under one. Hidden while the first
            payload loads â€” the skeleton list stands in for the whole feed. */}
        {showPickBanner && !matchesQuery.isLoading ? (
          <div
            role="region"
            aria-label={t("matches.pickBanner")}
            className="mt-md flex min-h-12 items-center gap-2 rounded-card border border-line bg-elevated ps-3 pe-0.5"
          >
            <StarFilledIcon className="h-4 w-4 flex-none text-gold" aria-hidden="true" />
            <p className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-xs font-bold text-ink">{t("matches.pickBanner")}</p>
            <Link
              to="/onboarding"
              onClick={() => haptic("light")}
              // A pill, not a block glued to the banner's edge: 36px tall
              // inside the 48px banner, with an invisible band to 44px.
              className="relative inline-flex h-9 flex-none items-center rounded-full bg-volt px-4 text-[13px] font-bold text-black after:absolute after:-inset-1 after:content-['']"
            >
              {t("matches.pickBannerCta")}
            </Link>
            <button
              type="button"
              aria-label={t("matches.pickBannerDismiss")}
              onClick={() => {
                haptic("light");
                setBannerDismissed(true);
              }}
              className="grid h-11 w-11 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full text-ink-3"
            >
              <CloseIcon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {/* Feed states: three skeletons while the first payload lands, an error
            card only when there is nothing cached, and an empty day that offers
            the next day with football instead of a dead end. */}
        {matchesQuery.isLoading ? (
          <ul className="mt-md space-y-3" aria-busy="true">
            <LeagueGroupSkeleton />
          </ul>
        ) : hasFeedError ? (
          <div className="mt-md">
            <ErrorState onRetry={() => matchesQuery.refetch()} />
          </div>
        ) : dayMatches.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="mt-md">
            <EmptyState
              icon="âš½"
              title={t("matches.emptyDay", { day: dayLabel(selectedDay) })}
              hint={
                nextMatchDay
                  ? t("matches.emptyNext", { day: dayLabel(nextMatchDay) })
                  : t("matches.emptyHint")
              }
              action={
                nextMatchDay ? (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      haptic("light");
                      setPicked(nextMatchDay);
                    }}
                    className="min-h-11 rounded-lg border border-volt px-5 text-sm font-bold text-volt transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated"
                  >
                    {t("matches.goToDay", { day: dayLabel(nextMatchDay) })}
                  </button>
                ) : null
              }
            />
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {/* The day's matches, grouped by competition â€” followed leagues first
            (the user's own follow order), then by earliest kickoff. Each group
            is one rounded container: header row (teal square dot, league name,
            count on the far end) over kick-off-sorted rows with hairline
            dividers. The sponsored card keeps its between-groups slot, after
            the leading group when there are several. */}
        {!matchesQuery.isLoading && !hasFeedError && leagueGroups.length > 0 ? (
          <div key={selectedKey} className="day-swap mt-4 space-y-3">
            {leagueGroups.map((group, index) => (
              <Fragment key={group.league}>
                <LeagueGroup
                  league={group.league}
                  matches={group.matches}
                  isFavoriteTeam={isFavorite}
                  index={index}
                />
                {index === 0 && leagueGroups.length > 1 ? <InlineAd /> : null}
              </Fragment>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {/* Sync note: the feed resyncs through pull-to-refresh, so this line
            only reports when the data last moved. */}
        <p className="mt-xl text-center text-xs text-ink-3">
          {t("matches.hint")}
          {syncStale
            ? ` Â· ${t("matches.syncDelayed")}`
            : syncAt
              ? ` Â· ${t("matches.dataAsOf", { time: formatClock(syncAt, locale) })}`
              : lastSync
                ? ` Â· ${t("matches.lastSync", { time: formatClock(lastSync, locale) })}`
                : ""}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\pages\NotFound.tsx =====

import { useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { AlertIcon } from "../components/icons";
import { TopBar } from "../components/TopBar";
import { Button, Card } from "../components/ui";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";

/**
 * Unknown route. The wildcard used to redirect to /browse silently, which hid
 * broken deep links from the bot and from shared URLs; an explicit screen
 * makes the mistake visible and gives the user a way back.
 */
export default function NotFound() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const location = useLocation();

  return (
    <>
      <TopBar title={t("notFound.title")} back />
      <Card className="mt-2xl flex flex-col items-center p-6 text-center">
        <AlertIcon className="h-12 w-12 mx-auto mb-4 text-volt" />
        <p className="font-num text-display font-black text-ink-3" aria-hidden="true">
          404
        </p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm font-bold text-ink">{t("notFound.body")}</p>
        <p className="mt-1 break-all text-xs text-ink-3">{location.pathname}</p>
        <Button className="mt-lg" onClick={() => window.history.back()}>
          {t("common.back")}
        </Button>
        <p className="mt-md text-xs text-ink-3">{t("notFound.browse")}</p>
      </Card>
    </>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\pages\Onboarding.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { TopBar } from "../components/TopBar";
import { LegalLinks } from "../components/LegalLinks";
import { useToast } from "../components/toastContext";
import { PanelSkeleton } from "../components/Skeletons";
import { ErrorState, EmptyState } from "../components/ui";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";
import { Button, Chip, Num, Segmented, TeamBadge } from "../components/ui";
import { ArrowIcon, CheckIcon, CloseIcon, PencilIcon, StarFilledIcon, TargetIcon } from "../components/icons";
import { useTeams, useCompetitions } from "../hooks/useApi";
import {
  MAX_FAVORITES,
  MAX_FOLLOWED_LEAGUES,
  MAX_INTERNATIONALS,
  useFavorites,
  useFollowedLeagues,
} from "../hooks/useFavorites";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import type { StringKey } from "../i18n/strings";
import { serverErrorKey } from "../lib/api";
import { haptic, selectionHaptic, notifyHaptic, telegram } from "../lib/telegram";
import { buildPickerGroups, teamsInGroup } from "../lib/pickerGroups";
import { PREDICTIONS_ENABLED, RATINGS_ENABLED } from "../lib/features";

const TOTAL_STEPS = 3;

/** localStorage flag for the animated intro (separate from the picker gate). */
const INTRO_KEY = "verdikick.introSeen";

function introSeen() {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(INTRO_KEY) === "1";
  } catch {
    return true; // private mode: never show the intro rather than loop on it
  }
}

/** The ideas, in the order the product teaches them. */
const ALL_SLIDES = [
  { key: "follow", titleKey: "intro.follow.title", bodyKey: "intro.follow.body", Glyph: StarFilledIcon },
  { key: "predict", titleKey: "intro.predict.title", bodyKey: "intro.predict.body", Glyph: TargetIcon },
  { key: "rate", titleKey: "intro.rate.title", bodyKey: "intro.rate.body", Glyph: PencilIcon },
] as const;

// Predictions and ratings are off for now: each slide stays in the repo and
// comes back with its flag. Only the list is filtered â€” copy and glyph are
// untouched.
const SLIDES = ALL_SLIDES.filter(
  (s) => (PREDICTIONS_ENABLED || s.key !== "predict") && (RATINGS_ENABLED || s.key !== "rate"),
);

/**
 * First-run onboarding in the concept's order:
 *   1. pick the favorite club (the anchor)
 *   2. pick up to four more clubs
 *   3. follow up to five leagues
 * Each step is skippable except the first â€” the anchor club is the product.
 */
export default function Onboarding() {
  const { t, lang, localize } = useI18n();
  const { push } = useToast();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { teams, anchorId, count, clubCount, intlCount, isFavorite, toggle, toggleAndAnchor } =
    useFavorites();
  const followed = useFollowedLeagues();
  const [intro, setIntro] = useState(() => {
    if (SLIDES.length >= 2) return !introSeen();
    // With a single idea left the intro is a screen that explains one thing and
    // asks for a tap: skip it, but still mark it seen so nothing reappears when
    // a flag is turned back on and the list grows.
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(INTRO_KEY, "1");
    } catch {
      /* private mode */
    }
    return false;
  });
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  // Counts inside Arabic-script copy carry Persian digits (Â«Ú¯Ø§Ù… Û² Ø§Ø² Û³Â») â€”
  // Latin numerals break the bidi run the way they did in match countdowns.
  // EN locale (undefined) passes plain digits straight through.
  const nf = useMemo(() => new Intl.NumberFormat(lang === "fa" ? "fa-IR" : undefined), [lang]);

  // Android hardware back walks the picker back instead of closing the app.
  // The global hook treats /onboarding as a root (it hides Telegram's back
  // button), so while the user sits deeper than the first step this screen
  // owns the back affordance; step 0 and the intro give it back to Telegram.
  useEffect(() => {
    const app = telegram();
    if (!app?.BackButton || intro || step === 0) return;
    const bb = app.BackButton;
    bb.show();
    const onBack = () => setStep((s) => Math.max(0, s - 1));
    bb.onClick(onBack);
    return () => {
      bb.offClick(onBack);
      bb.hide();
    };
  }, [intro, step]);

  const teamsQuery = useTeams();
  // Stable identity: `groupTeams` and the picker memos below depend on it.
  const allTeams = useMemo(() => teamsQuery.data ?? [], [teamsQuery.data]);
  const [group, setGroup] = useState<string>("PL");
  // The tabs reach the cup-only clubs and national teams too, not just the
  // big five â€” an onboarding flow that can only pick big-five clubs would
  // silently make everything else in the catalog unfollowable.
  const groupTeams = useMemo(() => teamsInGroup(allTeams, group), [allTeams, group]);

  // Team-grid states: loading skeleton, error retry, or empty hint â€” never a
  // silent blank grid while the data layer is still warming up or has gone
  // quiet (fresh deploy, ESPN hiccup, cold cache).
  function teamGridStates() {
    if (teamsQuery.isLoading) {
      return { kind: "loading" as const };
    }
    if (teamsQuery.isError) {
      return {
        kind: "error" as const,
        retry: () => teamsQuery.refetch(),
      };
    }
    if (allTeams.length === 0) {
      return { kind: "empty" as const };
    }
    return { kind: "content" as const };
  }

  const compsQuery = useCompetitions();
  const leagueComps = (compsQuery.data?.competitions ?? []).filter((c) => c.kind === "league");

  // Favorite-team tabs are the big five plus Other/International only â€” the
  // picker is a Top-5 surface (see lib/pickerGroups). The league-FOLLOWING
  // step below still lists every league from the catalog.
  const pickerGroups = useMemo(() => buildPickerGroups((key) => t(key)), [t]);

  const anchorTeam = useMemo(() => allTeams.find((team) => team.id === anchorId), [allTeams, anchorId]);
  const anchorName = useMemo(
    () => (anchorTeam ? localize(anchorTeam.shortName) : ""),
    [anchorTeam, localize],
  );

  function pickClub(team: (typeof allTeams)[number]) {
    selectionHaptic();
    if (isFavorite(team.id)) return;
    if (count === 0) {
      // First pick becomes the anchor automatically â€” but only AFTER the
      // follow has persisted (the anchor endpoint 404s otherwise). The
      // ordering + retry live in toggleAndAnchor / lib/favoriteAnchor.ts.
      toggleAndAnchor(team);
      return;
    }
    // The caps are per bucket â€” five clubs plus, separately, five national
    // teams â€” so a full club shelf says so without ever blocking the
    // international tab.
    const result = toggle(team);
    if (!result.ok) {
      haptic("medium");
      push({
        text: t(result.reason === "intlCap" ? "myTeams.errIntlCap" : "myTeams.errCap"),
        tone: "danger",
        icon: "âœ•",
      });
    }
  }

  function pickLeague(slug: string) {
    const onError = (err: unknown) => {
      haptic("medium");
      push({ text: t(serverErrorKey(err) as StringKey), tone: "danger", icon: "âœ•" });
    };
    selectionHaptic();
    if (followed.isFollowing(slug)) {
      followed.unfollow.mutate(slug, { onError });
    } else if (followed.count < MAX_FOLLOWED_LEAGUES) {
      followed.follow.mutate(slug, { onError });
    } else {
      haptic("medium");
    }
  }

  function finish() {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem("verdikick.onboarded", "1");
    } catch {
      /* private mode */
    }
    notifyHaptic("success");
    navigate("/matches", { replace: true });
  }

  /** Advance a step; leaving the favorite step persists the gate flag. */
  function advance() {
    haptic("light");
    if (step === 0) {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem("verdikick.onboarded", "1");
      } catch {
        /* private mode */
      }
    }
    setStep((s) => s + 1);
  }

  function dismissIntro() {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(INTRO_KEY, "1");
    } catch {
      /* private mode */
    }
    setIntro(false);
  }

  const canAdvance = (step === 0 && count >= 1) || step === 1 || step === 2;

  if (intro) return <Intro onDone={dismissIntro} />;

  return (
    <>
      <TopBar
        title={t("onboarding.title")}
        subtitle={t("onboarding.step", { step: nf.format(step + 1), total: nf.format(TOTAL_STEPS) })}
      />

      {/* Step dots */}
      <div className="mt-md flex justify-center gap-1.5" aria-hidden="true">
        {Array.from({ length: TOTAL_STEPS }, (_, i) => (
          <span
            key={i}
            className={cx(
              "h-1.5 rounded-full transition-[width,background-color] duration-[var(--t-std)]",
              i === step ? "w-6 bg-volt" : i < step ? "w-1.5 bg-volt/50" : "w-1.5 bg-line",
            )}
          />
        ))}
      </div>

      {step === 0 ? (
        <section className="mt-lg rise-in">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-ink">{t("onboarding.favoriteTitle")}</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-ink-2">{t("onboarding.favoriteHint")}</p>

          {/* The pick so far, named and faced: survives tab switches, so the
              user never has to remember what they tapped two tabs ago. */}
          {anchorTeam ? (
            <div
              className="mt-md flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-card border border-gold bg-elevated px-3 py-2.5"
              role="status"
            >
              <TeamBadge team={anchorTeam} size="md" />
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <p className="truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">{anchorName}</p>
                <p className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-gold">
                  <StarFilledIcon className="star-pop h-3 w-3 flex-none" />
                  {t("onboarding.isFavorite")}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : null}

          <div className="mt-md">
            <Segmented value={group} onChange={setGroup} options={pickerGroups} />
          </div>

          {(() => {
            const state = teamGridStates();
            if (state.kind === "loading") {
              return <PanelSkeleton height={220} />;
            }
            if (state.kind === "error") {
              return (
                <ErrorState
                  title={t("onboarding.teamsError")}
                  hint={t("onboarding.teamsErrorHint")}
                  onRetry={state.retry}
                />
              );
            }
            if (state.kind === "empty") {
              return <EmptyState title={t("onboarding.teamsEmpty")} hint={t("onboarding.teamsEmptyHint")} />;
            }
            return (
              <ul className="mt-md grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                {groupTeams.map((team) => {
                  const pickedHere = isFavorite(team.id);
                  const isAnchor = team.id === anchorId;
                  return (
                    <li key={team.id}>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => pickClub(team)}
                        className={cx(
                          "flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-card border p-2.5 text-start transition-[color,background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.98]",
                          pickedHere
                            ? isAnchor
                              ? "border-gold bg-elevated"
                              : "border-volt bg-elevated"
                            : "border-line bg-surface hover:bg-elevated",
                        )}
                      >
                        <TeamBadge team={team} size="sm" />
                        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">
                          {localize(team.shortName)}
                        </span>
                        {isAnchor ? (
                          <StarFilledIcon className="star-pop h-4 w-4 flex-none text-gold" />
                        ) : pickedHere ? (
                          <CheckIcon className="marker-pop h-4 w-4 flex-none text-volt" />
                        ) : null}
                      </button>
                    </li>
                  );
                })}
              </ul>
            );
          })()}
        </section>
      ) : null}

      {step === 1 ? (
        <section className="mt-lg rise-in">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-ink">{t("onboarding.clubsTitle")}</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-ink-2">{t("onboarding.clubsHint")}</p>

          {/* Picked clubs so far, removable: the count chip alone makes people
              back out to fix a mistaken pick. */}
          {count > 0 ? (
            <ul className="-mx-3 mt-md flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-3 pb-1">
              {[...teams]
                .sort((a, b) => Number(b.id === anchorId) - Number(a.id === anchorId))
                .map((team) => (
                  <li key={team.id} className="relative flex-none">
                    <div className="flex w-[76px] flex-col items-center gap-1 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-2 text-center">
                      <TeamBadge team={team} size="sm" />
                      <span className="w-full truncate text-xs font-bold text-ink">
                        {localize(team.shortName)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    {team.id === anchorId ? (
                      <span
                        className="star-pop absolute -top-1 -start-1 grid h-5 w-5 place-items-center rounded-full border border-gold bg-elevated text-gold"
                        aria-label={t("onboarding.isFavorite")}
                      >
                        <StarFilledIcon className="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
                      </span>
                    ) : null}
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => pickClub(team)}
                      aria-label={t("myTeams.removed", { team: localize(team.shortName) })}
                      className="absolute -top-1 -end-1 grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-full"
                    >
                      <span
                        className="grid h-5 w-5 place-items-center rounded-full border border-line bg-elevated text-ink-2 transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:border-danger hover:text-danger"
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      >
                        <CloseIcon className="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  </li>
                ))}
            </ul>
          ) : null}

          <div className="mt-md">
            <Segmented value={group} onChange={setGroup} options={pickerGroups} />
          </div>

          {(() => {
            const state = teamGridStates();
            if (state.kind === "loading") {
              return <PanelSkeleton height={220} />;
            }
            if (state.kind === "error") {
              return (
                <ErrorState
                  title={t("onboarding.teamsError")}
                  hint={t("onboarding.teamsErrorHint")}
                  onRetry={state.retry}
                />
              );
            }
            if (state.kind === "empty") {
              return <EmptyState title={t("onboarding.teamsEmpty")} hint={t("onboarding.teamsEmptyHint")} />;
            }
            return (
              <ul className="mt-md grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                {groupTeams.map((team) => {
                  const pickedHere = isFavorite(team.id);
                  return (
                    <li key={team.id}>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => pickClub(team)}
                        className={cx(
                          "flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-card border p-2.5 text-start transition-[color,background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.98]",
                          pickedHere ? "border-volt bg-elevated" : "border-line bg-surface hover:bg-elevated",
                        )}
                      >
                        <TeamBadge team={team} size="sm" />
                        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">
                          {localize(team.shortName)}
                        </span>
                        {pickedHere ? (
                          <CheckIcon className="marker-pop h-4 w-4 flex-none text-volt" aria-hidden="true" />
                        ) : null}
                      </button>
                    </li>
                  );
                })}
              </ul>
            );
          })()}

          {/* Two honest counters, one per shelf: clubs on the left, the
              optional national-team shelf on the right. */}
          <div className="mt-md flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2">
            <Chip tone={clubCount >= MAX_FAVORITES ? "gold" : "volt"}>
              {t("myTeams.picked", { count: nf.format(clubCount), max: nf.format(MAX_FAVORITES) })}
            </Chip>
            {intlCount > 0 || clubCount >= MAX_FAVORITES ? (
              <Chip tone={intlCount >= MAX_INTERNATIONALS ? "gold" : "volt"}>
                {t("myTeams.pickedIntl", { count: nf.format(intlCount), max: nf.format(MAX_INTERNATIONALS) })}
              </Chip>
            ) : null}
          </div>
          <p className="mt-2 text-center text-xs text-ink-3">{t("onboarding.intlHint")}</p>
        </section>
      ) : null}

      {step === 2 ? (
        <section className="mt-lg rise-in">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-ink">{t("onboarding.leaguesTitle")}</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-ink-2">{t("onboarding.leaguesHint")}</p>

          <ul className="mt-md space-y-2">
            {leagueComps.map((comp) => {
              const following = followed.isFollowing(comp.slug);
              return (
                <li key={comp.slug}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => pickLeague(comp.slug)}
                    className={cx(
                      "flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-card border p-3 text-start transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)]",
                      following ? "border-volt bg-elevated" : "border-line bg-surface hover:bg-elevated",
                    )}
                  >
                    {/* No monogram tile: a single letter next to the full name
                        reads as doubled text ("PPremier League"). */}
                    <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">
                      {localize(comp.name)}
                    </span>
                    {following ? <Chip tone="volt">{t("leagues.following")}</Chip> : null}
                  </button>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>

          <div className="mt-md flex justify-center">
            <Chip tone={followed.count >= MAX_FOLLOWED_LEAGUES ? "gold" : "volt"}>
              {t("myTeams.picked", { count: nf.format(followed.count), max: nf.format(MAX_FOLLOWED_LEAGUES) })}
            </Chip>
          </div>
        </section>
      ) : null}

      {/* Actions. */}
      <div className="mt-2xl space-y-2 pb-6">
        {step === 0 && count >= 1 ? (
          <Button
            className="w-full"
            onClick={() => {
              notifyHaptic("success");
              advance();
            }}
          >
            {t("onboarding.next")}
          </Button>
        ) : null}
        {canAdvance && step > 0 && step < TOTAL_STEPS - 1 ? (
          <Button className="w-full" onClick={advance}>
            {t("onboarding.next")}
          </Button>
        ) : null}
        {step === TOTAL_STEPS - 1 ? (
          <Button className="w-full" onClick={finish}>
            {t("onboarding.done")}
          </Button>
        ) : null}
        {step === 0 && count === 0 ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={finish}
            className="inline-flex min-h-11 w-full items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs font-bold text-ink-3 transition-colors hover:text-ink-2"
          >
            {t("onboarding.skip")}
          </button>
        ) : null}
        {step > 0 ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setStep((s) => s - 1)}
            className="inline-flex min-h-11 w-full items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold text-ink-3 transition-colors hover:text-ink-2"
          >
            <ArrowIcon className="h-4 w-4" />
            {t("common.back")}
          </button>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <LegalLinks className="mt-md" />

      <Num className="sr-only">{step}</Num>
    </>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ intro */

/**
 * The first-launch intro, now one screen instead of three slides. A first-timer
 * used to pay six screens (three intro slides, then three picker steps) before
 * seeing a fixture â€” on the one app whose whole promise is "open it because a
 * match is on". The ideas still get explained, as a single scannable list, with
 * one "Get started" tap into the picker (the top-right Skip is the only other
 * exit; the duplicate bottom Skip is gone). Shown once ever (its flag is
 * separate from the picker's own gate).
 */
function Intro({ onDone }: { onDone: () => void }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();

  return (
    <div className="pad-safe-top flex min-h-[70vh] flex-col px-3">
      <div className="flex justify-end">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            haptic("light");
            onDone();
          }}
          className="min-h-11 rounded-full px-3 text-xs font-bold text-ink-3 transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:text-ink-2"
        >
          {t("intro.skip")}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="pitch-watermark flex flex-1 flex-col justify-center px-2 py-6">
        <span
          className="intro-glyph grid h-20 w-20 place-items-center rounded-card border border-volt/50 bg-elevated text-volt"
          aria-hidden="true"
        >
          {PREDICTIONS_ENABLED ? (
            <TargetIcon className="h-9 w-9" />
          ) : (
            <StarFilledIcon className="h-9 w-9" />
          )}
        </span>
        {/* The intro has no TopBar, so this brand line is the screen's only
            heading â€” h1, not h2, or the document opens at a level down. */}
        <h1 className="intro-in mt-lg text-lg font-extrabold text-ink">{t("app.name")}</h1>

        <ul className="intro-in mt-lg space-y-lg">
          {SLIDES.map(({ key, titleKey, bodyKey, Glyph }) => (
            <li key={key} className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <span
                className="grid h-9 w-9 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full border border-volt/50 bg-elevated text-volt"
                aria-hidden="true"
              >
                <Glyph className="h-4 w-4" />
              </span>
              <span className="min-w-0">
                <span className="block text-sm font-bold text-ink">{t(titleKey)}</span>
                <span className="block text-xs leading-relaxed text-ink-2">{t(bodyKey)}</span>
              </span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 space-y-2 pb-6">
        <Button
          className="w-full"
          onClick={() => {
            haptic("light");
            onDone();
          }}
        >
          {t("onboarding.next")}
        </Button>
        <LegalLinks className="mt-2" />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { TopBar } from "../components/TopBar";
import { RowSkeleton } from "../components/Skeletons";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "../components/ui";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";
import { Card, Chip, Num, SectionHeading, Segmented, TeamBadge } from "../components/ui";
import {
  slugForLeague,
  useLeaguePlayers,
  useLeagueTabs,
  useLeagueTeamStats,
  type LeaderPlayerRow,
  type LeaderSort,
} from "../hooks/useApi";
import { TEAM_FALLBACK, readableInk } from "../lib/colors";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { haptic } from "../lib/telegram";
import type { StringKey } from "../i18n/strings";

/** Leaderboard columns: i18n key + the server sort field. */
const SORTS: Array<{ key: LeaderSort; labelKey: StringKey }> = [
  { key: "goals", labelKey: "stats.goals" },
  { key: "assists", labelKey: "stats.assists" },
  { key: "stat_score_total", labelKey: "ratings.points" },
  { key: "appearances", labelKey: "table.played" },
  { key: "minutes", labelKey: "stats.minutes" },
  { key: "shots", labelKey: "stats.shots" },
  { key: "shots_on_target", labelKey: "stats.onTarget" },
  { key: "yellow_cards", labelKey: "stats.yellow" },
  { key: "red_cards", labelKey: "stats.red" },
  { key: "saves", labelKey: "stats.saves" },
  { key: "goals_conceded", labelKey: "stats.conceded" },
  { key: "fouls_committed", labelKey: "stats.fouls" },
  { key: "own_goals", labelKey: "stats.ownGoals" },
];

/**
 * Player stats tables â€” season leaderboards per league (most goals, assists,
 * minutes, cards, saves, everything) plus a team-stats tab. All real, all
 * recomputed server-side after every sync.
 */
export default function PlayerStats() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const tabs = useLeagueTabs();
  const [league, setLeague] = useState<string>("PL");
  const [view, setView] = useState<"players" | "teams">("players");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<LeaderSort>("goals");

  // Tab values are big-five aliases; anything else is already a slug.
  const slug = slugForLeague(league);

  return (
    <>
      <TopBar title={t("statsPage.title")} subtitle={t("statsPage.subtitle")} back />

      <div className="mt-3">
        <Segmented
          value={view}
          onChange={setView}
          options={[
            { value: "players", label: t("statsPage.players") },
            { value: "teams", label: t("statsPage.teams") },
          ]}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-3">
        <Segmented
          value={league}
          onChange={setLeague}
          options={tabs.map((tab) => ({ value: tab.value, label: tab.label }))}
        />
      </div>

      {view === "players" ? (
        <PlayerBoard league={slug} sort={sort} onSort={setSort} />
      ) : (
        <TeamBoard league={slug} />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

function PlayerBoard({
  league,
  sort,
  onSort,
}: {
  league: string;
  sort: LeaderSort;
  onSort: (s: LeaderSort) => void;
}) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const query = useLeaguePlayers(league, sort, 50);
  const rows = query.data ?? [];
  const activeSort = SORTS.find((s) => s.key === sort) ?? SORTS[0];

  return (
    <>
      <div className="mt-4">
        <SectionHeading
          title={t(activeSort.labelKey)}
          action={
            <select
              value={sort}
              onChange={(e) => {
                haptic("light");
                onSort(e.target.value as LeaderSort);
              }}
              className="min-h-11 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface px-3 text-xs font-bold text-ink focus:border-volt focus:outline-none"
              aria-label={t("statsPage.sortBy")}
            >
              {SORTS.map((s) => (
                <option key={s.key} value={s.key}>
                  {t(s.labelKey)}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          }
        />
      </div>

      {query.isLoading ? (
        <div className="mt-3">
          <RowSkeleton rows={8} />
        </div>
      ) : query.isError ? (
        <ErrorState onRetry={() => query.refetch()} />
      ) : rows.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title={t("stats.emptyTitle")}
          hint={t("stats.noneHint")}
          action={
            <Link
              to="/matches"
              className="min-h-11 rounded-full border border-volt px-5 text-xs font-bold leading-[2.75rem] text-volt transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated"
            >
              {t("stats.emptyAction")}
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2 fade-swap">
          {rows.map((row, index) => (
            <li
              key={row.playerId}
              className={cx(
                "flex items-center gap-3 rounded-card border p-2.5",
                index === 0 ? "border-gold/50 bg-elevated" : "border-line bg-surface",
              )}
            >
              <Num
                className={cx(
                  "w-6 flex-none text-center text-sm",
                  index === 0 ? "text-gold" : index < 3 ? "text-gold/80" : "text-ink-3",
                )}
              >
                {index + 1}
              </Num>
              <div
                className="grid h-9 w-9 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full text-xs font-bold"
                style={{ backgroundColor: row.teamColor ?? TEAM_FALLBACK, color: readableInk(row.teamColor) }}
                aria-hidden="true"
              >
                {row.jersey ?? row.position[0]}
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <p className="truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">{localize(row.name)}</p>
                <p className="truncate text-xs text-ink-3">
                  {localize(row.teamShort ?? row.teamName)} Â· {t(`pos.${row.position}` as StringKey)} Â·{" "}
                  <Num>{row.appearances}</Num>
                  {t("table.played" as StringKey)}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex-none text-end">
                <Num className={cx("block text-lg leading-none", index === 0 ? "text-gold" : "text-ink")}>
                  {formatValue(row, sort)}
                </Num>
                <span className="label text-ink-3">{t(activeSort.labelKey)}</span>
              </div>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

function formatValue(row: LeaderPlayerRow, sort: LeaderSort): string {
  const value =
    sort === "stat_score_total" ? row.statScoreTotal : (row[sort as keyof LeaderPlayerRow] as number);
  if (sort === "minutes" && typeof value === "number" && value >= 1000) {
    return `${Math.round(value / 100) / 10}k`;
  }
  return String(value ?? 0);
}

function TeamBoard({ league }: { league: string }) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const query = useLeagueTeamStats(league);
  const rows = query.data ?? [];

  if (query.isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="mt-4">
        <RowSkeleton rows={8} />
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (query.isError) {
    return <ErrorState onRetry={() => query.refetch()} />;
  }
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return (
      <EmptyState
        title={t("stats.emptyTitle")}
        hint={t("stats.noneHint")}
        action={
          <Link
            to="/matches"
            className="min-h-11 rounded-full border border-volt px-5 text-xs font-bold leading-[2.75rem] text-volt transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated"
          >
            {t("stats.emptyAction")}
          </Link>
        }
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <section className="mt-4">
      <SectionHeading title={t("statsPage.teamTable")} />
      <div className="space-y-2">
        {rows.map((row) => (
          <Card key={row.teamId} className="p-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <TeamBadge
                team={{
                  id: row.teamId,
                  name: row.name,
                  shortName: row.shortName ?? row.name,
                  abbreviation: row.shortName?.slice(0, 3) ?? "â€”",
                  color: row.color ?? TEAM_FALLBACK,
                  league: "PL",
                }}
                size="sm"
              />
              <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">
                {localize(row.shortName ?? row.name)}
              </span>
              <Chip tone="muted">
                <Num>{row.played}</Num>
                {t("table.played")}
              </Chip>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-4 gap-2 border-t border-line pt-2 text-center">
              <Metric label={t("table.pts")} value={`${row.wins * 3 + row.draws}`} />
              <Metric label={t("stats.goals")} value={`${row.goalsFor}:${row.goalsAgainst}`} />
              <Metric label={t("stats.possession")} value={`${Math.round(row.avgPossession)}%`} />
              <Metric label={t("stats.passAccuracy")} value={`${Math.round(row.avgPassAccuracy)}%`} />
            </div>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function Metric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <p className="text-xs text-ink-3">{label}</p>
      <Num className="text-sm text-ink">{value}</Num>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\pages\Predictions.tsx =====

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { TopBar } from "../components/TopBar";
import { CompRowSkeleton } from "../components/Skeletons";
import { Chip, EmptyState, ErrorState, Num, SectionHeading } from "../components/ui";
import { useMyPredictionHistory, type PredictionHistoryRow } from "../hooks/useApi";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import {
  breakdownItems,
  pickSummary,
  MECHANIC_LABELS,
  STATUS_LABELS,
  type BreakdownLang,
} from "../lib/breakdown";
import { formatCountdown, isOpen, type MatchStatus, type Mechanic } from "../lib/predictionWindow";
import { useNow } from "../hooks/useNow";
import { formatKickoff } from "../lib/format";

/**
 * The "why did I get X" panel (roadmap Phase 2). Every pick the caller has made,
 * with the resolver's own line items â€” the scoring that produced the number,
 * not a separate explanation that could drift from it.
 *
 * Public board identity is the @username, but this page is private history, so
 * it needs no ranking affordances beyond the points each pick earned.
 */
export default function Predictions() {
  const { t, lang, localize } = useI18n();
  const query = useMyPredictionHistory();
  const rows = query.data ?? [];
  // Ticking clock for the pending rows' "locks in Xh Ym" state.
  const now = useNow(30_000);

  const settled = rows.filter((r) => r.status !== "pending");
  const pending = rows.filter((r) => r.status === "pending");

  return (
    <>
      <TopBar title={t("predictions.title")} subtitle={t("predictions.subtitle")} />

      {query.isLoading ? (
        <div className="mt-4">
          <CompRowSkeleton rows={4} />
        </div>
      ) : query.isError ? (
        <div className="mt-4">
          <ErrorState onRetry={() => query.refetch()} />
        </div>
      ) : rows.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="mt-4">
          <EmptyState title={t("predictions.empty")} hint={t("predictions.emptyHint")} />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <section className="mt-4 fade-swap">
            <SectionHeading
              title={t("predictions.settled")}
              action={
                <Chip tone="muted">
                  <Num>{settled.length}</Num>
                </Chip>
              }
            />
            {settled.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState title={t("predictions.noneSettled")} />
            ) : (
              <ul className="space-y-2">
                {settled.map((row) => (
                  <PredictionCard key={row.id} row={row} lang={lang} localize={localize} now={now} />
                ))}
              </ul>
            )}
          </section>

          {pending.length > 0 ? (
            <section className="mt-6 fade-swap">
              <SectionHeading
                title={t("predictions.pending")}
                action={
                  <Chip tone="muted">
                    <Num>{pending.length}</Num>
                  </Chip>
                }
              />
              <ul className="space-y-2">
                {pending.map((row) => (
                  <PredictionCard key={row.id} row={row} lang={lang} localize={localize} now={now} />
                ))}
              </ul>
            </section>
          ) : null}
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

const STATUS_TONE: Record<string, "volt" | "gold" | "danger" | "muted"> = {
  correct: "volt",
  partial: "gold",
  wrong: "danger",
  void: "muted",
  pending: "muted",
};

function PredictionCard({
  row,
  lang,
  localize,
  now,
}: {
  row: PredictionHistoryRow;
  lang: BreakdownLang;
  localize: (text: string) => string;
  now: number;
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const items = breakdownItems(row.breakdown, lang);
  const hasScore = row.homeScore !== null && row.awayScore !== null;
  // The same state machine the hub renders, fed by the window the server built
  // from the write path's own rules â€” so a pending row says honestly whether a
  // pick can still be changed and when it locks.
  const windowOpen =
    row.status === "pending" && row.window
      ? isOpen(row.mechanic as Mechanic, row.window, {
          matchStatus: row.matchStatus as MatchStatus,
          lineupsPublished: row.lineupPublished,
          now,
        })
      : false;
  const countdown = windowOpen && row.window ? formatCountdown(row.window.lockAt, now) : null;
  // Names come from the server (one lookup for the page, not one per row);
  // localize() renders them in the user's language, and an id the server
  // couldn't resolve degrades to #id rather than a blank line.
  const pick = pickSummary(row.mechanic, row.payload, {
    t,
    nameOf: (id) => {
      const name = row.playerNames[id];
      return name ? localize(name) : `#${id}`;
    },
  });

  return (
    <li className="rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-3">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Chip tone="pitch" normalCase>
          {MECHANIC_LABELS[row.mechanic]?.[lang] ?? row.mechanic}
        </Chip>
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">
          {localize(row.homeShort)} <span className="text-ink-3">v</span> {localize(row.awayShort)}
        </span>
        <Chip tone={STATUS_TONE[row.status] ?? "muted"}>
          {STATUS_LABELS[row.status]?.[lang] ?? row.status}
        </Chip>
      </div>

      {pick ? (
        <p className="mt-1 truncate text-xs text-ink-2" dir="auto">
          {pick}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t border-line pt-2 text-xs text-ink-3">
        <span>
          {row.kickoffAt ? formatKickoff(new Date(row.kickoffAt), lang === "fa" ? "fa-IR" : undefined) : ""}
          {hasScore ? ` Â· ${row.homeScore}â€“${row.awayScore}` : ""}
        </span>
        <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {row.status === "pending" ? (
            row.matchStatus === "finished" ? (
              <span className="text-ink-3">{t("predictions.notSettled")}</span>
            ) : windowOpen ? (
              <Chip tone="volt">
                {countdown
                  ? t("predictions.windowOpenLock", { time: countdown })
                  : t("predictions.windowOpen")}
              </Chip>
            ) : (
              <Chip tone="muted">{t("predictions.windowLocked")}</Chip>
            )
          ) : (
            <Chip tone={row.points > 0 ? "volt" : "muted"}>
              {t("predictions.points", { points: row.points })}
            </Chip>
          )}
          <Link
            to={`/match/${row.matchId}`}
            className="-my-3 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center px-1 text-xs font-bold text-ink-2 transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:text-ink"
          >
            {t("match.summary")}
          </Link>
        </span>
      </div>

      {items.length > 0 ? (
        <dl className="mt-2 space-y-1 border-t border-line pt-2">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <div key={item.key} className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
              <dt className={`text-xs ${item.flag ? "text-ink-3" : "text-ink-2"}`}>{item.label}</dt>
              <dd className={`font-num text-xs font-bold ${item.flag ? "text-ink-3" : "text-ink"}`}>
                {item.value}
              </dd>
            </div>
          ))}
        </dl>
      ) : null}
    </li>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\pages\Profile.tsx =====

import { useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { BottomSheet, SheetActions } from "../components/BottomSheet";

import { ChevronRightIcon, CloseIcon, PencilIcon } from "../components/icons";
import { TopBar, LanguageToggle } from "../components/TopBar";
import { useToast } from "../components/toastContext";
import { Avatar, Button, Card, Chip, ListRow, SectionHeading, Switch, TeamBadge } from "../components/ui";
import { useAuth } from "../hooks/authContext";
import { useFavorites } from "../hooks/useFavorites";
import { useNotificationPrefs, useReferral } from "../hooks/useApi";
import { LeaguePickerSheet } from "../components/panels/LeaguePickerSheet";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { PanelSkeleton } from "../components/Skeletons";
import { api, serverErrorKey } from "../lib/api";
import { APP_VERSION, WHATS_NEW } from "../lib/appInfo";
import { toFaDigits } from "../lib/format";
import { PREDICTIONS_ENABLED, RATINGS_ENABLED } from "../lib/features";
import { LEGAL_ROUTES } from "../lib/legal";
import { useLocalState } from "../lib/useLocalState";
import { haptic, notifyHaptic } from "../lib/telegram";

/**
 * Reject after `ms` so a request that never settles cannot pin the UI in a
 * busy state. The underlying fetch is left alone â€” it may still complete â€” but
 * the caller gets an error and can recover.
 */
function withTimeout<T>(promise: Promise<T>, ms: number): Promise<T> {
  return Promise.race([
    promise,
    new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
      setTimeout(() => reject(new Error("REQUEST_TIMEOUT")), ms);
    }),
  ]);
}

export default function Profile() {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n();
  const { push } = useToast();
  const { user, logout, updateDisplayName } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [notificationsOpen, setNotificationsOpen] = useState(false);
  // Saved leagues are edited in place: the same picker sheet Matches uses.
  const [leaguesOpen, setLeaguesOpen] = useState(false);

  // Rename display name: draft state for the sheet, error mirrors the form
  // pattern on the Login screen.
  const [nameOpen, setNameOpen] = useState(false);
  const [nameDraft, setNameDraft] = useState("");
  const [nameError, setNameError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [nameBusy, setNameBusy] = useState(false);

  // Delete account: a confirm sheet, an explicit destructive action, and the
  // busy/error state the form pattern uses everywhere else.
  const [deleteOpen, setDeleteOpen] = useState(false);
  const [deleteBusy, setDeleteBusy] = useState(false);
  const [deleteError, setDeleteError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  /** Reset local data: a confirm sheet, no silent wipe. */
  const [resetOpen, setResetOpen] = useState(false);

  // The runtime badge is a dev indicator: stripped entirely from production
  // builds instead of just hidden, so no styling slip can resurrect it.
  const isDev = import.meta.env.DEV;

  // Push preferences live on the SERVER (user_notification_prefs) â€” the bot
  // checks them before sending, so these toggles actually gate sends. Only
  // the haptics switch stays local (it's a device-side concern).
  const { prefs, set } = useNotificationPrefs();
  const serverPrefs = prefs ?? { goals: true, deadline: true, ratings: true };
  const [hapticsOn, setHapticsOn] = useLocalState<boolean>("matchday.haptics", true);

  const setPref = (key: "goals" | "deadline" | "ratings") => (next: boolean) => {
    set.mutate({ [key]: next });
  };

  // Identity comes from the server session (username login or Telegram link).
  const displayName = user?.displayName || t("profile.guest");
  const viaTelegram = user?.authMode === "telegram";
  const viaUsername = user?.authMode === "session" && user.username !== null;

  const signOut = async () => {
    haptic("medium");
    await logout();
    // useAuth's guard takes over and lands the user on /login.
  };

  /**
   * Irreversible: the server removes every row the user owns, then we clear
   * the local credential. logout()'s own request 401s (its session row is
   * already gone) and is swallowed â€” clearing local state is what matters.
   *
   * The request is bounded: a stalled DELETE (flaky network, a backend busy
   * cascading the rows) must not leave the confirm sheet spinning forever with
   * both of its buttons disabled and no way out.
   */
  const confirmDelete = async () => {
    if (deleteBusy) return;
    setDeleteBusy(true);
    setDeleteError(null);
    try {
      await withTimeout(api.deleteAccount(), 20_000);
      notifyHaptic("success");
      await logout();
    } catch (err) {
      notifyHaptic("error");
      setDeleteError(t(serverErrorKey(err) as never));
      setDeleteBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const confirmReset = () => {
    haptic("medium");
    try {
      // Keep auth + device identity: the device id (so the same guest account is
      // waiting when the user comes back), the session token (so the user stays
      // signed in), and the signed-out flag (so this doesn't silently sign them in).
      const keep = new Set([
        "verdikick.deviceId",
        "verdikick.loggedOut",
        "matchday.deviceId",
        "matchday.loggedOut",
        "matchday.session",
      ]);
      Object.keys(localStorage)
        .filter((key) => (key.startsWith("verdikick.") || key.startsWith("matchday.")) && !keep.has(key))
        .forEach((key) => localStorage.removeItem(key));
    } catch {
      /* private mode */
    }
    setResetOpen(false);
    push({ text: t("profile.resetDone"), tone: "danger", icon: "âŸ²" });
  };

  const openPicker = () => {
    haptic("light");
    navigate("/onboarding");
  };

  const openNameEditor = () => {
    haptic("light");
    setNameDraft(displayName);
    setNameError(null);
    setNameOpen(true);
  };

  const saveName = async () => {
    if (nameBusy) return;
    const next = nameDraft.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!next) {
      notifyHaptic("error");
      setNameError(t("profile.nameEmpty"));
      return;
    }
    if (next === displayName) {
      setNameOpen(false);
      return;
    }
    setNameBusy(true);
    try {
      await updateDisplayName(next);
      haptic("medium");
      push({ text: t("toast.nameSaved"), tone: "volt", icon: "âœ“" });
      setNameOpen(false);
    } catch (err) {
      notifyHaptic("error");
      setNameError(t(serverErrorKey(err) as never));
    } finally {
      setNameBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <TopBar title={t("profile.title")} />

      <Card className="mt-md flex items-center gap-3 p-4">
        <Avatar name={displayName} tone="volt" size="lg" />
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <p className="truncate text-md font-bold text-ink">{displayName}</p>
          <p className="truncate text-xs text-ink-2">
            {viaTelegram ? (
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                {/* The small always-on dot: this account is linked to Telegram. */}
                <span aria-hidden="true" className="h-1.5 w-1.5 flex-none rounded-full bg-[var(--success,#22c55e)]" />
                {t("profile.connectedTg")}
              </span>
            ) : viaUsername && user?.username ? (
              `@${user.username}`
            ) : isDev ? (
              t("profile.browser")
            ) : null}
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={openNameEditor}
          aria-label={t("profile.editName")}
          title={t("profile.editName")}
          className="group relative grid h-11 w-11 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full text-ink-2 transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:text-volt"
        >
          {/* 36px visual ring on an 44px hit area: the border stays a hairline,
              the target meets the touch floor. */}
          <span
            aria-hidden="true"
            className="absolute inset-[3px] rounded-full border border-line transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] group-hover:border-volt"
          />
          <PencilIcon />
        </button>
      </Card>

      <ClubSlots openPicker={openPicker} />

      {/* One grouped list â€” every row that has a real destination. Rows are
          inside a single rounded container separated by hairlines. */}
      <section className="mt-xl">
        <SectionHeading title={t("profile.settings")} />
        <div className="divide-y divide-line overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-surface">
          <GroupRow
            label={t("profile.notifications")}
            hint={
              serverPrefs.goals ||
              (RATINGS_ENABLED && serverPrefs.ratings) ||
              (PREDICTIONS_ENABLED && serverPrefs.deadline)
                ? t("profile.notificationsOn")
                : t("profile.notificationsOff")
            }
            onClick={() => {
              haptic("light");
              setNotificationsOpen(true);
            }}
          />
          <GroupRow
            label={t("profile.myLeagues")}
            onClick={() => {
              haptic("light");
              setLeaguesOpen(true);
            }}
          />
          <GroupRow right={<LanguageToggle />} label={t("profile.language")} />
          <GroupRow
            label={t("profile.haptics")}
            hint={t("profile.hapticsHint")}
            right={<Switch checked={hapticsOn} label={t("profile.haptics")} onChange={setHapticsOn} />}
          />
          <GroupRow
            label={t("profile.resetLocal")}
            hint={t("profile.resetHint")}
            onClick={() => {
              haptic("light");
              setResetOpen(true);
            }}
          />
          <GroupRow label={t("legal.privacyTitle")} onClick={() => navigate(LEGAL_ROUTES.privacy)} />
          <GroupRow label={t("legal.termsTitle")} onClick={() => navigate(LEGAL_ROUTES.terms)} />
        </div>
        {/* Version line: the About prose card is gone; one muted line under
            the group suffices and doesn't compete with the rows. FA drops the
            "v" prefix and renders the number in Persian-Indic digits. */}
        <p className="mt-sm px-1 text-xs text-ink-3">
          {t("profile.version")} {lang === "fa" ? toFaDigits(APP_VERSION) : `v${APP_VERSION}`}
        </p>
      </section>

      <InviteFriends />

      {user?.isAdmin ? (
        <section className="mt-xl">
