          <Button variant="ghost" className="w-full" onClick={() => navigate("/admin")}>
            {t("profile.admin")}
          </Button>
        </section>
      ) : null}

      <WhatsNew />

      <section className="mt-xl">
        <SectionHeading title={t("profile.dangerZone")} />
        {/* The one destructive row on the page: the label carries the live
            (coral) colour so the stakes are visible without reading the hint. */}
        <ListRow
          danger
          label={t("profile.deleteAccount")}
          hint={t("profile.deleteHint")}
          onClick={() => {
            haptic("medium");
            setDeleteError(null);
            setDeleteOpen(true);
          }}
          right={<ChevronRightIcon className="text-danger" />}
        />
      </section>

      <section className="mt-xl mb-2">
        <Button variant="ghost" className="w-full text-danger" onClick={signOut}>
          {t("profile.signOut")}
        </Button>
      </section>

      <LeaguePickerSheet open={leaguesOpen} onClose={() => setLeaguesOpen(false)} />

      <BottomSheet
        open={notificationsOpen}
        onClose={() => setNotificationsOpen(false)}
        title={t("profile.notifications")}
        subtitle={t("profile.notificationsSub")}
        footer={
          <Button className="w-full" onClick={() => setNotificationsOpen(false)}>
            {t("common.done")}
          </Button>
        }
      >
        <div className="space-y-sm">
          <ListRow
            label={t("profile.notifGoals")}
            hint={t("profile.notifGoalsHint")}
            onToggle={() => setPref("goals")(!serverPrefs.goals)}
            right={
              <Switch
                checked={serverPrefs.goals}
                label={t("profile.notifGoals")}
                onChange={setPref("goals")}
              />
            }
          />
          {PREDICTIONS_ENABLED ? (
            <ListRow
              label={t("profile.notifDeadline")}
              hint={t("profile.notifDeadlineHint")}
              onToggle={() => setPref("deadline")(!serverPrefs.deadline)}
              right={
                <Switch
                  checked={serverPrefs.deadline}
                  label={t("profile.notifDeadline")}
                  onChange={setPref("deadline")}
                />
              }
            />
          ) : null}
          {/* The rating-window push is gated off, so its switch is too â€”
              leaving it would promise a notification nothing can send. */}
          {RATINGS_ENABLED ? (
            <ListRow
              label={t("profile.notifRatings")}
              hint={t("profile.notifRatingsHint")}
              onToggle={() => setPref("ratings")(!serverPrefs.ratings)}
              right={
                <Switch
                  checked={serverPrefs.ratings}
                  label={t("profile.notifRatings")}
                  onChange={setPref("ratings")}
                />
              }
            />
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </BottomSheet>

      <BottomSheet
        open={nameOpen}
        onClose={() => setNameOpen(false)}
        title={t("profile.editName")}
        subtitle={t("auth.displayNamePlaceholder")}
        footer={
          <SheetActions>
            <Button variant="ghost" className="flex-1" onClick={() => setNameOpen(false)}>
              {t("common.cancel")}
            </Button>
            <Button className="flex-1" onClick={() => void saveName()} disabled={nameBusy}>
              {nameBusy ? t("common.loading") : t("common.save")}
            </Button>
          </SheetActions>
        }
      >
        <label className="block space-y-1">
          <span className="label text-ink-2">{t("auth.displayName")}</span>
          <input
            className="min-h-12 w-full rounded-card border border-line bg-surface px-3.5 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-3 focus:border-volt focus:outline-none focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--volt)_16%,transparent)]"
            value={nameDraft}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setNameDraft(e.target.value);
              setNameError(null);
            }}
            maxLength={40}
            autoComplete="nickname"
            dir="auto"
            enterKeyHint="done"
            onKeyDown={(e) => {
              if (e.key === "Enter") void saveName();
            }}
          />
        </label>
        {nameError ? (
          <p
            className="mt-2 rounded-card border border-danger px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-danger"
            role="alert"
          >
            {nameError}
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </BottomSheet>

      <BottomSheet
        open={deleteOpen}
        onClose={() => {
          if (!deleteBusy) setDeleteOpen(false);
        }}
        title={t("profile.deleteConfirmTitle")}
        subtitle={t("profile.deleteHint")}
        footer={
          <SheetActions>
            <Button variant="ghost" className="flex-1" onClick={() => setDeleteOpen(false)} disabled={deleteBusy}>
              {t("common.cancel")}
            </Button>
            <Button
              className="flex-1 border border-danger text-danger"
              variant="ghost"
              onClick={() => void confirmDelete()}
              disabled={deleteBusy}
            >
              {deleteBusy ? t("profile.deleting") : t("profile.deleteConfirm")}
            </Button>
          </SheetActions>
        }
      >
        <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-ink-2">{t("profile.deleteConfirmBody")}</p>
        {deleteError ? (
          <p
            className="mt-md rounded-card border border-danger px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-danger"
            role="alert"
          >
            {deleteError}
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </BottomSheet>

      <BottomSheet
        open={resetOpen}
        onClose={() => setResetOpen(false)}
        title={t("profile.resetLocal")}
        subtitle={t("profile.resetHint")}
        footer={
          <SheetActions>
            <Button variant="ghost" className="flex-1" onClick={() => setResetOpen(false)}>
              {t("common.cancel")}
            </Button>
            <Button
              className="flex-1 border border-danger text-danger"
              variant="ghost"
              onClick={confirmReset}
            >
              {t("profile.resetConfirm")}
            </Button>
          </SheetActions>
        }
      >
        <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-ink-2">{t("profile.resetHint")}</p>
      </BottomSheet>
    </>
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- settings */

/** A plain row inside the grouped settings container (no own card border). */
function GroupRow({
  label,
  hint,
  onClick,
  right,
}: {
  label: string;
  hint?: string;
  onClick?: () => void;
  right?: React.ReactNode;})  {
  return (
    <div
      {...(onClick
        ? {
            onClick,
            role: "button" as const,
            tabIndex: 0,
            onKeyDown: (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
              if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
                e.preventDefault();
                onClick();
              }
            },
          }
        : {})}
      className="flex min-h-12 cursor-pointer items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 select-none active:bg-elevated"
    >
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <span className="block truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">{label}</span>
        {hint ? <span className="block text-xs text-ink-3">{hint}</span> : null}
      </div>
      {right ?? <ChevronRightIcon className="text-ink-3" />}
    </div>
  );
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- clubs */

/**
 * The five club slots, matching onboarding's 1 main club + 4 picks rule. The
 * main club on top with a star outline; the rest follow. Tapping any slot
 * (filled or not) opens the onboarding club picker. Empty: slots only â€” they
 * are their own prompt.
 */
function ClubSlots({ openPicker }: { openPicker: () => void }) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const { teams, anchorId, loading } = useFavorites();
  // The anchored favorite is the main club (falls back to the first pick);
  // the rest fill the small slots in follow order.
  const main = teams.find((team) => team.id === anchorId) ?? teams[0] ?? null;
  const rest = teams.filter((club) => club.id !== main?.id);

  const open = openPicker;

  return (
    <section className="mt-xl">
      <SectionHeading title={t("profile.myClubs")} />
      {loading ? (
        <PanelSkeleton height={78} />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2.5 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-4">
          <Slot club={main} star onClick={open} localize={localize} />
          {Array.from({ length: Math.max(4, teams.length - 1) }).map((_, i) => (
            <Slot
              key={i}
              club={rest[i] ?? null}
              onClick={open}
              localize={localize}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function Slot({
  club,
  star,
  onClick,
  localize,
}: {
  club: ReturnType<typeof useFavorites>["teams"][number] | null;
  star?: boolean;
  onClick: () => void;
  localize: ReturnType<typeof useI18n>["localize"];
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      aria-label={club ? localize(club.shortName) : undefined}
      className="group relative grid h-16 w-16 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full border border-dashed border-line bg-elevated text-ink-3 transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:border-volt hover:text-volt"
    >
      {star ? (
        <span aria-hidden="true" className="absolute -top-0.5 right-1.5 text-xs text-ink-3">
          â˜…
        </span>
      ) : null}
      {club ? (
        <span className="grid place-items-center">
          <TeamBadge team={club} size="sm" />
        </span>
      ) : (
        <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-lg font-bold">
          +
        </span>
      )}
    </button>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ invite */

/**
 * Invite friends as one card: copy the link, then redeem someone else's code
 * underneath it. Kept above the tab bar's breathing room.
 *
 * Server failure cases arrive as machine-readable codes on the thrown error
 * (POST /api/me/referral/redeem), and `serverErrorKey` maps each one:
 *
 *   INVALID_CODE     -> 404, code unknown (or empty) -> referral.errInvalid
 *   SELF_REFERRAL    -> 400, redeeming your own code  -> referral.errSelf
 *   ALREADY_REDEEMED -> 400, account redeemed once already (UNIQUE race
 *                       included)                      -> referral.errAlready
 *
 * The toast for those lives centrally in useReferral's onError, so this form
 * only submits and clears its draft on success.
 */
function InviteFriends() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const { push } = useToast();
  const { referral, loading, redeem: redeemMutation, redeeming } = useReferral();
  const [codeDraft, setCodeDraft] = useState("");

  // The hook hands back react-query's mutation object; unwrap it so the
  // submit path is a plain awaited call that resolves with the server body.
  const redeem = async (code: string) => redeemMutation.mutateAsync(code);

  const copyInvite = async () => {
    haptic("light");
    const code = referral?.code;
    if (!code) return;
    const url = `${window.location.origin}?startapp=${code}`;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
      push({ text: t("referral.copied"), tone: "volt", icon: "âœ“" });
    } catch {
      /* clipboard unavailable (private mode) */
    }
  };

  const submit = async () => {
    const code = codeDraft.trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!code || redeeming) return;
    try {
      const res = await redeem(code);
      if (res.ok) setCodeDraft("");
    } catch {
      /* failure already toasted by useReferral (see the code map above) */
    }
  };

  return (
    <Card className="mt-xl p-3">
      <p className="text-sm font-bold text-ink">{t("referral.title")}</p>
      <Button className="mt-sm w-full" onClick={() => void copyInvite()} disabled={loading || !referral}>
        {t("referral.copyInvite")}
      </Button>
      {/* Redeem row: codes are Latin, so the field is pinned LTR, uppercased
          and capped at the server's widest code length. */}
      <form
        className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2"
        onSubmit={(event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          void submit();
        }}
      >
        <input
          className="min-h-11 w-full min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface px-3.5 text-sm uppercase text-ink placeholder:normal-case placeholder:text-ink-3 focus:border-volt focus:outline-none focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--volt)_16%,transparent)]"
          value={codeDraft}
          onChange={(event) => setCodeDraft(event.target.value.toUpperCase().slice(0, 12))}
          maxLength={12}
          dir="ltr"
          autoComplete="off"
          autoCapitalize="characters"
          autoCorrect="off"
          spellCheck={false}
          placeholder={t("referral.redeemPlaceholder")}
          aria-label={t("referral.redeemPlaceholder")}
        />
        <Button type="submit" disabled={redeeming || loading || !referral || !codeDraft.trim()}>
          {redeeming ? t("common.loading") : t("referral.redeem")}
        </Button>
      </form>
    </Card>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ what's new */

/**
 * User-facing release notes: a dismissible card at the bottom of Profile,
 * shown when the latest release hasn't been dismissed yet. Copy is rewritten
 * from CHANGELOG.md for players (see src/lib/appInfo.ts), never pasted.
 */
function WhatsNew() {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n();
  const [seenRelease, setSeenRelease] = useLocalState<string>("verdikick.whatsNewSeen", "");
  const latest = WHATS_NEW[0];
  if (latest.version === seenRelease) return null;

  return (
    <section className="mt-xl">
      <SectionHeading title={t("whatsNew.title")} />
      <Card className="relative p-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <Chip tone="volt">
            v{latest.version} Â· {latest.date}
          </Chip>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              haptic("light");
              setSeenRelease(latest.version);
            }}
            className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-3 text-xs font-bold text-ink-3 transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:text-ink-2"
          >
            <CloseIcon className="h-3 w-3" />
            {t("whatsNew.dismiss")}
          </button>
        </div>
        <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1.5">
          {(lang === "fa" ? latest.linesFa : latest.lines).map((line, i) => (
            <li key={i} className="flex gap-2 text-xs text-ink-2">
              <span className="flex-none text-volt" aria-hidden="true">
                â€¢
              </span>
              <span>{line}</span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </Card>
    </section>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\pages\Ratings.tsx =====

import { useState } from "react";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { RateSheet } from "../components/panels/RateSheet";
import { RatingRow } from "../components/RatingRow";
import { RowSkeleton } from "../components/Skeletons";
import { TopBar } from "../components/TopBar";
import { Card, Chip, EmptyState, ErrorState, SectionHeading, Segmented } from "../components/ui";
import { api, serverErrorKey } from "../lib/api";
import { TEAM_FALLBACK } from "../lib/colors";
import { useMatches } from "../hooks/useApi";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import type { PlayerRating } from "../types";
import { haptic, notifyHaptic } from "../lib/telegram";
import { useToast } from "../components/toastContext";
import type { StringKey } from "../i18n/strings";
import { useSearchParams, Link } from "react-router-dom";

/**
 * The eye test across your matches: latest finished (or live) match first,
 * real crowd ratings beside the stat score. Rating writes go to the server.
 */
export default function Ratings() {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const matchesQuery = useMatches();
  const matches = matchesQuery.data ?? [];

  // The match whose rating sheet is open (deep-linkable via ?match=).
  const requested = params.get("match");
  const openMatchId = requested ?? (matches.find((m) => m.status === "finished") ?? matches[0])?.id ?? "";
  const openMatch = matches.find((m) => m.id === openMatchId);

  const setMatchParam = (id: string) =>
    setParams(
      (prev) => {
        prev.set("match", id);
        prev.delete("rate");
        return prev;
      },
      { replace: true },
    );

  const finished = matches.filter((m) => m.status !== "scheduled").slice(0, 8);

  return (
    <>
      <TopBar title={t("ratings.title")} subtitle={t("ratings.subtitle")} />

      {finished.length > 1 ? (
        <div className="mt-3">
          <Segmented
            value={openMatchId}
            onChange={setMatchParam}
            options={finished.map((m) => ({
              value: m.id,
              label: `${localize(m.home.shortName)}â€“${localize(m.away.shortName)}`,
            }))}
          />
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {matchesQuery.isLoading ? (
        <div className="mt-3">
          <RowSkeleton rows={6} />
        </div>
      ) : !openMatch ? (
        <div className="mt-3">
          <EmptyState
            title={t("ratings.emptyTitle")}
            hint={t("ratings.emptyHint")}
            action={
              <Link
                to="/matches"
                className="min-h-11 rounded-full border border-volt px-5 text-xs font-bold leading-[2.75rem] text-volt transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated"
              >
                {t("ratings.emptyAction")}
              </Link>
            }
          />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <MatchRatings matchId={openMatch.id} match={openMatch} league={openMatch.home.league} />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

function MatchRatings({
  matchId,
  match,
  league,
}: {
  matchId: string;
  match: { status: string; home: { shortName: string }; away: { shortName: string } } | undefined;
  /** The match's competition (LeagueId or cup slug) for the row's team chip. */
  league: string;
}) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const { push } = useToast();
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["ratings", matchId],
    queryFn: () => api.getRatings(matchId),
    refetchInterval: match?.status === "live" ? 30_000 : false,
  });
  const players = (query.data?.players ?? []).map((p) => adapt(p, league));
  // Real histograms straight from crowd_ratings (server GROUP BY); the zero
  // array is only a brand-new player's honest "no votes yet".
  const distributions = query.data?.distributions ?? {};
  const cardsUsed = query.data?.cardsUsed ?? 0;
  const cardsMax = query.data?.cardsMax ?? 3;
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<"crowd" | "points" | "divergence">("crowd");

  const sheetFor = players.find((row) => row.playerId === params.get("rate")) ?? null;
  const setSheetFor = (player: PlayerRating | null) =>
    setParams(
      (prev) => {
        if (player) prev.set("rate", player.playerId);
        else prev.delete("rate");
        return prev;
      },
      { replace: true },
    );

  type RatingsData = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof api.getRatings>>;

  const rate = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (input: { playerId: string; value: number; comment?: string }) =>
      api.ratePlayer(matchId, input.playerId, input.value, input.comment),
    // Optimistic: the row shows the user's score the moment they save â€” no
    // round-trip wait. Rollback restores the server list on failure.
    onMutate: async (input) => {
      await queryClient.cancelQueries({ queryKey: ["ratings", matchId] });
      const prev = queryClient.getQueryData<RatingsData>(["ratings", matchId]);
      if (prev) {
        const target = prev.players.find((p) => p.player_id === input.playerId);
        const wasNew = target?.my_rating == null;
        queryClient.setQueryData<RatingsData>(["ratings", matchId], {
          ...prev,
          cardsUsed: wasNew ? Math.min(prev.cardsMax, prev.cardsUsed + 1) : prev.cardsUsed,
          players: prev.players.map((p) =>
            p.player_id === input.playerId
              ? {
                  ...p,
                  my_rating: input.value,
                  my_comment: input.comment ?? p.my_comment ?? null,
                  votes: wasNew ? p.votes + 1 : p.votes,
                }
              : p,
          ),
        });
      }
      return { prev };
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      // Server truth (crowd average, real vote counts) replaces the optimistic row.
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["ratings", matchId] });
    },
    onError: (err, _input, ctx) => {
      if (ctx?.prev) queryClient.setQueryData(["ratings", matchId], ctx.prev);
      // e.g. "player did not feature" or the 3-card cap â€” localized, not raw.
      notifyHaptic("error");
      push({ text: t(serverErrorKey(err) as StringKey), tone: "danger", icon: "âœ•" });
    },
  });

  // The whole point of the feature: players the crowd rates highly but who
  // returned little on the stat sheet, side by side rather than merged.
  const divergence = (rating: PlayerRating) => (rating.crowdRating ?? 0) - rating.fantasyPoints / 2;

  const rows = [...players].sort((a, b) => {
    if (sort === "points") return b.fantasyPoints - a.fantasyPoints;
    if (sort === "divergence") return divergence(b) - divergence(a);
    return (b.crowdRating ?? 0) - (a.crowdRating ?? 0);
  });

  if (query.isLoading) return <RowSkeleton rows={6} />;
  if (query.isError) return <ErrorState onRetry={() => query.refetch()} />;
  if (players.length === 0) return <EmptyState title={t("ratings.noneYet")} hint={t("ratings.noneHint")} />;

  return (
    <>
      <Card className="mt-3 p-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <p className="truncate text-xs text-ink-2">
              {localize(match?.home.shortName ?? "")} {t("common.versus")}{" "}
              {localize(match?.away.shortName ?? "")}
            </p>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-ink-3">
              {t("ratings.cards", { used: cardsUsed, max: cardsMax })} Â· {t("ratings.pickThree")}
            </p>
          </div>
          <Chip tone="gold">{t("ratings.eyeTest")}</Chip>
        </div>
      </Card>

      <div className="mt-4">
        <Segmented
          value={sort}
          onChange={setSort}
          options={[
            { value: "crowd", label: t("ratings.sortCrowd") },
            { value: "points", label: t("ratings.sortPoints") },
            { value: "divergence", label: t("ratings.sortGap") },
          ]}
        />
      </div>

      <section className="mt-4">
        <SectionHeading
          title={t("match.tabRatings")}
          action={<span className="text-xs text-ink-3">{t("ratings.tapToRate")}</span>}
        />
        <ul className="mt-2 space-y-2">
          {rows.map((rating, index) => (
            <RatingRow
              key={rating.playerId}
              index={index}
              rating={rating}
              team={rating._team}
              onTap={(player) => {
                // 3-card cap: block a brand-new card when full, edits stay open.
                if (cardsUsed >= cardsMax && player.myRating === null) return;
                setSheetFor(player);
              }}
            />
          ))}
        </ul>
      </section>

      {sheetFor ? (
        <RateSheet
          rating={sheetFor}
          open
          onClose={() => setSheetFor(null)}
          distribution={distributions[sheetFor.playerId] ?? ZERO_DISTRIBUTION}
          cardsUsed={cardsUsed}
          cardsMax={cardsMax}
          onSave={(playerId, value, comment) => {
            haptic("medium");
            rate.mutate({ playerId, value, comment });
          }}
        />
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}

type Team = import("../types").Team;

function adapt(p: import("../lib/api").ApiPlayerRating, league: string): PlayerRating & { _team: Team } {
  return {
    playerId: p.player_id,
    name: p.name,
    teamId: p.team_id,
    position: (p.position ?? "MID") as PlayerRating["position"],
    fantasyPoints: p.stat_score ?? 0,
    crowdRating: p.crowd_rating,
    votes: p.votes ?? 0,
    myRating: p.my_rating ?? null,
    myComment: p.my_comment ?? null,
    latestComment: p.latest_comment ?? null,
    latestCommentId: p.latest_comment_id ?? null,
    _team: {
      id: p.team_id,
      name: p.team_name ?? "",
      shortName: p.team_short ?? p.team_name ?? "",
      abbreviation: (p.team_short ?? "").slice(0, 3) || "â€”",
      color: p.team_color ?? TEAM_FALLBACK,
      // The match's own competition, not a hardcoded default: a cup match's
      // rating rows no longer masquerade as Premier League sides.
      league: (league || "PL") as Team["league"],
    },
  };
}

/** Shared "no votes yet" histogram â€” a stable empty reference, not a render allocation. */
const ZERO_DISTRIBUTION: number[] = Array.from({ length: 10 }, () => 0);

===== D:\football\src\pages\Table.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type CSSProperties } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { TopBar } from "../components/TopBar";
import { RowSkeleton } from "../components/Skeletons";
import {
  EmptyState,
  ErrorState,
  LiveDot,
  Num,
  SectionHeading,
  Segmented,
  TeamBadge,
  CountUp,
} from "../components/ui";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";

import { StarFilledIcon } from "../components/icons";
import {
  slugForLeague,
  useKnockoutCompetitions,
  useLeagueTabs,
  useStandings,
  useMatches,
  type StandingRow,
} from "../hooks/useApi";
import { isCup } from "../lib/leagues";
import { useFavorites } from "../hooks/useFavorites";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { haptic } from "../lib/telegram";
import type { Match } from "../types";

/**
 * League tables for the synced domestic leagues â€” straight from the standings.
 * Followed clubs are highlighted INSIDE the table (star + row tint); there is
 * no separate "your clubs" rail above it, and with no picks the screen simply
 * shows the league the default tab lands on.
 */

/**
 * Zone rules per big-five league, position ranges â†’ zone. The standings feed
 * carries no promotion/relegation metadata, so these are this app's own map â€”
 * change the ranges here, not the table. Cups and group tables get no bars.
 */
const ZONES: Record<string, Record<Zone, [number, number]>> = {
  "eng.1": { ucl: [1, 4], uel: [5, 5], relegation: [18, 20] },
  "esp.1": { ucl: [1, 4], uel: [5, 6], relegation: [18, 20] },
  "ita.1": { ucl: [1, 4], uel: [5, 6], relegation: [18, 20] },
  "ger.1": { ucl: [1, 4], uel: [5, 5], relegation: [17, 18] },
  "fra.1": { ucl: [1, 3], uel: [4, 4], relegation: [17, 18] },
};

type Zone = "ucl" | "uel" | "relegation";

const ZONE_COLOR: Record<Zone, string> = {
  ucl: "#3b82f6", // Champions League blue
  uel: "#f97316", // Europa League orange
  relegation: "var(--danger)", // relegation red
};

function zoneOf(leagueSlug: string, rank: number | null): Zone | null {
  const rules = ZONES[leagueSlug];
  if (!rules || rank == null) return null;
  for (const zone of Object.keys(rules) as Zone[]) {
    const [from, to] = rules[zone];
    if (rank >= from && rank <= to) return zone;
  }
  return null;
}

/** The big-five alias a tab value maps to, for the zone lookup. */
const ALIAS_SLUG: Record<string, string> = {
  PL: "eng.1",
  LL: "esp.1",
  SA: "ita.1",
  BL: "ger.1",
  L1: "fra.1",
};

/** Small coloured dot + label for the legend line under the table. */
function LegendDot({ color, label }: { color: string; label: string }) {
  return (
    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
      <span
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="h-1.5 w-1.5 flex-none rounded-full"
        style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
      />
      {label}
    </span>
  );
}

/**
 * Horizontally scrollable league tabs with a fade on the edge where more tabs
 * hide. The fade is a sticky gradient at the visual INLINE-END, which flips
 * with direction â€” correct side for both RTL and LTR (the same trick the
 * Segmented control uses).
 */
function LeagueTabRow({
  tabs,
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  tabs: Array<{ value: string; label: string }>;
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
}) {
  const { dir } = useI18n();
  const trackRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const [overflows, setOverflows] = useState(true);
  useEffect(() => {
    const track = trackRef.current;
    if (!track) return;
    const check = () => setOverflows(track.scrollWidth > track.clientWidth + 2);
    check();
    const observer = typeof ResizeObserver !== "undefined" ? new ResizeObserver(check) : null;
    observer?.observe(track);
    return () => observer?.disconnect();
  }, [tabs.length]);

  return (
    <div ref={trackRef} role="tablist" className="relative flex gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto py-1">
      {overflows ? (
        <span
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="pointer-events-none sticky end-0 z-10 shrink-0"
          style={{
            width: 20,
            marginInlineEnd: -8,
            background:
              dir === "rtl"
                ? "linear-gradient(to left, transparent, var(--bg-base))"
                : "linear-gradient(to right, transparent, var(--bg-base))",
          }}
        />
      ) : null}
      {tabs.map((tab) => (
        <button
          key={tab.value}
          type="button"
          role="tab"
          aria-selected={value === tab.value}
          onClick={() => {
            if (value !== tab.value) haptic("light");
            onChange(tab.value);
          }}
          className={cx(
            "flex h-9 flex-none items-center rounded-full border px-3.5 text-xs font-bold transition-[color,border-color,background-color] duration-[var(--t-fast)]",
            value === tab.value
              ? "border-transparent bg-volt text-black"
              : "border-line bg-surface text-ink-2 hover:bg-elevated hover:text-ink",
          )}
        >
          {tab.label}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * Sentinel value for the Cups tab appended to the league strip. It is never
 * stored in `league` state â€” only routed on.
 */
const CUPS_TAB = "__cups";

export default function Table() {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { teams: clubs, isFavorite } = useFavorites();
  const tabs = useLeagueTabs();
  const [league, setLeague] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // The Cups page lost its Browse entry point, so the league strip carries a
  // Cups tab at the very end â€” a navigation shortcut, never a league state:
  // it only exists while the server actually has knockout competitions, and
  // tapping it routes to /cups without ever calling setLeague("cups").
  const knockout = useKnockoutCompetitions();
  const hasCups = (knockout.data?.length ?? 0) > 0;
  const tabList = useMemo(
    () => (hasCups ? [...tabs, { value: CUPS_TAB, label: t("table.cupsTab") }] : tabs),
    [hasCups, tabs, t],
  );
  const onTabChange = (value: string) => {
    if (value === CUPS_TAB) {
      navigate("/cups");
      return;
    }
    setLeague(value);
  };
  // Actual / Expected view: renders ONLY when a league has xPts coverage â€”
  // a switch with a single choice is chrome, not a control (the old
  // single-option "Ø¬Ø¯ÙˆÙ„" pill is gone).
  const [view, setView] = useState<"actual" | "expected">("actual");

  // The sticky column header's offset is the live topbar's height, measured
  // once so the pinned header slides in just under the header band (the band
  // height varies with the title/subtitle it carries).
  const [stickyTop, setStickyTop] = useState(56);
  useEffect(() => {
    const header = document.querySelector("header");
    if (header instanceof HTMLElement) setStickyTop(header.offsetHeight + 1);
  }, []);

  // Default to the league one of your clubs plays in, else the Premier League.
  // `clubs[].league` is a big-five alias (teamFromApi parks every other side
  // in "PL"), so the default always lands on a tab that exists.
  const active = league ?? clubs[0]?.league ?? "PL";
  const slug = slugForLeague(active);
  // Tab labels carry the translation: aliases come pre-translated, followed
  // extras read their catalog name â€” `league.<slug>` i18n keys don't exist.
  const tabLabel = (value: string) => tabs.find((tab) => tab.value === value)?.label ?? value;
  const standingsQuery = useStandings(slug);
  const rows = standingsQuery.data ?? [];

  // Expected points ride along on the standings payload (one left join
  // server-side, no extra request), and only the clubs Understat covers have
  // them â€” so "no data" is a normal state here, never an error.
  const hasExpected = rows.some((row) => row.xpts != null);
  // Guard the view itself too: if it somehow renders without data, fall back
  // to the real table instead of a grid of dashes.
  const showExpected = view === "expected" && hasExpected;
  // Ranked by xPts, with the uncovered clubs parked below in table order
  // (Array#sort is stable) rather than dropped or given a fake rank.
  const expectedRows = useMemo(() => [...rows].sort((a, b) => (b.xpts ?? -1) - (a.xpts ?? -1)), [rows]);

  // League heading, far end: "Ù‡ÙØªÙ‡ Û¸" â€” locale digits throughout.
  const weekFormatter = useMemo(
    () => new Intl.NumberFormat(lang === "fa" ? "fa-IR" : "en", { useGrouping: false }),
    [lang],
  );
  const gwLabel = standingsQuery.data?.length
    ? t("table.gw", { gw: weekFormatter.format(standingsQuery.data[0]?.played ?? 0) })
    : null;
  const zoneSlug = ALIAS_SLUG[active] ?? active;

  return (
    <>
      <TopBar title={t("table.title")} />

      {/* League tabs â€” scrollable when crowded, faded at the hidden edge. */}
      <div className="mt-md">
        <LeagueTabRow tabs={tabList} value={active} onChange={onTabChange} />
      </div>

      <section className="mt-lg">
        {isCup(slug) ? (
          <CupView slug={slug} rows={rows} stickyTop={stickyTop} />
        ) : (
          <>
            <SectionHeading
              title={tabLabel(active)}
              action={
                gwLabel ? (
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-3">
                    <Num>{gwLabel}</Num>
                  </span>
                ) : null
              }
            />
            {standingsQuery.isLoading ? (
              <RowSkeleton rows={8} />
            ) : standingsQuery.isError ? (
              <ErrorState onRetry={() => standingsQuery.refetch()} />
            ) : rows.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState title={t("table.emptyTitle")} hint={t("table.emptyHint")} />
            ) : (
              <>
                {hasExpected ? (
                  <div className="mb-2">
                    <Segmented
                      value={view}
                      onChange={setView}
                      options={[
                        { value: "actual", label: t("table.viewActual") },
                        { value: "expected", label: t("table.viewExpected") },
                      ]}
                    />
                  </div>
                ) : null}

                {showExpected ? (
                  <>
                    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-surface">
                      <div
                        aria-hidden="true"
                        className="grid grid-cols-[1.5rem_1fr_1.75rem_2.25rem_2.25rem_2.25rem] gap-1.5 border-b border-line bg-elevated px-3 py-2 label text-ink-3"
                      >
                        <span>{t("table.rank")}</span>
                        <span>{t("table.team")}</span>
                        <span className="text-end">{t("table.played")}</span>
                        <span className="text-end">{t("table.xpts")}</span>
                        <span className="text-end">{t("table.pts")}</span>
                        <span className="text-end">{t("table.diff")}</span>
                      </div>
                      <ul className="divide-y divide-line">
                        {expectedRows.map((row, index) => (
                          <ExpectedItem
                            key={row.team.id}
                            row={row}
                            index={index}
                            rank={row.xpts == null ? null : index + 1}
                            mine={isFavorite(row.team.id)}
                          />
                        ))}
                      </ul>
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-ink-3">{t("table.expectedHint")}</p>
                  </>
                ) : null}

                {view === "actual" ? (
                  <>
                    {/* overflow-clip (not hidden) clips the card's corners while
                        staying a NON-scroll container, so the sticky header
                        inside still pins to the page scroll. */}
                    <div className="overflow-clip rounded-card border border-line bg-surface">
                      <StandingsHeader stickyTop={stickyTop} />
                      <ul className="divide-y divide-line">
                        {rows.map((row, index) => (
                          <StandingItem
                            key={row.team.id}
                            row={row}
                            index={index}
                            mine={isFavorite(row.team.id)}
                            zone={zoneOf(zoneSlug, row.rank ?? index + 1)}
                          />
                        ))}
                      </ul>
                    </div>
                    {/* Zone legend: one small line, same order as the bars. */}
                    {ZONES[zoneSlug] ? (
                      <p className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-xs text-ink-3">
                        <LegendDot color={ZONE_COLOR.ucl} label={t("league.UCL")} />
                        <LegendDot color={ZONE_COLOR.uel} label={t("league.UEL")} />
                        <LegendDot color={ZONE_COLOR.relegation} label={t("table.zoneReleg")} />
                      </p>
                    ) : null}
                  </>
                ) : null}
              </>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

/**
 * Six columns â€” Rank, Team, P, GD, Pts, Form â€” across a 390px phone. The old
 * eleven-column grid carried W/D/L and GF/GA too, needed `min-w-[640px]` and
 * pushed Pts and Form off-screen behind a sideways scroll; those extra figures
 * live on the club page's Stats tab. The width string must stay ONE complete
 * literal so Tailwind can see it: 1.5rem rank, flexible team, then 1.75/2/2.25/3rem.
 */
const COLS = "grid-cols-[1.5rem_minmax(0,1fr)_1.75rem_2rem_2.25rem_3rem]";

function StandingsHeader({ stickyTop }: { stickyTop: number }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  return (
    <div
      aria-hidden="true"
      className={cx(
        "grid",
        COLS,
        "sticky z-10 gap-2 border-b border-line bg-elevated px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-ink-3",
      )}
      style={{ top: stickyTop }}
    >
      <span>{t("table.rank")}</span>
      <span>{t("table.team")}</span>
      <span className="text-end">{t("table.played")}</span>
      <span className="text-end">{t("table.gd")}</span>
      <span className="text-end">{t("table.pts")}</span>
      <span className="text-end">{t("table.form")}</span>
    </div>
  );
}

function StandingItem({
  row,
  index,
  mine,
  zone,
}: {
  row: StandingRow;
  index: number;
  mine: boolean;
  zone: Zone | null;
}) {
  const { localize, lang } = useI18n();
  // Persian digits: every figure renders through locale formatting â€” fa-IR
  // gives the Persian-Indic glyphs (Û±, +Û±Û±, Ù« separator for decimals); EN
  // keeps Latin digits and the counting animation.
  const fmt = (v: number, decimals = 0) =>
    new Intl.NumberFormat(lang === "fa" ? "fa-IR" : "en", {
      minimumFractionDigits: decimals,
      maximumFractionDigits: decimals,
    }).format(v);
  const gd = (row.goalsFor ?? 0) - (row.goalsAgainst ?? 0);
  const rank = row.rank ?? index + 1;
  return (
    <li
      className={cx(
        "list-in relative grid",
        COLS,
        "items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 transition-[background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.99]",
        mine ? "bg-elevated" : "hover:bg-elevated/60",
      )}
      style={{ "--stagger": `${Math.min(index, 8) * 40}ms` } as CSSProperties}
    >
      {/* Zone bar: a 3px pill on the row's START edge (flips with direction),
          coloured by the zone's rule. */}
      {zone ? (
        <span
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="absolute inset-y-1.5 start-0 w-[3px] rounded-full"
          style={{ backgroundColor: ZONE_COLOR[zone] }}
        />
      ) : null}
      <Num className={cx("text-sm", mine ? "text-volt" : rank <= 3 ? "text-gold" : "text-ink-3")}>
        {fmt(rank)}
      </Num>
      {/* Stretched link: the <a> sits in the team column (so the grid stays one
          row), and its ::after covers the whole row â€” every tap, rank to
          points, opens the club page, so the row is one 44px+ touch target. */}
      <Link
        to={`/team/${row.team.id}`}
        className="flex min-h-11 min-w-0 items-center gap-2 after:absolute after:inset-0"
      >
        <TeamBadge team={row.team} size="sm" />
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">
          {localize(row.team.shortName)}
        </span>
        {mine ? <StarFilledIcon className="h-3 w-3 flex-none text-volt" /> : null}
      </Link>
      <Num className="text-end text-xs text-ink-3">
        {row.played == null ? "â€“" : fmt(row.played)}
      </Num>
      <Num className={cx("text-end text-xs", gd >= 0 ? "text-ink-2" : "text-ink-3")}>
        {gd > 0 ? `+${fmt(gd)}` : fmt(gd)}
      </Num>
      {row.points != null ? (
        lang === "fa" ? (
          <Num className="text-end text-sm font-extrabold text-ink">{fmt(row.points)}</Num>
        ) : (
          <CountUp value={row.points} className="text-end text-sm font-extrabold text-ink" />
        )
      ) : (
        <Num className="text-end text-sm font-extrabold text-ink">â€“</Num>
      )}
      {row.form ? <FormStrip form={row.form} /> : <span className="text-end text-xs text-ink-3">â€“</span>}
    </li>
  );
}

/**
 * The last five results, oldest first â€” the server folds them from the stored
 * finished matches (server/standings/form.ts), so the strip always matches the
 * fixtures the club page shows; a club with fewer than five finished matches
 * simply carries fewer dots. Dots rather than letters: green win, grey draw,
 * red loss. One image for screen readers, so the label reads as a sentence
 * instead of five stray letters; `dir="ltr"` keeps the order oldestâ†’newest in
 * Farsi too.
 */
function FormStrip({ form }: { form: string }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const results = [...form];
  const label = results
    .map((r) => (r === "W" ? t("table.formW") : r === "L" ? t("table.formL") : t("table.formD")))
    .join(", ");
  return (
    <span
      role="img"
      aria-label={`${t("table.form")}: ${label}`}
      dir="ltr"
      className="flex flex-none items-center justify-end gap-1"
    >
      {results.map((result, index) => (
        <span
          key={index}
          aria-hidden="true"
          className={cx(
            "h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full",
            result === "W" ? "bg-pitch" : result === "L" ? "bg-danger" : "bg-ink-3",
          )}
        />
      ))}
    </span>
  );
}

/**
 * One row of the expected table: what a club's chances were worth set against
 * what it actually banked. `rank` is null for a club Understat doesn't cover â€”
 * it is still listed, unranked and dimmed, because dropping it would quietly
 * make this look like a different league.
 */
function ExpectedItem({
  row,
  index,
  rank,
  mine,
}: {
  row: StandingRow;
  index: number;
  rank: number | null;
  mine: boolean;
}) {
  const { t, localize, lang } = useI18n();
  const fmt = (v: number, decimals = 0) =>
    new Intl.NumberFormat(lang === "fa" ? "fa-IR" : "en", {
      minimumFractionDigits: decimals,
      maximumFractionDigits: decimals,
    }).format(v);
  const expected = row.xpts;
  const actual = row.points;
  const delta = expected != null && actual != null ? actual - expected : null;
  return (
    <li
      className={cx(
        "list-in relative grid grid-cols-[1.5rem_1fr_1.75rem_2.25rem_2.25rem_2.25rem] items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 transition-[background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.99]",
        mine ? "bg-elevated" : "hover:bg-elevated/60",
        // Uncovered clubs read as incomplete WITHOUT dimming the text: the row
        // darkens toward --bg-base instead, so the ink-3 numerals keep their
        // AA-passing contrast (opacity-55 on the whole row measured â‰ˆ2.4:1).
        expected == null && !mine && "bg-base/50",
      )}
      style={{ "--stagger": `${Math.min(index, 8) * 40}ms` } as CSSProperties}
    >
      <Num className="text-xs text-ink-3">{rank == null ? "â€“" : fmt(rank)}</Num>
      <Link
        to={`/team/${row.team.id}`}
        className="flex min-h-11 min-w-0 items-center gap-2 after:absolute after:inset-0"
      >
        <TeamBadge team={row.team} size="sm" />
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">
          {localize(row.team.shortName)}
        </span>
        {mine ? <StarFilledIcon className="h-3 w-3 flex-none text-volt" /> : null}
      </Link>
      <Num className="text-end text-xs text-ink-3">
        {row.played == null ? "â€“" : fmt(row.played)}
      </Num>
      {/* The header grid is decorative (aria-hidden, as in the real table), so
          every figure names itself; the wrappers keep the cell count even. */}
      <span className="text-end">
        {expected != null ? (
          lang === "fa" ? (
            <Num className="text-xs font-bold text-gold">{fmt(expected, 1)}</Num>
          ) : (
            <CountUp value={expected} decimals={1} className="text-xs font-bold text-gold" />
          )
        ) : (
          <Num className="text-xs font-bold text-gold">â€“</Num>
        )}
        <span className="sr-only"> {t("table.xpts")}</span>
      </span>
      <span className="text-end">
        {actual != null ? (
          lang === "fa" ? (
            <Num className="text-xs text-ink">{fmt(actual)}</Num>
          ) : (
            <CountUp value={actual} className="text-xs text-ink" />
          )
        ) : (
          <Num className="text-xs text-ink">â€“</Num>
        )}
        <span className="sr-only"> {t("table.pts")}</span>
      </span>
      <span className="text-end">
        <Num
          className={cx(
            "text-xs font-bold",
            delta == null ? "text-ink-3" : delta >= 0 ? "text-volt" : "text-danger",
          )}
        >
          {delta == null ? "â€“" : `${delta >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${fmt(Math.abs(delta), 1)}`}
        </Num>
        <span className="sr-only"> {t("xg.expected")}</span>
      </span>
    </li>
  );
}

function CupView({
  slug,
  rows,
  stickyTop,
}: {
  slug: string;
  rows: StandingRow[];
  stickyTop: number;
}) {
  const { localize } = useI18n();
  const { isFavorite } = useFavorites();

  if (rows.length > 0) {
    const groups = new Map<string, StandingRow[]>();
    for (const row of rows) {
      const key = row.groupName ?? "";
      if (!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key, []);
      groups.get(key)!.push(row);
    }
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {Array.from(groups.entries()).map(([groupName, groupRows]) => (
          <section key={groupName}>
            <SectionHeading title={localize(groupName)} />
            {/* Group tables share the league's six columns; no zone bars. */}
            <div className="overflow-clip rounded-card border border-line bg-surface">
              <StandingsHeader stickyTop={stickyTop} />
              <ul className="divide-y divide-line">
                {groupRows.map((row, index) => (
                  <StandingItem
                    key={row.team.id}
                    row={row}
                    index={index}
                    mine={isFavorite(row.team.id)}
                    zone={null}
                  />
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          </section>
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return <KnockoutView slug={slug} />;
}

function KnockoutView({ slug }: { slug: string }) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const matchesQuery = useMatches({ league: slug });
  const matches = matchesQuery.data ?? [];
  const cupMatches = matches.filter((m) => m.round != null);

  if (matchesQuery.isLoading) {
    return <RowSkeleton rows={8} />;
  }
  if (matchesQuery.isError) {
    return <ErrorState onRetry={() => matchesQuery.refetch()} />;
  }
  if (cupMatches.length === 0) {
    return <EmptyState icon="ðŸ“ˆ" title={t("table.emptyTitle")} hint={t("table.emptyHint")} />;
  }

  const rounds = new Map<string, Match[]>();
  for (const match of cupMatches) {
    const key = match.round!;
    if (!rounds.has(key)) rounds.set(key, []);
    rounds.get(key)!.push(match);
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {Array.from(rounds.entries()).map(([round, roundMatches]) => (
        <section key={round}>
          <SectionHeading title={localize(round)} />
          <ul className="space-y-sm">
            {roundMatches.map((match) => (
              <li key={match.id}>
                <Link
                  to={`/match/${match.id}`}
                  className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface px-3 py-2.5 transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated"
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-end gap-2">
                    <TeamBadge team={match.home} size="sm" />
                    <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-right text-sm font-bold text-ink">
                      {localize(match.home.shortName)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex flex-none items-center gap-2">
                    {match.status === "scheduled" ? (
                      <span className="text-xs text-ink-3">vs</span>
                    ) : (
                      <Num className="text-sm font-bold text-ink">
                        {match.homeScore ?? 0} - {match.awayScore ?? 0}
                      </Num>
                    )}
                    {match.status === "live" || match.status === "halftime" ? <LiveDot /> : null}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">
                      {localize(match.away.shortName)}
                    </span>
                    <TeamBadge team={match.away} size="sm" />
                  </div>
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </section>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\pages\TeamPage.tsx =====

import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { TopBar } from "../components/TopBar";
import { PanelSkeleton } from "../components/Skeletons";
import {
  Card,
  Chip,
  EmptyState,
  ErrorState,
  Num,
  PlayerAvatar,
  SectionHeading,
  Segmented,
  TeamBadge,
} from "../components/ui";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";
import { matchFromApi, useSquad, useTeamDetail, useTeamUnderstat, type SquadRow } from "../hooks/useApi";
import type { StringKey } from "../i18n/strings";
import { usePrefetchMatch } from "../hooks/usePrefetch";
import { TEAM_FALLBACK } from "../lib/colors";
import { useLocalState } from "../lib/useLocalState";
import { ChevronDownIcon, ChevronRightIcon } from "../components/icons";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { formatKickoff, formatKickoffDate, toFaDigits } from "../lib/format";
import type { ApiMatch } from "../lib/api";
import type { Position } from "../types";
import { competitionName } from "../lib/leagues";

type Tab = "squad" | "fixtures" | "season" | "xg";

const POS_ORDER: Position[] = ["GK", "DEF", "MID", "FWD"];

/** English ordinal suffix: 1st, 2nd, 3rd, 4th â€¦ 11th/12th/13th â€¦ 21st. */
function ordinal(rank: number): string {
  const mod100 = rank % 100;
  const suffix = mod100 >= 11 && mod100 <= 13 ? "th" : (["th", "st", "nd", "rd"][rank % 10] ?? "th");
  return `${rank}${suffix}`;
}

/**
 * A club's page: full squad (from the weekly roster sync), season stat line and
 * recent fixtures. Reached by tapping any team in the table or a match card.
 */
export default function TeamPage() {
  const { id = "" } = useParams();  const { t, lang, localize } = useI18n();
  const detailQuery = useTeamDetail(id);
  const squadQuery = useSquad(id);

  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>("squad");

  // Error BEFORE the loading check: `data` is undefined when the request
  // fails, so a `isLoading || !data` branch placed first would swallow the error
  // and leave the skeleton on screen forever. `!detailQuery.data` on the error
  // branch is deliberate â€” a failed background refetch keeps the data already
  // on screen instead of replacing a good page with an error card.
  if (detailQuery.isError && !detailQuery.data) {
    // A banned-league club 404s LEAGUE_NOT_SUPPORTED: that is a permanent
    // state, not a retryable failure, so it gets the empty state rather than
    // the error/retry card.
    const code = (detailQuery.error as { code?: string } | null)?.code;
    return (
      <>
        <TopBar title={t("team.title")} back />
        <div className="mt-md">
          {code === "LEAGUE_NOT_SUPPORTED" ? (
            <EmptyState title={t("team.leagueUnsupported")} />
          ) : (
            <ErrorState onRetry={() => detailQuery.refetch()} />
          )}
        </div>
      </>
    );
  }

  if (detailQuery.isLoading || !detailQuery.data) {
    return (
      <>
        <TopBar title={t("team.title")} back />
        <PanelSkeleton height={160} />
      </>
    );
  }

  const { team, seasonStats, standing, fixtures } = detailQuery.data;
  // Persian reads every number in the Persian script; English keeps the ordinal.
  const num = (value: number | string) => (lang === "fa" ? toFaDigits(value) : String(value));
  const positionLabel = (rank: number) => (lang === "fa" ? `${toFaDigits(rank)}Ù…` : ordinal(rank));
  const leagueId =
    team.league && ["eng.1", "esp.1", "ita.1", "ger.1", "fra.1"].includes(team.league)
      ? (
          { "eng.1": "PL", "esp.1": "LL", "ita.1": "SA", "ger.1": "BL", "fra.1": "L1" } as Record<
            string,
            "PL" | "LL" | "SA" | "BL" | "L1"
          >
        )[team.league]
      : null;

  const teamView = {
    id: team.id,
    name: team.name,
    shortName: team.short_name ?? team.name,
    abbreviation: team.abbreviation ?? (team.short_name ?? team.name).slice(0, 3).toUpperCase(),
    color: team.color ?? TEAM_FALLBACK,
    league: leagueId ?? "PL",
  };

  const teamLeagueLabel = leagueId ? t(`league.${leagueId}`) : competitionName(team.league, lang);

  return (
    <>
      <TopBar
        title={localize(teamView.shortName)}
        subtitle={teamLeagueLabel}
        back
      />

      {/* Club header: the club's own colour washed across the card from the top
          corner, so a page opens on the side's identity rather than on generic
          dark chrome. It is a gradient, never a decorative side stripe, and the
          crest sits at the hero size â€” this is the one place the badge leads. */}
      <div
        className="relative mt-md overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line p-4"
        style={{
          background: `linear-gradient(135deg, color-mix(in srgb, ${teamView.color} 30%, transparent) 0%, color-mix(in srgb, ${teamView.color} 10%, transparent) 45%, var(--bg-surface) 100%)`,
        }}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
          <TeamBadge team={teamView} size="xl" />
          {/* The club's name stays in the top bar; this card is the league line â€”
              competition, matches played, position and points. */}
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-3">
              <span className="font-bold text-ink">{teamLeagueLabel}</span>
              {seasonStats
                ? ` Â· ${t(
                    seasonStats.played === 1
                      ? "team.matchesPlayedOne"
                      : "team.matchesPlayedMany",
                    { played: num(seasonStats.played) },
                  )}`
                : ""}
              {standing?.rank ? ` Â· ${positionLabel(standing.rank)}` : ""}
              {standing?.points != null
                ? ` Â· ${t("team.points", { points: num(standing.points) })}`
                : ""}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-lg">
        <Segmented
          id="team-tabs"
          value={tab}
          onChange={setTab}
          options={[
            { value: "squad", label: t("team.tab.squad") },
            { value: "fixtures", label: t("team.tab.fixtures") },
            { value: "season", label: t("team.tab.season") },
            { value: "xg", label: t("team.tab.xg") },
          ]}
        />
      </div>

      {/* The panel the team-tabs tablist above points at (see MatchDetail). */}
      <section
        id="team-tabs-panel"
        role="tabpanel"
        aria-labelledby={`team-tabs-${tab}`}
        className="panel-in mt-lg"
        key={tab}
      >
        {tab === "squad" ? <SquadTab squadQuery={squadQuery} /> : null}
        {tab === "fixtures" ? <FixturesTab fixtures={fixtures} teamId={team.id} /> : null}
        {tab === "season" ? <StatsTab stats={seasonStats} /> : null}
        {tab === "xg" ? (
          <XgTab
            teamId={team.id}
            played={seasonStats?.played ?? 0}
            points={seasonStats ? seasonStats.wins * 3 + seasonStats.draws : null}
          />
        ) : null}
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ squad */

function SquadTab({ squadQuery }: { squadQuery: ReturnType<typeof useSquad> }) {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n();
  if (squadQuery.isLoading) return <PanelSkeleton height={192} />;
  if (squadQuery.isError) return <ErrorState onRetry={() => squadQuery.refetch()} />;

  const squad = squadQuery.data;
  if (!squad || squad.players.length === 0) {
    return <EmptyState icon="ðŸ‘¥" title={t("team.squadEmpty")} hint={t("team.squadEmptyHint")} />;
  }

  // Persian reads every number in the Persian script â€” jersey, counts and stats
  // alike. Mixing Û±Û³ with 00/01 in one row was the bug this fixes.
  const num = (value: number | string) => (lang === "fa" ? toFaDigits(value) : String(value));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-lg">
      {/* The squad rows carry colour-coded numbers with no key; a colour-blind
          reader cannot tell goals from assists. The legend names both. */}
      <div
        className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs font-bold"
        style={{ padding: "8px 0", borderBottom: "1px solid var(--border-subtle)" }}
      >
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1" style={{ color: "var(--volt)" }}>
          <span aria-hidden="true">âš½</span>
          {t("team.stats.goals")}
        </span>
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1" style={{ color: "var(--gold)" }}>
          <span aria-hidden="true">ðŸ…°ï¸</span>
          {t("team.stats.assists")}
        </span>
      </div>
      {POS_ORDER.map((pos) => {
        const group = squad.players.filter((p) => p.position === pos);
        if (group.length === 0) return null;
        return <SquadSection key={pos} pos={pos} players={group} num={num} />;
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * One position group (GK / DEF / MID / FWD) with a collapsible header. The MID
 * group is 17 rows on its own â€” a scroll wall â€” so each group folds under its
 * heading; the choice persists per position so a collapsed group stays folded
 * across reloads. GK and DEF start open, MID and FWD closed.
 */
function SquadSection({
  pos,
  players,
  num,
}: {
  pos: Position;
  players: SquadRow[];
  num: (value: number | string) => string;
}) {
  const { t, dir, localize } = useI18n();
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useLocalState<boolean>(
    `team.squad.collapsed.${pos}`,
    pos === "MID" || pos === "FWD",
  );

  return (
    <div>
      {/* The count rides NEXT to the heading, not on the far side of the row: a
          badge detached from its label reads as stray chrome. */}
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setCollapsed((value) => !value)}
        aria-expanded={!collapsed}
        className="mb-2 flex min-h-11 w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-1 text-start transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated"
      >
        <h2 className="label label-lg text-ink-2">{t(`pos.${pos}`)}</h2>
        <Chip tone="muted">
          <Num>{num(players.length)}</Num>
        </Chip>
        {/* ChevronDown (â–¾) open / ChevronRight (â–¸) closed. ChevronRightIcon
            already mirrors itself in RTL, so it points left in Persian. */}
        <span className="ms-auto flex flex-none items-center text-ink-3" aria-hidden="true">
          {collapsed ? <ChevronRightIcon /> : <ChevronDownIcon />}
        </span>
      </button>

      {/* Smooth fold: the grid-rows trick animates the height without measuring
          the list, and the inner clip keeps the rows out of the layout while
          collapsed. */}
      <div className="squad-collapse" data-collapsed={collapsed}>
        <div className="squad-collapse-inner">
          <ul className="space-y-2">
            {players.map((player) => (
              // One grid, flipped by `dir`: the jersey lands on the leading
              // edge (right in Persian, left in English) and the goals/assists
              // on the trailing edge, so there is no dead strip on either side.
              <li
                key={player.id}
                dir={dir}
                className="player-row rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-2.5"
              >
                <Num className="player-number text-xs font-bold text-ink-3">
                  {num(player.jersey ?? "â€“")}
                </Num>
                {/* A face for every squad member: the ESPN headshot where the
                    feed has one, the player's initials where it doesn't, on
                    the same footprint either way. */}
                <PlayerAvatar name={localize(player.name)} src={player.headshot} size="sm" />
                <span className="player-name min-w-0">
                  <span className="block truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">
                    {/* <bdi> isolates a Latin name so it cannot drift the row's
                        bidi run in Persian. */}
                    <bdi>{localize(player.name)}</bdi>
                  </span>
                  <span className="block truncate text-xs text-ink-3">
                    {player.espnPosition ?? t(`pos.${player.position}`)}
                    {player.appearances
                      ? ` Â· ${t("team.appearances", { count: num(player.appearances) })}`
                      : ""}
                  </span>
                </span>
                <span className="player-stats flex-none">
                  <Num className={cx("text-sm", (player.goals ?? 0) > 0 ? "text-volt" : "text-ink-3")}>
                    {num(player.goals ?? 0)}
                  </Num>
                  <Num className={cx("text-sm", (player.assists ?? 0) > 0 ? "text-gold" : "text-ink-3")}>
                    {" "}
                    {num(player.assists ?? 0)}
                  </Num>
                </span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ fixtures */

function FixturesTab({ fixtures, teamId }: { fixtures: ApiMatch[]; teamId: string }) {
  const { t, lang, localize } = useI18n();
  const prefetchMatch = usePrefetchMatch();
  const locale = lang === "fa" ? "fa-IR" : undefined;
  if (fixtures.length === 0) return <EmptyState title={t("team.noFixtures")} />;

  return (
    <ul className="space-y-2">
      {fixtures.map((match) => {
        // One scoreline per fixture: crest, club, the result or the kickoff,
        // club, crest. The club whose page this is wears volt, so picking your
        // side out of a neutral fixture list needs no reading.
        const { home, away } = matchFromApi(match);
        return (
          <li key={match.id}>
            <Link
              to={`/match/${match.id}`}
              onPointerDown={() => prefetchMatch(match.id)}
              aria-label={`${localize(home.shortName)} ${t("common.versus")} ${localize(away.shortName)}`}
              className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-2.5 transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated"
            >
              <span className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-2">
                <TeamBadge team={home} size="sm" />
                <span
                  className={cx(
                    "min-w-0 truncate text-xs font-bold",
                    home.id === teamId ? "text-volt" : "text-ink",
                  )}
                >
                  {localize(home.shortName)}
                </span>
              </span>
              <span className="flex-none">
                {match.status === "finished" ? (
                  <Chip tone="muted">
                    <Num>
                      {match.home_score ?? 0}â€“{match.away_score ?? 0}
                    </Num>
                  </Chip>
                ) : match.status === "live" ? (
                  <Chip tone="live">
                    <Num>{match.minute_display ?? ""}</Num>
                  </Chip>
                ) : (
                  /* Kickoff carries the date AND the time, so a fixture weeks
                     out is unambiguous at a glance. */
                  <Chip tone="volt">
                    <Num>{formatKickoffDate(new Date(match.kickoff_at), locale)}</Num>
                    {" Â· "}
                    <Num>{formatKickoff(new Date(match.kickoff_at), locale)}</Num>
                  </Chip>
                )}
              </span>
              <span className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center justify-end gap-2">
                <span
                  className={cx(
                    "min-w-0 truncate text-end text-xs font-bold",
                    away.id === teamId ? "text-volt" : "text-ink",
                  )}
                >
                  {localize(away.shortName)}
                </span>
                <TeamBadge team={away} size="sm" />
              </span>
            </Link>
          </li>
        );
      })}
    </ul>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ season stats */

type SeasonStats = NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof useTeamDetail>["data"]>["seasonStats"];

function StatsTab({ stats }: { stats: SeasonStats }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  if (!stats) return <EmptyState title={t("team.statsEmpty")} hint={t("team.statsEmptyHint")} />;

  // Two reads instead of one dump: what the side creates, and what it concedes
  // or controls. The flat list said everything at once and ranked nothing.
  const attackRows: Array<{ label: string; value: string }> = [
    { label: t("stats.goals"), value: `${stats.goals_for}` },
    { label: t("stats.shots"), value: `${stats.total_shots}` },
    { label: t("stats.onTarget"), value: `${stats.total_shots_on_target}` },
    { label: t("stats.corners"), value: `${stats.total_corners}` },
  ];
  const controlRows: Array<{ label: string; value: string }> = [
    { label: t("stats.conceded"), value: `${stats.goals_against}` },
    { label: t("stats.possession"), value: `${Math.round(stats.avg_possession)}%` },
    { label: t("stats.passAccuracy"), value: `${Math.round(stats.avg_pass_accuracy)}%` },
    { label: t("stats.fouls"), value: `${stats.total_fouls}` },
  ];

  // The season in one line. The grouped cards below say what the side does;
  // only the record says how it went, so it leads the tab rather than hiding
  // as row one of a list nobody scanned to the bottom of.
  const record: Array<{ letter: string; value: number }> = [
    { letter: "W", value: stats.wins },
    { letter: "D", value: stats.draws },
    { letter: "L", value: stats.losses },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-lg">
      <Card className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 p-4">
        <span className="label text-ink-3">{t("statsPage.teams")}</span>
        <span className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
          {record.map(({ letter, value }) => (
            <span key={letter} className="flex items-baseline gap-0.5">
              <Num className="text-lg font-black text-ink">{value}</Num>
              <span className="label text-ink-3">{letter}</span>
            </span>
          ))}
        </span>
      </Card>
      <StatCard title={t("team.statsAttack")} rows={attackRows} />
      <StatCard title={t("team.statsDefense")} rows={controlRows} />
    </div>
  );
}

/** One grouped stat sheet: a heading, then label/value rows on a hairline. */
function StatCard({ title, rows }: { title: string; rows: Array<{ label: string; value: string }> }) {
  return (
    <Card className="p-4">
      <SectionHeading title={title} />
      <dl className="divide-y divide-line">
        {rows.map((row) => (
          <div key={row.label} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 py-2.5">
            <dt className="text-xs text-ink-3">{row.label}</dt>
            <dd>
              <Num className="text-md font-bold text-ink">{row.value}</Num>
            </dd>
          </div>
        ))}
      </dl>
    </Card>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ expected goals */

/** Understat stores these display labels; map them to our own copy. */
const SITUATION_KEY: Record<string, StringKey> = {
  "Open play": "xg.sitOpenPlay",
  "From corner": "xg.sitCorner",
  "Set piece": "xg.sitSetPiece",
  "Direct Freekick": "xg.sitFreekick",
  Penalty: "xg.sitPenalty",
};

/**
 * Expected goals, from Understat (a different source to the ESPN stat sheet).
 * A club's xG against its actual goals is the read that separates a good
 * attacking side from a lucky one, and the per-type split is the part the
 * broadcaster graphics never show.
 *
 * Empty is a normal outcome, not a failure: the scraper only runs for followed
 * big-five clubs, so cups and the long tail have nothing here.
 */
function XgTab({ teamId, played, points }: { teamId: string; played: number; points: number | null }) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const query = useTeamUnderstat(teamId);

  if (query.isLoading) return <PanelSkeleton height={192} />;
  if (query.isError) return <ErrorState onRetry={() => query.refetch()} />;

  const teamXg = query.data?.teamStats?.xg ?? null;
  const teamXga = query.data?.teamStats?.xga ?? null;
  const expected = query.data?.teamStats?.xpts ?? null;
  const ppda = query.data?.teamStats?.ppda ?? null;
  const deep = query.data?.teamStats?.deep ?? null;
  const situations = query.data?.situations ?? [];
  const players = [...(query.data?.players ?? [])].sort((a, b) => (b.xg ?? 0) - (a.xg ?? 0));

  if (teamXg == null && situations.length === 0 && players.length === 0) {
    return <EmptyState title={t("xg.emptyTitle")} hint={t("xg.emptyHint")} />;
  }

  const fixed = (value: number | null) => (value == null ? "â€“" : value.toFixed(2));
  const perMatch = (value: number | null) =>
    value != null && played > 0 ? (value / played).toFixed(2) : null;
  // Actual minus expected: the over/under-performance read.
  const delta = points != null && expected != null ? points - expected : null;
  const extra = [
    ppda != null ? { label: t("xg.ppda"), value: ppda.toFixed(2), hint: t("xg.ppdaHint") } : null,
    deep != null ? { label: t("xg.deep"), value: String(deep), hint: t("xg.deepHint") } : null,
  ].filter((row): row is { label: string; value: string; hint: string } => row != null);
  // Scale the bars against the biggest type so they read as shares of the total.
  const widest = Math.max(0.01, ...situations.map((s) => Math.max(s.xg ?? 0, s.xga ?? 0)));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-lg">
      <Card className="p-4">
        <SectionHeading title={t("xg.title")} />
        <div className="mt-md grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-ink-3">{t("xg.for")}</p>
            <Num className="text-lg font-black text-volt">{fixed(teamXg)}</Num>
            {perMatch(teamXg) ? (
              <p className="text-xs text-ink-3">
                <Num>{perMatch(teamXg)}</Num> {t("xg.perMatch")}
              </p>
            ) : null}
          </div>
          <div className="text-end">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-ink-3">{t("xg.against")}</p>
            <Num className="text-lg font-black text-ink-2">{fixed(teamXga)}</Num>
            {perMatch(teamXga) ? (
              <p className="text-xs text-ink-3">
                <Num>{perMatch(teamXga)}</Num> {t("xg.perMatch")}
              </p>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </div>

        {delta != null ? (
          <div className="mt-md flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2 border-t border-line pt-3">
            <span className="text-xs text-ink-2">{t("xg.points")}</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-ink-3">
              <Num className="text-sm font-bold text-ink">{points}</Num>
              {" Â· "}
              {t("xg.expected")} <Num className="text-sm font-bold text-gold">{expected?.toFixed(1)}</Num>{" "}
              <Num className={cx("font-bold", delta >= 0 ? "text-volt" : "text-danger")}>
                {delta >= 0 ? "+" : ""}
                {delta.toFixed(1)}
              </Num>
            </span>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {extra.length > 0 ? (
          <dl className="mt-md grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 border-t border-line pt-3">
            {extra.map((row) => (
              <div key={row.label}>
                <dt className="text-xs font-semibold text-ink-3">{row.label}</dt>
                <dd>
                  <Num className="text-sm font-bold text-ink">{row.value}</Num>
                </dd>
              </div>
            ))}
          </dl>
        ) : null}
      </Card>

      {situations.length > 0 ? (
        <Card className="p-4">
          <SectionHeading title={t("xg.situations")} />
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-ink-3">{t("xg.situationsHint")}</p>
          <ul className="mt-md space-y-3">
            {situations.map((situation) => {
              const key = SITUATION_KEY[situation.situation];
              return (
                <li key={situation.situation}>
                  <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
                    <span className="text-xs text-ink-2">{key ? t(key) : situation.situation}</span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-ink-3">
                      <Num>{situation.shots ?? 0}</Num> {t("stats.shots")} Â· <Num>{situation.goals ?? 0}</Num>{" "}
                      {t("stats.goals")}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="h-1.5 flex-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-elevated">
                      <span
                        className="block h-full rounded-full bg-volt"
                        style={{ width: `${(((situation.xg ?? 0) / widest) * 100).toFixed(1)}%` }}
                      />
                    </span>
                    <Num className="w-9 flex-none text-end text-xs font-bold text-ink">
                      {fixed(situation.xg ?? 0)}
                    </Num>
                  </div>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        </Card>
      ) : null}

      {players.length > 0 ? (
        <Card className="p-0">
          <div className="px-3 pt-3">
            <SectionHeading title={t("xg.players")} />
          </div>
          <ul className="mt-2 divide-y divide-line">
            {players.slice(0, 10).map((player) => (
              <li key={player.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2">
                <Num className="grid h-6 w-6 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full bg-elevated text-xs text-ink-2">
                  {player.jersey ?? "â€“"}
                </Num>
                <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-xs font-bold text-ink">
                  {localize(player.name)}
                </span>
                <span className="flex-none text-xs text-ink-3">
                  <Num className="me-2 text-ink-2">
                    {t("xg.xa")} {fixed(player.xa ?? 0)}
                  </Num>
                  <Num className="font-bold text-volt">{fixed(player.xg ?? 0)}</Num>
                </span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          <p className="px-3 pb-3 pt-2 text-xs text-ink-3">{t("xg.source")}</p>
        </Card>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\panels\CalendarSheet.tsx =====

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { BottomSheet } from "../BottomSheet";
import { dayKey } from "../DateStrip";
import { ChevronLeftIcon, ChevronRightIcon } from "../icons";
import { useI18n } from "../../i18n/context";
import {
  addJalaliMonths,
  jalaliMonthIndex,
  jalaliMonthLength,
  jalaliToGregorian,
  JALALI_MONTHS_FA,
  toJalali,
} from "../../lib/jalali";
import { cx } from "../../lib/cx";
import { selectionHaptic } from "../../lib/telegram";

/**
 * Persian gets its own calendar SYSTEM, not a transliterated month list: the
 * sheet under Persian runs on the Solar Hijri (Jalali) calendar â€” ÙØ±ÙˆØ±Ø¯ÛŒÙ†
 * first, Jalali year, Jalali day numbers â€” while the underlying cells stay
 * real `Date` objects so day keys and match dots never disagree. English keeps
 * the Gregorian month list. The year and day numbers still come from Intl,
 * because "Û²Û±" is not "21" in another font.
 */
const MONTH_NAMES_EN = [
  "January",
  "February",
  "March",
  "April",
  "May",
  "June",
  "July",
  "August",
  "September",
  "October",
  "November",
  "December",
];

/**
 * Weekday headers. The grid keeps the Sunday-first order in both languages â€”
 * only the names flip, so the two languages read the same grid.
 */
const DOW_EN = ["Sun", "Mon", "Tue", "Wed", "Thu", "Fri", "Sat"];
const DOW_FA = ["Ø´Ù†", "ÛŒÚ©", "Ø¯Ùˆ", "Ø³Ù‡", "Ú†Ù‡", "Ù¾Ù†", "Ø¬Ù…Ø¹"];

/**
