 * How many months the sheet may travel from today â€” 3 back, 3 ahead. The
 * month grid can only land on whole months of the active calendar, so the
 * clamp compares month indices of that calendar; the day feed covers the
 * full range (see Matches).
 */
const HORIZON_MONTHS = 3;

/**
 * The month under construction, one calendar per language: Jalali {jy, jm}
 * (1-based months) under Persian, Gregorian {y, m} (0-based) under English.
 */
interface MonthView {
  y: number;
  /** 1-based under Persian, 0-based under English â€” never mixed. */
  m: number;
}

/** Gregorian month arithmetic on a 0-based index (Decâ†’Jan rollover free). */
function addGregorianMonths(y: number, m: number, delta: number): MonthView {
  const idx = y * 12 + m + delta;
  return { y: Math.floor(idx / 12), m: ((idx % 12) + 12) % 12 };
}

/** Jalali month offset reshaped to a view tile (addJalaliMonths speaks {jy, jm}). */
function offsetJalali(jy: number, jm: number, delta: number): MonthView {
  const r = addJalaliMonths(jy, jm, delta);
  return { y: r.jy, m: r.jm };
}

/** Clamp a view into the reachable Â±HORIZON_MONTHS band of its calendar. */
function clampView(view: MonthView, min: MonthView, max: MonthView): MonthView {
  if (view.y * 12 + view.m < min.y * 12 + min.m) return min;
  if (view.y * 12 + view.m > max.y * 12 + max.m) return max;
  return view;
}

/**
 * Month-view date picker â€” the jump-to-any-day escape hatch at the end of the
 * date strip.
 *
 * The strip covers three weeks; this covers today Â± 3 months, in the ACTIVE
 * calendar (Solar Hijri in Persian, Gregorian in English), so a user looking
 * for a match months away hunts for it in their own month names without
 * browsing outside the synced fixture window. It is a picker first: a day
 * outside any fixtures shows no dot, and picking an empty day lands on the
 * feed's "no matches on this day" state rather than a lie.
 */
export function CalendarSheet({
  open,
  onClose,
  selected,
  onSelect,
  daysWithMatches,
}: {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  selected: Date;
  onSelect: (date: Date) => void;
  /** Day keys (DateStrip's `dayKey` format) that have at least one match. */
  daysWithMatches?: Set<string>;
}) {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n();
  const isFa = lang === "fa";
  const digits = useMemo(
    () => new Intl.NumberFormat(isFa ? "fa-IR" : undefined, { useGrouping: false }),
    [isFa],
  );

  // The reachable band, anchored at TODAY's month of each calendar. Computed
  // per render from the clock (cheap math), so an app left open across a month
  // boundary drifts its clamp along instead of sticking.
  const [minView, maxView] = useMemo<[MonthView, MonthView]>(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    if (isFa) {
      const t = toJalali(now);
      return [
        offsetJalali(t.jy, t.jm, -HORIZON_MONTHS),
        offsetJalali(t.jy, t.jm, HORIZON_MONTHS),
      ];
    }
    return [
      addGregorianMonths(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), -HORIZON_MONTHS),
      addGregorianMonths(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), HORIZON_MONTHS),
    ];
  }, [isFa]);

  // The month on screen. Re-opening the sheet always lands on the selected
  // day's month (clamped into the band) â€” otherwise a user who browsed to a
  // far month would come back to it after picking a day elsewhere.
  const [view, setView] = useState<MonthView>(() => clampView(viewOf(selected, isFa), minView, maxView));
  // Open/close phase, so the reset above runs on the open EDGE and not on
  // every render (the selected date is a fresh object each minute).
  const [wasOpen, setWasOpen] = useState(open);
  if (open && !wasOpen) {
    setWasOpen(true);
    setView(clampView(viewOf(selected, isFa), minView, maxView));
  }
  if (!open && wasOpen) setWasOpen(false);

  const monthName = isFa ? JALALI_MONTHS_FA[view.m - 1] : MONTH_NAMES_EN[view.m];
  const dowNames = isFa ? DOW_FA : DOW_EN;

  /** The month's days as real Dates, padded to whole weeks. */
  const cells = useMemo(() => {
    const out: Array<Date | null> = [];
    const first = isFa ? jalaliToGregorian(view.y, view.m, 1) : new Date(view.y, view.m, 1);
    const days = isFa ? jalaliMonthLength(view.y, view.m) : lastGregDay(view.y, view.m);
    // Leading blanks: the month's first day sits in its own weekday column.
    // Both languages read the grid Saturday-first in Persian (Sat=0) and
    // Sunday-first in English, so the shift survives the calendar switch.
    const startDow = isFa ? (first.getDay() + 1) % 7 : first.getDay();
    for (let i = 0; i < startDow; i++) out.push(null);
    if (isFa) {
      for (let d = 1; d <= days; d++) out.push(jalaliToGregorian(view.y, view.m, d));
    } else {
      for (let d = 1; d <= days; d++) out.push(new Date(view.y, view.m, d));
    }
    // Trailing blanks fill the last row.
    while (out.length % 7 !== 0) out.push(null);
    return out;
  }, [view.y, view.m, isFa]);

  const todayKey = dayKey(new Date());
  const selectedKey = dayKey(selected);

  const atMin = isFa
    ? jalaliMonthIndex(view.y, view.m) <= jalaliMonthIndex(minView.y, minView.m)
    : view.y * 12 + view.m <= minView.y * 12 + minView.m;
  const atMax = isFa
    ? jalaliMonthIndex(view.y, view.m) >= jalaliMonthIndex(maxView.y, maxView.m)
    : view.y * 12 + view.m >= maxView.y * 12 + maxView.m;

  const prevMonth = () =>
    setView((v) => (isFa ? offsetJalali(v.y, v.m, -1) : addGregorianMonths(v.y, v.m, -1)));
  const nextMonth = () =>
    setView((v) => (isFa ? offsetJalali(v.y, v.m, 1) : addGregorianMonths(v.y, v.m, 1)));

  return (
    <BottomSheet open={open} onClose={onClose} title={t("calendar.title")}>
      {/* Month navigation. The chevrons point at the side they move to, and
          mirror themselves in Persian â€” the document direction flips the row,
          so a fixed left arrow would point away from the month it opens.
          At the band's edge the step is a no-op and the chevron dimmed: the
          clamp, not a silently-drifting grid, is what holds the horizon. */}
      <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={prevMonth}
          disabled={atMin}
          aria-label={t("calendar.prevMonth")}
          className={cx(
            "grid h-11 w-11 flex-none place-items-center rounded-lg border transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.96]",
            atMin ? "border-line/50 text-ink-3/60" : "border-line text-ink-2 hover:bg-elevated",
          )}
        >
          <ChevronLeftIcon className="rtl:-scale-x-100" />
        </button>
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-center text-md font-bold text-ink">
          {monthName} {digits.format(view.y)}
        </span>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={nextMonth}
          disabled={atMax}
          aria-label={t("calendar.nextMonth")}
          className={cx(
            "grid h-11 w-11 flex-none place-items-center rounded-lg border transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.96]",
            atMax ? "border-line/50 text-ink-3/60" : "border-line text-ink-2 hover:bg-elevated",
          )}
        >
          <ChevronRightIcon />
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Back to today. The sheet opens on the selected day's month, so after
          browsing to a far month the current week is many taps away; this is
          the one tap back. 44px tall, like every other control in the sheet. */}
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => {
          selectionHaptic();
          onSelect(new Date());
          onClose();
        }}
        className="mb-4 flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-line text-sm font-bold text-ink-2 transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated active:scale-[0.98]"
      >
        {t("matches.today")}
      </button>

      {/* Weekday header. */}
      <div className="mb-1 grid grid-cols-7 gap-1">
        {dowNames.map((dow) => (
          <div key={dow} className="text-center text-xs font-bold text-ink-3">
            {dow}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* The month. In Persian mode the numbers are the JALALI day of the
          cell â€” the Jalali month can straddle two Gregorian months, and the
          display must follow the name in the header, not the host calendar. */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1">
        {cells.map((day, index) => {
          if (!day) {
            // Blank cell: it holds the grid's place so the first of the month
            // lands in its own weekday column.
            return <span key={`blank-${index}`} aria-hidden="true" />;
          }
          const key = dayKey(day);
          const isToday = key === todayKey;
          const isSelected = key === selectedKey;
          const hasMatches = daysWithMatches?.has(key) ?? false;
          const dayNum = isFa ? toJalali(day).jd : day.getDate();
          return (
            <button
              key={key}
              type="button"
              aria-pressed={isSelected}
              aria-current={isToday ? "date" : undefined}
              onClick={() => {
                selectionHaptic();
                onSelect(day);
                onClose();
              }}
              className={cx(
                "flex min-h-11 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 rounded-lg border text-sm font-bold transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.96]",
                isSelected
                  ? "border-volt bg-volt text-on-volt"
                  : isToday
                    ? "border-volt bg-transparent text-volt"
                    : "border-line bg-transparent text-ink",
              )}
            >
              <span className="num-display tabular-nums">{digits.format(dayNum)}</span>
              {hasMatches ? (
                // A day with fixtures carries a dot, so a busy Saturday reads as
                // busy before the tap. The selected day's fill IS the marker.
                <span
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  className={cx(
                    "mt-0.5 h-1 w-1 rounded-full",
                    isSelected ? "bg-on-volt" : "bg-volt",
                  )}
                />
              ) : (
                // Keeps every cell the same height whether or not it has a dot.
                <span aria-hidden="true" className="mt-0.5 h-1 w-1" />
              )}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </BottomSheet>
  );
}

/** The view (month tile) the given date belongs to, in the active calendar. */
function viewOf(date: Date, isFa: boolean): MonthView {
  if (isFa) {
    const j = toJalali(date);
    return { y: j.jy, m: j.jm };
  }
  return { y: date.getFullYear(), m: date.getMonth() };
}

/** Last local day of a Gregorian month (day-before-first-of-next). */
function lastGregDay(y: number, m: number): number {
  return new Date(y, m + 1, 0).getDate();
}

===== D:\football\src\components\panels\CommentsPanel.tsx =====

import { useState } from "react";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";

            {t("common.close")}

}

/**
 * The crowd thread under the match timeline: pinned minute-comments with the
 * optional clip link (https-only, enforced server-side). The composer pins to
 * a timeline event when one is selected, else to a typed minute.
 */
export function CommentsSection({
  matchId,
  events,
  myUserId,
  onOpenClip,
}: {
  matchId: string;
  /** Timeline events, for the event picker and the minute defaults. */
  events: Array<{ id: string; minuteDisplay: string; label: string }>;
  myUserId?: number;
  onOpenClip?: (comment: Comment) => void;
}) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const { push } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [mediaLink, setMediaLink] = useState("");
  const [eventId, setEventId] = useState<string>("");
  const [minuteDraft, setMinuteDraft] = useState("");
  const [clipUrl, setClipUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const commentsQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["comments", matchId],
    queryFn: () => api.getComments(matchId),
  });
  const comments = adaptComments(commentsQuery.data?.comments ?? [], myUserId);

  type CommentsData = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof api.getComments>>;

  const post = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (input: { text: string; eventId?: number; minuteDisplay?: string; mediaLink?: string }) =>
      api.addComment(matchId, input),
    // Optimistic: the comment appears the instant the user taps post â€” the
    // invalidation replaces it with the canonical row (real id) right after.
    onMutate: async (input) => {
      await queryClient.cancelQueries({ queryKey: ["comments", matchId] });
      const prev = queryClient.getQueryData<CommentsData>(["comments", matchId]);
      const optimistic: ApiComment = {
        id: -Date.now(),
        event_id: input.eventId ?? null,
        match_id: matchId,
        minute_display: input.eventId
          ? (events.find((e) => String(e.id) === String(input.eventId))?.minuteDisplay ?? null)
          : (input.minuteDisplay ?? null),
        minute_seconds: null,
        user_id: myUserId ?? -1,
        text: input.text,
        media_link: input.mediaLink ?? null,
        created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        author: "",
        author_pending: true,
      } as ApiComment;
      if (prev) {
        queryClient.setQueryData<CommentsData>(["comments", matchId], {
          comments: [...prev.comments, optimistic],
        });
      }
      return { prev };
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      notifyHaptic("success");
      setText("");
      setMediaLink("");
      setEventId("");
      setMinuteDraft("");
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["comments", matchId] });
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["timeline", matchId] });
    },
    onError: (err, _input, ctx) => {
      if (ctx?.prev) queryClient.setQueryData(["comments", matchId], ctx.prev);
      notifyHaptic("error");
      push({ text: t(serverErrorKey(err) as StringKey), tone: "danger", icon: "âœ•" });
    },
  });

  const canPost = text.trim().length > 0 && (Boolean(eventId) || /^\d+/.test(minuteDraft.trim()));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {/* The thread itself, minute-ordered (the server sorts). */}
      {commentsQuery.isLoading ? (
        <div className="shimmer h-16 rounded-card border border-line" />
      ) : comments.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title={t("comments.empty")} hint={t("comments.emptyHint")} />
      ) : (
        <ul className="space-y-2 fade-swap">
          {comments.map((comment) => (
            <li key={comment.id} className="rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Num className="grid h-6 min-w-[38px] place-items-center rounded-md border border-line bg-elevated px-1 text-xs text-ink-2">
                  {comment.minuteDisplay ?? "?"}
                </Num>
                <span
                  className={cx(
                    "min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-xs font-bold",
                    comment.isMine ? "text-gold" : "text-ink-2",
                  )}
                >
                  {comment.isMine ? t("comments.you") : localize(comment.author)}
                </span>
                {comment.mediaLink ? (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => (onOpenClip ? onOpenClip(comment) : setClipUrl(comment.mediaLink ?? ""))}
                    className="flex-none text-xs font-bold text-volt underline-offset-2 hover:underline"
                  >
                    {t("comments.attachOpen")} â†—
                  </button>
                ) : null}
              </div>
              <div className="mt-1.5 flex items-start gap-2">
                <p className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-sm text-ink">{localize(comment.text)}</p>
                {!comment.isMine ? (
                  <ReportButton commentType="timeline" commentId={Number(comment.id)} />
                ) : null}
              </div>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}

      {/* Composer: pin to an event, or to any minute. */}
      <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-3">
        <p className="mb-2 label text-ink-3">{t("comments.add")}</p>

        <label className="block">
          <span className="mb-1 block label text-ink-3">{t("comments.jumpToEvent")}</span>
          <select
            value={eventId}
            onChange={(e) => setEventId(e.target.value)}
            className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-card border border-line bg-elevated px-3 text-sm font-bold text-ink"
          >
            <option value="">{t("comments.minute")}â€¦</option>
            {events.map((e) => (
              <option key={e.id} value={e.id}>
                {e.minuteDisplay}' â€” {e.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>

        {!eventId ? (
          <label className="mt-2 block">
            <span className="mb-1 block label text-ink-3">{t("comments.minute")}</span>
            <input
              value={minuteDraft}
              onChange={(e) => setMinuteDraft(e.target.value)}
              inputMode="numeric"
              placeholder="57"
              className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-card border border-line bg-elevated px-3 text-sm font-bold text-ink placeholder:text-ink-3"
            />
          </label>
        ) : null}

        <textarea
          value={text}
          onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
          rows={2}
          maxLength={500}
          aria-label={t("comments.textAria")}
          placeholder={t("comments.placeholder")}
          className="mt-2 w-full resize-none rounded-card border border-line bg-elevated p-3 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-3 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-volt"
        />
        <div className="mt-1 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-xs text-ink-3">
            {t("comments.media")}: {t("comments.mediaHint")}
          </span>
          <Num className="text-xs text-ink-3">{text.length}/500</Num>
        </div>
        <input
          value={mediaLink}
          onChange={(e) => setMediaLink(e.target.value)}
          type="url"
          dir="ltr"
          aria-label={t("comments.mediaAria")}
          placeholder="https://â€¦"
          className="mt-1 min-h-11 w-full rounded-card border border-line bg-elevated px-3 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-3 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-volt"
        />

        <Button
          className="mt-3 w-full"
          disabled={!canPost || post.isPending}
          onClick={() => {
            haptic("medium");
            post.mutate({
              text: text.trim(),
              eventId: eventId ? Number(eventId) : undefined,
              minuteDisplay: eventId ? undefined : minuteDraft.trim() || undefined,
              mediaLink: mediaLink.trim() || undefined,
            });
          }}
        >
          {post.isPending ? t("common.loading") : t("comments.post")}
        </Button>
      </div>

      {clipUrl ? <ClipSheet url={clipUrl} onClose={() => setClipUrl(null)} /> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

/** Small count chip for headers/cards: the crowd-thread size at a glance. */
export function CommentCountChip({ count }: { count: number }) {
  if (count <= 0) return null;
  return (
    <Chip tone="gold">
      ðŸ’¬ <Num>{count}</Num>
    </Chip>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\panels\LeaguePickerSheet.tsx =====

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { BottomSheet } from "../BottomSheet";
import { CompRowSkeleton } from "../Skeletons";
import { Chip, EmptyState, ErrorState, Segmented } from "../ui";
import { CheckIcon } from "../icons";
import { cx } from "../../lib/cx";
import { useCompetitions, useFollowedLeagues } from "../../hooks/useApi";
import { MAX_FOLLOWED_LEAGUES } from "../../hooks/useFavorites";
import { useI18n } from "../../i18n/context";
import { serverErrorKey, type ApiCompetition } from "../../lib/api";
import {
  leaguePickerSections,
  type LeagueKindFilter,
  type LeaguePickerSectionId,
} from "../../lib/leaguePicker";
import { haptic, selectionHaptic } from "../../lib/telegram";
import { useToast } from "../toastContext";
import type { StringKey } from "../../i18n/strings";

/** The section heading copy â€” the same grouping Browse uses. */
const SECTION_TITLE: Record<LeaguePickerSectionId, StringKey> = {
  league: "browse.leagues",
  cup: "browse.cups",
  tournament: "browse.tournaments",
};

/**
 * Favorite Leagues â€” the Matches feed's league picker.
 *
 * Two things it must get right beyond "a list of toggles":
 *  - it lists the whole `/api/competitions` catalog, cups and international
 *    tournaments included, because following a competition is how a user
 *    subscribes to its fixtures, tables and matchday alerts (see
 *    server/notifications.ts: `leagueFollowers`), not just the big five;
 *  - the cap answer is immediate and in the user's language â€” a full list
 *    toasts `leagues.errCap` instead of a silent no-op, and the server's own
 *    MAX_LEAGUES stays the rule that holds under a double tap.
 *
 * Writes go through `useFollowedLeagues`, whose invalidate refreshes the
 * Matches chip row behind the sheet, so the feed re-scopes as the sheet closes.
 */
export function LeaguePickerSheet({ open, onClose }: { open: boolean; onClose: () => void }) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const { push } = useToast();
  const comps = useCompetitions();
  const followed = useFollowedLeagues();
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<LeagueKindFilter>("all");

  const sections = useMemo(
    () => leaguePickerSections(comps.data?.competitions ?? [], filter, followed.slugs),
    [comps.data, filter, followed.slugs],
  );

  const full = followed.count >= MAX_FOLLOWED_LEAGUES;

  function toggle(comp: ApiCompetition) {
    const onError = (err: unknown) => {
      haptic("medium");
      push({ text: t(serverErrorKey(err) as StringKey), tone: "danger", icon: "âœ•" });
    };
    if (followed.isFollowing(comp.slug)) {
      selectionHaptic();
      followed.unfollow.mutate(comp.slug, { onError });
      return;
    }
    if (full) {
      // The cap is a bucket, not an error state: say which rule stopped it.
      haptic("medium");
      push({ text: t("leagues.errCap"), tone: "danger", icon: "âœ•" });
      return;
    }
    selectionHaptic();
    followed.follow.mutate(comp.slug, { onError });
  }

  return (
    <BottomSheet
      open={open}
      onClose={onClose}
      title={t("leagues.title")}
      subtitle={t("leagues.subtitle")}
      footer={
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-x-3 gap-y-1 pb-1">
          <Chip tone={full ? "gold" : "volt"}>
            {t("leagues.count", { count: followed.count, max: MAX_FOLLOWED_LEAGUES })}
          </Chip>
          <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-end text-xs leading-tight text-ink-3">
            {t(full ? "leagues.capHint" : "leagues.notifyHint", { max: MAX_FOLLOWED_LEAGUES })}
          </span>
        </div>
      }
    >
      <Segmented
        value={filter}
        onChange={setFilter}
        options={[
          { value: "all", label: t("browse.all") },
          { value: "league", label: t("browse.leagues") },
          { value: "cup", label: t("browse.cups") },
        ]}
      />

      {comps.isLoading ? (
        <div className="mt-3">
          <CompRowSkeleton rows={6} />
        </div>
      ) : comps.isError ? (
        <div className="mt-3">
          <ErrorState title={t("leagues.error")} hint={t("error.hint")} onRetry={() => comps.refetch()} />
        </div>
      ) : sections.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="mt-3">
          <EmptyState title={t("leagues.empty")} hint={t("leagues.emptyHint")} />
        </div>
      ) : (
        // Keyed on the filter so the list crossfades when the tab changes
        // instead of swapping rows in place underneath the header.
        <div key={filter} className="fade-swap">
          {sections.map((section) => (
            <section key={section.id} className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="label mb-2 text-ink-3">{t(SECTION_TITLE[section.id])}</h3>
              <ul className="space-y-1.5">
                {section.rows.map((comp) => {
                  const on = followed.isFollowing(comp.slug);
                  const pending =
                    (followed.follow.isPending && followed.follow.variables === comp.slug) ||
                    (followed.unfollow.isPending && followed.unfollow.variables === comp.slug);
                  const name = localize(comp.name);
                  // A coming-soon competition has no synced data behind it, so
                  // following it would subscribe the user to fixtures that never
                  // arrive. Rendered â€” the scope is worth seeing â€” but inert,
                  // exactly like Browse's disabled rows.
                  const soon = Boolean(comp.comingSoon);
                  const Wrapper = soon ? "div" : "button";
                  return (
                    <li key={comp.slug}>
                      <Wrapper
                        {...(soon
                          ? {}
                          : {
                              type: "button" as const,
                              onClick: () => toggle(comp),
                              "aria-pressed": on,
                              "aria-label": t(on ? "leagues.unfollow" : "leagues.follow", {
                                league: name,
                              }),
                            })}
                        className={cx(
                          "flex min-h-12 w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-card border p-2.5 text-start transition-[color,background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.98]",
                          soon
                            ? "border-dashed border-line bg-transparent opacity-70"
                            : on
                              ? "border-volt bg-elevated"
                              : "border-line bg-surface hover:bg-elevated",
                          pending && "opacity-60",
                        )}
                      >
                        {/* No monogram tile: a single letter beside the full
                            name reads as doubled text ("PPremier League"). */}
                        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">{name}</span>
                        {soon ? (
                          <Chip tone="muted">{t("browse.soon")}</Chip>
                        ) : on ? (
                          <span className="flex flex-none items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-volt">
                            <CheckIcon className="marker-pop h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                            {t("leagues.following")}
                          </span>
                        ) : null}
                      </Wrapper>
                    </li>
                  );
                })}
              </ul>
            </section>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </BottomSheet>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\panels\LineupPicker.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api } from "../../lib/api";
import { useMatch, useSquad, type SquadRow } from "../../hooks/useApi";
import { useI18n } from "../../i18n/context";
import { haptic, selectionHaptic } from "../../lib/telegram";
import {
  FORMATIONS,
  UNITS,
  XI_SIZE,
  compareSquad,
  fillFormation,
  isValidFormationId,
  isValidXI,
  pitchRows,
  requiredUnits,
  unitTally,
  type SlotCandidate,
  type Unit,
} from "../../lib/formations";
import { cx } from "../../lib/cx";
import { Button, Chip, EmptyState, ErrorState, Num, TeamBadge } from "../ui";
import { PanelSkeleton } from "../Skeletons";
import { CheckIcon } from "../icons";

type Side = "home" | "away";

/** SquadRow -> the shape src/lib/formations.ts reasons about. */
const asCandidate = (p: SquadRow): SlotCandidate => ({
  id: p.id,
  espnPosition: p.espnPosition,
  jersey: p.jersey,
  unit: p.position,
});

/**
 * The predicted XI on the pitch, in the chosen shape: attack at the top, the
 * keeper's goal at the bottom, and every player placed by their granular
 * position (a left-back left, a right-back right) rather than dropped into a
 * generic band. Empty slots stay hollow so the shape you're short of is
 * visible without counting.
 */
function LineupPitch({
  formationId,
  squad,
  picked,
}: {
  formationId: string;
  squad: SquadRow[];
  picked: string[];
}) {
  const rows = pitchRows(formationId);
  const candidates = squad.filter((p) => picked.includes(p.id)).map(asCandidate);
  const slots = fillFormation(formationId, candidates);

  return (
    <div className="pitch-watermark rounded-card border border-line bg-base px-3 py-3">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        {rows.map((row, rowIndex) => (
          <div
            key={`${row.unit}-${row.size}-${row.goal ? "g" : "f"}`}
            className={cx("flex items-center gap-1.5", row.goal && "border-t border-line pt-2")}
          >
            {slots[rowIndex].map((player, index) => (
              <span
                key={player ? player.id : `empty-${rowIndex}-${index}`}
                className={cx(
                  "grid h-6 flex-1 place-items-center rounded-lg border text-xs font-bold tabular-nums",
                  player ? "border-transparent bg-volt text-black" : "border-line border-dashed text-ink-3",
                )}
              >
                {player?.jersey ?? "Â·"}
              </span>
            ))}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <p className="mt-2 border-t border-line pt-1.5 text-center text-xs text-ink-3">
        {candidates.length}/{XI_SIZE} Â· {formationId}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

function PlayerRow({
  player,
  picked,
  onToggle,
}: {
  player: SquadRow;
  picked: boolean;
  onToggle: () => void;
}) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  return (
    <li>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onToggle}
        aria-pressed={picked}
        className={cx(
          "flex min-h-11 w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-card border p-2 text-start transition-[color,background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.98]",
          picked ? "border-volt bg-elevated" : "border-line bg-surface hover:bg-elevated",
        )}
      >
        <Num className="grid h-7 w-7 flex-none place-items-center rounded-md border border-line bg-elevated text-xs text-ink-2">
          {player.jersey ?? "â€“"}
        </Num>
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <span className="block truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">{localize(player.name)}</span>
          {player.appearances ? (
            <span className="block text-xs text-ink-3">
              <Num>{player.appearances}</Num> {t("lineup.apps")}
              {player.goals ? (
                <>
                  {" Â· "}
                  <Num>{player.goals}</Num> {t("lineup.goalsShort")}
                </>
              ) : null}
            </span>
          ) : null}
        </span>
        {player.espnPosition ? (
          <Chip tone="muted" normalCase>
            {player.espnPosition}
          </Chip>
        ) : null}
        {picked ? <CheckIcon className="marker-pop h-4 w-4 flex-none text-volt" /> : null}
      </button>
    </li>
  );
}

/**
 * Lineup Predictor editor: choose the club, pick exactly 11 starters out of its
 * full squad, and name the formation. Locks 2h before kickoff (the server
 * enforces; the UI shows the window).
 *
 * The club selector is presentation only. The payload stays `{ playerIds,
 * formation }`, which the resolver scores side-agnostically â€” a pick doesn't
 * carry a side (prediction-mechanics.md Â§1).
 */
export function LineupPicker({
  matchId,
  initial,
  onSubmit,
  submitting,
}: {
  matchId: string;
  initial: unknown;
  onSubmit: (payload: { playerIds: string[]; formation: string }) => void;
  submitting: boolean;
}) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const matchQuery = useMatch(matchId);

  // ESPN's recorded shapes, once published â€” they prefill the picker instead of
  // making the user retype a shape the app already knows.
  const officialQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["lineups", matchId],
    queryFn: () => api.getLineups(matchId),
    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,
  });

  const init = (initial ?? null) as { playerIds?: string[]; formation?: string } | null;
  const [side, setSide] = useState<Side>("home");
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<string[]>(init?.playerIds ?? []);
  const [formation, setFormation] = useState<string>(init?.formation ?? FORMATIONS[0].id);
  const [touchedFormation, setTouchedFormation] = useState(Boolean(init?.formation));

  const match = matchQuery.data;
  const team = side === "home" ? match?.home : match?.away;
  const squadQuery = useSquad(team?.id ?? "");
  const players = useMemo(() => squadQuery.data?.players ?? [], [squadQuery.data]);

  const positions = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, Unit>();
    for (const p of players) map.set(p.id, (p.position ?? "MID") as Unit);
    return map;
  }, [players]);

  const tally = useMemo(() => unitTally(picked, positions, formation), [picked, positions, formation]);
  const required = requiredUnits(formation);
  const complete = isValidXI(picked, positions);
  const shapeMatches = tally.every((t) => t.picked === t.required);

  // Follow the club's own shape until the user picks one themselves.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (touchedFormation) return;
    const official =
      side === "home" ? officialQuery.data?.home.formation : officialQuery.data?.away.formation;
    if (official && isValidFormationId(official)) setFormation(official);
  }, [side, officialQuery.data, touchedFormation]);

  // Switching club must not carry the other club's players into the XI.
  useEffect(() => {
    setPicked((prev) => prev.filter((id) => positions.has(id)));
  }, [positions]);

  const toggle = (playerId: string) => {
    selectionHaptic();
    setPicked((prev) =>
      prev.includes(playerId)
        ? prev.filter((id) => id !== playerId)
        : prev.length < XI_SIZE
          ? [...prev, playerId]
          : prev,
    );
  };

  if (matchQuery.isLoading) return <PanelSkeleton height={220} />;
  if (matchQuery.isError) return <ErrorState onRetry={() => matchQuery.refetch()} />;
  if (!match) return <EmptyState title={t("lineup.squadEmpty")} hint={t("lineup.squadEmptyHint")} />;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {/* Which club's XI is being predicted. */}
      <div>
        <p className="label mb-1.5 text-ink-2">{t("lineup.pickClub")}</p>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          {(["home", "away"] as Side[]).map((option) => {
            const club = option === "home" ? match.home : match.away;
            const active = side === option;
            return (
              <button
                key={option}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  selectionHaptic();
                  setSide(option);
                }}
                aria-pressed={active}
                className={cx(
                  "flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-card border px-2.5 text-start transition-[color,background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.98]",
                  active ? "border-volt bg-elevated" : "border-line bg-surface hover:bg-elevated",
                )}
              >
                <TeamBadge team={club} size="sm" />
                <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-xs font-bold text-ink">
                  {localize(club.shortName)}
                </span>
                {active ? <CheckIcon className="marker-pop h-4 w-4 flex-none text-volt" /> : null}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <Chip tone={complete ? "pitch" : "volt"}>
          <Num>{picked.length}</Num>/{XI_SIZE}
        </Chip>
        <span className="text-xs text-ink-3">
          {t("lineup.perStarter")} Â· {t("lineup.exactFormation")}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div>
        <p className="label mb-1.5 text-ink-2">{t("lineup.shape")}</p>
        <LineupPitch formationId={formation} squad={players} picked={picked} />
        <div className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5">
          {FORMATIONS.map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                haptic("light");
                setTouchedFormation(true);
                if (isValidFormationId(f.id)) setFormation(f.id);
              }}
              aria-pressed={formation === f.id}
              className={cx(
                "min-h-11 rounded-md border text-xs font-bold transition-[color,background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.97]",
                formation === f.id
                  ? "border-volt bg-elevated text-volt"
                  : "border-line bg-surface text-ink-2",
              )}
            >
              {f.id}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* The club's full squad, grouped by unit so the shape maps onto the list. */}
      <div>
        <p className="label mb-1.5 text-ink-2">{t("lineup.squad")}</p>
        {squadQuery.isLoading ? (
          <PanelSkeleton height={140} />
        ) : squadQuery.isError ? (
          <ErrorState onRetry={() => squadQuery.refetch()} />
        ) : players.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState title={t("lineup.squadEmpty")} hint={t("lineup.squadEmptyHint")} />
        ) : (
          <ul className="max-h-[38vh] space-y-2 overflow-y-auto pe-0.5">
            {UNITS.map((unit) => {
              // Ordered the way the pitch reads: left-back, centre-backs,
              // right-back â€” not by shirt number.
              const group = players.filter((p) => p.position === unit);
              group.sort((a, b) => compareSquad(asCandidate(a), asCandidate(b)));
              if (group.length === 0) return null;
              const count = tally.find((entry) => entry.unit === unit);
              const done = count ? count.picked === count.required : false;
              return (
                <li key={unit}>
                  <div className="sticky top-0 z-10 flex items-center justify-between bg-surface/95 py-1 backdrop-blur-sm">
                    <span className="label text-ink-2">{t(`lineup.unit.${unit}`)}</span>
                    <span
                      className={cx("text-xs font-bold tabular-nums", done ? "text-volt" : "text-ink-3")}
                    >
                      {count?.picked ?? 0}/{required[unit]}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <ul className="mt-1 space-y-1.5">
                    {group.map((player) => (
                      <PlayerRow
                        key={player.id}
                        player={player}
                        picked={picked.includes(player.id)}
                        onToggle={() => toggle(player.id)}
                      />
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        )}
      </div>

      {picked.length === XI_SIZE && !shapeMatches ? (
        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gold">{t("lineup.shapeMismatch")}</p>
      ) : null}

      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          className="flex-none"
          onClick={() => {
            haptic("light");
            setPicked([]);
          }}
          disabled={picked.length === 0 || submitting}
        >
          {t("lineup.reset")}
        </Button>
        <Button
          className="flex-1"
          disabled={!complete || submitting}
          onClick={() => onSubmit({ playerIds: picked, formation })}
        >
          {submitting ? t("common.loading") : t("lineup.confirm")}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\panels\LineupsPanel.tsx =====

import { useState } from "react";
import { useI18n } from "../../i18n/context";
import type { LineupPlayer, MatchLineup, Team } from "../../types";
import { Card, Chip, Num, PlayerAvatar, Segmented, TeamBadge } from "../ui";
import { placePublishedXI, type SlotCandidate } from "../../lib/formations";

/** `LineupPlayer` â†’ the shape the placement helpers reason about. */
const asCandidate = (player: LineupPlayer): SlotCandidate => ({
  id: player.id,
  espnPosition: player.espnPosition ?? null,
  // A missing shirt number is not number 0 (0 is a legal keeper number).
  jersey: player.number > 0 ? player.number : null,
  unit: player.position,
  formationPlace: player.formationPlace ?? null,
});

/**
 * The published XI on a pitch: turf, markings, and the eleven standing in the
 * shape they were named in.
 *
 * The shape comes from ESPN's own `formation` string when it is one the
 * catalogue knows AND the XI fits it, so a 4-2-3-1 really draws a holding pair
 * and an attacking three (see `placePublishedXI`, which also folds ESPN's
 * winger-as-midfielder habit back into the forward band). When the feed leaves
 * the shape blank, sends a form the catalogue doesn't carry, or the XI simply
 * does not fit it, the rows are derived from what the XI contains â€” one band
 * per unit â€” so a leaked lineup still shows every player it has instead of an
 * assumed 4-3-3 that drops the extras.
 *
 * Attack is at the top, the keeper's goal at the bottom (`pitchRows`). The
 * block inherits the page direction, so in the FA (RTL) layout the shape
 * mirrors â€” the left-back reads from the right and a side attacks toward the
 * same edge it does in its own half of the pitch. The painted markings stay
 * where they are: every one is horizontally centred, so a mirrored row of
 * players still lines up with the penalty areas and the halfway line.
 */
function LineupPitch({ lineup }: { lineup: MatchLineup }) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  // Carries the player itself plus the fields the placement helpers read, so a
  // placed slot can still render a face and a name.
  const candidates = lineup.starters.map((player) => ({ ...player, ...asCandidate(player) }));
  const { rows, slots } = placePublishedXI(lineup.formation, candidates);

  if (rows.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div>
      <p className="mb-1 text-center label text-ink-3">â†‘ {t("shot.attackingEnd")}</p>
      <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-pitch/20 p-2">
        {/* One big pitch (~280px), not two small ones: the rows are spread
            evenly down the full height so an XI breathes on a phone. */}
        <div className="relative flex min-h-[264px] flex-col justify-evenly gap-2.5">
          {rows.map((row, rowIndex) => (
            <div key={`${row.unit}-${row.size}-${row.goal ? "g" : "f"}`} className="flex items-start gap-1">
              {slots[rowIndex].map((player, index) => (
                <PitchSlot
                  key={player ? player.id : `empty-${rowIndex}-${index}`}
                  player={player}
                  name={player ? localize(player.name) : ""}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Markings, painted over the slots in the real pitch's proportions â€”
            goal lines, penalty areas, six-yard boxes, spots, halfway line and
            centre circle â€” so a band reads as a place on a pitch rather than a
            row of avatars. Purely decorative and pointer-transparent. */}
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-2" aria-hidden="true">
          <span className="absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-px bg-white/30" />
          <span className="absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-px bg-white/40" />
          <span className="absolute bottom-0 left-1/2 h-[16%] w-[57%] -translate-x-1/2 border-x border-t border-white/25" />
          <span className="absolute bottom-0 left-1/2 h-[6%] w-[28%] -translate-x-1/2 border-x border-t border-white/20" />
          <span className="absolute bottom-[11%] left-1/2 h-1 w-1 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-full bg-white/50" />
          <span className="absolute top-1/2 left-1/2 aspect-square h-[22%] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full border border-white/15" />
          <span className="absolute inset-x-0 top-1/2 h-px bg-white/15" />
          <span className="absolute top-0 left-1/2 h-[14%] w-[51%] -translate-x-1/2 border-x border-b border-white/15" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** One player on the pitch: face (or initials), shirt number, name. */
function PitchSlot({ player, name }: { player: LineupPlayer | null; name: string }) {
  if (!player) {
    return (
      <span className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 justify-center">
        <span className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full border border-dashed border-white/25" aria-hidden="true" />
      </span>
    );
  }
  return (
    <span className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col items-center">
      <span className="relative">
        <PlayerAvatar name={name} src={player.headshot} size="md" className="border-pitch/60" />
        <Num className="absolute -end-1 -bottom-1 grid h-4 min-w-4 place-items-center rounded-full bg-volt px-0.5 text-xs leading-none text-black">
          {player.number > 0 ? player.number : "â€“"}
        </Num>
      </span>
      <span className="mt-1 block w-full truncate text-center text-xs font-bold text-ink-2">{name}</span>
    </span>
  );
}

/**
 * The official lineups, as they were published: the shape drawn on a pitch,
 * then the bench below it. Two sides, one at a time, because an XI on a phone
 * is already a lot of names.
 */
export function LineupsPanel({
  home,
  away,
  lineups,
}: {
  home: Team;
  away: Team;
  lineups: { home: MatchLineup; away: MatchLineup };
}) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const [side, setSide] = useState<"home" | "away">("home");

  const team = side === "home" ? home : away;
  const lineup = side === "home" ? lineups.home : lineups.away;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <Segmented
        value={side}
        onChange={setSide}
        options={[
          { value: "home", label: localize(home.shortName) },
          { value: "away", label: localize(away.shortName) },
        ]}
      />

      <Card className="p-3">
        <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
          <TeamBadge team={team} size="sm" />
          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-ink">{localize(team.shortName)}</span>
          <span className="flex-1" />
          <Chip tone="pitch">{lineup.formation}</Chip>
        </div>

        <LineupPitch lineup={lineup} />

        {/* The coach is not in every feed, and the lineups route has no coach
            endpoint at all â€” a bare "Coach:" label is worse than no line. */}
        {lineup.coach ? (
          <p className="mt-3 border-t border-line pt-2 text-xs text-ink-3">
            {t("lineups.coach")}: {localize(lineup.coach)}
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </Card>

      {/* The bench, as a horizontal strip of small chips: shirt number over
            the name's initial. A row per sub made the bench longer than the
            pitch; a scroll keeps it to one line. Chips are LTR-safe: numbers
            are figures and an initial is direction-neutral. */}
      {lineup.subs.length > 0 ? (
        <Card className="p-3">
          <p className="mb-2 label text-ink-2">{t("lineups.bench")}</p>
          <ul className="-mx-2 flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-2 pb-1">
            {lineup.subs.map((player) => (
              <li key={player.id} className="flex-none">
                <div
                  className="flex w-[52px] flex-col items-center gap-1 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-2 text-center"
                  title={localize(player.name)}
                >
                  <Num className="grid h-6 w-6 place-items-center rounded-full bg-elevated px-0.5 text-xs font-black text-ink-2">
                    {player.number > 0 ? player.number : "â€“"}
                  </Num>
                  <span className="w-full truncate text-xs font-bold text-ink">
                    {localize(player.name).slice(0, 1)}.
                  </span>
                </div>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </Card>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\panels\PitchView.tsx =====

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api, type ApiTimelineEvent } from "../../lib/api";
import { useI18n } from "../../i18n/context";
import { Card, Chip, Num, Segmented } from "../ui";
import { cellZone, cellFromFieldPosition, GRID_COLS, GRID_ROWS, ZONE_LABEL_KEY } from "../../lib/zones";

type Side = { id: string; shortName: string; color: string };

/** An event the feed actually placed on the pitch â€” the only kind we can draw. */
type LocatedEvent = ApiTimelineEvent & { field_x: number; field_y: number };

function isLocated(event: ApiTimelineEvent): event is LocatedEvent {
  return (
    event.field_x !== null &&
    event.field_y !== null &&
    Number.isFinite(event.field_x) &&
    Number.isFinite(event.field_y)
  );
}

/**
 * The live shot plot: every event ESPN gave a position to, drawn twice â€” in the
 * 6Ã—4 grid the Shot Predictor is scored against, and on a to-scale pitch.
 *
 * WHERE THE DATA COMES FROM, AND WHAT IT CAN'T SHOW
 * ESPN fills `fieldPositionX/Y` on key events, and in practice only on goals and
 * the occasional shot â€” the sync stores exactly what arrives (`timeline_events.
 * field_x/field_y/goal_y`) and this plot draws exactly that. There is no pass
 * feed in the payload at all, so nothing here invents one: an empty pitch means
 * the feed located nothing, and the hint says so rather than implying the match
 * had no shots. It refreshes with the timeline query the Timeline tab owns, so
 * a goal that lands mid-match appears here without a reload.
 */
export function PitchView({
  matchId,
  live,
  home,
  away,
}: {
  matchId: string;
  /** Live matches refresh on the same 20s beat as the header and timeline. */
  live: boolean;
  home: Side;
  away: Side;
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<"grid" | "pitch">("grid");

  // Same key as the Timeline tab, so the two share one cache entry and one
  // in-flight request instead of polling ESPN-synced rows twice.
  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["timeline", matchId],
    queryFn: () => api.getTimeline(matchId),
    refetchInterval: live ? 20_000 : false,
  });

  const located = useMemo(() => (query.data?.events ?? []).filter(isLocated), [query.data]);

  return (
    <Card className="p-3">
      {/* The title and count live here rather than in a SectionHeading above:
          the count is this panel's own data, and a heading plus a header row
          printed "Pitch View" twice. */}
      <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
        <span className="label label-lg text-ink-2">{t("pitchView.title")}</span>
        <span className="flex-1" />
        <Chip tone="pitch">
          <Num>{located.length}</Num>
        </Chip>
      </div>

      <Segmented
        value={mode}
        onChange={setMode}
        options={[
          { value: "grid", label: t("pitchView.grid") },
          { value: "pitch", label: t("pitchView.full") },
        ]}
      />

      <p className="mt-2 mb-2 text-xs text-ink-3">{t("pitchView.hint")}</p>

      {mode === "grid" ? (
        <GoalGrid events={located} home={home} away={away} />
      ) : (
        <FullPitch events={located} home={home} away={away} />
      )}

      {located.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="mt-3 rounded-card border border-dashed border-line px-3 py-4 text-center text-xs text-ink-3">
          <span className="block font-bold text-ink-2">{t("pitchView.empty")}</span>
          {t("pitchView.emptyHint")}
        </p>
      ) : (
        <Legend events={located} home={home} away={away} />
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}

/** Which club a located event belongs to, as a colour. */
function colorFor(teamId: string | null, home: Side, away: Side): string {
  if (teamId === home.id) return home.color;
  if (teamId === away.id) return away.color;
  return "var(--text-muted)";
}

/**
 * Who is who, and what a marker means. The counts live in the header chip â€” a
 * number next to a singular noun ("3 Goal") is what two unpluralised labels
 * produce, and the app has no plural machinery to fix it with. The two marker
 * styles are the thing a reader cannot infer.
 */
function Legend({ events, home, away }: { events: LocatedEvent[]; home: Side; away: Side }) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const hasShot = events.some((e) => e.type !== "goal");

  return (
    <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1.5 border-t border-line pt-2">
      {[home, away].map((side) => (
        <span key={side.id} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-ink-2">
          <span
            className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full"
            style={{ backgroundColor: side.color }}
            aria-hidden="true"
          />
          <span className="truncate">{localize(side.shortName)}</span>
        </span>
      ))}
      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-ink-3">
        <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full ring-1 ring-white/70" aria-hidden="true" />
        {t("pitchView.goal")}
      </span>
      {hasShot ? (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-ink-3">
          <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full border border-white/70" aria-hidden="true" />
          {t("pitchView.shot")}
        </span>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * The Shot Predictor's own grid, with the match's real shots dropped into their
 * cells. Same geometry as the picker (`cellFromFieldPosition`, mirrored by
 * `src/lib/zones.ts` and pinned by tests/shotGrid.test.ts), so a goal plotted in
 * "inside left" is in the cell the picker would have scored for it.
 */
function GoalGrid({ events, home, away }: { events: LocatedEvent[]; home: Side; away: Side }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();

  const byCell = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, LocatedEvent[]>();
    for (const event of events) {
      const { gridX, gridY } = cellFromFieldPosition(event.field_x, event.field_y);
      const key = `${gridX}-${gridY}`;
      const bucket = map.get(key);
      if (bucket) bucket.push(event);
      else map.set(key, [event]);
    }
    return map;
  }, [events]);

  const cells = Array.from({ length: GRID_ROWS * GRID_COLS }, (_, i) => ({
    gridX: i % GRID_COLS,
    gridY: Math.floor(i / GRID_COLS),
  }));

  return (
    // A pitch never mirrors with the reading direction â€” left stays left in fa.
    <div dir="ltr">
      <p className="mb-1 text-center label text-ink-3">â†‘ {t("shot.attackingEnd")}</p>
      <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-pitch/20 p-2">
        <div className="relative grid gap-0.5" style={{ gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${GRID_COLS}, 1fr)` }}>
          {cells.map(({ gridX, gridY }) => {
            const pins = byCell.get(`${gridX}-${gridY}`) ?? [];
            const zone = cellZone(gridX, gridY);
            return (
              <div
                key={`${gridX}-${gridY}`}
                role="img"
                aria-label={`${t(ZONE_LABEL_KEY[zone])} Â· ${pins.length}`}
                className="relative flex aspect-[4/3] flex-wrap content-center items-center justify-center gap-0.5 rounded-[4px] border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-0.5"
              >
                {pins.slice(0, 4).map((pin) => (
                  <span
                    key={pin.id}
                    title={`${pin.minute_display} Â· ${pin.description}`}
                    className="block h-2 w-2 rounded-full ring-1 ring-white/70"
                    style={{
                      backgroundColor:
                        pin.type === "goal" ? colorFor(pin.team_id, home, away) : "transparent",
                      borderColor: colorFor(pin.team_id, home, away),
                    }}
                  />
                ))}
                {pins.length > 4 ? (
                  <Num className="text-xs font-bold text-ink-2">+{pins.length - 4}</Num>
                ) : null}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Markings painted over the cells â€” goal line, penalty area, six-yard
            box, spot and the halfway line â€” the same geometry the Shot
            Predictor draws, so the grid reads as a pitch, not a keypad. */}
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-2" aria-hidden="true">
          <span className="absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-px bg-white/40" />
          <span className="absolute top-0 left-1/2 h-[18%] w-[57%] -translate-x-1/2 border-x border-b border-white/25" />
          <span className="absolute top-0 left-1/2 h-[7%] w-[28%] -translate-x-1/2 border-x border-b border-white/20" />
          <span className="absolute top-[13%] left-1/2 h-1 w-1 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-full bg-white/50" />
          <span className="absolute inset-x-0 top-1/2 h-px bg-white/15" />
          <span className="absolute top-1/2 left-1/2 aspect-square h-[24%] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full border border-white/15" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------- full pitch */

/** A real pitch is 105m Ã— 68m, and the diagram is drawn to those proportions. */
const PITCH_W = 68;
const PITCH_H = 105;
const BOX_W = 40.32;
const BOX_D = 16.5;
const SIX_W = 18.32;
const SIX_D = 5.5;
const GOAL_W = 7.32;
const ARC_R = 9.15;
/** Where a 9.15m arc crosses the 16.5m box edge: 34 Â± sqrt(9.15Â² âˆ’ 5.5Â²). */
const ARC_X1 = (34 - 7.31).toFixed(2);
const ARC_X2 = (34 + 7.31).toFixed(2);

/**
 * ESPN field coordinates â†’ diagram points. `fieldPositionX` runs to the
 * opponent's goal (drawn at the top) and `fieldPositionY` across the width, so
 * both sides' shots converge on one goal â€” the standard shot-map read.
 */
function toPoint(fieldX: number, fieldY: number): { x: number; y: number } {
  return { x: (fieldY / 100) * PITCH_W, y: ((100 - fieldX) / 100) * PITCH_H };
}

function FullPitch({ events, home, away }: { events: LocatedEvent[]; home: Side; away: Side }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  return (
    <div dir="ltr" className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-pitch/20 p-2">
      <svg
        viewBox={`0 0 ${PITCH_W} ${PITCH_H}`}
        className="block h-auto w-full"
        role="img"
        aria-label={t("pitchView.aria")}
      >
        <g fill="none" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.28)" strokeWidth="0.35">
          <rect x="0.2" y="0.2" width={PITCH_W - 0.4} height={PITCH_H - 0.4} rx="0.6" />
          <line x1="0.2" y1={PITCH_H / 2} x2={PITCH_W - 0.2} y2={PITCH_H / 2} />
          <circle cx={PITCH_W / 2} cy={PITCH_H / 2} r={ARC_R} />
          {/* Own end (bottom): penalty area, six-yard box, the D. */}
          <rect x={(PITCH_W - BOX_W) / 2} y={PITCH_H - BOX_D} width={BOX_W} height={BOX_D} />
          <rect x={(PITCH_W - SIX_W) / 2} y={PITCH_H - SIX_D} width={SIX_W} height={SIX_D} />
          <path d={`M ${ARC_X1} ${PITCH_H - BOX_D} A ${ARC_R} ${ARC_R} 0 0 1 ${ARC_X2} ${PITCH_H - BOX_D}`} />
          {/* Attacking end (top). */}
          <rect x={(PITCH_W - BOX_W) / 2} y="0" width={BOX_W} height={BOX_D} />
          <rect x={(PITCH_W - SIX_W) / 2} y="0" width={SIX_W} height={SIX_D} />
          <path d={`M ${ARC_X1} ${BOX_D} A ${ARC_R} ${ARC_R} 0 0 0 ${ARC_X2} ${BOX_D}`} />
        </g>
        <g fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.55)">
          <circle cx={PITCH_W / 2} cy={PITCH_H / 2} r="0.7" />
          <circle cx={PITCH_W / 2} cy={PITCH_H - 11} r="0.7" />
          <circle cx={PITCH_W / 2} cy="11" r="0.7" />
        </g>
        <g stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.55)" strokeWidth="0.9">
          <line x1={(PITCH_W - GOAL_W) / 2} y1={PITCH_H} x2={(PITCH_W + GOAL_W) / 2} y2={PITCH_H} />
          <line x1={(PITCH_W - GOAL_W) / 2} y1="0" x2={(PITCH_W + GOAL_W) / 2} y2="0" />
        </g>

        {/* The shots. A goal also draws the line the ball took to the goal
            mouth (`goal_y`), which is the part a scoreline can never show. */}
        {events.map((event) => {
          const point = toPoint(event.field_x, event.field_y);
          const color = colorFor(event.team_id, home, away);
          const isGoal = event.type === "goal";
          const crossing =
            isGoal && event.goal_y !== null ? { x: (event.goal_y / 100) * PITCH_W, y: 0 } : null;
          return (
            <g key={event.id}>
              <title>{`${event.minute_display} Â· ${event.description}`}</title>
              {crossing ? (
                <>
                  <line
                    x1={point.x}
                    y1={point.y}
                    x2={crossing.x}
                    y2={crossing.y}
                    stroke={color}
                    strokeWidth="0.3"
                    strokeDasharray="1 1"
                    opacity="0.7"
                  />
                  <circle
                    cx={crossing.x}
                    cy={crossing.y}
                    r="1.1"
                    fill={color}
                    stroke="white"
                    strokeWidth="0.3"
                  />
                </>
              ) : null}
              <circle
                cx={point.x}
                cy={point.y}
                r="2.3"
                fill={isGoal ? color : "none"}
                stroke={isGoal ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.8)" : color}
                strokeWidth={isGoal ? "0.4" : "0.7"}
              />
              <text x={point.x + 3.2} y={point.y + 1} fontSize="2.8" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.75)">
                {event.minute_display}
              </text>
            </g>
          );
        })}
      </svg>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\panels\PredictionsHub.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient, type UseMutateFunction } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api, serverErrorKey, type ApiPrediction } from "../../lib/api";
import { useI18n } from "../../i18n/context";
import { haptic, notifyHaptic, selectionHaptic } from "../../lib/telegram";
import { pickSummary } from "../../lib/breakdown";
import { useToast } from "../toastContext";
import { BottomSheet } from "../BottomSheet";
import { RulesButton } from "../RulesSheet";
import { PredictionsHubSkeleton, PanelSkeleton } from "../Skeletons";
import { cx } from "../../lib/cx";
import { Button, Card, Chip, EmptyState, ErrorState, Num } from "../ui";
import { SubBoard } from "./SubBoard";
import { LineupPicker } from "./LineupPicker";
import { ShotPredictor } from "./ShotPredictor";
import { StarFilledIcon, SwapIcon, TargetIcon } from "../icons";
import { formatCountdown, isOpen, requiresLineup, type MatchStatus } from "../../lib/predictionWindow";
import { useNow } from "../../hooks/useNow";

type Mechanic = ApiPrediction["mechanic"];
type PredictionsData = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof api.getMyPredictions>>;
type SubmitResult = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof api.submitPrediction>>;

// Abbreviations stay text (they are labels); pictographic mechanics get the
// shared icon set so they don't shift weight with the system font.
const MECHANIC_META: Record<
  Mechanic,
  {
    key: string;
    hintKey: string;
    lockKey: string;
    text?: string;
    Glyph?: typeof StarFilledIcon;
    tone: "volt" | "live" | "gold";
  }
> = {
  lineup: {
    key: "predict.lineup",
    hintKey: "predict.lineupHint",
    lockKey: "predict.lockLineup",
    text: "XI",
    tone: "volt",
  },
  sub: {
    key: "predict.sub",
    hintKey: "predict.subNewHint",
    lockKey: "predict.lockHT",
    Glyph: SwapIcon,
    tone: "live",
  },
  shot_predict: {
    key: "predict.shotNew",
    hintKey: "predict.shotNewHint",
    lockKey: "predict.lockKickoff",
    Glyph: TargetIcon,
    tone: "volt",
  },
  player_watch: {
    key: "predict.watch",
    hintKey: "predict.watchHintNew",
    lockKey: "predict.lockKickoff",
    Glyph: StarFilledIcon,
    tone: "gold",
  },
  versus: {
    key: "predict.versus",
    hintKey: "predict.versusNewHint",
    lockKey: "predict.lockKickoff",
    text: "VS",
    tone: "volt",
  },
};

/**
 * The stakes layer on each match: all five mechanics, each with its own open
 * window and lock time (prediction-mechanics.md). Points resolve into the
 * season leaderboards; nothing here touches ratings.
 */
export function PredictionsHub({ matchId }: { matchId: string }) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const { push } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["predictions", matchId],
    queryFn: () => api.getMyPredictions(matchId),
  });
  const rows = query.data?.predictions ?? [];
  const windows = query.data?.windows ?? {};
  const matchStatus = (query.data?.matchStatus ?? "scheduled") as MatchStatus;
  // True once the official lineup is out â€” the gate the lineup-dependent
  // mechanics wait on, alongside their own clock windows.
  const lineupsPublished = query.data?.lineupPublished ?? false;
  const live = matchStatus === "live";
  const halftime = matchStatus === "halftime";
  const finished = matchStatus === "finished";

  const [editor, setEditor] = useState<Mechanic | null>(null);
  // Save confirmation: the just-saved card wears the volt goal sweep for one
  // beat, so the optimistic row reads as "accepted" rather than "maybe".
  const [flash, setFlash] = useState<Mechanic | null>(null);
  // Ticking clock: the countdown strings below are derived, not polled, so
  // without this they would freeze until an unrelated refetch.
  const now = useNow(30_000);

  // goal-flash paints over --t-slow (450ms); clear after the sweep lands so
  // the class (and its ::after) can't replay on a later re-render.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!flash) return;
    const id = setTimeout(() => setFlash(null), 650);
    return () => clearTimeout(id);
  }, [flash]);

  const invalidate = () => void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["predictions", matchId] });

  /*
   * Optimistic upsert: the editor sheet closes and the card shows the saved
   * pick instantly â€” the server round-trip lands in the background. Rollback
   * restores the previous list if the submit fails (lock windows, capsâ€¦).
   */
  const optimisticUpsert = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (vars: { mechanic: Mechanic; payload: unknown }) =>
      api.submitPrediction(matchId, vars.mechanic, vars.payload),
    onMutate: async (vars) => {
      await queryClient.cancelQueries({ queryKey: ["predictions", matchId] });
      const prev = queryClient.getQueryData<PredictionsData>(["predictions", matchId]);
      if (prev) {
        const optimisticRow: ApiPrediction = {
          id: -Date.now(),
          match_id: matchId,
          mechanic: vars.mechanic,
          payload: JSON.stringify(vars.payload),
          locked_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          status: "pending",
          points_awarded: 0,
          breakdown: null,
          resolved_at: null,
        };
        const rest = prev.predictions.filter((r) => r.mechanic !== vars.mechanic);
        queryClient.setQueryData<PredictionsData>(["predictions", matchId], {
          ...prev,
          predictions: [...rest, optimisticRow],
        });
      }
      return { prev, mechanic: vars.mechanic };
    },
    onError: (_err, _vars, ctx) => {
      if (ctx?.prev) queryClient.setQueryData(["predictions", matchId], ctx.prev);
    },
  });

  const byMechanic = (m: Mechanic) => rows.find((r) => r.mechanic === m) ?? null;

  // Player names for the saved-pick summaries ("Saka v Vinicius", not "#123 v
  // #456"). Same query key as the lineups tab and the pick editors, so once
  // any of them loaded, this is a cache hit â€” no extra request on the page.
  const lineups = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["lineups", matchId],
    queryFn: () => api.getLineups(matchId),
    staleTime: 60_000,
    enabled: rows.some(
      (r) => r.mechanic === "player_watch" || r.mechanic === "shot_predict" || r.mechanic === "versus",
    ),
  });
  const nameOf = (id: string): string => {
    const p = lineups.data?.players.find((x) => x.player_id === id);
    return p ? localize(p.full_name) : `#${id}`;
  };

  const statusChip = (row: ApiPrediction | null) => {
    if (!row) return null;
    if (row.status === "pending")
      return (
        <Chip tone="muted" className="marker-pop">
          {t("predict.pending")}
        </Chip>
      );
    // Awards pop in (marker-pop, transform + opacity only) rather than
    // appearing at full size â€” the one moment the pick paid off.
    if (row.status === "correct")
      return (
        <Chip tone="pitch" className="marker-pop">
          +{row.points_awarded}
        </Chip>
      );
    if (row.status === "partial")
      return (
        <Chip tone="gold" className="marker-pop">
          +{row.points_awarded}
        </Chip>
      );
    if (row.status === "void") return <Chip tone="muted">Â±0</Chip>;
    return <Chip tone="danger">+0</Chip>;
  };

  const windowInfo = (m: Mechanic) => {
    const w = windows[m];
    if (!w) return null;
    if (finished) return t("predict.locked");
    if (halftime) {
      // The break IS the sub lock: only the sub mechanic's copy changes.
      return m === "sub" ? t("sub.lockedHalftime") : t("predict.locked");
    }
    if (live) {
      return m === "sub" ? t("predict.lockHT") : t("predict.locked");
    }
    return formatCountdown(w.lockAt, now) ?? t("predict.locked");
  };

  const mechanics: Mechanic[] = ["lineup", "sub", "shot_predict", "player_watch", "versus"];

  // One state machine for the whole app (src/lib/predictionWindow.ts): the
  // same rules the server enforces, so a card never looks tappable when the
  // POST would answer LOCKED_MATCH_STARTED. Notably this closes every non-Sub
  // mechanic the instant the match kicks off, instead of trusting opensAt.
  const opensNow = (m: Mechanic) => isOpen(m, windows[m], { matchStatus, lineupsPublished, now });

  // Open-and-unpicked first: the list reads as the next step, not a five-row
  // checklist. Re-ranked ONLY when the open/picked picture actually changes â€”
  // the 30s countdown tick must not shuffle cards under the user's thumb.
  const orderKey = mechanics.map((m) => `${opensNow(m) ? "o" : "-"}${byMechanic(m) ? "p" : "-"}`).join("");
  const orderedRef = useRef<Mechanic[]>(mechanics);
  const orderKeyRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
  if (orderKeyRef.current !== orderKey) {
    orderKeyRef.current = orderKey;
    const rank = (m: Mechanic) => Number(!(opensNow(m) && !byMechanic(m)));
    orderedRef.current = [...mechanics].sort((a, b) => rank(a) - rank(b));
  }
  const ordered = orderedRef.current;

  // One action leads, not five: the first mechanic that is open and still
  // unpicked wears volt. Five identical primary buttons read as a checklist
  // with no next step, which is the opposite of a deadline.
  const primaryMechanic = ordered.find((m) => !byMechanic(m) && opensNow(m));

  const kickoffMs = windows.shot_predict ? new Date(windows.shot_predict.lockAt).getTime() : null;
  const minutesToKickoff = kickoffMs ? (kickoffMs - now) / 60_000 : null;
  const showLockSoon =
    matchStatus === "scheduled" &&
    minutesToKickoff !== null &&
    minutesToKickoff > 0 &&
    minutesToKickoff <= 60 &&
    rows.length < 5;
  const showHowTo = matchStatus === "scheduled" && rows.length === 0;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <p className="min-w-0 text-xs text-ink-3">{t("predict.hubHint")}</p>
        {/* The scoring tables live in the app now, not only in scoring-rules.md. */}
        <RulesButton variant="link" />
      </div>
      {showLockSoon ? (
        <Card className="border-danger/50 bg-danger/5 p-2.5">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-danger animate-pulse">{t("predict.lockSoon")}</span>
        </Card>
      ) : null}
      {showHowTo ? (
        <Card className="border-volt/40 bg-elevated p-3">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-ink">{t("predict.howToTitle")}</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-ink-2">{t("predict.howToBody")}</p>
        </Card>
      ) : null}
      {halftime ? (
        <Card className="border-live/40 p-2.5">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-live">{t("sub.halftimeBanner")}</span>
        </Card>
      ) : null}

      {query.isLoading ? (
        <PredictionsHubSkeleton />
      ) : (
        ordered.map((m) => {
          const meta = MECHANIC_META[m];
          const row = byMechanic(m);
          const notOpenYet = !opensNow(m);
          // Gated and still waiting on ESPN's XI: the card explains itself
          // rather than showing a kickoff countdown that hasn't started.
          const lineupLocked = !finished && requiresLineup(m, windows[m]) && !lineupsPublished;
          return (
            <Card
              key={m}
              className={cx(
                // Skeleton â†’ content: the card crossfades in once when the
                // hub finishes loading (its key is stable, so saves and the
                // 30s countdown tick don't replay it).
                "fade-swap p-3",
                row && "border-volt/40",
                // relative + overflow-hidden frame goal-flash's ::after sweep.
                flash === m && "relative overflow-hidden goal-flash",
              )}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span
                  className={cx(
                    "grid h-8 min-w-8 place-items-center rounded-full border px-1 text-xs font-extrabold",
                    meta.tone === "gold"
                      ? "border-gold text-gold"
                      : meta.tone === "live"
                        ? "border-live text-live"
                        : "border-volt text-volt",
                  )}
                  aria-hidden="true"
                >
                  {meta.Glyph ? <meta.Glyph className="h-4 w-4" /> : meta.text}
                </span>
                <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <span className="block truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">{t(meta.key as never)}</span>
                  <span className="block truncate text-xs text-ink-3">{t(meta.hintKey as never)}</span>
                </span>
                {statusChip(row)}
              </div>

              <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <span className="label text-ink-3">
                  {lineupLocked
                    ? t("predict.waitLineupHint")
                    : m === "sub"
                      ? t("predict.lockHT")
                      : `${t(meta.lockKey as never)}${!notOpenYet && !live && !finished ? ` Â· ${windowInfo(m) ?? ""}` : ""}`}
                </span>
                {row?.payload ? <MechanicSummary mechanic={m} payload={row.payload} nameOf={nameOf} /> : null}
                {!finished && !halftime ? (
                  <Button
                    variant={m === primaryMechanic ? "primary" : "ghost"}
                    className="px-3 text-xs"
                    disabled={notOpenYet}
                    onClick={() => {
                      haptic("light");
                      setEditor(m);
                    }}
                  >
                    {row ? t("versus.change") : t("match.predict")}
                  </Button>
                ) : null}
              </div>
            </Card>
          );
        })
      )}

      {editor ? (
        <MechanicEditor
          mechanic={editor}
          matchId={matchId}
          live={live}
          existing={byMechanic(editor)}
          onClose={() => setEditor(null)}
          onSaved={() => {
            // Invalidate (don't await): the optimistic row already shows; the
            // refetch replaces it with server truth (lock windows, ids).
            if (editor) setFlash(editor);
            invalidate();
            setEditor(null);
          }}
          onError={(err) => {
            notifyHaptic("error");
            push({ text: t(serverErrorKey(err) as never), tone: "danger", icon: "âœ•" });
          }}
          optimisticUpsert={optimisticUpsert.mutate}
        />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ editors */

function MechanicEditor({
  mechanic,
  matchId,
  live,
  existing,
  onClose,
  onSaved,
  onError,
  optimisticUpsert,
}: {
  mechanic: Mechanic;
  matchId: string;
  live: boolean;
  existing: ApiPrediction | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSaved: () => void;
  onError: (err: unknown) => void;
  optimisticUpsert: UseMutateFunction<SubmitResult, Error, { mechanic: Mechanic; payload: unknown }>;
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const submit = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (payload: unknown) => {
      // Optimistic path: cache updates immediately; the sheet closes on settle.
      optimisticUpsert({ mechanic, payload });
      return api.submitPrediction(matchId, mechanic, payload);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      notifyHaptic("success");
      onSaved();
    },
    onError,
  });

  if (mechanic === "lineup") {
    return (
      <BottomSheet
        open
        onClose={onClose}
        title={t("predict.lineup" as never)}
        subtitle={t("predict.lockLineup" as never)}
      >
        <LineupPicker
          matchId={matchId}
          initial={existing ? safeParse(existing.payload) : null}
          onSubmit={(payload) => submit.mutate(payload)}
          submitting={submit.isPending}
        />
      </BottomSheet>
    );
  }

  if (mechanic === "sub") {
    return (
      <BottomSheet
        open
        onClose={onClose}
        title={t("sub.boardTitle" as never)}
        subtitle={t("predict.lockHT" as never)}
      >
        <SubBoard
          matchId={matchId}
          live={live}
          initial={existing ? safeParse(existing.payload) : null}
          onSubmit={(payload) => submit.mutate(payload)}
          submitting={submit.isPending}
        />
      </BottomSheet>
    );
  }

  if (mechanic === "shot_predict") {
    return (
      <BottomSheet
        open
        onClose={onClose}
        title={t("predict.shotNew" as never)}
        subtitle={t("predict.shotNewHint" as never)}
      >
        <ShotPredictor
          matchId={matchId}
          initial={existing ? safeParse(existing.payload) : null}
          onSubmit={(payload) => submit.mutate(payload)}
          submitting={submit.isPending}
        />
      </BottomSheet>
    );
  }

  if (mechanic === "player_watch") {
    return (
      <BottomSheet
        open
        onClose={onClose}
        title={t("predict.watch" as never)}
        subtitle={t("predict.watchHintNew" as never)}
      >
        <WatchEditor
          matchId={matchId}
          initial={existing ? safeParse(existing.payload) : null}
          onSubmit={(payload) => submit.mutate(payload)}
