          submitting={submit.isPending}
        />
      </BottomSheet>
    );
  }

  return (
    <BottomSheet
      open
      onClose={onClose}
      title={t("versus.title" as never)}
      subtitle={t("predict.versusNewHint" as never)}
    >
      <VersusEditor
        matchId={matchId}
        initial={existing ? safeParse(existing.payload) : null}
        onSubmit={(payload) => submit.mutate(payload)}
        submitting={submit.isPending}
      />
    </BottomSheet>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ watch editor */

function WatchEditor({
  matchId,
  initial,
  onSubmit,
  submitting,
}: {
  matchId: string;
  initial: unknown;
  onSubmit: (payload: { playerId: string }) => void;
  submitting: boolean;
}) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["lineups", matchId],
    queryFn: () => api.getLineups(matchId),
  });
  const players = query.data?.players ?? [];
  const featured = players.filter((p) => p.started === 1 || (p.minutes_played ?? 0) > 0);
  const [pick, setPick] = useState<string | null>(
    initial && typeof initial === "object" && "playerId" in (initial as Record<string, unknown>)
      ? String((initial as Record<string, unknown>).playerId)
      : null,
  );

  if (query.isLoading) return <PanelSkeleton height={160} />;
  if (query.isError) return <ErrorState onRetry={() => query.refetch()} />;
  if (featured.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="py-4">
        <EmptyState title={t("predict.awaitLineupTitle")} hint={t("predict.awaitLineupHint")} />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <ul className="max-h-[50vh] space-y-1.5 overflow-y-auto">
        {featured.map((p) => (
          <li key={p.player_id}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                selectionHaptic();
                setPick(p.player_id);
              }}
              className={cx(
                "flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-card border p-2.5 text-start transition-[color,background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.98]",
                pick === p.player_id ? "border-gold bg-elevated" : "border-line bg-surface hover:bg-elevated",
              )}
            >
              <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">
                {localize(p.full_name)}
              </span>
              <Chip tone="muted" normalCase>
                {p.position}
              </Chip>
              {pick === p.player_id ? (
                <Chip tone="gold" className="marker-pop">
                  {t("watch.selected")}
                </Chip>
              ) : null}
            </button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <Button
        className="w-full"
        disabled={!pick || submitting}
        onClick={() => pick && onSubmit({ playerId: pick })}
      >
        {submitting ? t("common.loading") : t("common.save")}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ versus editor */

function VersusEditor({
  matchId,
  initial,
  onSubmit,
  submitting,
}: {
  matchId: string;
  initial: unknown;
  onSubmit: (payload: { playerAId: string; playerBId: string }) => void;
  submitting: boolean;
}) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["lineups", matchId],
    queryFn: () => api.getLineups(matchId),
  });
  const players = query.data?.players ?? [];
  const [sideA, setSideA] = useState<string | null>(
    initial && typeof initial === "object" && "playerAId" in (initial as Record<string, unknown>)
      ? String((initial as Record<string, unknown>).playerAId)
      : null,
  );
  const [sideB, setSideB] = useState<string | null>(
    initial && typeof initial === "object" && "playerBId" in (initial as Record<string, unknown>)
      ? String((initial as Record<string, unknown>).playerBId)
      : null,
  );

  if (query.isLoading) return <PanelSkeleton height={160} />;
  if (query.isError) return <ErrorState onRetry={() => query.refetch()} />;
  const featured = players.filter((p) => p.started === 1 || (p.minutes_played ?? 0) > 0);
  const teams = [...new Set(featured.map((p) => p.team_id))];

  if (featured.length === 0 || teams.length < 2) {
    return (
      <div className="py-4">
        <EmptyState title={t("predict.awaitLineupTitle")} hint={t("predict.awaitLineupHint")} />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const groupA = featured.filter((p) => p.team_id === teams[0]);
  const groupB = featured.filter((p) => p.team_id === teams[1]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <p className="text-xs text-ink-3">{t("predict.versusNewHint")}</p>
      {teams.map((teamId, index) => (
        <div key={teamId}>
          <p className="mb-1.5 label text-ink-2">{index === 0 ? "A" : "B"}</p>
          <ul className="max-h-44 space-y-1.5 overflow-y-auto">
            {(index === 0 ? groupA : groupB).map((p) => (
              <li key={p.player_id}>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    selectionHaptic();
                    if (index === 0) setSideA(p.player_id);
                    else setSideB(p.player_id);
                  }}
                  className={cx(
                    "flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-card border p-2.5 text-start transition-[color,background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.98]",
                    (index === 0 ? sideA : sideB) === p.player_id
                      ? "border-volt bg-elevated"
                      : "border-line bg-surface hover:bg-elevated",
                  )}
                >
                  <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">
                    {localize(p.full_name)}
                  </span>
                  {(index === 0 ? sideA : sideB) === p.player_id ? (
                    <Chip tone="volt" className="marker-pop">
                      {t("watch.selected")}
                    </Chip>
                  ) : null}
                </button>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      ))}
      <Button
        className="w-full"
        disabled={!sideA || !sideB || submitting}
        onClick={() => sideA && sideB && onSubmit({ playerAId: sideA, playerBId: sideB })}
      >
        {submitting ? t("common.loading") : t("common.save")}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ shared */

function safeParse(json: string): unknown {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(json);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function MechanicSummary({
  mechanic,
  payload,
  nameOf,
}: {
  mechanic: Mechanic;
  payload: string;
  /** Player id â†’ localized name (falls back to #id when lineups aren't in yet). */
  nameOf: (id: string) => string;
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const parsed = safeParse(payload) as Record<string, unknown> | null;
  if (!parsed) return null;

  // One implementation for every mechanic, shared with the history page
  // (src/lib/breakdown.ts) so the two screens can never disagree about what a
  // pick says. `nameOf` supplies this match's lineups â€” a cache hit here.
  const summary = pickSummary(mechanic, payload, { t, nameOf });
  if (summary) {
    return (
      <span className="max-w-[50%] truncate text-xs text-ink-3" dir="auto">
        {summary}
      </span>
    );
  }
  // No payload we can summarize: fall back to a bare count rather than nothing.
  if (mechanic === "sub") {
    const subs = Array.isArray(parsed.subs) ? parsed.subs.length : 0;
    return <Num className="text-xs text-ink-3">{subs}</Num>;
  }
  if (mechanic === "lineup") {
    const ids = Array.isArray(parsed.playerIds) ? parsed.playerIds.length : 0;
    return (
      <Num className="text-xs text-ink-3">
        {ids} Â· {String(parsed.formation ?? "")}
      </Num>
    );
  }
  return null;
}

===== D:\football\src\components\panels\RateSheet.tsx =====

import { useState } from "react";
import { useI18n } from "../../i18n/context";
import { haptic, notifyHaptic, selectionHaptic } from "../../lib/telegram";
import { useToast } from "../toastContext";
import { BottomSheet } from "../BottomSheet";
import { cx } from "../../lib/cx";
import { Button, Chip, Num, Stars } from "../ui";
import type { PlayerRating } from "../../types";

/**
 * One 3-card rating sheet: a 1â€“10 slider (integer snap pulse, signature
 * animation 6) plus the eye-test comment that makes the card worth reading.
 * Cosmetic only â€” never feeds points.
 */
export function RateSheet({
  rating,
  open,
  onClose,
  onSave,
  distribution,
  cardsUsed,
  cardsMax,
}: {
  rating: PlayerRating;
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (playerId: string, value: number, comment: string) => void;
  /** 10 buckets, counts per integer rating. Derived from crowd_ratings server-side. */
  distribution: number[];
  cardsUsed?: number;
  cardsMax?: number;
}) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const { push } = useToast();
  const [value, setValue] = useState(rating.myRating ?? 6);
  const [comment, setComment] = useState(rating.myComment ?? "");

  const totalVotes = distribution.reduce((sum, count) => sum + count, 0) || 1;
  const peak = Math.max(...distribution, 1);

  return (
    <BottomSheet
      open={open}
      onClose={onClose}
      title={localize(rating.name)}
      subtitle={`${t(`pos.${rating.position}`)} Â· ${t("ratings.points")} ${rating.fantasyPoints}`}
      footer={
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <Button variant="ghost" className="flex-1" onClick={onClose}>
            {t("common.cancel")}
          </Button>
          <Button
            className="flex-1"
            onClick={() => {
              haptic("medium");
              onSave(rating.playerId, value, comment.trim());
              notifyHaptic("success");
              push({
                text: t("toast.ratingSaved", { player: localize(rating.name) }),
                tone: "gold",
                icon: "â˜…",
              });
              onClose();
            }}
          >
            {t("ratings.save")}
          </Button>
        </div>
      }
    >
      <div className="text-center">
        {/* keyed so the pulse retriggers on every integer change */}
        <Num key={value} className="rating-pulse text-display leading-none text-gold">
          {value}
        </Num>
        <p className="text-xs text-ink-3">{t("ratings.yourRating")}</p>
        {typeof cardsUsed === "number" && typeof cardsMax === "number" ? (
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-ink-3">
            {t("ratings.cards", { used: cardsUsed, max: cardsMax })}
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <input
        type="range"
        min={1}
        max={10}
        step={1}
        value={value}
        aria-label={t("ratings.yourRating")}
        onChange={(event) => {
          selectionHaptic();
          setValue(Number(event.target.value));
        }}
        className="mt-4 h-11 w-full accent-[var(--gold)]"
      />

      <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-ink-3">
        <span>1</span>
        <span>5</span>
        <span>10</span>
      </div>

      {/* The eye-test comment â€” what separates a verdict from a number. */}
      <div className="mt-4">
        <label className="block">
          <span className="mb-1 block label text-ink-3">{t("ratings.commentLabel")}</span>
          <textarea
            value={comment}
            onChange={(e) => setComment(e.target.value)}
            rows={2}
            maxLength={280}
            placeholder={t("ratings.commentPlaceholder")}
            className="w-full resize-none rounded-card border border-line bg-elevated p-3 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-3 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-gold"
          />
        </label>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 border-t border-line pt-3">
        <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="label text-ink-2">{t("ratings.distribution")}</span>
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Stars value={rating.crowdRating ?? 0} />
            <Num className="text-sm text-gold">
              {rating.crowdRating !== null ? rating.crowdRating.toFixed(1) : "â€“"}
            </Num>
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex h-20 items-end gap-1">
          {distribution.map((count, index) => (
            <div key={index} className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center gap-1">
              <span
                className={cx(
                  "w-full rounded-t-sm transition-[height] duration-[var(--t-std)] ease-[var(--ease-out)]",
                  // All bars are evaluation, so all amber; yours is the solid one.
                  index + 1 === value ? "bg-gold" : "bg-gold/45",
                )}
                style={{ height: `${Math.max(3, (count / peak) * 64)}px` }}
              />
              <span className="text-xs text-ink-3">{index + 1}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-ink-3">
          <span>{t("ratings.votes", { count: totalVotes })}</span>
          <Chip tone="gold">{t("ratings.eyeTest")}</Chip>
        </div>
      </div>
    </BottomSheet>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\panels\ShotPredictor.tsx =====

import { useState } from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api } from "../../lib/api";
import { useI18n } from "../../i18n/context";
import { selectionHaptic } from "../../lib/telegram";
import { Button, Chip, EmptyState, ErrorState } from "../ui";
import { PanelSkeleton } from "../Skeletons";
import { cellZone, GRID_COLS, GRID_ROWS, ZONE_LABEL_KEY } from "../../lib/zones";

/** The 6 scoring zones in legend order (scoring-rules.md Â§3). */
const ZONE_KEYS = Object.values(ZONE_LABEL_KEY);

/**
 * Shot Predictor editor: ONE player, ONE of the 6 scoring zones (left/
 * center/right Ã— inside/outside box), rendered as a 6Ã—4 grid whose cells
 * map to zones â€” geometry mirrored from server/scoring/shotZones.ts
 * (scoring-rules.md: exact 5 / adjacent 2, +2 on target, +5 goal).
 */
export function ShotPredictor({
  matchId,
  initial,
  onSubmit,
  submitting,
}: {
  matchId: string;
  initial: unknown;
  onSubmit: (payload: { playerId: string; grid: { gridX: number; gridY: number } }) => void;
  submitting: boolean;
}) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const lineupsQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["lineups", matchId],
    queryFn: () => api.getLineups(matchId),
  });

  const init = (initial ?? null) as { playerId?: string; grid?: { gridX: number; gridY: number } } | null;
  const [playerId, setPlayerId] = useState<string | null>(init?.playerId ?? null);
  const [cell, setCell] = useState<{ gridX: number; gridY: number } | null>(init?.grid ?? null);

  const players = lineupsQuery.data?.players ?? [];
  const featured = players.filter((p) => p.started === 1 || (p.minutes_played ?? 0) > 0);

  if (lineupsQuery.isLoading) return <PanelSkeleton height={160} />;
  if (lineupsQuery.isError) return <ErrorState onRetry={() => lineupsQuery.refetch()} />;
  if (featured.length === 0) {
    return <EmptyState title={t("predict.awaitLineupTitle")} hint={t("predict.awaitLineupHint")} />;
  }

  const cells = Array.from({ length: GRID_ROWS * GRID_COLS }, (_, i) => ({
    gridX: i % GRID_COLS,
    gridY: Math.floor(i / GRID_COLS),
  }));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {/* Player */}
      <label className="block">
        <span className="mb-1 block label text-ink-3">{t("watch.pick")}</span>
        <select
          value={playerId ?? ""}
          onChange={(e) => setPlayerId(e.target.value)}
          className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-card border border-line bg-elevated px-3 text-sm font-bold text-ink"
        >
          <option value="">â€”</option>
          {featured.map((p) => (
            <option key={p.player_id} value={p.player_id}>
              {localize(p.full_name)}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </label>

      {/* Grid: attacking end at the top, matches the server's row-0 convention.
          It is drawn on a turf plate with white markings â€” goal line, penalty
          area, six-yard box, halfway line â€” so a cell reads as a place on the
          pitch rather than a keypad key. */}
      <div dir="ltr">
        <p className="mb-1 text-center label text-ink-3">â†‘ {t("shot.attackingEnd")}</p>
        <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-pitch/20 p-2">
          <div
            className="relative grid gap-0.5"
            style={{ gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${GRID_COLS}, 1fr)` }}
            role="grid"
            aria-label={t("shot.cellLabel", { row: 1, col: 1 })}
          >
            {cells.map(({ gridX, gridY }) => {
              const pressed = cell?.gridX === gridX && cell?.gridY === gridY;
              return (
                <button
                  key={`${gridX}-${gridY}`}
                  type="button"
                  role="gridcell"
                  aria-pressed={pressed}
                  aria-label={`row ${gridY + 1}, column ${gridX + 1}`}
                  onClick={() => {
                    selectionHaptic();
                    setCell({ gridX, gridY });
                  }}
                  className="plot-cell relative aspect-[4/3] rounded-[4px]"
                >
                  {pressed ? (
                    <>
                      {/* Signature animation 4: pin drop with overshoot + ripple ring. */}
                      <span
                        key={`${gridX}-${gridY}`}
                        className="pin-drop absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center"
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      >
                        <span className="block h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-volt" />
                      </span>
                      <span
                        className="pin-ripple pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 rounded-[4px] border border-volt"
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      />
                    </>
                  ) : null}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          {/* Markings, painted over the cells. Geometry mirrors the server's
              field coords: the box starts 18% along the length and spans the
              middle 57% of the width (shotZones.ts). Decorative and
              pointer-transparent, so every cell stays tappable. */}
          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-2" aria-hidden="true">
            <span className="absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-px bg-white/40" />
            <span className="absolute top-0 left-1/2 h-[6%] w-[24%] -translate-x-1/2 border-x border-b border-white/30 bg-white/10" />
            <span className="absolute top-0 left-1/2 h-[18%] w-[57%] -translate-x-1/2 border-x border-b border-white/25" />
            <span className="absolute top-0 left-1/2 h-[7%] w-[28%] -translate-x-1/2 border-x border-b border-white/20" />
            <span className="absolute top-[13%] left-1/2 h-1 w-1 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-full bg-white/50" />
            <span className="absolute inset-x-0 top-1/2 h-px bg-white/15" />
            <span className="absolute top-1/2 left-1/2 aspect-square h-[24%] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full border border-white/15" />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-x-2 gap-y-0.5">
        {ZONE_KEYS.map((key) => (
          <span key={key} className="text-xs text-ink-3">
            {t(key as never)}
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="text-xs text-ink-3">{t("predict.shotNewHint")}</span>
        {cell ? (
          <Chip tone="volt">{t(ZONE_LABEL_KEY[cellZone(cell.gridX, cell.gridY)] as never)}</Chip>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <Button
        className="w-full"
        disabled={!playerId || !cell || submitting}
        onClick={() => playerId && cell && onSubmit({ playerId, grid: cell })}
      >
        {submitting ? t("common.loading") : t("common.save")}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\panels\SubBoard.tsx =====

import { useState } from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api } from "../../lib/api";
import { useI18n } from "../../i18n/context";
import { haptic, selectionHaptic } from "../../lib/telegram";
import { cx } from "../../lib/cx";
import { Button, Chip, EmptyState, ErrorState, Num } from "../ui";
import { PanelSkeleton } from "../Skeletons";

/**
 * Sub Predictor editor â€” the whole expected sub board in one submission
 * (concept v2): each row is one predicted pair (off -> on), editable until
 * half-time. Scoring: +1 correct off, +1 more for the correct replacement.
 */
export function SubBoard({
  matchId,
  live,
  initial,
  onSubmit,
  submitting,
}: {
  matchId: string;
  live: boolean;
  initial: unknown;
  onSubmit: (payload: { subs: Array<{ offId: string; onId: string }> }) => void;
  submitting: boolean;
}) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["lineups", matchId],
    queryFn: () => api.getLineups(matchId),
  });

  const init = (initial ?? null) as { subs?: Array<{ offId: string; onId: string }> } | null;
  const [pairs, setPairs] = useState<Array<{ offId: string; onId: string }>>(init?.subs ?? []);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<{ offId: string | null; onId: string | null }>({
    offId: null,
    onId: null,
  });

  if (query.isLoading) return <PanelSkeleton height={160} />;
  if (query.isError) return <ErrorState onRetry={() => query.refetch()} />;

  const players = query.data?.players ?? [];
  const starters = players.filter((p) => p.started === 1);
  const bench = players.filter((p) => p.started !== 1);
  const legal = players; // both directions draw from the full squad list

  if (players.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="py-4">
        <EmptyState title={t("lineups.loadingHint")} hint={t("lineups.emptyHint")} />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const draftComplete = draft.offId && draft.onId && draft.offId !== draft.onId;
  const addPair = () => {
    if (!draft.offId || !draft.onId || draft.offId === draft.onId) return;
    if (pairs.length >= 5 || pairs.some((p) => p.offId === draft.offId)) return;
    selectionHaptic();
    setPairs((prev) => [...prev, { offId: draft.offId!, onId: draft.onId! }]);
    setDraft({ offId: null, onId: null });
  };

  const nameOf = (playerId: string) => {
    const p = legal.find((row) => row.player_id === playerId);
    return p ? localize(p.full_name) : `#${playerId}`;
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {live ? <Chip tone="live">{t("sub.windowLive")}</Chip> : null}

      {/* Current board */}
      <div>
        <p className="mb-1.5 label text-ink-2">{t("sub.boardTitle")}</p>
        {pairs.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="rounded-card border border-dashed border-line px-3 py-3 text-center text-xs text-ink-3">
            {t("sub.emptyBoard")}
          </p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="space-y-1.5">
            {pairs.map((pair) => (
              <li
                key={pair.offId}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-2.5"
              >
                <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-xs font-bold text-ink">
                  {nameOf(pair.offId)}
                </span>
                <span className="flex-none text-xs text-ink-3">â†’</span>
                <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-xs font-bold text-volt">
                  {nameOf(pair.onId)}
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  aria-label={t("sub.removePair")}
                  onClick={() => setPairs((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.offId !== pair.offId))}
                  className="flex-none text-ink-3 transition-colors hover:text-danger"
                >
                  âœ•
                </button>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Draft a pair */}
      <div className="rounded-card border border-line p-3">
        <PlayerSelect
          label={t("sub.pairOff")}
          players={starters.length > 0 ? starters : legal}
          value={draft.offId}
          onChange={(id) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, offId: id }))}
        />
        <div className="mt-2">
          <PlayerSelect
            label={t("sub.pairOn")}
            players={bench.length > 0 ? bench : legal}
            value={draft.onId}
            onChange={(id) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, onId: id }))}
          />
        </div>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          className="mt-3 w-full text-xs"
          disabled={!draftComplete || pairs.length >= 5}
          onClick={addPair}
        >
          + {t("sub.addPair")}
        </Button>
      </div>

      <Button
        className="w-full"
        disabled={pairs.length === 0 || submitting}
        onClick={() => onSubmit({ subs: pairs })}
      >
        {submitting ? t("common.loading") : t("sub.saveBoard")}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

function PlayerSelect({
  label,
  players,
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  label: string;
  players: Array<{ player_id: string; full_name: string; jersey_number: number | null }>;
  value: string | null;
  onChange: (id: string) => void;
}) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  return (
    <label className="block">
      <span className="mb-1 block label text-ink-3">{label}</span>
      <select
        value={value ?? ""}
        onChange={(e) => {
          haptic("light");
          onChange(e.target.value);
        }}
        className={cx(
          "min-h-11 w-full rounded-card border border-line bg-elevated px-3 text-sm font-bold text-ink",
          "focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-volt",
        )}
      >
        <option value="">â€”</option>
        {players.map((p) => (
          <option key={p.player_id} value={p.player_id}>
            {p.jersey_number ? <Num>{p.jersey_number}</Num> : null} {localize(p.full_name)}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
      <span className="sr-only">{t("sub.pickOff")}</span>
    </label>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\AppShell.tsx =====

import { Suspense, useEffect, useRef, useLayoutEffect } from "react";
import { Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { initTelegram } from "../lib/telegram";
import { useBackNavigation } from "../hooks/useBackNavigation";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { LanguageSync } from "./LanguageSync";
import { RowSkeleton } from "./Skeletons";
import { TabBar } from "./TabBar";

/**
 * Mobile-first shell: header + scrollable page + persistent bottom tabs.
 * Screens render their own <TopBar>, so the shell stays a layout concern only.
 *
 * The keyed wrapper replays the 180ms page-in on every route change, so main
 * navigation feels intentional (fade + small rise) rather than an abrupt swap.
 * It collapses to a hard cut under prefers-reduced-motion via the global rule.
 *
 * Lazy routes suspend here, around the Outlet, so the tab bar and skip link
 * stay put while a page chunk loads instead of flashing out of the tree.
 */
export function AppShell() {
  const { pathname } = useLocation();
  const { t } = useI18n();

  useEffect(() => {
    initTelegram();
  }, []);

  // Measure the bottom tab bar height dynamically and set the CSS variable.
  const tabBarRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    // Measure synchronously on mount so --bottom-nav-h is set before first paint.
    if (tabBarRef.current) {
      const h = tabBarRef.current.offsetHeight;
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--bottom-nav-h', h + 'px');
    }
    if (!tabBarRef.current) return;
    const observer = new ResizeObserver((entries) => {
      const height = entries[0].borderBoxSize?.[0]?.blockSize ?? entries[0].contentRect.height;
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--bottom-nav-h', height + 'px');
    });
    observer.observe(tabBarRef.current);
    // Also listen for orientation / window resize changes.
    const handleResize = () => {
      if (tabBarRef.current) observer.observe(tabBarRef.current);
    };
    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);
    window.addEventListener('orientationchange', handleResize);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);
      window.removeEventListener('orientationchange', handleResize);
      observer.disconnect();
    };
  }, [tabBarRef]);

  // Android's hardware back inside Telegram: pop in-app history on detail
  // routes instead of closing the mini app.
  useBackNavigation();

  // Route change should start at the top, like a native app would.
  useEffect(() => {
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "auto" });
  }, [pathname]);

  return (
    <div className="floodlight flex min-h-[100svh] flex-col">
      {/* Renders nothing; keeps the server's copy of the UI language in step so
          bot pushes match what the user reads. */}
      <LanguageSync />
      {/* Keyboard and screen-reader users reach the page, not the tab strip,
          first. Hidden until focused. */}
      <a
        href="#main-content"
        className="sr-only focus:not-sr-only focus:absolute focus:left-3 focus:top-3 focus:z-50 focus:rounded-card focus:border focus:border-volt focus:bg-base focus:px-3 focus:py-2 focus:text-xs focus:font-bold focus:text-ink"
      >
        {t("common.skipToContent")}
      </a>
      {/* key={pathname} remounts the wrapper per route, replaying the animation.
          The bottom padding reserves space for the sticky TabBar (~56px); the ad
          is inline now, so the last match card / league row only has to clear
          the tab bar itself. */}
      <main
        id="main-content"
        tabIndex={-1}
        key={pathname}
        className="page-in mx-auto w-full max-w-[var(--app-max-width,768px)] flex-1 px-[var(--space-page-x,16px)] outline-none"
        // The sticky tab bar height is measured dynamically via ResizeObserver
        // and set as --bottom-nav-h on the root element.
        style={{
          paddingBottom: "calc(var(--bottom-nav-h, 60px) + 16px)",
        }}
      >
        <Suspense
          fallback={
            <div className="mt-4" aria-busy="true">
              <RowSkeleton rows={5} />
            </div>
          }
        >
          <Outlet />
        </Suspense>
      </main>
      <TabBar ref={tabBarRef} />
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\BottomSheet.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";
import { CloseIcon } from "./icons";

/**
 * Bottom sheet â€” the sheet in the design system's `--bg-elevated` treatment.
 * Slides up, drags down to dismiss, locks background scroll, closes on Escape.
 */
export function BottomSheet({
  open,
  onClose,
  title,
  subtitle,
  children,
  footer,
}: {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
  footer?: ReactNode;
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const [dragY, setDragY] = useState(0);
  // Enter/exit phase: keep the dialog mounted through the ~150ms leave, so it
  // animates out instead of vanishing. Derived from `open` during render (the
  // React-endorsed "adjust state when a prop changes" pattern), so the commit
  // that flips `open` to false already knows it should play the exit.
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<"closed" | "open" | "closing">(open ? "open" : "closed");
  if (open && phase !== "open") setPhase("open");
  if (!open && phase === "open") setPhase("closing");
  const dragStart = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const sheetRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  // Whatever had focus before the sheet opened gets it back on close.
  const restoreRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    setDragY(0);
    restoreRef.current = document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement ? document.activeElement : null;
    // Move focus into the dialog so keyboard and AT users are in the sheet,
    // not behind it.
    sheetRef.current?.focus();

    const FOCUSABLE =
      'a[href], button:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])';
    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        onClose();
        return;
      }
      // Trap Tab inside the sheet: the page behind it is inert to the keyboard.
      if (event.key !== "Tab") return;
      const root = sheetRef.current;
      if (!root) return;
      const items = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE)).filter(
        (el) => el.offsetParent !== null,
      );
      const first = items[0];
      const last = items[items.length - 1];
      if (!first || !last) {
        event.preventDefault();
        root.focus();
        return;
      }
      const active = document.activeElement;
      if (event.shiftKey && (active === first || active === root)) {
        event.preventDefault();
        last.focus();
      } else if (!event.shiftKey && active === last) {
        event.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    const previous = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
      document.body.style.overflow = previous;
      const restore = restoreRef.current;
      if (restore && document.contains(restore)) restore.focus();
    };
  }, [open, onClose]);

  if (phase === "closed") return null;
  const closing = phase === "closing";

  return createPortal(
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end justify-center">
      <div
        className={cx("absolute inset-0 bg-black/65", closing ? "backdrop-fade-out" : "backdrop-fade")}
        onClick={onClose}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />

      <div
        ref={sheetRef}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label={title}
        tabIndex={-1}
        onAnimationEnd={(event) => {
          // Only the sheet's own leave animation ends the mount; child
          // animations bubble (goal sweeps, pulses) and must not unmount it.
          if (closing && event.target === event.currentTarget) setPhase("closed");
        }}
        className={cx(
          "relative w-full max-w-[560px] rounded-t-[20px] border border-line bg-elevated outline-none",
          closing ? "sheet-out" : "sheet-in",
        )}
        style={{
          transform: dragY ? `translateY(${dragY}px)` : undefined,
          paddingBottom: "max(var(--safe-bottom, env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px)), 16px)",
        }}
      >
        {/* Drag handle: drag down past 90px to dismiss. */}
        <div
          className="flex cursor-grab touch-none justify-center pt-3 pb-1 active:cursor-grabbing"
          onPointerDown={(event) => {
            dragStart.current = event.clientY;
            event.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
          }}
          onPointerMove={(event) => {
            if (dragStart.current === null) return;
            setDragY(Math.max(0, event.clientY - dragStart.current));
          }}
          onPointerUp={() => {
            if (dragY > 90) onClose();
            dragStart.current = null;
            setDragY(0);
          }}
        >
          <span className="h-1 w-10 rounded-full bg-line" aria-hidden="true" />
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 border-b border-line px-4 pb-3">
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <h2 className="truncate text-md font-bold text-ink">{title}</h2>
            {subtitle ? <p className="truncate text-xs text-ink-2">{subtitle}</p> : null}
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label={t("common.close")}
            className="grid h-11 w-11 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full border border-line text-ink-2 transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-surface hover:text-ink"
          >
            <CloseIcon className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="max-h-[68svh] overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain px-4 py-3">{children}</div>

        {footer ? <div className="border-t border-line px-4 pt-3">{footer}</div> : null}
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body,
  );
}

/** Confirm/cancel row used by most sheets. */
export function SheetActions({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return <div className={cx("flex gap-2")}>{children}</div>;
}

===== D:\football\src\components\ClubCard.tsx =====

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { useMemo } from "react";
import { useMatches } from "../hooks/useApi";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { formatKickoff } from "../lib/format";
import type { Team } from "../types";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";
import { LiveDot, Num, TeamBadge } from "./ui";
import { CloseIcon, StarFilledIcon } from "./icons";
import { clubContext, opponentOf } from "../lib/clubContext";

/**
 * Compact club tile with its fixture state â€” the shared atom behind the home
 * page "My teams" strip and the picked-clubs row on the My Teams screen.
 * Tapping it opens the club's live/next match; `onRemove` renders the Ã— affordance;
 * `onMakeAnchor` renders the â˜… affordance that sets the club as the favorite.
 */
export function ClubFixtureCard({
  team,
  anchored = false,
  onMakeAnchor,
  onRemove,
}: {
  team: Team;
  anchored?: boolean;
  onMakeAnchor?: () => void;
  onRemove?: () => void;
}) {
  const { t, lang, localize } = useI18n();
  const locale = lang === "fa" ? "fa-IR" : undefined;
  const matchesQuery = useMatches();
  const all = useMemo(() => matchesQuery.data ?? [], [matchesQuery.data]);
  const { match, live, next, last } = clubContext(team.id, all);
  const name = localize(team.shortName);

  let status: ReactNode;
  if (live) {
    const goalsFor = live.home.id === team.id ? live.homeScore : live.awayScore;
    const goalsAgainst = live.home.id === team.id ? live.awayScore : live.homeScore;
    status = (
      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-live">
        <LiveDot />
        <Num>
          {goalsFor}-{goalsAgainst}
        </Num>
        <span className="text-xs font-normal text-ink-3">{live.minute}</span>
      </span>
    );
  } else if (next) {
    status = (
      <span className="flex w-full flex-col items-center gap-0.5 text-xs leading-tight text-ink-3">
        <span className="w-full truncate">
          {t("myTeams.vsOpp", { opp: localize(opponentOf(team.id, next).shortName) })}
        </span>
        <span>{formatKickoff(new Date(next.kickoffAt), locale)}</span>
      </span>
    );
  } else if (last) {
    status = (
      <span className="w-full truncate text-xs text-ink-3">
        <Num>{last.homeScore}</Num>-<Num>{last.awayScore}</Num>
      </span>
    );
  } else {
    status = <span className="text-xs text-ink-3">{t("matches.noMatch")}</span>;
  }

  const body = (
    <div
      className={cx(
        "flex w-[92px] flex-col items-center gap-1 rounded-card border bg-surface p-2.5 text-center",
        anchored ? "border-gold" : "border-line",
      )}
    >
      <TeamBadge team={team} size="md" />
      <span className="w-full truncate text-xs font-bold text-ink">{name}</span>
      {status}
      {anchored ? (
        <span className="text-gold">
          <StarFilledIcon className="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
        </span>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <li className="relative flex-none">
      {match ? (
        <Link
          to={`/match/${match.id}`}
          className="block rounded-card transition-[background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated active:scale-[0.98]"
        >
          {body}
        </Link>
      ) : (
        body
      )}
      {onMakeAnchor ? (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onMakeAnchor}
          aria-label={t("onboarding.makeFavorite")}
          aria-pressed={anchored}
          className="absolute -top-1 -start-1 grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-full"
        >
          <span
            className={cx(
              "grid h-5 w-5 place-items-center rounded-full border bg-elevated transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)]",
              // Keyed on `anchored`: promotion remounts the dot so the star
              // replays star-pop instead of the fill just blinking on.
              anchored ? "star-pop border-gold text-gold" : "border-line text-ink-3",
            )}
            key={anchored ? "on" : "off"}
            aria-hidden="true"
          >
            <StarFilledIcon className="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
          </span>
        </button>
      ) : null}
      {onRemove ? (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onRemove}
          aria-label={t("myTeams.removed", { team: name })}
          className="absolute -top-1 -end-1 grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-full"
        >
          <span
            className="grid h-5 w-5 place-items-center rounded-full border border-line bg-elevated text-ink-2 transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:border-danger hover:text-danger"
            aria-hidden="true"
          >
            <CloseIcon className="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
          </span>
        </button>
      ) : null}
    </li>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\Confetti.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";

interface Particle {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  vx: number;
  vy: number;
  size: number;
  rot: number;
  spin: number;
  color: string;
  life: number;
}

// Tokens only (tokens.css): volt, gold, turf, ink. No off-palette confetti.
const COLORS = ["#1FBFA0", "#F2B705", "#23A066", "#EDF7F3"];

/**
 * Signature animation 1 (second half): a 1.2s confetti burst on a goal.
 * Canvas rather than DOM nodes so a few hundred particles stay GPU-cheap on the
 * low-end Android devices Telegram's webview runs on.
 *
 * Bump `trigger` to fire. Reduced-motion users get nothing.
 */
export function ConfettiBurst({ trigger, particleCount = 140 }: { trigger: number; particleCount?: number }) {
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);
  const frameRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!trigger) return;
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    if (window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches) return;

    const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    const width = canvas.clientWidth;
    const height = canvas.clientHeight;
    canvas.width = width * dpr;
    canvas.height = height * dpr;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    if (!ctx) return;
    ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);

    const originX = width / 2;
    const originY = height * 0.32;

    const particles: Particle[] = Array.from({ length: particleCount }, () => {
      const angle = -Math.PI / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2.2;
      const speed = 5 + Math.random() * 9;
      return {
        x: originX + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 60,
        y: originY,
        vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
        vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
        size: 3 + Math.random() * 5,
        rot: Math.random() * Math.PI,
        spin: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4,
        color: COLORS[Math.floor(Math.random() * COLORS.length)],
        life: 1,
      };
    });

    const started = performance.now();
    let cancelled = false;

    const tick = (now: number) => {
      if (cancelled) return;
      const elapsed = now - started;
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

      for (const particle of particles) {
        particle.vy += 0.22;
        particle.vx *= 0.992;
        particle.vy *= 0.992;
        particle.x += particle.vx;
        particle.y += particle.vy;
        particle.rot += particle.spin;
        particle.life = Math.max(0, 1 - elapsed / 1200);

        ctx.save();
        ctx.globalAlpha = particle.life;
        ctx.translate(particle.x, particle.y);
        ctx.rotate(particle.rot);
        ctx.fillStyle = particle.color;
        ctx.fillRect(-particle.size / 2, -particle.size / 2, particle.size, particle.size * 1.6);
        ctx.restore();
      }

      if (elapsed < 1200) {
        frameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      } else {
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
        frameRef.current = null;
      }
    };

    frameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      if (frameRef.current !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(frameRef.current);
    };
  }, [trigger, particleCount]);

  return (
    <canvas
      ref={canvasRef}
      className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-0 z-[55] h-full w-full"
      aria-hidden="true"
    />
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\Countdown.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";

/**
 * Signature animation 7: deadline heat.
 * Under an hour it turns danger red; in the last ten seconds every tick pulses.
 */
function useCountdown(deadlineAt: string) {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now());

  useEffect(() => {
    const id = window.setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, []);

  const remainingMs = Math.max(0, new Date(deadlineAt).getTime() - now);
  const totalSeconds = Math.floor(remainingMs / 1000);
  const expired = remainingMs <= 0;

  return {
    expired,
    totalSeconds,
    hours: Math.floor(totalSeconds / 3600),
    minutes: Math.floor((totalSeconds % 3600) / 60),
    seconds: totalSeconds % 60,
    /** normal â†’ gold, under an hour â†’ danger, last ten seconds â†’ panic pulse. */
    heat: totalSeconds <= 10 ? "panic" : totalSeconds < 3600 ? "danger" : "normal",
  } as const;
}

const pad = (value: number) => String(value).padStart(2, "0");

export function Countdown({
  deadlineAt,
  size = "lg",
  onExpire,
}: {
  deadlineAt: string;
  size?: "sm" | "lg";
  onExpire?: () => void;
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const { expired, hours, minutes, seconds, heat, totalSeconds } = useCountdown(deadlineAt);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (expired) onExpire?.();
  }, [expired, onExpire]);

  const color = heat === "normal" ? "text-ink" : heat === "danger" ? "text-danger" : "text-danger";

  if (expired) {
    return (
      <span className={cx("font-bold text-danger", size === "lg" ? "text-lg" : "text-sm")}>
        {t("deadline.locked")}
      </span>
    );
  }

  return (
    <span
      // Remounting per second in panic heat is what animates the pulse.
      key={heat === "panic" ? totalSeconds : "steady"}
      className={cx(
        "num-display tabular-nums inline-flex items-baseline transition-colors duration-[var(--t-slow)]",
        color,
        size === "lg" ? "text-lg" : "text-md",
        heat === "panic" && "tick-panic",
      )}
    >
      {hours > 0 ? `${pad(hours)}:` : ""}
      {pad(minutes)}:{pad(seconds)}
    </span>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\DateStrip.tsx =====

import { useMemo } from "react";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";

/**
 * Catalog keys, not display labels: `dates.day.*` carries the short day name in
 * every language (Farsi ships single letters), so the lookup is the same in
 * both â€” building a Farsi key here instead rendered `dates.day.Ø¬` on the chip.
 */
const DAY_NAMES = ["sun", "mon", "tue", "wed", "thu", "fri", "sat"] as const;

interface DateStripProps {
  selected: Date;
  onSelect: (date: Date) => void;
  /** Day keys (same format as `dayKey`) where at least one FOLLOWED club plays
   *  â€” the chip carries a teal dot so the matchdays the user actually cares
   *  about stand out; a day full of unrelated fixtures stays quiet.
   *  Absent = no dots (call sites without the data unchanged). */
  daysWithFavorites?: Set<string>;
}

const startOfDay = (d: Date) => {
  const x = new Date(d);
  x.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  return x;
};

/**
 * Local calendar-day key â€” never `toISOString()`, which is UTC and files a
 * local midnight east of UTC under the previous day. Exported so call sites
 * that precompute per-day facts (the fixture dots) build keys that are
 * guaranteed to match the chips' own keys.
 */
export const dayKey = (d: Date) => `${d.getFullYear()}-${d.getMonth()}-${d.getDate()}`;

/** Chips per row. One row, never a carousel: seven is what fits at 360px. */
const STRIP_DAYS = 7;
/** Default window: yesterday â€¦ today+5, so yesterday's results AND today show. */
const DEFAULT_START = -1;
/** Slot (0-based) a far-picked day lands in when the window re-centres: the 4th. */
const SELECTED_SLOT = 3;

/**
 * Whole local calendar days from `b` to `a`. Built from the local Y/M/D
 * triplets through UTC, so a DST change (a 23 or 25 hour local day) never
 * shifts the difference by one and `toISOString()` is never involved.
 */
const dayDiff = (a: Date, b: Date) =>
  Math.round(
    (Date.UTC(a.getFullYear(), a.getMonth(), a.getDate()) -
      Date.UTC(b.getFullYear(), b.getMonth(), b.getDate())) /
      86_400_000,
  );

/**
 * Offset from today of the window's first chip.
 *
 * The window only moves when it has to: a day inside the default week keeps the
 * strip where it is (tapping tomorrow must not shuffle the row under the
 * thumb). A day picked from the calendar sheet â€” or reached past the week â€”
 * re-centres so that day is the 4th chip, which keeps three earlier days
 * (results) and three later ones (fixtures) reachable by one tap.
 */
function windowStart(selected: Date, today: Date): number {
  const offset = dayDiff(selected, today);
  if (offset >= DEFAULT_START && offset <= DEFAULT_START + STRIP_DAYS - 1) {
    return DEFAULT_START;
  }
  return offset - SELECTED_SLOT;
}

/**
 * Seven local calendar days starting at `start` (a day offset from today).
 * `setDate` (not millisecond arithmetic) because a DST change makes one local
 * day 23 or 25 hours long.
 */
function buildDays(today: Date, start: number): Date[] {
  return Array.from({ length: STRIP_DAYS }, (_, index) => {
    const day = new Date(today);
    day.setDate(today.getDate() + start + index);
    return day;
  });
}

/**
 * The matchday strip: exactly seven day chips across the full content width,
 * no horizontal scrolling and nothing clipped.
 *
 * The calendar lives in the header now (see `Matches`) â€” a 44px icon button
 * wedged beside 56px chips made the row read as eight uneven targets.
 */
export function DateStrip({ selected, onSelect, daysWithFavorites }: DateStripProps) {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n();
  const locale = lang === "fa" ? "fa-IR" : undefined;
  // Localized day number: Farsi reads Û±Û´ not 14.
  const digits = useMemo(() => new Intl.NumberFormat(locale, { useGrouping: false }), [locale]);
  // The chip's accessible name is the full date. `calendar: "gregory"` keeps
  // fa-IR on the same day number the chip prints (fa-IR's default Persian
  // calendar would name a different day than the digits show).
  const fullDate = useMemo(
    () =>
      new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, {
        weekday: "long",
        day: "numeric",
        month: "long",
        calendar: "gregory",
      }),
    [locale],
  );

  // Local midnight as a number: it changes once a day (the feed re-renders
  // every minute), so a strip left open past midnight rolls its window
  // forward instead of pointing at yesterday.
  const todayStamp = startOfDay(new Date()).getTime();
  const todayKey = dayKey(new Date(todayStamp));
  const selectedKey = dayKey(selected);
  // How far the selected day sits from today is what moves the window, and it
  // is a plain number â€” so both memos key on numbers instead of Date
  // identities: a pick inside the current week keeps the same offset and the
  // row where it is, and midnight moves the stamp.
  const windowOffset = useMemo(
    () => windowStart(selected, new Date(todayStamp)),
    [selected, todayStamp],
  );
  const days = useMemo(
    () => buildDays(new Date(todayStamp), windowOffset),
    [todayStamp, windowOffset],
  );

  return (
    // A group of pressable day buttons, not a tablist: they are toggles with an
    // explicit pressed state, and role=tab would promise arrow-key semantics
    // this row does not implement. No overflow container: each chip's invisible
    // hit band (after:-inset-x-1) reaches into the 6px gaps instead.
    //
    // -inset-x-1, not -inset-x-0.5: an absolutely positioned ::after is offset
    // from the PADDING box, so the 1px border already eats one of the 2px â€” a
    // half-step band left only 1px outside the visible edge (43.7px tappable at
    // 360). One step reaches 3px out on each side: 47.7px at 360, and the
    // neighbouring band still stops 3px short of it in the 6px gap.
    // gap-1 (4px), not gap-1.5: 7 chips at a 320px viewport give only ~36px
    // per chip; the tighter gap buys back 12px so the smallest screens keep
    // all seven days (never a second row, never a dropped day) while every
    // chip stays â‰¥56px tall â€” well over the 40px tap floor.
    <div role="group" aria-label={t("dateStrip.label")} className="flex items-stretch gap-1">
      {days.map((day) => {
        const key = dayKey(day);
        const isToday = key === todayKey;
        const isSelected = key === selectedKey;
        const hasFavorites = daysWithFavorites?.has(key) ?? false;
        return (
          <button
            key={key}
            type="button"
            aria-pressed={isSelected}
            // "Today" keeps its own marker even when another day is selected,
            // so the user always knows where they are in the week.
            aria-current={isToday ? "date" : undefined}
            aria-label={fullDate.format(day)}
            onClick={() => onSelect(day)}
            className={`relative flex h-14 min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 rounded-xl border text-center transition-all duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.96] after:absolute after:-inset-x-1 after:inset-y-0 after:content-[''] ${
              isSelected
                ? "border-volt bg-volt text-on-volt active:bg-volt/80"
                : isToday
                  ? "border-volt bg-transparent text-volt active:bg-volt/15"
                  : "border-line bg-transparent text-ink-3 active:bg-line/20"
            }`}
          >
            {/* Both labels truncate instead of overflowing: at 320px a chip
                is ~38px wide, and a large user font scale must never push a
                glyph into the neighbour chip. */}
            <span className="max-w-full truncate text-xs font-bold leading-[14px]">
              {t(`dates.day.${DAY_NAMES[day.getDay()]}` as never)}
            </span>
            <span className="max-w-full truncate text-md font-extrabold leading-5">{digits.format(day.getDate())}</span>
            {isToday ? (
              <span
                aria-hidden="true"
                className={`h-1 w-1 rounded-full ${isSelected ? "bg-on-volt" : "bg-volt"}`}
              />
            ) : hasFavorites && !isSelected ? (
              // Only days where a FOLLOWED club plays get the teal dot so the
              // week reads at a glance; a day full of fixtures the user doesn't
              // follow stays quiet. The selected chip needs no marker (the
              // volt fill IS the marker).
              <span aria-hidden="true" className="h-1 w-1 rounded-full bg-volt" />
            ) : null}
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\ErrorBoundary.tsx =====

import { Component, type ErrorInfo, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { reportClientError } from "../lib/errorReporting";

/**
 * Render-crash insurance. Without this, one throwing component white-screens
 * the whole Mini App; with it, the user gets an honest "something broke" card
 * and a one-tap retry instead of a dead webview.
 *
 * The fallback copy is HARDCODED ENGLISH on purpose. This boundary sits
 * OUTSIDE <I18nProvider> (see main.tsx) and React renders a boundary's fallback
 * at the boundary's own position â€” so the provider is below the fallback, never
 * above it. Reading t() here threw "useI18n must be used inside <I18nProvider>"
 * and the recovery card became the white screen it exists to prevent. English
 * copy on a crashed screen beats a dead webview in Persian; localizing this
 * again means moving the provider ABOVE the boundary, not calling the hook here.
 */
interface Props {
  children: ReactNode;
}

interface State {
  error: Error | null;
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<Props, State> {
  state: State = { error: null };

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): State {
    return { error };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: ErrorInfo): void {
    console.error("[ui] render crash:", error, info.componentStack);
    // Forward to the server so the crash is reviewable after the fact, not
    // lost when the tab closes. Best-effort: see lib/errorReporting.ts.
    reportClientError({
      message: error.message,
      stack: info.componentStack ?? error.stack,
      url: window.location.href,
      source: "react-boundary",
    });
  }

  private reset = () => {
    this.setState({ error: null });
  };

  render() {
    if (!this.state.error) return this.props.children;
    return <FallbackCard onRetry={this.reset} />;
  }
}

/**
 * Exported rather than module-private so this file's export surface is
 * components only: React Fast Refresh keys its HMR boundary off that, and
 * eslint-plugin-react-refresh does not recognise a class export (`ErrorBoundary`)
 * as a component â€” without this the file trips `only-export-components`.
 */
export function FallbackCard({ onRetry }: { onRetry: () => void }) {
  return (
    <div role="alert" className="grid min-h-[100svh] place-items-center px-6">
      <div className="w-full max-w-[380px] rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-5 text-center">
        <p className="text-lg" aria-hidden="true">
          âš½
        </p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-md font-bold text-ink">Something went wrong</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-ink-2">
          The screen hit an unexpected error. Retrying usually fixes it.
        </p>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onRetry}
          className="mt-4 min-h-11 w-full rounded-full bg-volt px-4 text-sm font-bold text-black transition-[filter] duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:brightness-95"
        >
          Try again
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import type { CSSProperties } from "react";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { IS_EMBED } from "../lib/embed";

/**
 * The feed's inline ad: a hosted iframe (A-ADS, adaptive size) inside a dark
 * rounded slot â€” deliberately capped at 60px with the overflow clipped, so a
 * tall third-party creative can never blow open the feed's rhythm, and the
 * slot's own surface stays on-token even while the iframe itself paints.
 * The small "ØªØ¨Ù„ÛŒØº" label keeps the monetization surface branded, not bare.
 */
export function InlineAd() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  // Share mode (?embed=1 inside /frame.html): clean screenshots, no ad iframe.
  if (IS_EMBED) return null;
  return (
    <div
      className="my-3 flex items-center gap-2 overflow-hidden rounded-card bg-elevated p-1.5"
      style={{ maxHeight: "60px" }}
    >
      {/* Labelling: the strap matches the app's other micro-labels, not an
          arbitrary pill, so the slot reads as branded furniture. */}
      <span
        aria-label={t("ads.label")}
        className="grid h-6 min-w-[30px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-md border border-line bg-surface px-1 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-wide text-ink-3"
      >
        {t("ads.label")}
      </span>
      <iframe
        data-aa="2458182"
        src="//acceptable.a-ads.com/2458182/?size=Adaptive"
        style={{
          border: "0",
          padding: "0",
          margin: "0",
          display: "block",
          width: "100%",
          height: "48px",
          minHeight: "0",
          overflow: "hidden",
          flex: 1,
          borderRadius: "8px",
          colorScheme: "dark",
        } as CSSProperties}
        title={t("ads.label")}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\LanguageSync.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { api } from "../lib/api";

/**
 * Reports this device's language to the server so the bot's pushes are written
 * in the language the user is actually reading (`server/messages.ts`, which
 * picks the copy per recipient from `users.language`).
 *
 * Mounted inside the authenticated shell, so there is always a user row to
 * write to. It is a "last device wins" sync on purpose: the language is a
 * per-device choice, so whichever device the user is reading right now is the
 * one the bot should speak to. A user who signs in on someone else's English
 * laptop flips their pushes to English until they next open the app in Persian,
 * which is the intended, self-healing behaviour.
 *
 * Failures are swallowed: a missed language sync must never surface as an error,
 * and clearing the sentinel means the next app open (or language change) retries
 * rather than looping.
 */
export function LanguageSync() {
  /** Last value we successfully told the server about, for this session. */
  const sent = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const { lang } = useI18n();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (sent.current === lang) return;
    sent.current = lang;
    void api.setLanguage(lang).catch(() => {
      sent.current = null;
    });
  }, [lang]);

  return null;
}

===== D:\football\src\components\LeagueGroup.tsx =====

import { memo, type CSSProperties } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import type { Match, Team } from "../types";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { usePrefetchMatch } from "../hooks/usePrefetch";
import { useCompetitionLabel } from "../hooks/useApi";
import { Num, TeamBadge } from "./ui";
import { StarFilledIcon } from "./icons";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";

/**
 * The Matchday feed, per the shell redesign: the day's matches stop repeating
 * the league name on every card and group into ONE rounded container per
 * competition â€” a header row (teal square dot, league name, count on the far
 * end) above match rows separated by hairline dividers. Accent stays reserved
 * for followed clubs, live matches and the selected day; the rows themselves
 * carry no per-match border, shadow or edge accent.
 */

/** "Û²Û²" under fa-IR, "22" under en â€” every count here is localized digits. */
const digitFormatter = (locale: string | undefined) =>
  new Intl.NumberFormat(locale ?? "en", { useGrouping: false });

/**
 * Kickoff clock only â€” the weekday deliberately left the cards. `hour12:false`
 * keeps the 24h clock in English instead of "03:00 PM", which blew the 44px
 * time column; the string renders LTR-isolated inside the RTL row.
 */
const clockFormatter = (locale: string | undefined) =>
  new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", hour12: false });

function LeagueHeader({ label, count }: { label: string; count: number }) {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n();
  return (
    // 30px header band: teal square dot, league name, count at the far end.
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-[7px]">
      <span aria-hidden="true" className="h-2 w-2 flex-none rounded-sm bg-volt" />
      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-[12px] font-bold text-ink-2">{label}</span>
      <span className="flex-none whitespace-nowrap text-[11px] leading-4 text-ink-3">
        <Num>{t(count === 1 ? "matches.matchCountOne" : "matches.matchCount", { count: digitFormatter(lang === "fa" ? "fa-IR" : "en").format(count) })}</Num>
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

/** One team line of a match row: 22px crest + localized short name (+ star). */
function TeamLine({
  team,
  followed,
}: {
  team: Team;
  followed: boolean;
}) {
  const { localize } = useI18n();
  return (
    // The middle column is the scan path, so flex-none only the crest and
    // star; the name truncates before anything else yields width.
    <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-[9px]">
      <span className="flex-none">
        <TeamBadge team={team} size="xs" />
      </span>
      <span className="min-w-0 truncate text-sm font-medium">{localize(team.shortName)}</span>
      <StarFilledIcon
        className={cx("h-3 w-3 flex-none text-volt", !followed && "hidden")}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />
    </div>
  );
}

type MatchRowProps = {
  match: Match;
  /** `isFavorite` from the page's favorites hook â€” the rows never open their
   *  own favorites query (one SELECT per row would n-query the endpoint). */
  isFavoriteTeam: (teamId: string) => boolean;
  /** Position within the WHOLE day's render, for the entrance stagger. */
  index: number;
};

/** Memoized: the live feed re-renders its page every tick; rows never change. */
export const MatchRow = memo(function MatchRow({
  match,
  isFavoriteTeam,
  index = 0,
}: MatchRowProps) {
  const { t, lang, localize } = useI18n();
  const prefetchMatch = usePrefetchMatch();
  const isHalftime = match.isHalftime === true;
  const isLive = match.status === "live";
  const isFinished = match.status === "finished";
  const hasScore = isLive || isHalftime || isFinished;
  const home = localize(match.home.shortName);
  const away = localize(match.away.shortName);
  const clock = clockFormatter(lang === "fa" ? "fa-IR" : "en").format(new Date(match.kickoffAt));
  // Live minute, Persian digits under fa: "Û´Û´â€²". The feed's `minute` is the
  // server's normalised clock ("44'"); only the numeric part is localized.
  const minuteNum = /\d+/.exec(match.minute ?? "")?.[0] ?? null;
  const minuteLabel = minuteNum ? digitFormatter(lang === "fa" ? "fa-IR" : "en").format(Number(minuteNum)) : null;

  // The accessible name carries the state: score + minute when in play, final
  // score when finished, kickoff time when upcoming.
  const status = isLive
    ? `${t("status.live")} ${minuteLabel ?? ""}`.trim()
    : isHalftime
      ? t("matches.groupHalftime")
      : isFinished
        ? t("status.finished")
        : clock;
  const label = hasScore
    ? `${home} ${match.homeScore ?? 0}-${match.awayScore ?? 0}, ${away} ${match.awayScore ?? 0}, ${status}`
    : `${home} ${t("common.versus")} ${away}, ${clock}`;

  const homeFollowed = isFavoriteTeam(match.home.id);
  const awayFollowed = isFavoriteTeam(match.away.id);

  return (
    <li
      className="list-in"
      style={{
        "--stagger": `${Math.min(index, 8) * 30}ms`,
        contentVisibility: "auto",
        containIntrinsicSize: "auto 72px",
      } as CSSProperties}
    >
      <Link
        to={`/match/${match.id}`}
        onPointerDown={() => prefetchMatch(match.id)}
        aria-label={label}
        dir={lang === "fa" ? "rtl" : "ltr"}
        className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-[9px] transition-[background-color] duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated active:bg-elevated focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-volt focus-visible:-outline-offset-2"
      >
        {/* TIME + STATUS column. Upcoming: kickoff clock. Live: the minute in
            the live coral, in place of the kickoff time. Halftime: the break
            label. Finished: "Ù¾Ø§ÛŒØ§Ù†" in muted ink. 44px floor, centred. */}
        <div dir="ltr" className="flex min-w-11 flex-none flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-center">
          {isLive ? (
            minuteLabel ? (
              <span className="whitespace-nowrap text-[15px] font-bold leading-5 text-live">
                <Num>{minuteLabel}â€²</Num>
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[15px] font-bold leading-5 text-live">
                <span className="live-dot" aria-hidden="true" />
              </span>
            )
          ) : isHalftime ? (
            <span className="flex-nowrap text-[10px] font-bold leading-4 text-gold">
              {t("matches.groupHalftime")}
            </span>
          ) : isFinished ? (
            <span className="text-[11px] font-bold leading-5 text-ink-3">{t("status.finished")}</span>
          ) : (
            <span className="whitespace-nowrap text-[15px] font-bold leading-5 text-ink">
              <Num>{clock}</Num>
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Middle column: home above, away below â€” the stacked-pairs layout.
            Persian names always win; English only where no name exists (the
            localize pass-through already guarantees that). */}
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-[7px]">
          <TeamLine team={match.home} followed={homeFollowed} />
          <TeamLine team={match.away} followed={awayFollowed} />
        </div>

        {/* SCORE column: bold, one number per team line, aligned with that
            team (the cells share the team line's 22px height and the same
            7px row gap). Blank for upcoming matches â€” an upcoming row only
            carries the kickoff clock. */}
        {hasScore ? (
          <div className="flex flex-none flex-col justify-end gap-[7px]">
            <span className="flex h-[22px] items-center">
              <Num className="text-[15px] font-extrabold text-ink">
                {match.homeScore ?? 0}
              </Num>
            </span>
            <span className="flex h-[22px] items-center">
              <Num className="text-[15px] font-extrabold text-ink">
                {match.awayScore ?? 0}
              </Num>
            </span>
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </Link>
    </li>
  );
});

type LeagueGroupProps = {
  /** Real competition slug of every match in `matches`. */
  league: string;
  /** The league's matches for the selected day, kickoff-sorted by the page. */
  matches: Match[];
  isFavoriteTeam: (teamId: string) => boolean;
  index: number;
};

export function LeagueGroup({ league, matches, isFavoriteTeam, index }: LeagueGroupProps) {
  // Competition from the MATCH, not the team (`home.league` is a big-five
  // alias that mislabels cups); the label hook dedupes its competition list
  // query across groups, so one row per league costs no extra request.
  const label = useCompetitionLabel(league);
  const first = matches[0];
  if (!first) return null;

  return (
    <section
      aria-label={label}
      className="list-in rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-surface border border-line"
      style={{ "--stagger": `${Math.min(index, 8) * 30}ms` } as CSSProperties}
    >
      <LeagueHeader label={label} count={matches.length} />
      {/* Rows under the header: 1px hairline dividers, no per-match borders. */}
      <ul className="divide-y divide-[#1d2b26]">
        {matches.map((match, rowIndex) => (
          <MatchRow key={match.id} match={match} isFavoriteTeam={isFavoriteTeam} index={index + rowIndex} />
        ))}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}
