===== D:\football\src\components\PickerTabs.tsx =====

/**
 * Club-picker tab strip: a light scrollable chip row (the same pattern as the
 * followed-league filter on Matches) instead of the boxed Segmented pill â€”
 * with one tab per catalog league plus the catch-alls, a boxed tablist reads
 * as a heavy gray slab that is always half-scrolled. Scrollable chips keep
 * every tab reachable and make the active one obvious.
 */
import { haptic } from "../lib/telegram";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";
import type { PickerGroup } from "../lib/pickerGroups";

export function PickerTabs({
  groups,
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  groups: PickerGroup[];
  value: string;
  onChange: (key: string) => void;
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  return (
    <div
      role="tablist"
      aria-label={t("picker.clubGroups")}
      className="-mx-3 mt-3 flex gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto px-3 pb-1"
    >
      {groups.map((group) => (
        <button
          key={group.value}
          role="tab"
          type="button"
          aria-selected={value === group.value}
          onClick={() => {
            if (value !== group.value) haptic("light");
            onChange(group.value);
          }}
          className={cx(
            "min-h-11 flex-none rounded-lg border px-3.5 text-xs font-bold transition-[color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.97]",
            value === group.value ? "border-volt text-volt" : "border-line text-ink-3 hover:text-ink-2",
          )}
        >
          {group.label}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\PullToRefresh.tsx =====

import { BallIcon } from "./icons";
import { THRESHOLD } from "../hooks/usePullToRefresh";

const TRAVEL = 56;
/** The progress ring in the indicator's 36px box. */
const RING_RADIUS = 16;
const RING_LENGTH = 2 * Math.PI * RING_RADIUS;

/**
 * The pull progress marker: a ball turning inside a volt ring that fills as the
 * gesture grows, so the drag reads as winding the feed up rather than as a
 * generic spinner. The disc also scales in with the pull.
 *
 * Transform- and stroke-dashoffset-only (no height animation): it slides in
 * under the sticky TopBar and turns volt once armed, so the drag costs a
 * composite layer rather than a layout pass on the low-end webviews we target.
 *
 * Decorative by design â€” the TopBar refresh button carries the same action for
 * anyone not dragging, so this stays out of the accessibility tree.
 */
export function PullIndicator({ pull }: { pull: number }) {
  if (pull <= 0) return null;
  const progress = Math.min(pull / THRESHOLD, 1);
  const armed = pull >= THRESHOLD;
  const tone = armed ? "var(--volt)" : "var(--text-muted)";
  return (
    <div
      aria-hidden="true"
      className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 top-1 z-10 flex justify-center"
      style={{
        transform: `translateY(${Math.round((progress - 1) * TRAVEL)}px)`,
        opacity: progress,
      }}
    >
      <span
        className="relative grid h-9 w-9 place-items-center rounded-full border bg-elevated"
        style={{
          borderColor: armed ? "var(--volt)" : "var(--border-subtle)",
          boxShadow: armed ? "0 0 14px -4px var(--volt)" : undefined,
          transform: `scale(${(0.86 + progress * 0.14).toFixed(3)})`,
        }}
      >
        {/* Progress ring: starts at twelve o'clock and draws clockwise as the
            pull approaches the threshold. */}
        <svg viewBox="0 0 36 36" className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full -rotate-90" aria-hidden="true">
          <circle
            cx="18"
            cy="18"
            r={RING_RADIUS}
            fill="none"
            stroke="var(--volt)"
            strokeWidth="2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeDasharray={RING_LENGTH}
            strokeDashoffset={RING_LENGTH * (1 - progress)}
          />
        </svg>
        <BallIcon
          className="h-4 w-4"
          strokeWidth={2}
          style={{ color: tone, transform: `rotate(${Math.round(progress * 360)}deg)` }}
        />
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\RatingRow.tsx =====

import type { CSSProperties } from "react";
import type { PlayerRating, Team } from "../types";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { haptic, selectionHaptic } from "../lib/telegram";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";
import { Chip, Num, TeamBadge } from "./ui";
import { ReportButton } from "./ReportButton";

/**
 * The differentiator in one row: the stat score on one side, the crowd's
 * eye test on the other, deliberately never merged into a single number.
 */
export function RatingRow({
  rating,
  team,
  onRate,
  onTap,
  index = 0,
}: {
  rating: PlayerRating;
  team: Team;
  onRate?: (playerId: string, value: number) => void;
  /** When set, the whole row opens the rating sheet instead of showing a slider. */
  onTap?: (player: PlayerRating) => void;
  /** Position in its list, for the entrance stagger. */
  index?: number;
}) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  const hasCrowd = rating.crowdRating !== null;

  // Always a list item, so `ul > li` stays valid markup even when the row is tappable.
  const Tag = onTap ? "button" : "div";

  return (
    <li className="list-in" style={{ "--stagger": `${Math.min(index, 8) * 40}ms` } as CSSProperties}>
      <Tag
        {...(onTap
          ? {
              type: "button" as const,
              onClick: () => {
                haptic("light");
                onTap(rating);
              },
            }
          : {})}
        className={cx(
          "flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-3 text-start",
          onTap &&
            "transition-[background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated active:scale-[0.99]",
        )}
      >
        <TeamBadge team={team} size="sm" />

        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <p className="truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">{localize(rating.name)}</p>
          <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Chip tone="muted" normalCase>
              {t(`pos.${rating.position}`)}
            </Chip>
            {hasCrowd ? (
              <span className="text-xs text-ink-3">{t("ratings.votes", { count: rating.votes })}</span>
            ) : (
              <span className="text-xs text-ink-3">{t("ratings.pending")}</span>
            )}
          </div>
          {/* The eye-test comment â€” the crowd's why, not just its number. */}
          {(rating.myComment || rating.latestComment) && onTap ? (
            <div className="mt-1 flex items-start gap-2">
              <p className="min-w-0 flex-1 line-clamp-2 text-xs italic text-ink-3">
                â€œ{rating.myComment || rating.latestComment}â€
              </p>
              {!rating.myComment && rating.latestCommentId ? (
                <ReportButton commentType="rating" commentId={rating.latestCommentId} />
              ) : null}
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>

        {/* Crowd rating â€” gold, the only accent that means crowd approval. */}
        <div className="flex-none text-center">
          <Num className={cx("block text-lg leading-none", hasCrowd ? "text-gold" : "text-ink-3")}>
            {hasCrowd ? rating.crowdRating!.toFixed(1) : "â€“"}
          </Num>
          <span className="label text-ink-3">{t("ratings.crowd")}</span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-none text-center">
          <Num className="block text-lg leading-none text-ink">{rating.fantasyPoints}</Num>
          <span className="label text-ink-3">{t("ratings.points")}</span>
        </div>

        {onRate ? (
          <div className="flex-none">
            <RatingSlider
              value={rating.myRating}
              disabled={rating.myRating !== null}
              onChange={(value) => {
                haptic("light");
                onRate(rating.playerId, value);
              }}
            />
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {onTap ? (
          rating.myRating !== null ? (
            <span className="grid h-9 min-w-9 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full border border-gold px-2 text-sm font-bold text-gold">
              <Num>{rating.myRating}</Num>
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="flex-none text-xs font-bold text-ink-3">{t("ratings.tapToRate")}</span>
          )
        ) : null}
      </Tag>
    </li>
  );
}

/**
 * 1â€“10 rating control. Rendered as a stepped range input so it stays accessible on
 * low-end Android, with an integer snap pulse per the design system.
 */
export function RatingSlider({
  value,
  disabled,
  onChange,
}: {
  value: number | null;
  disabled?: boolean;
  onChange: (value: number) => void;
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  if (disabled) {
    return (
      // Your rating is evaluation: amber in every context (it used to flip to
      // volt here, so the same number wore two colours across screens).
      <span className="grid h-9 min-w-9 place-items-center rounded-full border border-gold text-sm font-bold text-gold">
        <Num>{value}</Num>
      </span>
    );
  }

  return (
    <label className="flex flex-col items-center">
      <span className="sr-only">{t("ratings.rateLabel")}</span>
      <input
        type="range"
        min={1}
        max={10}
        step={1}
        defaultValue={value ?? 6}
        onChange={(e) => {
          selectionHaptic();
          onChange(Number(e.target.value));
        }}
        onPointerUp={(e) => onChange(Number((e.target as HTMLInputElement).value))}
        className="h-11 w-20 accent-[var(--volt)]"
      />
      <Num className="text-xs text-ink-3">{value ?? "â€“"}</Num>
    </label>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\ReportButton.tsx =====

import { useState } from "react";
import { api } from "../lib/api";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { useToast } from "./toastContext";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";

/**
 * One-tap "report" on someone else's comment. The server keeps it to one
 * report per user per comment (a second tap is a no-op), and rate-limits it.
 * Once filed the control latches, so the user gets a clear terminal state.
 */
export function ReportButton({
  commentType,
  commentId,
  className,
}: {
  commentType: "timeline" | "rating";
  commentId: number;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const { push } = useToast();
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);

  const submit = async () => {
    if (busy || done) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await api.reportComment(commentType, commentId);
      setDone(true);
      push({ text: t("report.done"), tone: "volt", icon: "âœ“" });
    } catch {
      push({ text: t("report.failed"), tone: "danger", icon: "âœ•" });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={(e) => {
        // Rows are sometimes tappable containers; a report must not open them.
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        void submit();
      }}
      disabled={busy || done}
      className={cx(
        "flex-none text-xs font-bold text-ink-3 underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:text-danger hover:underline disabled:opacity-60",
        className,
      )}
    >
      {done ? t("report.done") : t("report.button")}
    </button>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\RulesSheet.tsx =====

import { useState } from "react";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { BottomSheet } from "./BottomSheet";
import { InfoIcon } from "./icons";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";

const RULE_KEYS = [
  "rules.lineup",
  "rules.sub",
  "rules.shot",
  "rules.watch",
  "rules.versus",
  "rules.streak",
] as const;

/**
 * The scoring rules, inside the product. They used to live only in
 * scoring-rules.md, so a "+3" on screen had nothing explaining where it came
 * from and every lock window read as arbitrary. One control opens one sheet;
 * the Predictions hub and the boards share the same copy, so the rules cannot
 * drift between the place you earn points and the place you spend them.
 *
 * Values are copied from scoring-rules.md (the locked tables), not re-derived.
 *
 * `variant="icon"` is the compact circular button for a TopBar slot;
 * `variant="link"` is a labelled text action for inline copy, where an icon
 * alone would carry no standalone meaning for a screen reader.
 */
export function RulesButton({
  variant = "icon",
  className,
}: {
  variant?: "icon" | "link";
  className?: string;
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

  return (
    <>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
        aria-label={variant === "icon" ? t("rules.title") : undefined}
        className={
          variant === "icon"
            ? cx(
                "grid h-11 w-11 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full border border-line text-ink-2 transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated hover:text-ink",
                className,
              )
            : cx(
                "-my-2 inline-flex min-h-11 flex-none items-center px-1 text-xs font-bold text-volt transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:underline",
                className,
              )
        }
      >
        {variant === "icon" ? <InfoIcon /> : t("rules.title")}
      </button>

      <BottomSheet open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} title={t("rules.title")}>
        <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-ink-2">{t("rules.intro")}</p>
        <ul className="mt-3 divide-y divide-line border-y border-line">
          {RULE_KEYS.map((key) => (
            <li key={key} className="py-2.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-ink-2">
              {t(key)}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
        <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-ink-3">{t("rules.settle")}</p>
      </BottomSheet>
    </>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\ScoreOdometer.tsx =====

import { cx } from "../lib/cx";

/**
 * Signature animation 2: score odometer.
 * Each character is keyed by its own value, so only the digit that actually
 * changed remounts and animates â€” the rest of the number stays perfectly still.
 */
export function ScoreOdometer({ value, className }: { value: number | null; className?: string }) {
  const text = value === null ? "â€“" : String(value);

  return (
    <span className={cx("num-display tabular-nums inline-flex overflow-hidden", className)}>
      {[...text].map((char, index) => (
        <span key={`${index}:${char}`} className="score-tick inline-block">
          {char}
        </span>
      ))}
    </span>
  );
}

import { cx } from "../lib/cx";

const bar = "shimmer rounded";

/**
 * Matchday feed loading state, shaped exactly like the live layout's league
 * group (rounded-16px container, teal-dot header band, kickoff-sorted rows
 * with a 44px time column and 22px crests) so the real feed swaps in without
 * a jump. Cups keeps the old MatchCardSkeleton â€” this one is Matches-only.
 */
export function LeagueGroupSkeleton({ groups = 2, rows = 3 }: { groups?: number; rows?: number }) {

    <div className="space-y-3" aria-hidden="true">

        <div key={group} className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface">

            <span className={cx(bar, "h-2.5 w-12")} />
          </div>
          {Array.from({ length: rows }, (_, row) => (

              className="flex items-center gap-3 border-t border-[#1d2b26] px-3.5 py-[9px]"
            >

      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
