===== D:\football\src\components\StreakWidget.tsx =====

import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api, type ApiStreak } from "../lib/api";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";
import { Card, Chip } from "./ui";
import { FlameIcon } from "./icons";

/**
 * Favorite-club streak (scoring-rules.md Â§6): how many consecutive anchor-club
 * matches you've scored on, and how far to the next one-time threshold bonus
 * (+2 at 3, +5 at 5, +10 at 10 â€” once each per season). A miss â€” including no
 * predictions at all â€” resets the run, so the widget shows the reset state
 * just as plainly as the hot hand.
 */
export function StreakWidget({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  // Streaks change on resolution (post-match), so a slow poll is enough.
  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["myStreak"],
    queryFn: () => api.getMyStreak(),
    staleTime: 60_000,
    refetchInterval: 120_000,
  });

  const streak: ApiStreak | null = query.data?.streak ?? null;
  if (query.isLoading || !streak) return null;

  const next = streak.nextThreshold;
  const gap = streak.nextThresholdGap ?? 0;
  const dotsNeeded = next ?? streak.current; // when all thresholds are paid, show the full run
  const dots = Math.min(Math.max(dotsNeeded, 1), 10);
  const filled = next !== null ? Math.min(streak.current, next) : dots;

  return (
    <Card className={cx("p-3", className)}>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <span
          className={cx(
            "grid h-8 w-8 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full border text-sm",
            streak.active ? "border-volt text-volt" : "border-line text-ink-3",
          )}
          aria-hidden="true"
        >
          <FlameIcon className="h-4 w-4" />
        </span>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <p className="truncate text-sm font-bold text-ink">{t("streak.title")}</p>
          <p className="truncate text-xs text-ink-3">
            {streak.active
              ? t("streak.activeSubtitle", { count: streak.current })
              : t("streak.idleSubtitle", { count: streak.current })}
          </p>
        </div>
        {next !== null ? (
          <Chip tone="volt">{t("streak.next", { count: gap, bonus: streak.nextBonus ?? 0 })}</Chip>
        ) : (
          <Chip tone="gold">{t("streak.maxed")}</Chip>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Threshold dots: one per match toward the next bonus. */}
      <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1" aria-hidden="true">
        {Array.from({ length: dots }, (_, i) => (
          <span
            key={i}
            className={cx(
              "h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full",
              i < filled ? cx(streak.active ? "bg-volt" : "bg-ink-3/40", "dot-pop") : "bg-line",
            )}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\TabBar.tsx =====

import { NavLink, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { forwardRef } from "react";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { haptic } from "../lib/telegram";
import { BallIcon, ListIcon, UserIcon } from "./icons";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";
import type { StringKey } from "../i18n/strings";

/**
 * Three tabs: Matches, Tables, Profile. Browse is now inside Tables.
 */
const tabs: Array<{ to: string; key: StringKey; Icon: typeof BallIcon }> = [
  { to: "/matches", key: "nav.matches", Icon: BallIcon },
  { to: "/table", key: "nav.table", Icon: ListIcon },
  { to: "/profile", key: "nav.profile", Icon: UserIcon },
];

export const TabBar = forwardRef<HTMLElement, {}>((_, ref) => {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const { pathname } = useLocation();
  // A team sub-page belongs to no top-level tab: highlighting one would claim a
  // section the user has drilled out of.
  const isTeamSubPage = pathname.startsWith("/team/");

  return (      // 95% opaque, so no backdrop blur â€” same reasoning as TopBar: the blur was
    // imperceptible but ran on every scroll frame.
    // Ad is now inline and scrolls with content, so TabBar sits at the very bottom.
    <nav
      ref={ref}
      className="pad-safe-bottom fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-20 border-t border-line bg-base/95"
    >
      <ul className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-[var(--app-max-width,768px)] items-stretch justify-between px-2 pt-1">
        {tabs.map(({ to, key, Icon }) => (
          <li key={to} className="flex-1">
            <NavLink
              to={to}
              onClick={() => haptic("light")}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                cx(
                  "relative flex min-h-14 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-xl px-1 text-xs font-bold transition-[color,background-color] duration-[var(--t-fast)]",
                  // Inactive tabs sit at secondary, not muted: "available but
                  // not selected", never "disabled".
                  isActive && !isTeamSubPage
                    ? "bg-elevated/70 text-volt"
                    : "text-ink-2 hover:text-ink",
                )
              }
            >
              {({ isActive }) => {
                const active = isActive && !isTeamSubPage;
                return (
                  <>
                    {/* Shape cue, not just colour: a colour-blind user (or one
                        in grayscale) reads "you are here" from this bar even
                        when the volt text is indistinguishable. aria-current on
                        the link already carries it for screen readers. */}
                    <span
                      aria-hidden="true"
                      className={cx(
                        "absolute top-0.5 h-[3px] w-6 rounded-full bg-volt transition-opacity duration-[var(--t-fast)]",
                        active ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0",
                      )}
                    />
                    <Icon
                      strokeWidth={active ? 2.2 : 1.8}
                      className={cx(
                        "transition-transform duration-[var(--t-fast)]",
                        active && "tab-pop scale-105",
                      )}
                    />
                    <span className="truncate">{t(key)}</span>
                  </>
                );
              }}
            </NavLink>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </nav>
  );
});

===== D:\football\src\components\TimelineList.tsx =====

import { Fragment, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import type { Comment, TimelineEvent } from "../types";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import type { StringKey } from "../i18n/strings";
import { BallIcon, CardIcon, SwapIcon } from "./icons";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";
import { toFaDigits } from "../lib/format";
import { formatScore, runningScore } from "../lib/timelineScore";
import { LiveDot, Num } from "./ui";

const typeKey: Record<TimelineEvent["type"], StringKey> = {
  goal: "timeline.goal",
  card: "timeline.card",
  substitution: "timeline.sub",
  var: "timeline.var",
  kickoff: "timeline.kickoff",
  halftime: "timeline.halftime",
  fulltime: "timeline.fulltime",
  other: "timeline.other",
};

/**
 * The 16px event icon (design-system "drawn, never emoji"): a goal is the ball
 * in --volt, a booking --gold (or --danger for a sending-off â€” ESPN folds a
 * red, including a second yellow, into the `card` type, so the description is
 * the only signal).
 */
function eventIcon(
  type: TimelineEvent["type"],
  description: string,
): { glyph: ReactNode; className: string } {
  const isRedCard = /\bred card\b|second yellow/i.test(description);
  switch (type) {
    case "goal":
      return { glyph: <BallIcon className="h-4 w-4" />, className: "text-volt" };
    case "card":
      return {
        glyph: <CardIcon className="h-4 w-4" />,
        className: isRedCard ? "text-danger" : "text-gold",
      };
    case "substitution":
      return { glyph: <SwapIcon className="h-4 w-4" />, className: "text-ink-2" };
    case "var":
      return { glyph: "â—‡", className: "text-ink-3" };
    case "kickoff":
      return { glyph: "â€¢", className: "text-ink-3" };
    case "other":
      return { glyph: "Â·", className: "text-ink-3" };
    default:
      return { glyph: "Â·", className: "text-ink-3" };
  }
}

/**
 * A pinned comment belongs to a minute either directly (minute_seconds) or
 * through its event. Minute-only pins render in the gap after the last event
 * at or before their minute â€” the timeline stays chronological.
 */
function commentsForWindow(
  comments: Comment[],
  fromSeconds: number,
  toSeconds: number | null,
  eventId: string | null,
): Comment[] {
  return comments.filter((c) => {
    if (eventId && c.eventId === eventId) return true;
    if (eventId && c.eventId) return false; // pinned to a different event
    if (c.eventId) return false; // event pins render under their own event
    const s = c.minuteSeconds ?? 0;
    return s >= fromSeconds && (toSeconds === null || s < toSeconds);
  });
}

function PinnedComment({
  comment,
  onOpenClip,
}: {
  comment: Comment;
  onOpenClip: (comment: Comment) => void;
}) {
  const { t, localize } = useI18n();
  return (
    <div
      className={cx(
        "mt-1 rounded-card border p-2.5",
        comment.isMine ? "border-gold/40 bg-elevated" : "border-line bg-base",
      )}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <span
          className="grid h-6 w-6 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full border border-line bg-surface text-xs font-extrabold text-ink-2"
          aria-hidden="true"
        >
          {(comment.isMine ? t("comments.you") : comment.author).slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()}
        </span>
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-xs font-bold text-ink-2">
          {comment.isMine ? t("comments.you") : comment.author}
        </span>
        {comment.mediaLink ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onOpenClip(comment)}
            className="flex-none text-xs font-bold text-volt underline-offset-2 hover:underline"
          >
            {t("comments.attachOpen")} â†—
          </button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      <p className="mt-1.5 text-sm leading-relaxed text-ink">{localize(comment.text)}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * The two spine cells. Both wrap (never truncate) and render: bold name, the
 * second line under it, and the icon pinned to the spine edge. A goal gets a
 * volt-tinted cell so it reads at a glance. Children = the comment trigger.
 */
function EventBody({ name, line2 }: { name: string; line2: string | null }) {
  return name ? (
    <span className="min-w-0 text-start" dir="auto">
      <span className="block text-sm font-bold text-ink">{name}</span>
      {line2 ? (
        <span className="block text-xs text-ink-3">{line2}</span>
      ) : null}
    </span>
  ) : null;
}

function HomeSpineCell({
  parts,
  goal,
  children,
}: {
  parts: { icon: { glyph: ReactNode; className: string }; name: string; line2: string | null };
  goal: boolean;
  children?: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cx(
        "col-start-1 row-start-1 -me-2 flex min-w-0 items-center justify-end gap-2",
        goal && "rounded-card border border-volt/30 bg-elevated px-2 py-1",
      )}
    >
      {children}
      <EventBody name={parts.name} line2={parts.line2} />
      <span aria-hidden="true" className={cx("flex-none", parts.icon.className)}>
        {parts.icon.glyph}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

function AwaySpineCell({
  parts,
  goal,
  children,
}: {
  parts: { icon: { glyph: ReactNode; className: string }; name: string; line2: string | null };
  goal: boolean;
  children?: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cx(
        "col-start-3 row-start-1 -ms-2 flex min-w-0 items-center justify-start gap-2",
        goal && "rounded-card border border-volt/30 bg-elevated px-2 py-1",
      )}
    >
      <span aria-hidden="true" className={cx("flex-none", parts.icon.className)}>
        {parts.icon.glyph}
      </span>
      <EventBody name={parts.name} line2={parts.line2} />
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * A timeline with a CENTER SPINE: minutes live in the middle column, home-team
 * events hang on the home side of it (the home column â€” start edge in both
 * directions, matching the home-first scoreboard), away events on the other.
 * Each event cell hugs the spine: text reads toward the minute it happened at.
 * Names never truncate â€” they wrap.
 */
export function TimelineList({
  events,
  comments = [],
  liveMinute,
  onOpenClip,
  homeTeamId,
  awayTeamId,
}: {
  events: TimelineEvent[];
  /** Pinned crowd comments, opened from the event row that owns them. */
  comments?: Comment[];
  liveMinute?: number;
  /** Handler for opening a comment's clip link (validated https by the server). */
  onOpenClip?: (comment: Comment) => void;
  /** The match's two sides: the event's teamId picks ITS column. */
  homeTeamId?: string;
  awayTeamId?: string;
}) {
  const { t, localize, dir } = useI18n();
  // Which event's comment thread is expanded â€” only rows with comments carry
  // the trigger, so only those rows ever grow.
  const [openCommentsId, setOpenCommentsId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const openClip = (comment: Comment) =>
    onOpenClip ? onOpenClip(comment) : undefined;

  // The score beside each goal is a prefix count of the timeline's own goals,
  // credited to the side each event names (src/lib/timelineScore.ts), so a
  // goal reads "1â€“0" the moment it lands rather than leaving the reader to
  // scroll up to the scoreboard. Displayed UNDER the event's minute, so the
  // center spine shows when AND what the score became.
  const { byGoal } = useMemo(
    () => runningScore(events, homeTeamId ?? "", awayTeamId ?? ""),
    [events, homeTeamId, awayTeamId],
  );

  // --- pre-pass: find the halftime event + the score at half time ---
  const halftimeEvent = events.find((e) => e.type === "halftime") ?? null;
  const halftimeScore = useMemo(() => {
    if (!halftimeEvent) return { home: 0, away: 0 };
    let lastGoalId: string | null = null;
    for (const e of events) {
      if (e.type === "goal" && e.minuteSeconds < halftimeEvent.minuteSeconds) {
        lastGoalId = e.id;
      }
    }
    return lastGoalId
      ? (byGoal.get(lastGoalId) ?? { home: 0, away: 0 })
      : { home: 0, away: 0 };
  }, [events, halftimeEvent, byGoal]);

  // --- pre-pass: final score (for the full-time line) ---
  const finalScore = useMemo(() => {
    const vals = [...byGoal.values()];
    return vals.length > 0 ? vals[vals.length - 1]! : { home: 0, away: 0 };
  }, [byGoal]);

  /** Digits through locale conversion: Persian-Indic glyphs under FA, Latin
      under EN (toFaDigits passes non-digits through untouched). */
  const digits = (value: string) => (dir === "rtl" ? toFaDigits(value) : value);

  /** Comment-thread trigger shared by event rows and the HT/FT separators:
      36px visual with an invisible 44px+ hit band (the same pattern as the
      feed's league chips). */
  const commentTrigger = (eventId: string, count: number) => {
    const commentsOpen = openCommentsId === eventId;
    return (
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-expanded={commentsOpen}
        onClick={() => setOpenCommentsId(commentsOpen ? null : eventId)}
        className="relative inline-flex h-9 flex-none items-center gap-1 rounded-lg px-2 text-xs font-bold text-gold after:absolute after:-inset-x-1 after:-inset-y-2.5 after:content-['']"
      >
        <span aria-hidden="true">ðŸ’¬</span>
        <Num>{count}</Num>
        <span className="sr-only">{t("comments.liveThread")}</span>
      </button>
    );
  };

  /** The event parts the side cells render: icon, bold name, second line
      (assist / swap partner / card kind). No English feed sentence in the FA
      page â€” the event kind is localized here, names come through the person
      map with the English fallback, and neither ever truncates. */
  function eventParts(event: TimelineEvent): {
    icon: { glyph: ReactNode; className: string };
    name: string;
    line2: string | null;
  } {
    const icon = eventIcon(event.type, event.description);
    const names = event.participants.map(localize);
    const [first, second] = names;

    // Second line: what the FIRST name did with the second one.
    let line2: string | null = null;
    if (event.type === "goal" && second) {
      line2 = t("timeline.assist", { name: second });
    } else if (event.type === "goal" && /\bown goal\b/i.test(event.description)) {
      line2 = t("timeline.ownGoal");
    } else if (event.type === "card") {
      line2 = t(
        /\bred card\b|second yellow/i.test(event.description)
          ? "timeline.red"
          : /yellow/i.test(event.description)
            ? "timeline.yellow"
            : "timeline.card",
      );
    } else if (event.type === "substitution" && second) {
      // ESPN's sub direction varies by feed, so the pair reads as a swap (â‡„)
      // rather than the page asserting an off/on order it can't verify.
      line2 = `â‡„ ${second}`;
    } else if (!first) {
      line2 = t(typeKey[event.type]);
    }
    return { icon, name: first ?? "", line2 };
  }

  // Shared grid: side cells flex, fixed center for the minute (+ the score a
  // goal produced, directly under its minute).
  const SPINE_GRID = "grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_56px_minmax(0,1fr)] items-center gap-2 px-1";

  return (
    <ol className="relative">
      {/* Live-minute indicator: a slim status row at the top, replaced by real
          events as they land. The dot is the shared --live pulse. */}
      {liveMinute !== undefined ? (
        <li
          role="status"
          className="flex items-center gap-2 px-1 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-live"
        >
          <LiveDot />
          <span>{t("status.live")}</span>
          <span dir="ltr">
            <Num>{digits(String(liveMinute))}â€²</Num>
          </span>
        </li>
      ) : null}

      {events.map((event, index) => {
        const isGoal = event.type === "goal";
        const isSeparator = event.type === "halftime" || event.type === "fulltime";
        const goalScore = byGoal.get(event.id);
        const side: "home" | "away" | null =
          event.teamId == null
            ? null
            : event.teamId === homeTeamId
              ? "home"
              : "away";
        const next = events[index + 1];
        // Comments pinned to this event, plus minute-pins falling in this
        // event's window [event.start, next.start).
        const pinned = commentsForWindow(
          comments,
          event.minuteSeconds,
          next ? next.minuteSeconds : null,
          event.id,
        );
        const count = event.commentCount + (event.commentCount > 0 ? 0 : pinned.length);
        const commentsOpen = openCommentsId === event.id;

        // Half-time and full-time are not rows: a centred divider with the
        // score as it stood at that moment.
        if (isSeparator) {
          const label = t(typeKey[event.type]);
          const score =
            event.type === "halftime" ? formatScore(halftimeScore) : formatScore(finalScore);
          return (
            <li
              key={event.id}
              role="separator"
              aria-label={
                count > 0 ? `${label} Â· ${t("comments.liveThread")}: ${count}` : label
              }
              className="my-2 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs font-bold text-ink-3"
            >
              <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-line" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span>{label}</span>
                {/* Homeâ€“away and LTR, so the numbers read home-first in both
                    directions â€” Persian digits still read left-to-right. */}
                <span dir="ltr" className="text-ink-2">
                  <Num>{digits(score)}</Num>
                </span>
                {count > 0 ? commentTrigger(event.id, count) : null}
              </span>
              <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-line" aria-hidden="true" />
            </li>
          );
        }

        // No side (kickoff): a small centered note carrying the minute.
        if (side === null) {
          return (
            <li
              key={event.id}
              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-1.5 text-xs text-ink-3"
            >
              <span dir="ltr" className="text-sm font-extrabold text-ink-2">
                <Num>{digits(event.minuteDisplay)}</Num>
              </span>
              <span>{t(typeKey[event.type])}</span>
              {count > 0 ? commentTrigger(event.id, count) : null}
            </li>
          );
        }

        return (
          <Fragment key={event.id}>
            <li className={cx(SPINE_GRID, index === 0 ? "pt-2" : "py-2")}>
              {/* MINUTE â€” the middle of the spine. Persian digits under FA. */}
              <span className="flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5">
                <span dir="ltr" className="text-sm font-extrabold text-ink-2">
                  <Num>{digits(event.minuteDisplay)}</Num>
                </span>
                {/* The scoreline this goal produced: under its own minute. */}
                {isGoal && goalScore ? (
                  <span dir="ltr">
                    <Num className="text-xs font-extrabold text-volt">
                      {digits(formatScore(goalScore))}
                    </Num>
                  </span>
                ) : null}
              </span>

              {/* HOME â€” first column, content hugging the spine (its END edge
                  in both directions): trigger, text, icon-at-spine. */}
              {side === "home" ? (
                <HomeSpineCell parts={eventParts(event)} goal={isGoal}>
                  {count > 0 ? commentTrigger(event.id, count) : null}
                </HomeSpineCell>
              ) : (
                <span aria-hidden="true" className="col-start-1 row-start-1" />
              )}

              {/* AWAY â€” third column, content hugging the spine: icon-at-spine,
                  text, trigger. */}
              {side === "away" ? (
                <AwaySpineCell parts={eventParts(event)} goal={isGoal}>
                  {count > 0 ? commentTrigger(event.id, count) : null}
                </AwaySpineCell>
              ) : (
                <span aria-hidden="true" className="col-start-3 row-start-1" />
              )}
            </li>

            {/* The row's comment thread, once opened. Substitution direction is
                shown in the name pair, so the icon swap is cosmetic-only
                below. */}
            {commentsOpen ? (
              <li className="px-1 pb-2">
                {/* The composer is intentionally unmounted for now, so the
                    thread must SAY it is view-only: a thread the user can
                    open but never answer otherwise reads as a broken form. */}
                <p role="note" className="mb-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-ink-3">
                  {t("comments.readOnly")}
                </p>
                <ul>
                  {pinned.map((comment) => (
                    <li key={comment.id}>
                      <PinnedComment comment={comment} onOpenClip={openClip} />
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </li>
            ) : null}
          </Fragment>
        );
      })}
    </ol>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\Toast.tsx =====

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { AlertIcon, BallIcon, CheckIcon, CloseIcon, RefreshIcon, StarFilledIcon } from "./icons";
import { ToastContext, type ToastItem, type ToastTone } from "./toastContext";

/**
 * Call sites pass a one-character glyph ("â˜…", "âœ•"); they resolve to the same
 * SVG set as the rest of the app here, so toasts don't drift into system-font
 * symbols sitting next to drawn icons.
 */
const GLYPHS: Record<string, ReactNode> = {
  "â˜…": <StarFilledIcon className="h-4 w-4" />,
  "âœ•": <CloseIcon className="h-4 w-4" />,
  "â†»": <RefreshIcon className="h-4 w-4" />,
  "âš ": <AlertIcon className="h-4 w-4" />,
  "âš½": <BallIcon className="h-4 w-4" />,
  "âœ“": <CheckIcon className="h-4 w-4" />,
};

/*
 * The tone owns a 4px accent bar on the card's leading edge (`border-s-2`, so
 * it moves to the right in Persian like everything else) over the 1px tone
 * border: success volt, error danger, evaluative gold. It is colour + width
 * only â€” the toast keeps one tint, one icon and one line of copy.
 */
const toneClass: Record<ToastTone, string> = {
  volt: "border-volt border-s-2 text-volt",
  gold: "border-gold border-s-2 text-gold",
  danger: "border-danger border-s-2 text-danger",
  // "Last sync" / "Updated" reads as a successful sync, so it borrows the
  // success tint: a volt border + wash rather than a grey disabled-looking one.
  muted: "border-volt border-s-2 text-volt",
};

const toneTint: Record<ToastTone, string | undefined> = {
  volt: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--volt) 14%, var(--bg-elevated))",
  gold: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--gold) 14%, var(--bg-elevated))",
  danger: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--danger) 14%, var(--bg-elevated))",
  muted: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--volt) 12%, transparent)",
};

/** How long the copy stays readable before it starts to leave. */
const DWELL_MS = {
  muted: 1500,
  volt: 2500,
  gold: 2500,
  danger: 3000,
} as const;
/** The leave animation's own duration (see `.toast-out` in index.css) â€” the
 *  reap timer waits it out so the exit is visible. */
const LEAVE_MS = 320;

/** App-wide transient alerts: goals, deadline warnings, saved confirmations. */
export function ToastProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<ToastItem[]>([]);
  // Every toast owns two timers; they are cleared on unmount so a hot reload
  // never sets state on a dead provider.
  const timers = useRef<number[]>([]);
  useEffect(
    () => () => {
      for (const timer of timers.current) window.clearTimeout(timer);
      timers.current = [];
    },
    [],
  );

  const push = useCallback((toast: Omit<ToastItem, "id">) => {
    const id = `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`;
    setToasts((prev) => [...prev.slice(-2), { ...toast, id }]);
    timers.current.push(
      window.setTimeout(() => {
        // Mark leaving rather than remove: the exit animation plays first.
        setToasts((prev) => prev.map((item) => (item.id === id ? { ...item, leaving: true } : item)));
      }, DWELL_MS[toast.tone] ?? 2500),
      // Belt and braces: reap on a timer as well as on `animationend`. The event
      // is not guaranteed â€” a backgrounded webview, an element whose animation
      // never starts, or a stale class without a keyframes rule all leave the
      // toast on screen permanently, which is exactly the stuck-toast bug.
      window.setTimeout(
        () => setToasts((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id)),
        (DWELL_MS[toast.tone] ?? 2500) + LEAVE_MS,
      ),
    );
  }, []);

  const value = useMemo(() => ({ push }), [push]);

  return (
    <ToastContext.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
      <div
        // `auto` (not inherited rtl/ltr): an English "Updated 9:41 PM" inside a
        // Persian app must read LTR, while a Persian message reads RTL.
        dir="auto"
        className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-x-0 top-0 z-[60] flex flex-col items-center gap-2 px-3 pt-[max(var(--safe-top, env(safe-area-inset-top, 0px)),10px)]"
        role="status"
        aria-live="polite"
      >
        {toasts.map((toast) => (
          <div
            key={toast.id}
            onAnimationEnd={() => {
              // Only the leave reaps the toast; the enter animation also ends
              // here and must be ignored.
              if (toast.leaving) setToasts((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== toast.id));
            }}
            className={cx(
              // A card, not a pill: an accent bar needs a straight leading edge
              // to sit on. Slides in from above the header and leaves upward
              // (.toast-in / .toast-out).
              "flex w-full max-w-[420px] items-center gap-2 rounded-card border px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold backdrop-blur-md",
              toast.leaving ? "toast-out" : "toast-in",
              toneClass[toast.tone],
            )}
            style={{ backgroundColor: toneTint[toast.tone] ?? "rgba(18,44,37,0.95)" }}
          >
            {toast.icon ? (
              <span aria-hidden="true" className="flex flex-none items-center">
                {typeof toast.icon === "string" ? (GLYPHS[toast.icon] ?? toast.icon) : toast.icon}
              </span>
            ) : null}
            <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{toast.text}</span>
            {/* Only errors get an explicit out: a dismissed failure stays in
                the user's memory; the success/muted dwell is short enough not
                to need one. */}
            {toast.tone === "danger" ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label={t("toast.dismiss")}
                onClick={() =>
                  setToasts((prev) => prev.map((item) => (item.id === toast.id ? { ...item, leaving: true } : item)))
                }
                className="flex-none text-current opacity-70 transition-opacity hover:opacity-100"
              >
                <CloseIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </button>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </ToastContext.Provider>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\TopBar.tsx =====

import { useLocation, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import { haptic } from "../lib/telegram";
import { ChevronLeftIcon, GlobeIcon } from "./icons";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";

export function TopBar({
  title,
  subtitle,
  back = false,
  right,
}: {
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  back?: boolean;
  right?: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { dir, t } = useI18n();
  // A back button on the session's FIRST entry (a shared deep link) has no
  // history to pop: navigate(-1) would leave the mini app entirely. The
  // first entry has React Router's "default" location key â€” route to the
  // feed instead, replacing the URL so the back gesture can't loop.
  const firstEntry = useLocation().key === "default";
  const goBack = () => {
    if (firstEntry) navigate("/matches", { replace: true });
    else navigate(-1);
  };

  return (
    // No backdrop blur: the bar is 95% opaque, so the blur was invisible while
    // still forcing a full-width backdrop read on every scroll frame â€” exactly
    // the cost the low-end Android target can't afford.
    // Full-bleed BAND on phones (<480px): the header renders inside <main>'s
    // page gutter, whose padding inset the band's background + hairline from
    // the screen edge â€” the floating-inset rectangle that read as "cropped
    // sides". The -mx- breaks the header out of its parent's page-gutter padding
    // so the header background and its bottom hairline run full-bleed (left = 0 to
    // right = viewport width), while the inner row keeps the gutter.
    // â‰¥480px keeps today's inset-within-column look.
    <header className="pad-safe-top sticky top-0 z-20 border-b border-line bg-base/95 -mx-[var(--space-page-x,16px)]">
      {/* px-[var(--space-page-x)] aligns the header's gutters with the page below
          and pb-4 gives more breathing room from the content underneath. */}
      <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-[var(--app-max-width,768px)] items-center gap-3 px-[var(--space-page-x,16px)] pb-4 pt-2">
        {back ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              haptic("light");
              goBack();
            }}
            aria-label={t("common.back")}
            className="-ms-1 grid h-11 w-11 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full border border-line text-ink transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated"
          >
            {/* The chevron points toward the start edge, which flips in RTL. */}
            <span className={cx(dir === "rtl" && "block rotate-180")}>
              <ChevronLeftIcon />
            </span>
          </button>
        ) : null}

        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <h1 className="truncate text-lg font-bold text-ink">{title}</h1>
          {subtitle ? <p className="truncate text-xs text-ink-2">{subtitle}</p> : null}      </div>
        {right}
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

/**
 * EN/FA switch. Kept in the shell rather than a settings screen because Persian
 * is a v1 launch language, not a preference buried two taps down.
 */
export function LanguageToggle() {
  const { lang, toggleLang, t } = useI18n();
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => {
        haptic("light");
        toggleLang();
      }}
      title={t("profile.language")}
      aria-label={t("profile.language")}
      className="flex min-h-11 flex-none items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-line px-3 text-xs font-bold text-ink-2 transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated hover:text-ink"
    >
      <GlobeIcon />
      <span className="uppercase">{lang}</span>
    </button>
  );
}

===== D:\football\src\components\ui.tsx =====

import {
  useEffect,
  useLayoutEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type ButtonHTMLAttributes,
  type KeyboardEvent as ReactKeyboardEvent,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";
import type { Team } from "../types";
import { useI18n } from "../i18n/context";
import type { StringKey } from "../i18n/strings";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx";
import { crestSources } from "../lib/logos";
import { haptic } from "../lib/telegram";
import { useLocalState } from "../lib/useLocalState";
import { AlertIcon, BallIcon, ChartIcon, ClockIcon, ChevronDownIcon, ListIcon, RefreshIcon, ShirtIcon, StarFilledIcon, SwapIcon, TargetIcon } from "./icons";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ surfaces */

export function Card({
  children,
  className,
  style,
  as: Tag = "div",
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  style?: React.CSSProperties;
  as?: "div" | "section" | "li";
}) {
  return (
    <Tag className={cx("rounded-card border border-line bg-surface", className)} style={style}>
      {children}
    </Tag>
  );
}

export function SectionHeading({ title, action }: { title: string; action?: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="mb-2 flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
      <h2 className="label label-lg text-ink-2">{title}</h2>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * A section whose body folds away under its heading â€” the APG disclosure
 * pattern, with the heading OUTSIDE the toggle: the heading wraps the button,
 * so screen-reader users navigating by headings still find
 * Live/Upcoming/Previous (the old span-inside-button made these sections
 * invisible to H-key nav).
 *
 * `level` sets the heading element. Default h3 for a section sitting under an
 * h2; a top-level page section passes `level={2}` so heading order never jumps
 * straight from the page h1 to an h3.
 * The heading row is the toggle (min-height keeps the 44px touch floor), the
 * count badge and chevron ride along as affordances, and the panel carries
 * aria-labelledby/aria-expanded so it reads as one control. The open/closed
 * choice persists per key, so a collapsed "Previous matches" stays collapsed
 * across reloads. Mount the body only while open â€” hidden lists stay out of
 * the DOM and the layout.
 *
 * `openOverride` supports bulk expand/collapse (Cups bracket rounds): while
 * set, it wins over the stored state, and the first individual toggle releases
 * it via `onUserToggle` so the per-section choice becomes the ongoing truth.
 */
export function CollapsibleSection({
  id,
  title,
  count,
  defaultOpen = true,
  openOverride,
  onUserToggle,
  /** Optional badge replacement, shown while the section is collapsed â€” the
   *  pulsing LiveDot on the folded "Live now" header is the reason it exists. */
  collapsedBadge,
  /** Heading level; 3 unless this section is a top level of the page itself. */
  level = 3,
  /** Spacing override for the outer <section>: the feed's 8px rhythm places
   *  its groups 16px apart, while a standalone collapsible keeps the default
   *  24px page-level break. */
  className,
  children,
}: {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  count?: number;
  /** Live/Upcoming start open; dense history sections start closed. */
  defaultOpen?: boolean;
  openOverride?: { open: boolean };
  onUserToggle?: () => void;
  collapsedBadge?: ReactNode;
  level?: 2 | 3;
  className?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const [storedOpen, setOpen] = useLocalState<boolean>(`section.open.${id}`, defaultOpen);
  const open = openOverride ? openOverride.open : storedOpen;
  const headingId = `${id}-heading`;
  const panelId = `${id}-panel`;const Heading = level === 2 ? "h2" : "h3";
  const headerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const handleToggle = () => {
    haptic("light");
    if (openOverride) {
      // First individual toggle during a bulk action: release the override and
      // land on the opposite of the bulk state, so this section moves while
      // the rest keep the bulk position.
      onUserToggle?.();
      setOpen(!openOverride.open);
      return;
    }
    // Folding unmounts the panel: catch keyboard focus hiding inside it and
    // move it to the header, or the next Tab would restart from the page top.
    const active = document.activeElement;
    if (!open && active instanceof Node && panelRef.current?.contains(active)) {
      headerRef.current?.focus();
    }
    setOpen((value) => !value);
  };
  return (
    <section className={className ?? "mt-6"}>
      <Heading id={headingId} className="mb-2 border-b border-line pb-2 -mx-[var(--space-page-x,16px)] px-[var(--space-page-x,16px)]">
        <button
          ref={headerRef}
          type="button"
          aria-expanded={open}
          aria-controls={panelId}
          onClick={handleToggle}
          className="flex min-h-11 w-full items-center justify-between gap-3 text-start transition-transform duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.98]"
        >
          <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
            <span className={cx("label label-lg", open ? "text-ink" : "text-ink-2")}>{title}</span>
            {!open && collapsedBadge ? (
              collapsedBadge
            ) : count ? (
              <Chip tone={open ? "muted" : "volt"}>
                <Num>{count}</Num>
              </Chip>
            ) : null}
          </span>
          <span
            className={cx(
              "flex-none text-ink-3 transition-transform duration-[var(--t-fast)] ease-[var(--ease-out)]",
              open && "rotate-180",
            )}
            aria-hidden="true"
          >
            <ChevronDownIcon />
          </span>
        </button>
      </Heading>
      {open ? (
        <div ref={panelRef} id={panelId} role="region" aria-labelledby={headingId} className="panel-in">
          {children}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------- pieces */

/**
 * Club identity. Where a crest is bundled (src/lib/logos.ts) the badge is the
 * artwork, centred in the same square footprint at every size; clubs without
 * one fall back to their abbreviation on a club-coloured tile. Both states
 * share one shape so a list of fixtures reads as a single row rhythm.
 */
export function TeamBadge({ team, size = "md" }: { team: Team; size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "xs" }) {
  // `xs` is the Matchday feed row's crest: exactly 22px and fully round (the
  // league-group spec); every other size keeps its square-rounded footprint.
  // Radius lives in the size map so only the size decides the shape â€” no
  // class-order-dependent override between rounded-md and rounded-full.
  const dims: Record<string, string> = {
    xs: "h-[22px] w-[22px] text-[10px] rounded-full",
    sm: "h-6 w-6 text-xs rounded-md",
    md: "h-9 w-9 text-xs rounded-md",
    lg: "h-12 w-12 text-sm rounded-md",
    xl: "h-16 w-16 text-md rounded-md",
  };
  const box = dims[size];
  const [broken, setBroken] = useState(false);
  const [remoteBroken, setRemoteBroken] = useState(false);
  // Crests that arrive over the network otherwise pop in from nothing; fade
  // them up on load (bundled and remote share it) so the tile settles in.
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
  const crestImgClass = cx(
    "h-full w-full object-contain transition-opacity duration-[var(--t-fast)] ease-[var(--ease-out)]",
    loaded ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0",
  );
  // Bundled crest first: it is normalized to one footprint so a row of badges
  // reads evenly, and it needs no network. When a club has none, fall back to
  // the ESPN crest URL carried on every team payload â€” that URL used to be
  // stored and never rendered, so those clubs showed a bare abbreviation tile
  // even though artwork existed for them.
  const crest = broken ? null : crestSources(team.id);
  const remote = !crest && !remoteBroken ? (team.logoUrl ?? null) : null;
  const hasArt = Boolean(crest || remote);
  // The abbreviation is the last resort, so it must never itself be blank â€” a
  // team with no ESPN abbreviation falls back to the first letters of its name.
  const abbreviation = team.abbreviation?.trim() || team.shortName.slice(0, 3).toUpperCase();
  return (
    <span
      className={cx(
        "grid flex-none place-items-center font-num font-extrabold border",
        box,
      )}
      style={
        {
          borderColor: hasArt
            ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.18)"
            : `color-mix(in srgb, ${team.color} 55%, transparent)`,
          backgroundColor: hasArt
            ? undefined
            : `color-mix(in srgb, ${team.color} 18%, transparent)`,
          color: "var(--text-primary)",
        }
      }
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      {crest ? (
        <picture className="contents">
          <source type="image/webp" srcSet={crest.webp} />
          <img
            src={crest.png}
            alt=""
            onError={() => setBroken(true)}
            onLoad={() => setLoaded(true)}
            loading="lazy"
            decoding="async"
            className={crestImgClass}
          />
        </picture>
      ) : remote ? (
        <img
          src={remote}
          alt=""
          onError={() => setRemoteBroken(true)}
          onLoad={() => setLoaded(true)}
          loading="lazy"
          decoding="async"
          className={crestImgClass}
        />
      ) : (
        abbreviation
      )}
    </span>
  );
}

export function LiveDot() {
  return <span className="live-dot" aria-hidden="true" />;
}

export type ChipTone = "volt" | "live" | "gold" | "danger" | "pitch" | "muted";

const chipStyles: Record<ChipTone, { className: string; style?: React.CSSProperties }> = {
  volt: {
    className: "text-volt border-volt",
    style: { backgroundColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--volt) 12%, transparent)" },
  },
  live: {
    className: "text-live border-live",
    style: { backgroundColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--live) 12%, transparent)" },
  },
  gold: {
    className: "text-gold border-gold",
    style: { backgroundColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--gold) 12%, transparent)" },
  },
  danger: {
    className: "text-danger border-danger",
    style: { backgroundColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--danger) 12%, transparent)" },
  },
  /* Turf green: positive states (tokens.css). Not LIVE, not evaluation. */
  pitch: {
    className: "text-pitch border-pitch",
    style: { backgroundColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--pitch) 12%, transparent)" },
  },
  muted: { className: "text-ink-2 border-line bg-elevated" },
};

export function Chip({
  children,
  tone = "muted",
  className,
  normalCase = false,
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
  tone?: ChipTone;
  className?: string;
  /** Opt out of the badge default for inline labels (names, positions). */
  normalCase?: boolean;
}) {
  const { className: toneClass, style } = chipStyles[tone];
  return (
    <span
      className={cx(
        "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold",
        !normalCase && "uppercase",
        toneClass,
        className,
      )}
      style={style}
    >
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

/** Numbers use the display face with tabular figures so scores never jitter. */
export function Num({ children, className }: { children: ReactNode; className?: string }) {
  return <span className={cx("num-display tabular-nums", className)}>{children}</span>;
}

/**
 * A player's face, in the circle every roster row and pitch slot uses.
 *
 * The feed carries an ESPN headshot URL for most players but not all, and a
 * CDN URL can 404 long after the sync stored it, so both cases fall back to the
 * player's initials on the same footprint â€” a roster reads evenly either way,
 * and a squad with no artwork at all still looks deliberate. The image is
 * decorative (the name is always rendered next to it) and lazy, since a squad
 * list is 25 of these.
 */
export function PlayerAvatar({
  name,
  src,
  size = "md",
  className,
}: {
  name: string;
  src?: string | null;
  size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg";
  className?: string;
}) {
  const [broken, setBroken] = useState(false);
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
  const dims = { sm: "h-8 w-8 text-xs", md: "h-10 w-10 text-xs", lg: "h-12 w-12 text-sm" }[size];
  const photo = src && !broken ? src : null;
  return (
    <span
      className={cx(
        "relative grid flex-none place-items-center overflow-hidden rounded-full border border-line bg-elevated font-bold text-ink-2",
        dims,
        className,
      )}
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      {photo ? (
        <img
          src={photo}
          alt=""
          onError={() => setBroken(true)}
          onLoad={() => setLoaded(true)}
          loading="lazy"
          decoding="async"
          className={cx(
            "h-full w-full object-cover transition-opacity duration-[var(--t-fast)] ease-[var(--ease-out)]",
            loaded ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0",
          )}
        />
      ) : (
        initialsOf(name)
      )}
    </span>
  );
}

/** Up to two initials from a player name â€” "Cole Palmer" â†’ "CP", "Ronald" â†’ "R". */
function initialsOf(name: string): string {
  const words = name.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (words.length === 0) return "?";
  const first = words[0]!.charAt(0);
  const last = words.length > 1 ? words[words.length - 1]!.charAt(0) : "";
  return (first + last).toUpperCase();
}

/**
 * Evaluative (gold) totals count up from zero on mount â€” season points,
 * standings points, xPts â€” so a number that just appeared reads as earned
 * rather than snapped in. Integer-only; a reduced-motion user sees the value
 * immediately (no animation, no intermediate frames).
 */
export function CountUp({
  value,
  className,
  duration = 650,
  decimals = 0,
}: {
  value: number;
  className?: string;
  duration?: number;
  /** Decimal places (xPts carry one); integers round instead. */
  decimals?: number;
}) {
  const [progressValue, setProgressValue] = useState(value);
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const reduce =
      typeof window !== "undefined" && window.matchMedia?.("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
    if (reduce) {
      setProgressValue(value);
      return;
    }
    // Runs before paint, so the first frame is already 0 â€” no flash of the
    // final value before the count starts.
    setProgressValue(0);
    const start = performance.now();
    let frame = requestAnimationFrame(function tick(now) {
      const progress = Math.min(1, (now - start) / duration);
      const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3);
      setProgressValue(value * eased);
      if (progress < 1) frame = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    });
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, [value, duration]);
  const shown = decimals > 0 ? progressValue.toFixed(decimals) : String(Math.round(progressValue));
  return <Num className={className}>{shown}</Num>;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------- buttons */

type Variant = "primary" | "ghost" | "volt-outline";

const variants: Record<Variant, string> = {
  primary: "bg-volt text-black hover:brightness-95 active:brightness-90",
  ghost: "border border-line text-ink hover:bg-elevated",
  "volt-outline": "border border-volt text-volt hover:bg-elevated",
};

export function Button({
  variant = "primary",
  className,
  children,
  ...rest
}: ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & { variant?: Variant }) {
  return (
    <button
      {...rest}
      className={cx(
        "inline-flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full px-4 text-sm font-bold transition-[filter,background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] active:scale-[0.98] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40",
        variants[variant],
        className,
      )}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- navigation */

export function Segmented<T extends string>({
  value,
  options,
  onChange,
  className,
  /** Optional id prefix that wires the tabs to their panel:
   *  tab `${id}-${value}`, panel `${id}-panel`, tabs carry aria-controls.
   *  Call sites that use this as a real tab control (MatchDetail, TeamPage)
   *  give their panel the matching id + role="tabpanel"; filter-style uses
   *  omit it and stay plain buttons-with-role="tab" until arrow-key APG is
   *  what they want (they are toggles, so they don't). */
  id,
}: {
  value: T;
  options: Array<{ value: T; label: string; badge?: ReactNode }>;
  onChange: (value: T) => void;
  className?: string;
  id?: string;
}) {
  const { dir } = useI18n();
  const activeIndex = options.findIndex((option) => option.value === value);
  // The selected pill slides along the track instead of the background
  // snapping between tabs. Position and width are MEASURED from the active
  // button (not derived from 1/n): a crowded track scrolls and its tabs can
  // carry different widths (Cups, Ratings), so equal-width math would misalign.
  // Only the travel is animated (transform); width settles instantly.
  const trackRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const [indicator, setIndicator] = useState<{ start: number; width: number } | null>(null);
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const track = trackRef.current;
    if (!track || activeIndex < 0) {
      setIndicator(null);
      return;
    }
    const measure = () => {
      const tab = track.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[role="tab"]')[activeIndex];
      if (!tab) return;
      // The pill is position:absolute INSIDE the scroll container, so it scrolls
      // with the content â€” its offset has to be measured in the track's CONTENT
      // space, not its viewport space. A viewport offset is only correct while
      // scrollLeft is 0: scroll a crowded row and switch tabs, and the pill lands
      // `scrollLeft` pixels to the left of the active tab (off-screen entirely on
      // the 8-tab Ratings row). Adding the scroll puts both on the same axis.
      const origin = track.getBoundingClientRect().left + track.clientLeft;
      const rect = tab.getBoundingClientRect();
      setIndicator({ start: rect.left - origin + track.scrollLeft, width: rect.width });
    };
    measure();
    // Guarded: legacy webviews without ResizeObserver still get a correct
    // indicator, it just won't re-measure on container resize.
    if (typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined") return;
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(measure);
    observer.observe(track);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [activeIndex, options.length, dir]);
  // Roving focus for the APG tab pattern: the selected tab is the only tab
  // stop, and Arrow keys walk the tabs (visual order â€” mirrored in RTL),
  // selecting as they go. A tablist that accepts focus but ignores arrows
  // breaks the expectation role="tab" itself creates.
  const selectAndFocus = (index: number) => {
    const next = options[Math.max(0, Math.min(options.length - 1, index))];
    if (!next) return;
    if (next.value !== value) {
      haptic("light");
      onChange(next.value);
    }
    // Focus follows selection even when the value didn't change (at the ends).
    const track = trackRef.current;
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      track
        ?.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(`[data-tab-value="${next.value}"]`)
        ?.focus();
    });
  };
  const onTabKeyDown = (event: ReactKeyboardEvent) => {
    // Visual arrows, not logical: in an RTL row the visually-rightmost tab is
    // options[0], so ArrowRight walks backwards through the array.
    const rtl = dir === "rtl";
    if (event.key === "ArrowRight") selectAndFocus(activeIndex + (rtl ? -1 : 1));
    else if (event.key === "ArrowLeft") selectAndFocus(activeIndex + (rtl ? 1 : -1));
    else if (event.key === "Home") selectAndFocus(0);
    else if (event.key === "End") selectAndFocus(options.length - 1);
    else return;
    event.preventDefault();
  };

  // Scroll hint: when a crowded track actually overflows, a faint inline-end
  // fade says "there is more past this edge" without a visible arrow to
  // maintain. Recomputed on the same observer that measures the pill.
  const [scrolled, setScrolled] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const track = trackRef.current;
    if (!track || typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined") return;
    const check = () => setScrolled(track.scrollWidth > track.clientWidth + 2);
    check();
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(check);
    observer.observe(track);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [options.length]);

  return (
    <div
      ref={trackRef}
      role="tablist"
      className={cx(
        "relative flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface p-1",
        className,
      )}
    >
      {scrolled ? (
        <span
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="pointer-events-none sticky end-0 shrink-0"
          style={{
            width: 14,
            marginInlineEnd: -4,
            // The fade sits on the END edge, which flips in RTL â€” so the
            // gradient flips with it.
            background:
              dir === "rtl"
                ? "linear-gradient(to left, transparent, var(--bg-surface))"
                : "linear-gradient(to right, transparent, var(--bg-surface))",
          }}
        />        ) : null}
      {indicator ? (
        <span
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="pointer-events-none absolute top-1 bottom-1 rounded-lg bg-elevated shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_color-mix(in_srgb,var(--volt)_35%,transparent)] transition-transform duration-[var(--t-fast)] ease-[var(--ease-pop)]"
          style={{ left: 0, width: indicator.width, transform: `translateX(${indicator.start}px)` }}
        />
      ) : null}
      {options.map((option, index) => (
        <button
          key={option.value}
          data-tab-value={option.value}
          id={id ? `${id}-${option.value}` : undefined}
          aria-controls={id ? `${id}-panel` : undefined}
          type="button"
          role="tab"
          aria-selected={value === option.value}
          tabIndex={index === activeIndex ? 0 : -1}
          onKeyDown={onTabKeyDown}
          onClick={() => {
            if (value !== option.value) haptic("light");
            onChange(option.value);
          }}
          className={cx(
            // relative keeps each label painted above the sliding pill; flex-1
            // fills the track, and with whitespace-nowrap each tab's min-content
            // width is its label, so a crowded row (the five match tabs in
            // Persian) scrolls rather than breaking a word.
            "relative flex min-h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-3 text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)]",
            value === option.value ? "text-volt" : "text-ink-2 hover:text-ink",
          )}
        >
          {option.label}
          {option.badge}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------- data display */

/** Two-sided comparison bar â€” used for team stats and rating distributions.
 *  Sides wear their own club colour: teal would imply broadcast/live state. */
export function StatBar({
  label,
  home,
  away,
  homeColor = "var(--volt)",
  awayColor = "var(--text-muted)",
}: {
  label: string;
  home: number;
  away: number;
  homeColor?: string;
  awayColor?: string;
}) {
  const total = home + away || 1;
  const homePct = (home / total) * 100;
  // Seed at 0 and grow on the next frame, so the width transition actually
  // runs on mount instead of the final value painting in one step.
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = requestAnimationFrame(() => setReady(true));
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(id);
  }, []);
  return (
    <li className="space-y-1">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
        <Num className={cx("w-8", home >= away ? "text-ink" : "text-ink-2")}>{home}</Num>
        <span className="text-ink-3">{label}</span>
        <Num className={cx("w-8 text-end", away >= home ? "text-ink" : "text-ink-2")}>{away}</Num>
      </div>
      <div className="flex h-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-elevated">
        <span
          className="h-full rounded-s-full transition-[width] duration-[var(--t-std)] ease-[var(--ease-out)]"
          style={{ width: ready ? `${homePct}%` : "0%", backgroundColor: homeColor }}
        />
        <span className="h-full flex-1 rounded-e-full" style={{ backgroundColor: awayColor }} />
      </div>
    </li>
  );
}

/** Horizontal progress with an optional label row above it. */
export function ProgressBar({
  value,
  max = 100,
  tone = "volt",
  label,
  valueLabel,
}: {
  value: number;
  max?: number;
  tone?: "volt" | "gold" | "live";
  label?: string;
  valueLabel?: ReactNode;
}) {
  const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (value / max) * 100));
  const colors = { volt: "var(--volt)", gold: "var(--gold)", live: "var(--live)" };
  // Same as StatBar: paint full-width on mount, then animate down to value.
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = requestAnimationFrame(() => setReady(true));
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(id);
  }, []);
  return (
    <div className="space-y-1">
      {label || valueLabel ? (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-ink-2">
          <span>{label}</span>
          {valueLabel}
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <div className="h-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-elevated">
        <span
          className="block h-full rounded-full transition-[width] duration-[var(--t-slow)] ease-[var(--ease-out)]"
          style={{ width: ready ? `${pct}%` : "0%", backgroundColor: colors[tone] }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Gold star row â€” crowd ratings and bonus points only. */
export function Stars({ value, max = 10 }: { value: number; max?: number }) {
  const filled = Math.round((value / max) * 5);
  return (
    <span className="inline-flex gap-0.5" aria-label={`${value} / ${max}`}>
      {Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, index) => (
        <StarFilledIcon
          key={index}
          className={cx("h-3.5 w-3.5", index < filled ? "text-gold" : "text-ink-3/40")}
          aria-hidden="true"
        />
      ))}
    </span>
  );
}

/** Initial-based avatar. Team or user, never a photo dependency. */
export function Avatar({
  name,
  tone = "muted",
  size = "md",
}: {
  name: string;
  tone?: "volt" | "muted";
  size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg";
}) {
  const dims = { sm: "h-7 w-7 text-xs", md: "h-9 w-9 text-sm", lg: "h-12 w-12 text-lg" }[size];
  return (
    <span
      className={cx(
        "grid flex-none place-items-center rounded-full border font-extrabold",
        dims,
        tone === "volt" ? "border-volt text-volt" : "border-line text-ink-2",
      )}
      style={{ backgroundColor: "var(--bg-elevated)" }}
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      {name.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()}
    </span>
  );
}

/** Tappable settings-style row. */
export function ListRow({
  label,
  hint,
  right,
  onClick,
  onToggle,
  icon,
  danger,
}: {
  label: string;
  hint?: string;
  right?: ReactNode;
  onClick?: () => void;
  /**
   * Whole-row tap for a row whose control is a switch on the right: users tap
   * the label, not the 44Ã—24 track. The row stays a <div> so the switch inside
   * it is never a button nested in a button â€” the switch remains the real,
   * keyboard-operable control.
   */
  onToggle?: () => void;
  icon?: ReactNode;
  /** Destructive rows: the label carries the --live coral so the action's
      stakes are visible before the tap, not only after. */
  danger?: boolean;
}) {
  const Tag = onClick ? "button" : "div";
  const active = Boolean(onClick || onToggle);
  return (
    <Tag
      {...(onClick
        ? {
            type: "button" as const,
            onClick: () => {
              haptic("light");
              onClick();
            },
          }
        : onToggle
          ? { onClick: onToggle }
          : {})}
      className={cx(
        "flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-3 text-start",
        active
          ? "cursor-pointer transition-[color,background-color,transform] duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated active:scale-[0.98]"
          : "transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)]",
      )}
    >
      {icon ? <span className="flex-none text-ink-2">{icon}</span> : null}
      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <span
          className={cx(
            "block truncate text-sm font-bold",
            danger ? "text-danger" : "text-ink",
          )}
        >
          {label}
        </span>
        {hint ? <span className="text-xs text-ink-3">{hint}</span> : null}
      </span>
      {right}
    </Tag>
  );
}

/** Switch used by the notification settings. */
export function Switch({
  checked,
  onChange,
  label,
}: {
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: (next: boolean) => void;
  label: string;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      role="switch"
      aria-checked={checked}
      aria-label={label}
      onClick={(e) => {
        // The row can toggle too (ListRow.onToggle): without this the tap would
        // reach both handlers and cancel itself out.
        e.stopPropagation();
        haptic("light");
        onChange(!checked);
      }}
      // The track is a control, not a badge: rounded-lg keeps it in the
      // "secondary control" family with the other switches, and the knob
      // stays a circle on top of it.
      className="relative h-6 w-11 flex-none rounded-lg border border-line transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] after:absolute after:-inset-2.5 after:content-['']"
      style={{
        backgroundColor: checked ? "var(--volt)" : "color-mix(in srgb, var(--text-muted) 35%, transparent)",
      }}
    >
      <span
        className="absolute top-0.5 h-4 w-4 rounded-full bg-black/80 transition-[inset-inline-start] duration-[var(--t-fast)] ease-[var(--ease-pop)]"
        style={{ insetInlineStart: checked ? "22px" : "3px" }}
      />
    </button>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ loading */

/**
 * Empty-state emoji shorthand â†’ drawn icons. The design system's icon rule is
 * "drawn, never emoji" (see TimelineList's eventIcon), so these strings are
 * swapped for the shared SVG set here, once, instead of at every call site.
 * Unknown emoji are stripped so a new call site can never ship a system-font
 * glyph that shifts weight against the drawn set.
 */
const GLYPHS: Record<string, ReactNode> = {
  "âš½": <BallIcon />,
  "â±": <ClockIcon />,
  "ðŸ“Š": <ChartIcon />,
  "ðŸ“‹": <ListIcon />,
  "ðŸ‘¥": <ShirtIcon />,
  "ðŸ“ˆ": <ChartIcon />,
  "â­": <StarFilledIcon />,
};

export function EmptyState({
  title,
  hint,
  action,
  icon,
}: {
  title: string;
  hint?: string;
  action?: ReactNode;
  /** A small pictogram above the title. Call sites still pass their original
   *  emoji shorthand; the strings are swapped for the drawn icon set here so
   *  every empty state obeys the design system's "drawn, never emoji" icon
   *  rule without touching 8 call sites. */
  icon?: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rise-in pitch-watermark rounded-card border border-dashed border-line px-4 py-10 text-center">
      {icon ? (
        <div
          className="grid place-items-center text-ink-3 [&>svg]:h-7 [&>svg]:w-7"
          aria-hidden="true"
        >
          {typeof icon === "string" ? (GLYPHS[icon] ?? icon.replace(/[\u{1F300}-\u{1FAFF}\u{2300}-\u{23FF}\u{2600}-\u{27BF}\u{2B00}-\u{2BFF}]/gu, "")) : icon}
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <p className={cx(icon ? "mt-3" : undefined, "text-sm font-bold text-ink-2")}>{title}</p>
      {hint ? <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-ink-3">{hint}</p> : null}
      {action ? <div className="mt-4 flex justify-center">{action}</div> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * The third state: the fetch failed. Distinct from empty (which means "no
 * data" and would be a lie here) â€” honest about the failure, with a retry
 * wired to the query's refetch so the user is never stuck on the screen.
 */
export function ErrorState({ title, hint, onRetry }: { title?: string; hint?: string; onRetry: () => void }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  return (
    <div
      role="alert"
      className="rise-in pitch-watermark rounded-card border border-danger/40 px-4 py-10 text-center"
    >
      <p className="text-danger">
        <AlertIcon className="mx-auto h-6 w-6" />
      </p>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm font-bold text-ink">{title ?? t("error.title")}</p>
      {hint ? <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-ink-3">{hint}</p> : null}
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => {
          haptic("light");
          onRetry();
        }}
        className="mt-4 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-volt px-5 text-xs font-bold text-volt transition-colors duration-[var(--t-fast)] hover:bg-elevated"
      >
        <RefreshIcon />
        {t("common.retry")}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Small labels previewing prediction mechanics played on a match. */
const PREDICT_LABEL: Record<string, StringKey> = {
  lineup: "predict.lineup",
  shot: "predict.shot",
  sub: "predict.sub",
  watch: "predict.watch",
  versus: "predict.versus",
};

export function PredictionIcons({ kinds }: { kinds: string[] }) {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n();
  if (kinds.length === 0) return null;
  // Abbreviations stay as text (they are labels); the pictographic kinds get the
  // shared icon set so they don't shift weight with the system font.
  const text: Record<string, string> = { lineup: "XI", versus: "VS" };
  const Icon: Record<string, typeof StarFilledIcon> = {
    shot: TargetIcon,
    sub: SwapIcon,
    watch: StarFilledIcon,
  };
  const title = kinds.map((k) => t(PREDICT_LABEL[k] ?? "predict.lineup")).join(lang === "fa" ? "ØŒ " : ", ");
  return (
    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1" title={title}>
      {/* The glyphs are decorative; the joined names carry the meaning for
          screen readers (a title attribute alone is hover-only on touch). */}
      <span className="sr-only">{title}</span>
      {kinds.map((k) => {
        const Glyph = Icon[k];
        return (
          <span
            key={k}
            className="grid h-5 min-w-5 place-items-center rounded-full border border-volt px-1 text-xs font-bold text-volt"
            style={{ backgroundColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--volt) 12%, transparent)" }}
            aria-hidden="true"
          >
            {Glyph ? <Glyph className="h-3 w-3" /> : (text[k] ?? "â€¢")}
          </span>
        );
      })}
    </span>
  );
}

@import "tailwindcss";

/* Single source of truth for colour/type/motion tokens. */
@import "../tokens.css";

/*
  Map the design tokens onto Tailwind's theme namespaces so we get utilities like
  bg-surface / text-ink-2 / border-line / rounded-card / font-num.
  Names deliberately avoid clashing with the raw token names in tokens.css
  (e.g. `--font-num` instead of `--font-display`) so nothing self-references.
*/
@theme {
  --color-base: var(--bg-base);
  --color-surface: var(--bg-surface);
  --color-elevated: var(--bg-elevated);
  --color-line: var(--border-subtle);

  --color-ink: var(--text-primary);
  --color-ink-2: var(--text-secondary);
  --color-ink-3: var(--text-muted);
  --color-on-volt: var(--text-on-volt);

  --color-volt: var(--volt);
  --color-live: var(--live);
  --color-gold: var(--gold);
  --color-danger: var(--danger);
  --color-pitch: var(--pitch);

  /* Type scale â€” 4 steps + display, from the --type-* tokens. xs/sm mirror
     Tailwind's old defaults (12/14px) so existing uses are unchanged; lg
     firms to 22px, and md/display are the named steps call sites map to. */
  --text-xs: var(--type-xs);
  --text-sm: var(--type-sm);
  --text-md: var(--type-md);
  --text-lg: var(--type-lg);
  --text-display: var(--type-display);

  /* Vertical spacing scale â€” named steps map from the --space-* tokens.
     Tailwind's bare-number scale (mt-2, mt-4â€¦) still works unchanged; the
     named steps are what call sites use once a value carries intent. */
  --spacing-xs: var(--space-xs);
  --spacing-sm: var(--space-sm);
  --spacing-md: var(--space-md);
  --spacing-lg: var(--space-lg);
  --spacing-xl: var(--space-xl);
  --spacing-2xl: var(--space-2xl);

  --radius-card: 14px;
  --radius-e-card: var(--radius-card);
  --radius-s-card: var(--radius-card);

  --ease-out: cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
  --ease-pop: cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1);
}

@layer components {
  /* Rounded only the end / start side of the card radius â€” used by the clustered
     timeline band so the band rounds off the far edge without rounding the rail
     edge (which would clip the marker dot). Mirrors itself via logical sides, so
     the band flips to the right in the FA layout. */
  .rounded-s-card {
    border-start-start-radius: var(--radius-s-card);
    border-end-start-radius: var(--radius-s-card);
    border-start-end-radius: 0;
    border-end-end-radius: 0;
  }
  .rounded-e-card {
    border-start-start-radius: 0;
    border-end-start-radius: 0;
    border-start-end-radius: var(--radius-e-card);
    border-end-end-radius: var(--radius-e-card);
  }
}

    overscroll-behavior-y: none;
  }

  /* Telegram webview + mobile browser chrome: keep the app inside the viewport. */
  #root {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    min-height: 100svh;
  }

  :focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid var(--volt);
    outline-offset: 2px;
  }

  /* Latin numerals get tight tracking; Persian text must never (breaks joining). */
  html[lang="en"] .num-display {
    letter-spacing: -0.02em;
  }

  /* Persian: letter-spacing opens gaps in the joined script, and uppercase labels
     are a Latin idiom â€” every micro-label reads as a plain label instead.
     Vazirmatn at 11px is also below the Persian reading floor, so the one rule
     that flips the case lifts the size too. */
  html[lang="fa"] .label {
    font-size: 12px;
  }
  html[lang="fa"] .label-lg {
    font-size: 14px;
  }

  /* Persian floor: Vazirmatn at 9-11px was unreadable, and every micro-size
     arbitrary class is now the named text-xs (12px = the floor itself), so
     this override is a no-op kept as a belt-and-braces guarantee that FA
     never renders below 12px, wherever a future size creeps in. */
  html[lang="fa"] .text-xs {
    font-size: 12px;
  }

  /* "Floodlit Night": a teal wash from the top, amber from the bottom, so the
     canvas reads as a pitch under stadium lights rather than flat dark mode. */
  .floodlight {
    background:
      radial-gradient(90% 40% at 50% 0%, color-mix(in srgb, var(--volt) 8%, transparent), transparent 70%),
      radial-gradient(80% 30% at 50% 100%, color-mix(in srgb, var(--gold) 6%, transparent), transparent 70%),
      var(--bg-base);
  }
