      box-shadow:
        inset 3px 0 0 0 var(--live),
        0 0 18px color-mix(in srgb, var(--live) 30%, transparent);
      border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--live) 55%, transparent);
    }
    to {
      box-shadow:
        inset 3px 0 0 0 transparent,
        0 0 0 0 transparent;
      border-color: var(--border-subtle);
    }
  }

  /* FIX 8: the newest live timeline event pulses. The marker dot gets a
     continuous coral ring (box-shadow spread 0 â†’ 6px, fading out), and the card
     body gets a persistent 2px coral ring + a soft glow beneath it â€” so the
     live moment separates from the surrounding events at a glance. The card's
     own event colour (volt/gold/danger) is kept on the start accent edge; the
     live colour is reserved for the ring + glow so the two never fight.
     
     The keyframe body uses `var(--live)` directly (not color-mix) so Tailwind v4's
     CSS processor keeps the @keyframes block â€” color-mix() inside keyframe bodies
     is stripped by the processor, which would leave the class animationless. */
  .timeline-live-pulse {
    animation: timeline-live-pulse 1.6s var(--ease-out) infinite;
  }
  @keyframes timeline-live-pulse {
    0%, 100% {
      box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 var(--live);
    }
    50% {
      box-shadow: 0 0 0 6px transparent;
    }
  }

  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    .timeline-live-pulse {
      animation: none;
    }
  }

  /* Generic panel reveal for expanding sections. */
  .panel-in {
    animation: panel-in var(--t-std) var(--ease-out) both;
  }
  @keyframes panel-in {
    from {
      opacity: 0;
      transform: translateY(-4px);
    }
    to {
      opacity: 1;
      transform: none;
    }
  }

  /* Page transition: every top-level route renders inside this keyed wrapper so
     tab switches get a ~180ms fade+rise instead of an abrupt swap. Transform +
     opacity only; collapses to a hard cut under prefers-reduced-motion via the
     global 1ms rule. */
  .page-in {
    animation: page-in 180ms var(--ease-out) both;
  }
  @keyframes page-in {
    from {
      opacity: 0;
      transform: translateY(6px);
    }
    to {
      opacity: 1;
      transform: none;
    }
  }

  /* Signature animation 6: rating snap â€” 10px scale pulse on the number. */
  .rating-pulse {
    animation: rating-pulse var(--t-fast) var(--ease-pop);
  }
  @keyframes rating-pulse {
    50% {
      transform: scale(1.1);
    }
  }

  /* A pin that scored gets one celebratory pulse when the result lands. */
  .pin-scored {
    animation: rating-pulse var(--t-slow) var(--ease-pop);
  }

  /* Signature animation 2: score odometer â€” old digit up, new digit in. */
  .score-tick {
    animation: score-tick var(--t-std) var(--ease-out);
  }
  @keyframes score-tick {
    from {
      opacity: 0;
      transform: translateY(-70%);
    }
    to {
      opacity: 1;
      transform: none;
    }
  }

  /* One volt wash from the top, nothing from below: an element wears one accent
     (PRODUCT.md rule 2), and amber stays reserved for evaluation. */
  .score-hero {
    background:
      radial-gradient(120% 90% at 50% 0%, color-mix(in srgb, var(--volt) 9%, transparent), transparent 55%),
      var(--bg-surface);
  }
}

===== D:\football\tokens.css =====

/* Verdikick design tokens â€” v2 "Floodlit Night"
   Teal = broadcast / live / action. Amber = evaluative (points, ratings).
   They never mix on the same element. Red is locked to danger. */
:root {
  /* Dark native chrome: select dropdowns, scrollbars and form controls render
     with the dark UI instead of light browser defaults in webviews. */
  color-scheme: dark;
  scrollbar-color: var(--border-subtle) transparent;

  /* surfaces â€” green-black pitch under floodlights */
  --bg-base: #081613;
  --bg-surface: #0e2520;
  --bg-elevated: #122c25;
  --border-subtle: #2a4d44;
  --text-primary: #edf7f3;
  --text-secondary: #93aea6;
  /* Ink for text drawn ON a volt surface (the selected date chip, primary
     surfaces). The base canvas colour is the darkest thing in the palette
     and clears 8:1 on the volt fill; a light ink here would drop to 2:1. */
  --text-on-volt: var(--bg-base);
  /* #7B958C clears 4.5:1 on --bg-elevated as well as --bg-surface; the old
     #6E8880 failed AA there and this token carries every 10-11px hint. */
  --text-muted: #7b958c;

  /* accents â€” strict semantics */
  --volt: #1fbfa0; /* primary actions, your predictions, goal flash (broadcast teal) */
  --live: #ff5a4d; /* LIVE state only â€” dot, pulse, live timeline (coral red) */
  --gold: #f2b705; /* crowd ratings, points, bonus â€” evaluative amber */
  --danger: #ff4d5e; /* errors, wrong, red cards */
  --pitch: #23a066; /* turf: positive chips, shot plotter grid (tinted, never dominant) */

  /* type */
  --font-display: "Archivo", "Vazirmatn", sans-serif;
  --font-body: "Vazirmatn", "Archivo", sans-serif;

  /* motion */
  --t-fast: 150ms;
  --t-std: 250ms;
  --t-slow: 450ms;
  --ease-out: cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
  --ease-pop: cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1);

  /* Type scale â€” 4 steps + display. Named so call sites express intent,
     not pixels. (Named --type-* rather than --text-* because Tailwind v4
     owns the --text-* namespace for font sizes â€” a --text-* token here
     plus a --text-* mapping in @theme would self-reference and collapse.)
     Persian floor (12px) is enforced by the fa overrides in index.css;
     anything below 12 is lifted there automatically. */
  --type-xs: 12px;      /* micro-labels, meta, hints â€” the floor */
  --type-sm: 14px;      /* body text, team names, card primary */
  --type-md: 16px;      /* section emphasis, list-row labels */
  --type-lg: 22px;      /* card headers, screen subtitles */
  --type-display: 48px; /* score hero, login wordmark */

  /* Page gutter â€” shared horizontal space between content and screen edge.
     16px from each edge on a 360px webview is the modern mobile norm; the
     previous 12px (px-3) read as cramped. */
  --space-page-x: 16px;
  /* App content max-width â€” caps how wide the centered content column can grow on tablets, desktop Telegram, and Android's expanded Mini-App view. 560px left 20â€“40px empty bands on each side of those wider viewports. 768px fills any reasonable phone or tablet-portrait while still capping cards on truly wide displays. The header bar, body, and tab bar all read this so they align as one column. */
  --app-max-width: 768px;

  /* Vertical spacing scale â€” section breaks, card stacks, group gaps.
     Page gutter is --space-page-x (16px, horizontal). These are vertical. */
  --space-xs: 4px;    /* tight: inside a card, between label+value */
  --space-sm: 8px;    /* default: between match cards, chip rows */
  --space-md: 12px;   /* comfortable: between a header and its content */
  --space-lg: 16px;   /* section break: between groups on the feed */
  --space-xl: 24px;   /* page break: between major sections (Profile, Table) */
  --space-2xl: 32px;  /* screen-level: top of page before first content */

}
body {
  background: var(--bg-base);
  color: var(--text-primary);
  font-family: var(--font-body);
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}
.num-display {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 800;
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *,
  *::before,
  *::after {
    animation-duration: 1ms !important;
    transition-duration: 1ms !important;
  }
}
